patippurimi aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉittup patippurimikaḷiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēviyilli.

ēṣiyaṉ piriṇṭars,

ceṉṉi-5.





uṭtalippukaḷ

muṉṉuri

1. kuṟiñci

2. cērvarāyaṉ mali

3. kolli mali

4. nīlakiri malikaḷ

5. paḻaṉi malikaḷ

6. kuṟṟālam





﻿muṉṉuri

“oru coṟṟāeṭar eḻutuvataṟku nūṟu nūlkaḷ paṭittēṉ. oru varuṇaṉi eḻutuvataṟku nūṟu kal cuṟṟup payaṇam ceytēṉ” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭāṉ āṅkilac collēruḻavaṉ mekālē. itip paṭittavuṭaṉ eṉṉiyaṟiyāmal oruvaki uḷḷakkiḷarcci peṟṟēṉ. ‘nāmum ēṉ tamiḻakattic cuṟṟivarakkūṭātu?’ eṉṟa eṇṇam eṉ uḷḷattil ēṟpaṭṭatu. mutaṉ mutalāka iyaṟki eḻilmikka tamiḻakattu malikaḷic cuṟṟip pārkkavēṇṭum eṉṟu muṭivuceytēṉ. inta muṭivu eṉ uḷḷattil karukkoṇṭa poḻutu, ippayaṇatti ōr iṉpap payaṇamākavē mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉittēṉ. āṉāl puṟappaṭum nērattil eṉ uḷḷam māṟiviṭṭatu. ippayaṇattip payaṉuḷḷa vaḻiyil celaviṭṭāl eṉṉa eṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ. ataṉ viḷivē innūl. āṉāl mutalil eṉ uḷḷattil karukkoṇṭa kuṟikkōḷ ﻿muṟṟup peṟāmal pōyviṭṭatu. varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷiyum, puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷiyum tēṭittirinta kāraṇattāl iyaṟki eḻilil eṉ uḷḷatti muḻukka muḻukkat tōyaviṭṭu, iṉpam nukara muṭiyāmal pōyviṭṭatu. akkuṟiyi niṟivu ceyya, nāṉ mīṇṭumorumuṟi tamiḻakattu malikaḷic cuṟṟittāṉ ākavēṇṭum.

innūl veḷivarap pala naṇparkaḷ eṉakkup pērutavi purintaṉar. avarkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavar cēlam māvaṭṭa viḷampara atikāriyum, eṉ keḻutaki naṇparumāṉa tiru. T. M. kāḷiyappā, M. A. avarkaḷ. ciṟanta mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷi avar eṉakku aritiṉ muyaṉṟu tēṭittantār. nīlakirik kāṭukaḷiṉ uṭpakutiyil nuḻintu pala ceytikaḷi aṟivataṟkum, tōṭarkaḷ vāḻum toṭṭapeṭṭā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu avarkaḷ paṇpāṭṭi aṟivataṟkum eṉakkup peruntuṇi purinta paṭakā iḷiñaṉāṉa ‘liṅkamūrtti’yi eṉṉāl iṅkuk kuṟippiṭāmal irukka muṭiyavilli. avviḷiñaṉ eṉṉik kutiri mītu amartti, eḷitil ceṉṟaṭiya muṭiyāta iṭaṅkaṭkellām iṭṭuc ceṉṟu, pala ariya ceytikaḷi aṟivataṟkup peruntuṇi purintāṉ.

innūl ciṟanta muṟiyil acciṭappaṭṭu uṅkaḷ kiyil vaṇṇamalarāy, virintiruppataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉavar, matippiṟkuriya tiru. paḻaṉiyappā avarkaḷē aṉṉārkku eṉ naṉṟi.

murukucuntaram

cēlam

1-12-61





uṭtalippukaḷ

muṉṉuri

1. kuṟiñci

2. cērvarāyaṉ mali

3. kolli mali

4. nīlakiri malikaḷ

5. paḻaṉi malikaḷ

6. kuṟṟālam





uṭtalippukaḷ

muṉṉuri

1. kuṟiñci

2. cērvarāyaṉ mali

3. kolli mali

4. nīlakiri malikaḷ

5. paḻaṉi malikaḷ

6. kuṟṟālam





﻿3. kolli mali



peyark kāraṇam :

‘kolli mali’ eṉṟa peyar iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpiruntē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. puṟanāṉuṟu, naṟṟiṇi, cilappatikāram, cīvaka cintāmaṇi mutaliya nūlkaḷil im mali paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ip peyariṉ kāraṇamākak koḷḷakkūṭiya ceyti oṉṟu uḷatu. atutāṉ kollip pāvi paṟṟiyatākum. kollip pāviyit taṉṉiṭattē koṇṭirunta mali kolli mali eṉak koḷḷiṉ ēṟpuṭittām.



kollip pāvi :

itu kolli maliyil ceytu vikkappaṭṭirunta teyvap paṭimam. ‘avuṇar koṭiya pōr ceyyac camitta pōrk kōlattuṭaṉ mōkittu viḻumpaṭi, kollip pāvi vaṭivāyc ceyyavaḷākiya tirumakaḷāl āṭappaṭṭa pāvi eṉṉuṅ kūttu’ (cilap. 6 : 60-1) eṉa varuvatu kollip pāviyiṉ iyalpi viḷakkum. cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil vicayikkup pāviyi uvamiyākat tiruttakka tēvar kūṟiyataṉ nayatti viḷakka vanta nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, ‘vēṟu karuttuc cellāmal taṉṉiyē karutuvittaliṉ kollip pāvi eṉṟār’ eṉak kūṟum viḷakkamum itaṟku ātaravākiṉṟatu.

itu kolli maliyil oru pūtattāl amikkappaṭṭa aḻakiya pāvi eṉpataṉip ‘payaṉ niṟinta palā maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya kolli maliyiṉ mēṟpakutiyil muṉpu pūtam amitta putumiyāṉa iyakkamuṭiya pāvi iḷa veyiliṉkaṇ tōṉṟiṉālotta niṉ aḻakiya nalam’ (naṟ. 192) eṉa varun toṭar viḷakkum. maṟṟum itu aḻiyāta iyalpiṉateṉpati, ‘cevviya vērp palaviṉ payaṉ niṟinta kolli maliyilē, teyvattāṟ kākkappeṟum kuṟṟam nīṅkiya nīṇṭa koṭumuṭiyiyum aḻakiya veḷḷiya aruviyiyum uṭiya mēl variyilē, kāṟṟu mōti aṭittālum, mikka maḻi kaṭumiyākap peytālum, pēriṭi tākkiṉālum, vēṟu pala keṭutikaḷ uṇṭāṉālum, perunilaṅ kiḷarntālum taṉ aḻakiya nalla uruvam keṭāta pāvi’ (naṟ. 201) eṉṉuṅ karuttu viḷakkukiṟatu.

ik kollip pāvi paṟṟi vaḻaṅkum katiyoṉṟuṇṭu. kolli maliyiṉ vaḷamikunta niliyik kaṇṭu muṉivarum amararum itaṉi iruppiṭamākak koṇṭaṉar. appōtu avarkaḷukku acurarkaḷālum, irākkatarālum iṭiyūṟu nērntatu. ataṉit taṭukkat teyvataccaṉik koṇṭu ippāviyi amittaṉar. iyaṅkum taṉmiyuḷḷa ip pāvi arakkar mutaliyōriṉ vāṭi paṭṭavuṭaṉ nakikkum. kaṇṭavaruṭiya uḷḷattiyum viḻiyiyum kavarntu, avarkaṭkup peruṅ kāma nōyi uṟuvittu, mayakkik kollum taṉmiyuṭiya itaṉik kaṇṭa arakkar mutaliyōr mayaṅki maṭiyalāyiṉar.



amippu:

kolli mali tamiḻ nāṭṭil cēlam māvaṭṭattil, nāmakkal āttūr vaṭṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kuṉṟut toṭar. im maliyāṉatu, tammampaṭṭi variyil nīṇṭu cellum tuṟiyūrp paḷḷattākkāl pacci malikaḷiṉiṉṟum pirikkappaṭukiṟatu. ayilpaṭṭik kaṇavāyiṉāl pōta malikaḷiṉiṉṟum pirik﻿kappaṭukiṟatu. ivvaḻakiya toṭar, teṉvaṭalākap patiṉeṭṭuk kallum, kiḻa mēṟkākap paṉṉiraṇṭu kallum nīḷamuṭiyatu. im maliyiṉ teṉ carivum, mēṟkuc carivum, kiḻakkuc carivum camaveḷiyiṉiṉṟum 4000 aṭi ceṅkuttāka uyarntu cellukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭakkuc carivu kāṭṭāṟukaḷāl aṟuttuc cellap paṭṭa varici variciyāṉa paṭukikaḷ niṟintu piḷavupaṭṭuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ip paṭukikaḷ vaṭakiḻakkup pakkamāka ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavi, varakūr kōmpi, mūlikkuricci, periya kōmpi, vālak kōmpi eṉpaṉa. nāmakkal kōṭṭattilirukkum im malip pakuti uyarnta oru pīṭapūmiyit taṉṉakattil koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ap pīṭapūmiyāṉatu oru kavikalaṉ (basin) pōl naṭuvil tāḻntum, pakkaṅkaḷil uyarntum uḷḷatu. uyarntu cellum itaṉ pakkaṅkaḷil aṭukkaṭukkāka nilaṅkaḷip paṇpaṭuttip payirt toḻil ceykiṟārkaḷ. payirkaḷ vaḷarntu, paccip pacēleṉṟu eḻil nalam koḻittu viḷaṅkum ak kāṭciyippōl vēṟu eṅkum kāṇṭal aritu.

āttūr vaṭṭattilirukkum kolli malip pakuti nāmakkal pakutiyiṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭatākum. im maliyiṉ teṉmēṟkup pakutiyāṉatu payil nāṭ (Pail Nad)ṭiṉ paruvuyar cikaraṅkaḷik koṇṭatu. ic cikaraṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum vaṭa cariviluḷḷa paḷḷattākkiṉ iṉiya kāṭcikaḷik kāṇalām. aruvikaḷ accarivil iṉiya oliyōṭu iḻintu cellum. acciṟṟāṟṟup paṭukikaḷiṉ ucciyik kaṭantu cellum mali vaḻip pātiyiliruntu nōkkiṉāl camaveḷikaḷiyum, avaṟṟi vaṭakkil taṭuttu niṟuttum cērvarāyaṉ, kalrāyaṉ, tēṉānti (Tenande) malikaḷiṉ iyaṟki yaḻakiyum kaṇṭu makiḻalām. mēṟkiluḷḷa pīṭapūmiyiṉ ucci kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 4000 aṭi uyaramuḷḷatu. vaṭamēṟkup pīṭapūmiyiṉ ucci 400 aṭi tāḻntatu. vaṭakkiluḷḷa paḷḷattākkukaḷip pirikkum malittoṭar 3000 aṭi uyaramuḷḷatu. āttūr vaṭṭattuk kolli maliyiluḷḷa mika uyarnta cikaram vēṭakkāra maliyākum. ataṉ uyaram 4,663 aṭi.



āṟukaḷ :

cērvarāyaṉ malittoṭaril tōṉṟum āṟukaḷ pala. āṉāl kolli maliyil āṟukaḷ atikamilli. cuvēta nati, vaciṭṭa nati eṉṟa ciṟṟāṟukaḷ kolli mali, pacci mali, kalrāyaṉ malikaḷil peyyum maḻi nīriṉiṉṟum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. cuvēta nati eṉṟa peyar vaṭa coṟkaḷālāṉatu. atit tamiḻākkum pōtu ‘veḷḷāṟu’ eṉṟu amiyum. āṉāl cuvēta nati, vaciṭṭa nati ākiya iraṇṭiyum veḷḷāṟu eṉṟē ellōrum aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl putu veḷḷak kālaṅkaḷil, im māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa maṟṟa āṟukaḷiviṭa mikavum cenniṟamāṉa nīri, ivikaḷ koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. veḷḷāṟu eṉṟa peyar ivikaṭkup poruntāta oṉṟu eṉṟu tāṉ kūṟa vēṇṭum. cuvēta nati eṉṟa peyarukkuk kāraṇamāka oru kati vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. pañca pāṇṭavaril oruvaṉāṉa aruccuṉaṉ teṉṉāṭṭiṟku yāttiri vantiruntapōtu kolli maliyi aṭintāṉām. cuvēta nati tōṉṟumiṭattil oru pūci naṭatta virumpiṉāṉ. āṉāl pūcikku vēṇṭiya nīrilli. taṉ kāṇṭīpattil kaṇi toṭuttup pāṟimītu eytāṉ. uṭaṉē avviṭattil oru piḷavu ēṟpaṭṭu ataṉiṉṟum nīr peruki āṟāka ōṭat toṭaṅkiyatu. aruccuṉaṉukkuc ‘cuvēta vākaṉaṉ’ eṉṟa oru peyarum uṇṭu. cuvētavākaṉaṉāl koṇarap paṭṭa nati cuvēta natiyāyiṟṟu.

vaciṭṭa nati, vaciṭṭa muṉivariṉ peyarāl ēṟpaṭṭatu. avar in natik kariyil pēḷūrukkarukil oru vēḷvi ceytār eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. avvūrukku vaṭakkil veṇmiyāṉa pāṟiyoṉṟu uḷḷatu. vaciṭṭar ceyta vēḷviyāl viḷinta cāmpalē av veṇmiyāṉa pāṟiyāka māṟiviṭṭateṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vaciṭṭa natikkum pērāṟu eṉṟa vēṟu peyarum uṇṭu.



kāṭukaḷ :

kolli maliyiṉ mītuḷḷa kāṭukaḷ vaḷamāṉavi. im maliyiliruntu iḻintu varum ciṟṟāṟukaḷiṉ karikaḷil mikavum aṭarttiyāṉa, pacumi māṟāta iḷa marak kāṭukaḷ niṟintuḷḷaṉa. cēlam māvaṭṭak kāṭukaḷil kiṭikkak kūṭiya mukkiyamāṉa malipaṭu poruḷ mūṅkil. im mūṅkilkaḷ veṭṭap paṭṭu īrōṭu, karūr, ceṉṉi mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷukku niṟinta aḷavil ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

mūṅkilik koṇṭu uṟutiyāṉa taṭṭi (paṭal) kaḷ ceykiṉṟaṉar. mūṅkiliṉ veḷippuṟattil pacumiyākavum uṟutiyākavum uḷḷa pākam taṉiyākap piritteṭukkappaṭṭu it taṭṭikaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vīṭṭu maṟippukaḷukku itu peritum payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. mēlum mūṅkilāl periya pāykaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷ virippukaḷākavum, pantalkaḷākavum payaṉpaṭum. kāḵpi nāṟṟu (Seedling)kaḷukkāka 3 aṅkulak kuṟukkaḷavum, 9 aṅkula uyaramumuḷḷa ciṟu kūṭikaḷ niṟiyac ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi āyiram, 50 rūpāykku viṟkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. māmpaḻam, ārañcup paḻam, mīṉ, roṭṭi mutaliyavaṟṟic cētamillāmal aṉuppa mūṅkiṟ kūṭikaḷ periya aḷavil niṟiyat tēvippaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vayalukku nīr iṟik﻿kum iṟi kūṭikaḷ, kōḻik kūṭikaḷ, paṟavik kūṭukaḷ, viciṟikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum mūṅkililiruntu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ it toḻilāl vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.

cantaṉa maraṅkaḷ iṅku niṟiya uṇṭu. immaliyil kiṭikkum taḻikaḷic cēkarittu, tirucci māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa nañci nilaṅkaḷukku uramākap payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. kolli malit tēṉ, ilakkiyap pukaḻ peṟṟatu. kollit talivaṉ oruvaṉip pārāṭṭap pukunta oru pulavar, am malipaṭu tēṉiṉ ciṟappukku mutaliṭm koṭuttu, “kolli malit tēṉ coriyum koṟṟavā!” eṉṟu viḷakkiṉār.



vilaṅkukaḷ :

tamiḻ nāṭṭu malikaḷil potuvākak kāṇappaṭum karaṭi (Sloth bear) kolli maliyilum niṟiyak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl aṅku vāḻum maliyāḷikaḷ avaṟṟik kolvatilli. kāraṇam, irāmapirāṉiṉ ilaṅkip paṭiyeṭuppiṉpōtu uṟṟa naṇpaṉāka uṭaṉiruntu utaviya jāmpavāṉiṉ vaḻi vantavikaḷ eṉṟu karutik karaṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. kolli maliyiṉ aṭivāraṅkaḷil karutta variyāṭukaḷ kūṭṭaṅkūṭṭamākat tiriyum. avi accam mikkavi. yārāvatu accuṟuttiviṭṭāl, nāṉkintu kal tolivu ōṭiya piṟakutāṉ avi niṟkum. kāṭṭup paṉṟikaḷum iṅku niṟiya uṇṭu. avaṟṟiṉ iṟicciyi maliyāḷikaḷ virumpi uṇpar. ituvum tirumāliṉ avatāramtāṉ ! ellāvaṟṟiyum iṟivaṉiṉ avatāram eṉṟu otukki viṭṭāl kāṭṭil vāḻum maliyāḷikaḷ pāvam, etittāṉ uṇpar...... !



payirt toḻil:

cērvarāyaṉ mali, nīlakiri mali, paḻaṉi malikaḷōṭu oppiṭumpōtu, kolli mali muṉṉēṟṟattil mikavum piṉtaṅkiyatu eṉṟutāṉ colla vēṇṭum. maṟṟa malikaḷil utakamaṇṭalam, ērkkāṭu, kūṉūr, kōṭikkāṉal mutaliya kuṟiñci nakaraṅkaḷ tōṉṟi, mikunta nākarikam peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. aḻakiya malip pātikaḷ camaveḷikaḷiyum im malikaḷiyum iṇikkiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟiya nākarika vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiya ellā vacatikaḷiyum in nakaraṅkaḷ peṟṟu, piṟa nāṭṭu ullācikaḷiyum, tamiḻakattuc celvarkaḷiyum tampāl īrkkiṉṟaṉa. kāḵpi, tēyili, irappar, paḻavakikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit tampāl viḷivittu nāṭṭiṉ varuvāyip perukkukiṉṟaṉa. pala toḻiṟcālikaḷit tammakaktē koṇṭu toḻilvaḷam ciṟakkat tuṇipurikiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl ittakiya muṉṉēṟṟam kolli maliyil illi. kollimali iṉṉum pūraṇamāka ārāyappaṭavilli eṉṟē collalām. pōtiya pāti vacati kiṭiyātu. ippoḻututāṉ pāti amittu muṭittirukkiṉṟaṉar.

āṉāl cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷa maliyāḷikaḷi viṭak kolli maliyāḷikaḷ uḻippāḷikaḷ. cērvarāyaṉ maliyil kāḵpit tōṭṭaṅkaḷ niṟiya iruppatāl, maliyāḷikaḷellām taṅkaḷ payirt toḻiliviṭṭu viṭṭuk kāḵpittōṭṭak kūlikaḷāyp paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar. kāraṇam tāmākap payirceytu campātippativiṭak kūlikaḷāka vēliceyvatil atika varuvāy avarkaḷukkuk kiṭṭukiṟatu. kolli maliyāḷikaḷukku ittakiya vāyppuk kiṭiyātu. kuṟiñci nilattip paṇpaṭuttit tam pēruḻippāl payirt toḻilic ciṟanta muṟiyil ceytu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ﻿camaveḷiyil vāḻpavarkaḷiviṭac ciṟanta muṟiyil uḻavu purikiṉṟaṉar immaliyāḷikaḷ.

kollimaliyiṉ mītuḷḷa puñci nilaṅkaḷi iraṇṭu vitaṅkaḷākap pirikkalām. ēriṉāl uḻutu paṇpaṭuttum nilatti ‘uḻavukkāṭu’ eṉṟum, kaḷik koṭṭāl kottip paṇṭuttum nilattik ‘kottuk kāṭu’ eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nañcinilap payirt toḻilum kolli maliyil uṇṭu. 1000 ēkkarukku mēṟpaṭṭa nañci nilaṅkaḷ iṅkuḷḷaṉa. mika uyarnta pīṭapūmikaḷilē innilaṅkaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. malimītuḷḷa īrattāl ūṟivarum ocumpalnīr, innilaṅkaḷil pāykiṟatu. cila nañci nilaṅkaḷ paḷḷattākkukaḷil amintuḷḷaṉa. ap paḷḷattākkukaḷil eppoḻutum nīr kacintu maṇ īramākavē irukkum. avviṭaṅkaḷip paṇpaṭutti, vayalkaḷākki viṭukiṉṟaṉar. avviṭattil kaciyum nīr vayalkaḷil eppoḻutum tēṅkiniṟkum. tāḻnta pīṭapūmikaḷiṉ mītum nañci nilaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. maliyucciyiliruntu ōṭivarum aruvikaḷit taṭuttu niṟuttic ciṟuciṟu kālvāykaḷ mūlamāka an nañci nilaṅkaḷiluḷḷa nel vayalkaḷukkup pāyccukiṟārkaḷ.



nel :

eppoḻutum īram kacintu koṇṭirukkum paḷḷattākkukaḷil amikkappaṭṭirukkum nañci nilaṅkaḷ āḻamāṉa cēṟṟuk kuḻikaḷik koṇṭirukkum. ak kuḻikaḷil cila camayaṅkaḷil uḻavarkaḷ kaḻuttaḷavukūṭa mūḻkiviṭuvatuṇṭu. avvāṟu āḻvatit taṭukkavē nāṟṟu naṭumpōtu palakikaḷiṉ mēl iruntukoṇṭu naṭuvārkaḷ. uyarnta pīṭapūmikaḷil irukkum nañci nilaṅkaḷ eḷitil uḻak kūṭiyavi yākiyāl āṇṭukku irupōkam viḷiyum. āṉāl tāḻnta pīṭapūmikaḷiluḷḷa catuppu﻿nila nañcikaḷ, uḻavut tolliyiṉ kāraṇamāka āṇṭukku oru pōkamē viḷiyum. oru pōkamē viḷintālum muṉkūṟiya nañci nilaṅkaḷiviṭa itu irumaṭaṅku atikamāka viḷiccalik koṭukkum.



vāḻi :

kolli maliyil payil nāṭṭiṟku mēṟkil uḷḷa carivukaḷ aruviyiṉ nīrvaḷattāl ceḻippuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avvaḷatti nalla muṟiyil payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu niṟiya vāḻi maraṅkaḷip payirceykiṉṟaṉar. aṅku atikamāka viḷiyak kūṭiyatu ‘irastāli’ vāḻi. kolli maliyil mikavum uyarnta rakap paḻamāṉa ‘karuvāḻi’yum niṟiya viḷikiṉṟatu. itu mikac cuviyāṉatu. āṉāl vili atikam. ‘paṭṭu vāḻi’ eṉṟu collak kūṭiya maṟṟāeru vaki vāḻiyum iṅkup payir ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. itu nīḷamākavum cenniṟamākavum irukkum.



irāki :

kollimaliyil viḷiyum mukkiyap payirkaḷil irākiyum oṉṟu. mē allatu jūṉ tiṅkaḷkaḷil nilaṅkaḷ uḻappaṭṭu, irāki vitikkappaṭukiṟatu. nāṉku aintu tiṅkaḷkaḷ kaḻittu, itu aṟuvaṭi ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. aṟukkumpōtu oru kōḻic cēvaliyō, āṭṭiyō paliyiṭṭu ataṉ kurutiyil veṇcōṟṟik kalantu vayalkaḷil iṟippar. piṟaku katirkaḷi aṟuttuc cila nāṭkaḷ veyilil ulartti māṭukaḷāl mitikkac ceytu, maṇikaḷit taṉiyē piritteṭuppar. katir aṟuttup pattu nāṭkaḷ kaḻintatum ataṉ tāḷi aṟukkāmal tīyiṭṭu erittu viṭuvar. ﻿

mocci :

kollimaliyil irāki vayalkaḷil varici variciyāka mocci payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu. āmaṇakkōṭum, kaṭukōṭumkūṭa itu cila camayaṅkaḷil payiriṭappaṭum. itaṉuṭiya ilikaḷ nilattiṟku nalla uramākum. jūli ākasṭut tiṅkaḷkaḷil mocci vitikaḷ ūṉṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru tiṅkaḷ kaḻintatum, maṇṇik kotti viṭuvar. maṟṟumōr tiṅkaḷ kaḻintatum kaḷiyeṭuppar. mocci āṟumātap payir. irāki aṟuvaṭiyāṉatum ippayir vayalil paravippaṭarum. mikupaṉik kālaṅkaḷāṉa ṭicampar caṉavarit tiṅkaḷkaḷil malarat toṭaṅkum; piṟaku kāykkum. kāykaḷ ellām pacumiyāka irukkumpōtu aṟuvaṭi ceyya māṭṭārkaḷ. avikaḷ naṉṟāka ularnta piṟaku caṉavari iṟutiyilō allatu ḵpepruvari mutalilō aṟuppar. cila nāṭkaḷ maṟupaṭiyum naṉṟākak kāya viṭuvar. ataṉ piṟaku kāykaḷit taṉiyē piritteṭuttu māṭukaḷiṉ kālil mitikka viṭṭō, allatu taṭiyāl aṭittō mocciyit taṉiyākap piritteṭuppar.





kaṉipporuḷkaḷ

cēlam māvaṭṭattil pūmiyil putintu kiṭakkum kaṉip poruḷkaḷil irumputtātu kuṟippiṭattakka oṉṟu eṉpati mutalilēyē kūṟiṉōm. kolli maliyil aḷavaṟṟa irumputtātu maṇṭikkiṭakkiṟatu. irumpu urukkum toḻil cēlam māvaṭṭa makkaḷukkuk kivanta oru kali; toṉmiyāṉa kali. cēlam, āttūr, ōmalūr, tirucceṅkōṭu ākiya kōṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, ōcūr, kiruṭṭiṉakiri elliyilum ittoḻil mikavum vaḷarcci peṟṟiruntatu. āṉāl irumpi urukkuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttap﻿paṭṭa eriporuḷiṉ (kari) vili aḷavukku mīṟi uyarntatālum, veḷināṭukaḷiliruntu iṅku iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭa irumpu mikavum malivākak kiṭittatālum, it toḻilāṉatu cēlam māvaṭṭattil ciṟukac ciṟuka aḻintu viṭṭatu.

cēlam māvaṭṭattil irumpit tātuviliruntu piritteṭukkap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa uli kaḷimaṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. oru kaṇṇāṭip puṭṭiyippōl tōṟṟamaḷikkum avvuli, 4 aṭi uyaramum, aṭittaḷattil 2 aṭi kuṟukkaḷavum, ucciyil 9 aṅkulak kuṟukkaḷavum koṇṭatākum. avvuliyiṉ aṭittaḷam pūmiyiṉ maṭṭattiliruntu 1/2 aṭi tāḻntirukkum. attaḷattil 10 aṅkula caturamāṉa oruvaḻi irukkum. avvuliyil pātippakutiyik kariyāl nirappi, ataṟku mēl irumput tātik koṭṭuvar. āṭṭut tōlāl ceyta turuttiyoṉṟi avvuliyil poruttiyiruppar. atturuttiyi amukkik kāṟṟi uliyiṉuḷ celuttuvar. uliyiṉ maṟṟa vaḻikaḷellām paccik kaḷimaṇṇāl mūṭappaṭum. ivvāṟu 3 1/2 maṇi nēram ulikkāṟṟi ūtik koṇṭiruntāl 12 rāttal eṭiyuḷḷa irumpu veḷippaṭum. itu tāṉ paṇṭiya muṟi.

je. em. hīt (J. M. Heath) eṉṟa ōr āṅkilēyar, kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉiyiṉ vaṇikattuṟip poṟuppāḷarākac cēlattil iruntār. cēlam māvaṭṭattil putintu kiṭakkum irumputtātuviṉ vaḷatti aṟinta avar ki. pi. 1825 ām āṇṭu kampeṉiyiṉ aluvaliliruntu vilaki, irumput tāti eṭuttu ākka vēlikkup payaṉpaṭuttum tuṟiyil kalvipayila iṅkilāntu ceṉṟār. 1830 il intiyāviṟkut tirumpi, teṉ āṟkāṭu māvaṭṭattil ‘pōrṭṭō nōvā irumput toḻiṟ﻿cāli’ (Porto Novo Iron Company) eṉṟa oṉṟit tuvakkiṉār.

ki.pi. 1858 il ‘kiḻakkintiya irumput toḻiṟcāliyum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. cēlam māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa irumputtātu tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu. kāvirik kariyiluḷḷa pūlām paṭṭiyil irumpurukkum ulikaḷ ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. kañca maliyiṉiṉṟum veṭṭi eṭukkappaṭṭa irumputtātu 23 kal toliviluḷḷa pūlāmpaṭṭikkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭatu. avvulikaḷil piritteṭukkappaṭṭa irumpu mikavum uyarnta rakamāṉatu. tuḷip pālaṅ (Tubular bridge) kaḷum, vaḷintu koṭukkum pālaṅkaḷum (Suspension bridge) amikka avvirumpu mikavum ēṟṟatāka iruntatu. pūlāmpaṭṭi ulikku vēṇṭiya kari (Charcoal) kāvirikkappāl 18 kal toliviluḷḷa cōḻappāṭiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭatu. avviṭattil kariyāṉatu niṟinta aḷavil avikkappaṭṭuk kāviriyāṟṟiṉ mūlam paṭakukaḷil pūlām paṭṭikku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. oruṭaṉkari rū 6 kkup peṟappaṭṭatu. āṉāl oḻuṅkāṉa muṟiyil kiṭikkavilli. cōḻappāṭiyil kari avikkum toḻilāḷarkaḷukkuk kāṭṭiṉ taṭpa veppa nili ottu varāta kāraṇattāl, aṭikkaṭi avarkaḷ nōyvāyppaṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl ulivēliyum taṭippaṭṭatu.

19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil cēlam māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa irumputtātup paṭivaṅkaḷ virivāṉa muṟiyil vāṇipa nōkkōṭu ārāyappaṭṭaṉa. putuvitamāṉa pēruli (blast furnaces) kaḷi niṟuvi ittoḻili varuvāyuṭiyatāka ākkuvataṟkuriya vaḻivakikaḷiyum puḷḷivivaraṅkaḷiyum āyntaṉar. oruṭaṉ irumpu uruvāvataṟku 3 1/2 ṭaṉ kari tēvippaṭum eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷuṭiya ﻿kaṇakkuppaṭi 1 ṭaṉ tūymiyaṟṟa irumpi uṟpatti ceyya 8 1/2 ēkar kāṭu aḻikkappaṭavēṇṭum. 1 ṭaṉ tūymiyākkappaṭṭa irumpu (wrought iron or steel) uṟpatti ceyya 35 ēkar kāṭu aḻikkappaṭa vēṇṭum. eṉavē eriporuḷ taṭṭuppāṭṭāl irumpurukkum toḻil cēlam māvaṭṭattil kiviṭap paṭṭatu.

kollimaliyilum cēlam māvaṭṭattiṉ maṟṟap pakutikaḷilum paṭintirukkum irumputtātu mikavum tūymiyāṉatu. kantakak kalappum, pāsparas kalappum (Sulphur and phospherous) aṟṟatu. eṉavē itiliruntu mikavum uyarntarakamāṉa eḵku uṟpatti ceyya iyalum. alekcāntar kālattiliruntu mārkkōpōlō kālam variyil nam nāṭu eḵku uṟpattiyil peyar peṟṟiruntatu. cēlam māvaṭṭattil kūṭa eḵkut toḻil mikavum ciṟappuṟṟiruntatu. 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil cēlattil vāḻnta [1] aruṇācala ācāriyiṉāl tūya eḵku koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa vēṭṭik katti (hunting knives) kaḷukkum, kuttuk kōlkaḷukkum (pig-sticking lances) intiya nāṭu muḻuvatum nalla varavēṟpiruntatu. veḷḷi allatu taṅka mulām pūcappaṭṭa ‘ciṟuttippal’ (Cheetah tooth) eṉṟa maṭakkuk katti (Pen knives) kaḷ ceyyum toḻil ceṉṟa cila āṇṭukaḷ vari cēlattil ciṟappāka iruntatu.

ittakiya nuṇ kaliyāṟṟal niṟinta cēlam māvaṭṭak kaliñarkaḷiṉ kikaḷ cōrntu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. irumputtātu pūmikkaṭiyil uṟaṅkukiṟatu. āṉāl ivvuṟakkam iṉi atika nāḷ nīṭikkātu. neyvēli nilakkari, kañcamali irumpukkut tañcamaḷikkum. kolli mali taṉ kaṉivaḷattit tamiḻariṉ ﻿toḻil vaḷattiṟkuk kāṇikkiyākkum. cēraṉ āṇṭa cē(ra)lattil jem ṣeṭpūr uruvākum.



varalāṟu:

kolli maliyip paṟṟiya toṉmiyāṉa varalāṟu ki. pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭic cārntatākum. an nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻakattil veṟṟik koṭi kaṭṭiyāṇṭa māvīraṉ, cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉpatic caṅka maruviya nūlkaḷ paṟi cāṟṟum. kaṅkip pēriyāṟum imayap perumaliyum avaṉ veṟṟic ciṟappi iṉṟum etirolittuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. pēriyāṟu mēlik kaṭalil viḻum iṭattil cēranāṭṭiṉ talinakaram amintiruntatu. avvañci nakaril vīṟṟiruntu eñciyirunta nāvalan tīvi āṭci purintaṉar cērar. koṅkunāṭum ataṉ vaḷamikka kuṟiñciyāṉa kolliyum avarkaḷ āṇikkuṭpaṭṭiruntaṉa. cēra maṉṉarkaḷ iḷamiyilēyē taṅkaḷ iḷaṅkōkkaḷi araciyalil payiṟṟum vaḻakka muṭiyavarkaḷ. iḷavaracaṉ māntarañ cēralirumpoṟi, koṅku nāṭṭiṉ kōmāṉāka aṉuppappaṭṭāṉ. ivaṉ ceṅkōliyuṭiyavaṉ, yāṉiyiṉatu pārvi pōlum nōkkuṭiyavaṉ. ‘viḷaṅkil’ eṉṉum ūrārkkup pakivarāṉ vanta tuṉpattit tīrttōṉ; kapilaruṭiya naṇpaṉ; oru kālattup pāṇṭiyaṉ taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭup piṉpu ataṉi nīkkik koṇṭu pukaḻ peṟṟāēṉ; cōḻaṉ irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷiyōṭu pōr ceytōṉ; ivaṉ peyar ‘yāṉikkaṭcēy māntarañcēral irumpoṟi’ eṉavum, cēramāṉ yāṉikkaṭcēy māntarañcēral irumpoṟi’ eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. eṭṭuttokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya aiṅkuṟu nūṟṟit tokuppittōṉ ivaṉē. kuṟuṅkōḻiyūr kiḻār, poruntil iḷaṅ﻿kīraṉār, kūṭalūr kiḻār ākiya mupperum pulavarum ivaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar.

cēranāṭu kuṟiñcināṭu. kuṟiñci nāṭṭil vāḻnta iḷaṅkō ātaliṉ, koṅku nāṭṭi āḷat taṉakku ēṟṟa iṭamākak kolli maliyit tērnteṭuttu aṅkut taṉ āṭcip pīṭatti amittāṉ. āṉatu paṟṟik kollit talivaṉ eṉṟu caṅka nūlkaḷil ivaṉ aḻikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. piṉṉāṭkaḷil cēra araciṟku urimi pūṇṭu oḻukiyapōtu, kaṭaṟkari nakarāṉa toṇṭiyit taṉ talinakarākak koṇṭāṉ. ‘kollip poruna’ (patiṟ. 73), ‘cēralaṟkītta, cevvērp palaviṉ payaṅkeḻu kolli’ (akanā. 209), ‘kolliyāṇṭa kuṭavar kōvē’ (cilap. 24) eṉṟa aṭikaḷ kollimali cērarkaḷukku urimiyuṭiyatāka iruntatu eṉpati viḷakkum.



ōri :

kollimalikkup peruṅkiḻami koṇṭavaṉ ōri. ivaṉ kaṭiēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷil oruvaṉ. villāṟṟalil ciṟantavaṉāṉa ivaṉi, acciṟappu nōkki ‘valvil ōri’ eṉṟu caṅka ilakkiyam kūṟum. ivaṉ villāṟṟalic ciṟappikkap pukunta vaṉparaṇar, ‘yāṉiyik koṉṟu vīḻtti, periya vāyiyuṭiya puliyiṉ uyirkuṭittu, tuḷiporuntiya kōṭṭiṉit taliyilē uṭiya puḷḷimāṉ kaliyiṉi uruṭṭi, ural pōlum taliyuṭiya kēḻalākiya paṉṟiyi vīḻac ceytu, ataṟkarukilirunta āḻtaliyuṭiya puṟṟiṉ kaṭkiṭakkiṉṟa uṭumpiṉkaṭ ceṉṟu ceṟiyum valvil’ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.

mēlum kolliyiṉ vaḷattiyum ōriyiṉ īkiyiyum paṟṟi avar kūṟumpōtu, ﻿

“… … … veṟukkinaṉ kuṭiyaṉ

āraṅ tāḻnta vampakaṭṭu mārpiṟ

cāra laruvip payamalik kiḻavaṉ” (puṟanā. 152)





eṉṟum,

“ … … … vēṭṭattil

tāṉuyir cekutta māṉiṉap puḻukkoṭu

āṉuruk kaṉṉa vēriyi nalkit

taṉmalip piṟanta tāvi ṉaṉpoṉ

paṉmaṇik kuviyoṭum viriik koṇmeṉac

curattiṭi nalkiyōṉē viṭarccimi

ōṅkiruṅ kollip perunaṉ” (puṟanā. 152)





eṉṟum,

“maḻiyaṇi kuṉṟattuk kiḻavaṉ nāḷum

iḻiyaṇi yāṉi irappōrk kīyum

… … … …

aṭupōr āṉā ātaṉ ōri ” (puṟanā. 153)





eṉṟum,

“paṉinīrp pūvā maṇimiṭi kuvaḷi

vāṉārt toṭutta kaṇṇiyum kalaṉum

yāṉi iṉattoṭu peṟṟaṉar. (puṟanā. 153)





eṉṟum pāṭi makiḻkiṟār.



maṟṟāeru cennāp pulavarāṉa kaḻitiṉ yāṉi yār,

“karuvi vāṉam pōla

variyātu curakkum vaḷḷiyōy ....”





(puṟanā. 204)

eṉṟu pukaḻntu pāṭukiṟār.



“aṭupōrāṉā ātaṉ ōri” eṉṟa puṟanāṉūṟṟaṭiyiliruntu, ivaṉ iyaṟpeyar ‘ātaṉ’ eṉpatūum, kuṭip peyar ‘ōri’ eṉpatūum pulaṉām. kolli mali ōrikkuriyateṉpatu, “kolliyāṇṭa valvil ﻿ōri” (puṟanā. 158), “ōri, palpaḻap palaviṉ payaṅkeḻu kolli” (akanā. 208) eṉṟa toṭarkaḷāl viḷaṅkum. kollivāḻ makkaḷ, ōri vāḻnta iṭamāka oru maṇmēṭṭic cuṭṭik kāṭṭi iṉṟum perumippaṭukiṉṟaṉar.

maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāriyum, ōriyum camakālattavar. tirumuṭikkāri peṇṇiyāṟṟaṅkariyiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa ‘malāṭu’ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum maliyamāṉāṭṭiṉ aracaṉ. kōvalūr ivaṉatu talinakar. kaṭiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷil ivaṉum oruvaṉ. muḷḷūr maliyiyuṭiyāṉ. ivaṉ kutirikkuk kāri eṉṟu peyar. kārik kutiriyūrntu tirintatāl ivaṉum kāri eṉappaṭṭāṉ pōlum. ivaṉ kuṟunila maṉṉaṉāka iruntālum āṟṟalum, payiṟciyum mikka ciṟanta paṭikku urimiyuṭiyavaṉāka iruntāṉ. pōr ēṟpaṭum pōtu pēraracarkaḷellām ivaṉ tuṇiyi nāṭiṉar. kāriyum ōriyum pakivarkaḷāka iruntu pōriṭṭaṉar eṉṟa ceyti, “kārikkutirik kāriyoṭu malinta, vōrik kutiri ōriyum” (ciṟupāṇ 110-111) eṉṟa aṭikaḷāl pulappaṭum. mēlum cēraṉum ōriyum māṟukoṇṭa poḻutu, kāri cērar pakkam niṉṟu pōriṭṭu ōriyik koṉṟu kolli maliyic cēraṉukku akappaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpati, “muḷḷūr maṉṉaṉ kaḻal toṭik kāri, cellā nallici niṟutta valvil, ōrik koṉṟu cēralaṟ kītta, cevvērp palaviṉ payaṅkeḻu kolli” (akanā. 209) eṉṟa aṭikaḷāl aṟiyalām.



maliyāḷikaḷ :

maliyāḷikaḷ eṉṟu collak kūṭiya ivviṉattār, cērvarāyaṉ mali, kolli mali, kalrāyaṉ mali, pacci mali, cittēri mali ākiyavaṟṟil vāḻum paḻaṅ﻿kuṭi makkaḷ. camaveḷiyil vāḻum makkaḷōṭu atikat toṭarpu koṇṭirāta kāraṇattāl ivarkaḷuṭiya paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum paṇpāṭukaḷum ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭē irukkiṉṟaṉa. pēcum moḻi tamiḻē eṉṟālum citittup pēcukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ eṇṇikki piṉ varumāṟu.

āttūr vaṭṭam — 14,000

cēlam vaṭṭam — 10,000

ūttaṅkari vaṭṭam — 10,000

nāmakkal vaṭṭam — 12,000



immali vāḻ makkaḷil oru cilar malikaḷiṉiṉṟum nīṅki ōmalūr, ūttaṅkari mutaliya camaveḷi ūrkaḷilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. immaliyāḷikaḷum, malikkāyccalukkup pērpōṉa ivarkaḷ vāḻviṭaṅkaḷum, paḻamiyil ūṟippōṉa ivarkaḷ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum nammi viyappilāḻttuvatōṭu makkaḷiṉa nūl ārāycci (Ethnology)kkuriya pāṭaṅkaḷāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. immaliyāḷikaḷ, kāñcipuramē taṅkaḷ āti ūr eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ im malikaḷil kuṭiyēṟiyatu paṟṟik karṇa paramparik katiyoṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

periyaṇṇaṉ, naṭuvaṇṇaṉ, ciṉṉaṇṇaṉ eṉṟu peyariya mūṉṟu uṭaṉ piṟantārkaḷ oru nāḷ mūṉṟu vēṭṭi nāykaḷōṭu vēṭṭikkuc ceṉṟaṉarām. mūvarum kāṭṭiṉ mūṉṟu pakutikaḷukkup pirintu ceṉṟu vēṭṭiyāṭiṉar. appoḻutu perumaḻi piṭittu iraṇṭu nāḷ ōyavilli. avarkaḷālum kāṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟa muṭiyavilli. āṉāl avarkaḷuṭiya nāykaḷ maṭṭum eppaṭiyō vīṭu cērntaṉa. kaṇavaṉmār iṉṟit taṉiyē vīṭu tirumpiya nāykaḷik kaṇṭa maṉiviyar, taṅkaḷ kaṇavaṉmār kāṭṭil koṭu vilaṅkukaḷāl iṟantupaṭṭatākak karuti, akkālakkim peṇkaḷip pōl vīṭukaḷukkut tīyiṭṭu anneruppil tāṅkaḷum vīḻntu maṭintaṉar. mūṉṟām nāḷ maḻi viṭṭu vīṭu tirumpiya mūṉṟu cōtararum, illamum illak kiḻattiyarum ventu nīṟāṉatik kaṇṭaṉar; varuntiṉar; ēṅki aḻutaṉar. avvāṟṟāmi taṇintatum mūvarum vēṟu makaḷiri maṇamuṭittuk koṇṭaṉar. periyaṇṇaṉ oru kikkōḷa mātik kaṭimaṇam purintu koṇṭu, kalrāyaṉ maliyil kuṭi pukuntāṉ. naṭuvaṇṇaṉ oru vēṭarkula makaḷi maṇantu koṇṭu pacci maliyil vāḻac ceṉṟāṉ. ciṉṉaṇṇaṉ tēvēntirap paḷḷar kulattil peṇṇeṭuttuk koṇṭatōṭu, kollimaliyi vāḻviṭamākak koṇṭāṉ. immūvariṉ vaḻivantōrē periya maliyāḷikaḷ eṉṟum, pacci maliyāḷikaḷ eṉṟum, kolli maliyāḷikaḷ eṉṟum mūṉṟu piriviṉarāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.



pirivukaḷ :

maṇamuṟiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil pala pirivu (exogamous clans) kaḷākap pirintu ivarkaḷ vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. appirivukaḷukku ‘vakuppukaḷ’ eṉṟu peyar. ivvakuppukaḷil cilavaṟṟi oṉṟu cērttut ‘tāyāti vakuppu’ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ittāyāti vakuppiṉar taṅkaḷi, ‘aṇṇaṉ tampiyar’ eṉṟu aḻittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. oru tāyāti vakuppiṉar taṅkaḷukkuḷ peṇṇeṭuttuk koḷḷum vaḻakkamilli. vēṟu tāyāti vakuppilēyē peṇ koḷvar. eṭuttukkāṭṭākac cittūr nāṭṭiluḷḷa maliyāḷikaḷ ēḻu vakuppukaḷākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷil aintu vakuppiṉar (pīlaṉ, mūkkāṇṭi, pūcaṉ, māṇikkaṉ, tiruvicci) oru tāyāti vakuppiṉarākavum, eñciyuḷḷa iraṇṭu vakuppiṉar (kaṇṇaṉ, tillāṉ) ﻿vēṟṟut tāyāti vakuppiṉarākavum karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivviraṇṭu tāyāti vakuppiṉaril muṉṉavar piṉṉavariṭattum, piṉṉavar muṉṉavariṭattumē peṇ koḷḷalām. puli nāṭṭic cērnta māṭṭāyaṉ, imiyāṇṭi, kaṇṇātaṉ, alātti, puṉṉaṉ eṉṟa aintu kūṭṭattārum oru tāyāti vakuppic cērntavarkaḷ. mūṉūr maliyāḷikaḷil kōṉāṉ vakuppār, mēṟkūṟiya tiruppuli nāṭṭu mutal mūṉṟu vakuppāriṭamum peṇ koḷḷamāṭṭārkaḷ. piṉṉiru vakuppāriṭam peṇ koḷḷuvārkaḷ. paccimaliyāḷiṭiyē aimpatu vakuppukkaḷ uṇṭu. avvimpatu vakuppārum eṭṭut tāyātik kūṭṭaṅkaḷākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivvakuppukkaḷellām paḻamiyāṉatum vēṭikkiyāṉatumāṉa peyarkaḷit tāṅki , moḻiyārāycciyāḷariṉ eṇṇatti īrkkiṉṟaṉa.



vāḻkki muṟi :

ciṉṉaṇṇaṉ vaḻivanta kolli maliyāḷikaḷ paḻami virumpikaḷ; oḻuṅku paṭuttappaṭṭa amippukkuṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ nāmakkal, āttūr vaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kolli maliyilum, pōta maliyilum, pōta malikkum jerukumalikkum iṭippaṭṭa paḷḷattākkilum, pāla maliyilum, parukūr maliyilum, kāḷi maliyilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. kolli maliyil ivarkaḷ nāṉku piriviṉarākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷil mutal iraṇṭu piriviṉarāṉa munnāṭṭu maliyāḷikaḷum, nāṉāṭṭu maliyāḷikaḷum muṟiyē nāmakkal vaṭṭattil uḷḷa cēlūr, vēlappūr eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil perumpālum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟa iraṇṭu piriviṉarāṉa añcūr (aintu ūr) maliyāḷikaḷum, mūṉūr (mūṉṟu ūr) maliyāḷikaḷum āttūr vaṭṭattilē vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ﻿añcūr maliyāḷikaḷ payil, tiruppuli, eṭappuli, piṟakari, cittūr nāṭukaḷil 7000 pēr vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mūṉūr maliyāḷikaḷ kuṇṭuṉi, alattūr, palāppāṭi nāṭukaḷil 1500 pēr vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. añcūr maliyāḷikaḷ payil nāṭṭic cērnta ‘periya paṭṭakkāraṉiṉ’ āṇikku aṭaṅkiyavarkaḷ. ip paṭṭakkāraṉ patavi orē kuṭiyil talimuṟiyāka (Heriditory) varuvatu. āṉāl aracaṉ eṉṟu avaṉ aḻikkappaṭuvatilli. aracaṉukkuriya aṅkaṅkaḷum avaṉukkuk kiṭiyātu.

pala ciṟṟūrkaḷ cērntu oru nāṭu ākum. ovvoru aintu ūrkaḷum cērntu ‘ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ’ eṉṟa oruvaṉit tērnteṭukkum. ovvoru ūrilum vāḻumpaṭiyāṉa ovvoru vakuppārukkum oru kāriyakkāraṉ uṇṭu. ivaṉum tērnteṭukkappaṭupavaṉē. ētāvatu oru ūril cātic caṇṭi ēṟpaṭṭāl, ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ caṇṭi ēṟpaṭṭa ūriluḷḷa ellāk kāriyakkārariyum aḻittup pēcit tīrttu vippatu vaḻakkam. avvaḻakkil campantappaṭṭōr mēl vaḻak (Appeal) kāṭa virumpiṉul, payil nāṭṭup paṭṭakkāraṉiṭam cella vēṇṭum. appaṭṭakkāraṉ av vaḻakkuc campantappaṭṭa ūric cērnta ūrk kavuṇṭaṉiyum, payil nāṭṭu ūrk kavuṇṭaṉiyum kūṭṭivittup pēcit tīrppatu vaḻakkam. ellāk kāriyakkārarkaḷum, ivvaḻakku maṉṟattukku vara vēṇṭumeṉpatilli. āṉāl kuṟintatu mūṉṟu pērāvatu iruntāltāṉ vaḻakku eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭum.

mūṉūr maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē vaḻakku ēṟpaṭṭāl, avviṭattic cērnta ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ vaḻakkiṉ taṉmiyi vēlappūrilirukkum nāṉāṭṭu aracarukkut terivippatu vaḻakkam. avvaracar vaḻaṅkum tīrppē muṭivāṉatākum. pōtamaliyilum ataṟkaṭuttāṟpōluḷḷa paḷḷattākkilum vāḻumpaṭiyāṉa kolli maliyāḷikaḷ kīḻūrilirukkum ‘nāṭṭāṉ’ eṉṟa talivaṉiṉ āṇikkuṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ. pavāṉi vaṭṭattilirukkum kolli maliyāḷikaḷum ivaṉ āṇikkuṭpaṭṭavarkaḷē. āṉāl ivaṉuṭiya tīrppil tiruptiyaṭiyātavarkaḷ payil nāṭṭup periya paṭṭakkāraṉiṭam nīti kēṭṭuc celvatuṇṭu. vēlappūr aracaṉ iḷiñaṉāka iruntāl, avaṉ tāyākiya irāṇi mantiriyiṉ tuṇikoṇṭu āṭci celuttuvatuṇṭu.

pacci maliyāḷikaḷ mūṉṟu nāṭṭiṉarākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mutal iru nāṭukaḷāṉa veṇṇāṭum, teṉpuṟa nāṭum tirucci māvaṭṭa elliyil uḷḷaṉa. mūṉṟāvatu nāṭāṉa atti nāṭu, āttūr vaṭṭattiluḷḷa pacci maliyil uḷḷatu. pacci maliyāḷikaḷ iṉṉum āttūr vaṭṭattiṟkappāl, tumpal paḷḷattākkilum, vaciṭṭa natip paḷḷattākkiṉ ucciyilum, araṉūttu maliyilum, mañcavāṭik kaṇavāyilum, cērvarāyaṉ maliyiṉ mēṟku aṭivārattiluḷḷa kañcēri, pālamēṭu eṉṉum ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, toppūr āṟṟukkariyiluḷḷa vēppāṭiyilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.

cāti nirvākattiṟkāka ivarkaḷ palatuṇi nāṭukaḷākavum kari allatu tamukkukaḷākavum pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭākap pacci maliyiluḷḷa nalliyak kavuṇṭaṉ nāṭu, kāḷattik kavuṇṭaṉ nāṭu, pacci malikku mēṟkiluḷḷa maṇmali nāṭu, pittūr nāṭu eṉpaṉa kuṟippiṭattakkavi. tumpal paḷḷattākkil uḷḷa māmañciyilum, araṉūttu maliyiluḷḷa ālaṭippaṭṭiyilum, pēḷūrukku vaṭakkil vaciṭṭa natip paḷḷattākkil uḷḷa kīrippaṭṭiyilum, karamaṇṭiyilum, cērvarāyaṉ malic carivukaḷiluḷḷa ūrkaḷilum, mañcavāṭik kaṇavāykku vaṭakkiluḷḷa tompakkaḷḷaṉūr, paṭṭukkuṇam paṭṭi eṉṟa ūrkaḷilum nāṭṭāṉ eṉpavaṉit taṅkaḷ talivaṉāyp pacci maliyāḷikaḷ tērnteṭukkiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru tuṇi nāṭum pala paṭṭikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru paṭṭiyum, avvūrk kavuṇṭaṉiṉ āṇikkuṭpaṭṭirukkum. avvūrk kavuṇṭaṉukku mūppaṉ eṉṟu peyar. avaṉukkut tuṇiyākak kaṅkāṇi eṉṟa talivaṉum uṇṭu. ovvoru tuṇi nāṭṭiṟkum oru talivaṉuṇṭu. avaṉi nāṭṭāṉ eṉṟu aḻippar. avaṉukku nāṭṭuk kavuṇṭaṉ, kuṭṭik kavuṇṭaṉ eṉṟa vēṟu peyarkaḷum uṇṭu. ovvoru nāṭṭāṉukkum tuṇiyākap pala kāriyak kārarkaḷuṇṭu. avarkaḷip paṇiyil amarttum atikāram nāṭṭāṉuṭiyatu. in nāṭṭāṉ ēḻu ciṉṉaturikaḷaṭaṅkiya aviyiṉ atikārattukku uṭpaṭṭavaṉ. inta aviyiṉ talivaṉuka vīṟṟirup pavaṉ periyaturi. ivaṉi rājā eṉṟum cila camayaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭuvatuṇṭu. ivaṉ vāḻviṭam pacci maliyiluḷḷa cētakam. it turimārkaḷukkup pala pēramiccarkaḷuṇṭu. āṉal avvamiccarkaḷiṉ atikāram mikavum kuṟivutāṉ. ivvamiccarkaḷ pacci maliyiluḷḷa pakkalattilum, pittūrilum, kīrippaṭṭiyilum vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. pittūr amiccaṉ paṉṉiraṇṭu kari makkaḷālum, kīrippaṭṭi amiccaṉ āṟu kari makkaḷālum ēṟṟup pōṟṟappaṭuvāṉ. pittūr amiccaṉ pakkalam amiccaṉit taṉ āṇikkaṭaṅkiyavakik karutuvatuṇṭu.

periya maliyāḷikaḷ kalvarāyaṉ maliyilum, cērvarāyaṉ maliyilum, cittēriyilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷik kalrāyar eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷ. taṅkaḷik kārāḷar eṉṟa vēṟu peyarālum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. 'kārāḷar' eṉṟa peyar maliyāḷattiṉ maṟṟoru peyarāṉa 'kēraḷam' eṉpataṉōṭu toṭarpuṭiyatu eṉṟu cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. maliyāḷikaḷiṉ orucila paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ kēraḷa makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷi ottiruppatāl avvāṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar pōlum. kārāḷar eṉṟa colliṉ āṭci 'uḻavar' eṉṟa poruḷil paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷil malintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kampar kūṭa, "kār naṭakkumpaṭi naṭakkum kārāḷar tammuṭiya ēr naṭakkum eṉiṉ pukaḻcāl iyalici nāṭakam naṭakkum'- eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. ip periya maliyāḷikaḷ kaḷḷakkuricci vaṭṭattil 22,000 pērum, āttūr vaṭṭattil 12,000 pērum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. immaliyāḷikaḷ aintu talivarkaḷāl paṇṭi nāḷil iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟṟappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ip pakutikaḷ aintu jākīrkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu, iṉṟum avarkaḷ vaḻi vantōrāl āḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. it talivarkaḷiṉ urimi parampariyāṉatu. inta jākīr ovvoṉṟum pala ciṟu nāṭukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ik kalrāyarkaḷ cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷa maliyāḷikaḷōṭu maṇattoṭarpu koḷvatilli. tolivum, vakuppup pirivum kāraṇaṅkaḷāka irukkalām. cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷa periya maliyāḷikaḷ mūṉṟu piriviṉarkaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. cēla nāṭu, moka nāṭu, muttu nāṭu eṉpaviyē ap pirivukaḷ. ovvoru pirivum oru paṭṭakkāraṉiṉ āṇikku uṭpaṭṭatu. ovvoru pirivum oṉpatu paṭṭikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ovvoru paṭṭiyum mūppaṉ eṉṟa talivaṉiṉ āṇikku aṭaṅkiyatu. ovvoru mūppaṉum appaṭṭiyiluḷḷa makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṟāṉ, ovvoru paṭṭakkāraṉukkum tuṇiyāka maṇiyakkārarkaḷum, mūpparkaḷukkut tuṇiyākak kaṅkāṇikaḷum uḷḷaṉar. cittēriyiluḷḷa 'kuru' cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷa mūṉṟu nāṭṭup periya maliyāḷikaḷālum pōṟṟi matikkappaṭukiṟār.

pārppaṉap purōkitarkaḷ maliyāḷikaḷukkuk kiṭiyātu. mēṟkūṟiya cātit talivarkaḷē maṟṟa poṟuppukkaḷōṭu, tirumaṇam mutaliya caṭaṅkukaḷiyum muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl pavāṉi vaṭṭattiluḷḷa kolli maliyāḷikaḷ maṭṭum ōr ayyaṅkārik kuruvāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. paṭṭakkārarkaḷum, turikaḷum maliyāḷikaḷāl peritum matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷik kāṇpōr etirē kuppuṟa viḻuntu vaṇaṅkuvar.

maliyāḷikaḷum kēraḷattārum :

im maliyāḷikaḷip paṟṟiya kati kāñcīpurattōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭiruntālum, ivarkaḷuṭiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ maliyāḷa nāṭṭu makkaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ākiyiṉāl ivarkaḷ maliyāḷattiliruntu im malikaḷil vantu kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl ivarkaḷip paṟṟik kiṭittuḷḷa 'kalrāyaṉ kalveṭṭu'kaḷil ik kuṭiyēṟṟattip paṟṟi ec ceytiyum kāṇappaṭavilli. ivarkaḷuṭiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷil orucila, kēraḷattāriṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷōṭu ottiruppatāl ivarkaḷ kēraḷattārē eṉṟu nām kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. appaḻakkaṅkaḷ piṉ varumāṟu:

(1) kolli maliyiluḷḷa ciṟuvarkaḷum ciṟumikaḷum muṉ kuṭumi vittiruppar. taliyiṉ maṟṟa pākaṅkaḷi moṭṭiyaṭittu viṭuvar. maliyāḷak kaṭaṟkariyil vāḻum intukkaḷ ip paḻakkattip perum aḷavu kaṭippiṭittu varukiṉṟaṉar. kolli malic ciṟuvarkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭām vayatu muṭinta piṉ, muṉ kuṭumiyi eṭuttuviṭṭut tamiḻakattiṉ kīḻkkariyil vāḻum makkaḷippōl kuṭumi vittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. paruvam eytiyatum kollic ciṟumiyarum muṉ kuṭumiyi nīkkiviṭṭut tali muḻutum nīṇṭa kūntal vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉar. pacci maliyāḷikaḷiṭamum ip paḻakkam uṇṭu . āṉāl ciṟumiyar paruvam eytum variyilum muṉ kuṭumiyi nīkka avarkaḷ kāttiruppatilli.

(2) kolli maliyiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ veṇmiyāṉa parutti āṭiyi, mārpi mūṭi akkuḷil paṭiyumāṟu cuṟṟik kaṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. avvāṭi muḻu uṭaliyum maṟittukkoṇṭirā viṭṭālum muḻantāḷukkum caṟṟuk kīḻē toṅkik koṇṭirukkum. ip paḻakkattik kēraḷattil kāṇalām. pacci maliyāḷap peṇkaḷum, periya maliyāḷap peṇkaḷum camaveḷiyil vāḻpavarkaḷip pōlavē vaṇṇap puṭavi yaṇikiṉṟaṉar.

(3) kolli malip peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ āṭiyiṉuḷḷē 3 muḻa nīḷamum 1 aṭi akalamumuḷḷa veṇmiyāṉa tuṇi (Loin - cloth) oṉṟiyum aṇikiṉṟaṉar. ati entap payaṉ karutiyum aṇivatilli. aḻakukkākavē aṇikiṉṟaṉar. kēraḷa nāṭṭil vāḻum peṇkaḷukku ittuṇi mikavum eṭuppāṉa tōṟṟatti aḷikkiṟatu. kollimalip peṇkaḷippōlavē ittuṇiyi maṟṟa irucāti maliyāḷap peṇkaḷum taṅkaḷ puṭavikaḷukkuḷ aṇikiṉṟaṉar, tiruvāḷar kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkār, kēraḷattiluḷḷa. ippaḻakkattip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu, "iraṇṭu tuṇṭut tuṇikaḷ ellāp peṇkaḷālum paṇṭi nāḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa, āṉāl ip paḻakkam eppoḻutu māṟiyatu eṉṟu teḷivākat teriyavilli. camaskiruta ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ip paḻakkattip paṟṟi nāṉ pala taṭavi paṭittiruppatāl itu āriyap paṇpāṭu eṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ. āṉāl ip paḻakkam paravalākak kēraḷak kaṭaṟkariyil kāṇap paṭukiṟatu“ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.

(4) periya maliyāḷikaḷum pacci maliyāḷikaḷum pacci kuttik koḷḷum paḻakkatti aṉumatik kiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl kolli maliyāḷikaḷ iti aṟavē veṟukkiṉṟaṉar. pacci kuttik koṇṭa yāriyum taṅkaḷ vīṭṭiṟkuḷ ivarkaḷ nuḻiya viṭuvatilli. itaṉuṭiya uṇmiyāṉa kāraṇam eṉṉaveṉṟu puriyavilli. kolli maliyāḷikaḷip pōlavē kēraḷak kaṭaṟkariyil vāḻvōrum pacci kuttikkoḷḷum paḻakkatti mēṟkoḷvatilli. pacci maliyāḷikaḷiṉ pacci kuttik koḷḷum paḻakkam, naṭuvaṇaṇaṉ tirumaṇam ceytu koṇṭa vēṭacciyiṉiṭamiruntu vaḻi vaḻiyāka vantatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

(5) pūppeytiya maliyāḷap peṇ, oru tiṅkaḷ aḷavum taṉiyiṭattil otukki vikkappaṭukiruḷ. kēraḷa nāṭṭilum ivvāṟē. camaveḷiyil vāḻum makkaḷ ivvāṟu nīṇṭa nāḷ otukki vippatilli. ippoḻutu in nāṭkaḷiṉ aḷavu maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē kuṟintu varukiṟatu.

(6) kollimalip peṇkaḷ taṅkattāl ceyta vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa oru nakiyik kātil aṇikiṉṟa ṉar. vēṟu ulōkattāl ceytu, taṅka mulām pūciyum aṇintu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ viṭṭam 1 aṅkulamō allatu 1; aṅkulamō irukkum. iti aṇintu koḷvataṟkākak kātil periya tuḷi pōṭṭuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. in naki kēraḷattil vāḻum nāyar makaḷir aṇiyum tōṭākkaḷi uruvattil ottirukkiṟatu.



camaveḷiyiluḷḷa makkaḷiliruntu maliyāḷikaḷit taṉiyākap pirikkum ip paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, avarkaḷuṭiya muṉṉōrkaḷāṉa kēraḷattāriṭamiruntu peṟappaṭṭaviyē eṉṟu ellārum karutukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟa maliyāḷikaḷiviṭak kolli maliyāḷikaḷ taṅkaḷuṭiya paramparip paṇpāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum māṟu paṭātavarkaḷākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. mēṟkūṟiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiviṭa, ivarkaḷ kaṭippiṭittu varum maṇa muṟikaḷum, pala āṭavarkaḷi oru peṇ kaṇavarāka ēṟṟukkoḷḷum muṟi (Polyandry) yum, kēraḷa makkaḷōṭu ivarkaḷi neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷāka ākkukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāl pala kaṇavar koḷḷum immuṟi nāyarkaḷiṭamum, avarkaḷic cārnta iṉattāriṭamum iṉṟum arukik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.

mēṉarīkam :

mēṉarīkam eṉpatu tamiḻ nāṭṭilum, āntira nāṭṭilum paravalākak kāṇappaṭum oru vaki maṇa muṟi. ōr iḷiñaṉukku, avaṉuṭiya tāy māmaṉ makaḷē ēṟṟa maṇap peṇṇākak karutappaṭukiṟāḷ. tāy māmaṉ vīṭṭil peṇṇilliyeṉṟāl atti makaḷ maṇattiṟku uriyavaḷākat tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṟāḷ. cila cātiyāriṭiyē uṭaṉpiṟanta tamakkiyiṉ makaḷi maṇakkum urimi pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu. im muṟikaḷiṉpaṭi maṇakkum peṇṇi 'urimip peṇ' eṉpar. atuvum niṟinta celvattōṭum nilapulaṉkaḷōṭumvantu kaṇavaṉ poruḷātāratti uyarttupavaḷāka iruntāl avaḷip 'perumip peṇ' eṉṟum kūṟuvar. im muṟikaḷi mīṟip, peṇ koṭukkavō allatu peṇ eṭukkavō yār maṟuttālum ac ceyal peruṅ kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭum. ik kuṟṟattiṟkākac cātiyi viṭṭukkūṭa vilakki viṭuvatuṇṭu.

maliyāḷikaḷ im mēṉarīka muṟiyi mikavum tīviramākak kaṭip piṭikkiṉṟaṉar. cila camayaṅkaḷil im muṟi viparītamākakkūṭat tōṉṟum. urimip peṇṇāṉavaḷ kula vaḻakkappaṭi avaḷukkeṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa maṇamakaṉiviṭa mikavum vayatil mutirntavaḷāka iruppatum uṇṭu. nāṉkintu vayatuṭiya iḷiñarkaḷukkup paruva meytiya maṅkiyarit tirumaṇam ceytu vippārkaḷ. appōtu maṇamakaṉuṭiya tanti, tāṉē poṟuppēṟṟuk koṇṭu vamcavirutti ceyvatuṇṭu. taṉ makaṉ vayatukku vantatum ap poṟuppi avaṉiṭam viṭṭu viṭuvatu tantiyiṉ kaṭami. poṟuppēṟṟuk koṇṭa makaṉ, taṉ tantiyiṉ vaḻiyit tāṉum kaṭip piṭippāṉ. ōr iḷam maṇamakaṉukkut tanti illi eṉṟālō, allatu tanti iruntum avaṉ marumakaḷiṉpāl akkaṟi koḷḷātavaṉāka iruntālō, tantiyiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantavaṉō allatu neruṅkiya uṟaviṉaṉ oruvaṉō ip poṟuppi ēṟṟuk koḷḷa aḻikkappaṭuvatu vaḻakkam. iv vaḻakkattippaṟṟi maliyāḷikaḷ ippoḻutu kuṟivu paṭa eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. iti ōraḷavu etirttum varukiṉṟaṉar. muṉ nāṭkaḷil itu peru vaḻakkāka ivarkaḷiṭam paraviyiruntatu.

tirumaṇam :

maliyāḷikaḷiṉ tirumaṇam camaveḷiyiluḷḷōriṉ tirumaṇattiṉiṉṟum ciṟitē māṟupaṭukiṟatu. tirumaṇattiṟkumuṉ 'veṟṟili pākkup piṭittal' eṉṟa oru paḻakkamuṇṭu. iti vaṭamoḻiyil 'niccayatārttam' eṉpar. tirumaṇa oppantamē ic caṭaṅku. ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ muṉṉiliyil peṇ vīṭṭārum, māppiḷḷi vīṭṭārumkūṭi iv voppantatti muṭippar. iru vīṭṭārum vevvēṟu ūrkaḷic cārntavarāka iruppiṉ avviraṇṭu ūrkaḷiṉ ūrk kavuṇṭarkaḷum avviṭattilkūṭi iti naṭatti vippar. paṭṭakkāraṉiṉ aṉumati peṟṟē itic ceyya vēṇṭum.

maṇap peṇṇukku viliyākap paricam vāṅkum vaḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu. atu avaravarkaḷiṉ takutikkēṟpak kuṟittō kūṭṭiyō peṟappaṭum, piḷḷi vīṭṭār paṇamākavum, poruḷkaḷākavum iti aḷippar. ūttaṅkari vaṭṭattiluḷḷa pacci maliyāḷikaḷiṉ peṇṇukkup paricamāka nāṉku kaṇṭakam tāṉiyamum, nāṉku pakōṭā ( rū. 14)kkaḷum, kaṉṟōṭu oru pacuvum koṭukka vēṇṭum. maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa maliyāḷikaḷ rū. 10-liruntu rū. 50 vari paricam koṭukkiṉṟaṉar. maṇac caṭaṅkukaḷi naṭatti vikkum ūrkkavuṇṭar mutaliyavarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭaṇamāka rū. 10-8-0 aḷikka vēṇṭum. āṉāl cila ēḻikaḷāl ip pūrāt tokiyiyum koṭukka muṭiyātu. appōtu it tokiyiṉ oru pakutiyi maṭṭum koṭuppar. mītiyip paṭṭakkāraṉ koṭuttu viṭuvāṉ. āṉāl pala tavaṇikaḷil paṭṭakkāraṉukku ap paṇattik koṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭum. ikkaṭaṉ āṇṭuk kaṇakkāka nīṇṭu celvatumuṇṭu. peṟṟōr tam tirumaṇattiṉpōtu paṭṭa ik kaṭaṉi, makkaḷ tōṉṟip paṭṭakkāraṉukkut tīrppatuṇṭām. oru kiḻavaṉ kumariyit tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷa nēriṭṭāl, niṟiyap paricam koṭukka vēṇṭum.

pacci maliyil vāḻvōr maṇamakkaḷukku ñāyiṟṟukkiḻami yaṉṟu naluṅku vippar. putaṉ kiḻamiyaṉṟu iru vīṭṭiṉ muṉṉum pantalkaḷ pōṭappaṭum. viyāḻakkiḻami peṇ vīṭṭil tiru maṇam naṭipeṟum. potuvāka maliyāḷikaḷiṉ tirumaṇam māppiḷḷi vīṭṭilēyē naṭipeṟum. peṇ vīṭṭilum, māppiḷḷi vīṭṭilum mūṉṟu putup pāttiraṅkaḷi oṉṟiṉmēl oṉṟu aṭukkik kalacampōl vittu, putaṉkiḻami iravu vināyakar kōyilukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvar. appōtu tāmpūlam ellārukkum vaḻaṅkappaṭa vēṇṭum. im mariyāti mikavum kaṇṭippōṭu etirpārkkappaṭukiṟatu. it tāmpūlattil periya turi aintu paṅkum, maṟṟa turikaḷ ovvoruvarum nāṉku paṅkum, mantirikaḷ ovvoruvarum mūṉṟu paṅkum, kuṭṭa kavuṇṭaṉ iraṇṭu paṅkum, mūppaṉ oru paṅkum peṟuvatu vaḻakkam. ic caṭaṅku muṭintatum maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷukkuk kūṟi paricaḷippatu vaḻakkam. itu karuppuk kariyiṭṭa veḷḷic cēliyākavō allatu civappuc cēliyākavō irukkum. itaṉuṭiya nīḷam 12 muḻattiliruntu 17 muḻam uṭiyatākavum, akalam iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu muḻam uṭiyatākavum irukkum, āṉāl periya maliyāḷikaḷ maṭṭum 3 allatu 4 muḻamuḷḷa kūṟi aḷippatu vaḻakkam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kolli maliyil maṇap peṇ viṭiyaṟ kālattil ēḻu maṇikku muṉpāka maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭiṟku aḻittuc cellappaṭukiṟāḷ. tirumaṇac caṭaṅkukaḷ aṅku naṭipeṟum.

maṇamakaṉ tāliyi maṇamakaḷiṉ kaḻuttil vittatum, piṉṉāl niṉṟu koṇṭirukkum ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ atik kaṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. piṟaku maṇamakaṉiṉ kiyip peṇṇiṉ kiyil vittu, ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ tāri vārttuk koṭuppāṉ. āṉāl periya maliyāḷikaḷiṉ vaḻakkam itaṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭatu. 'kaṇiyāṉ' eṉpavaṉ maṇamakaḷiṉ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvāṉ. maṇamakaḷiṉ kalavikkuk kaṇiyāṉ mutal urimi peṟṟavaṉākak karutappaṭukiṟāṉ. (kēraḷattil taṭakkum tāli kaṭṭuk kaliyāṇaṅkaḷil 'maṇavāḷaṉ' eṉpavaṉ 'kaṇiyāṉ' ceyyum vēlikaḷic ceykiṟāṉ). ip paḻakkam maṟṟa maliyāḷikaḷāl muṟi keṭṭatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.



maṇavilakkum maṟumaṇamum :

periya maliyāḷikaḷ maṇavilakku {divorce) muṟiyi aṉumatippatilli. āṉāl kolli maliyāḷikaḷiṭam maṇavilakkuc ceytu. koḷḷum paḻakkam uṇṭu. āṉāl maṇavilakkiṉ pōtu ellāk kuḻantikaḷiyum (vēṟu kaṇavaṉukkup piṟantavikaḷāy iruntālum cari) kaṇavaṉiṭam oppaṭittu viṭavēṇṭum.

pacci maliyāḷikaḷ rū 25 oṟupput toki (Fine) yākap peṟṟuk koṇṭu maṇavilakku aḷippar. itu camaveḷiyiliruntu kaṟṟuk koṇṭa paḻakkam. oru pacci maliyāḷi maṇavilakkup peṟavirumpiṉāl atik kuruviṉ muṉṉiliyil kūṟa vēṇṭum. maṇa vilakkiṟku aṟikuṟiyāka oru vikkōl tuṇṭiyō allatu marattuṇṭiyō, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyiṭam koṭuppāṉ. maṇavilakkup peṟṟa peṇ muṉṉāḷ kaṇavaṉ iṟanta piṟakē maṟumaṇam ceytukoḷḷa aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟāḷ. kimpeṇ maṇam {Widow remarriage) ellā maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyilēyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. kolli maliyāḷikaḷ, kaṇavaṉiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāṉi maṇakka entak kimpeṇṇiyum aṉumatippatilli. pacci maliyāḷikaḷiṭam ik kaṭṭuppāṭu kiṭiyātu.

kolli maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē kimpeṇ maṇam naṭantāl maṇap peṇṇum maṇamakaṉum etiretiril muḻantāḷiṭṭu uṭkārntu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. naṭuvil oru tiriyiṭappaṭum. maṇamakaṉ tāliyit tiriyiṉaṭiyil koṇṭuvantu peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil vippāṉ. ūrkkavuṇṭaṉ tāliyik kaṭṭiya piṟakutāṉ maṇamakaṉ peṇṇiṉ mukattip pārkka muṭiyum. maṟumaṇattiṉ pōtu, kim peṇṇāṉavaḷ taṉ mutal kaṇavaṉukkup piṟanta kuḻantikaḷi, avaṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāriṭamō allatu neruṅkiya uṟaviṉariṭamō oppaṭittuviṭa vēṇṭum. tantiyiṉ cottukkaḷellām ak kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭṭā ceyyappaṭum. allatu muṉ kūṭṭiyē paṭṭā ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. ac cottukkaḷi iraṇṭām kaṇavaṉ aṉupavippatit taṭukkavē im muṟiyik kiyāḷukiṉṟaṉar.

pūppeytal :

oru peṇ pūppeytiyatum, avaḷi vīṭṭiliruntu vilakkit taṉiyiṭattil vippatu maliyāḷikaḷiṉ vaḻakkam. 30 nāṭkaḷ vilakku nāṭkaḷākak (Pollutionperiod) karutappaṭum. āṉāl camaveḷiyiluḷḷa makkaḷip pārttu ivarkaḷum ippōtu annāṭkaḷik kuṟittuk koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. pacci maliyāḷikaḷ oru peṇ pūppeytiyati aṟintatum ūrukku veḷiyiluḷḷa oru kuṭiciyil aintu nāṭkaḷ vittiruppar. āṟām nāḷ ap peṇṇi naṉṉīrāṭṭi vīṭṭiṟku aḻittu varuvar. aṭutta muppatu nāṭkaḷ vilakku nāṭkaḷākak karutappaṭum. annāṭkaḷ kaḻiyum variyilum ūr makkaḷ yārum av vīṭṭiṟkuḷ cella māṭṭārkaḷ. anta muppatu nāṭkaḷil peṇṇāṉavaḷ nāḷ tōṟum taliyil nīrpeytu kuḷikka vēṇṭum. vāram oru muṟi vīṭṭit tūymippaṭutta vēṇṭum. ūttaṅkariyiluḷḷa pacci maliyāḷikaḷ 12 nāḷ vilakkivikkiṉṟaṉar. kolli maliyāḷikaḷil cilar 30 nāṭkaḷum, vēṟu cilar 15 nāṭkaḷum vilakku nāṭkaḷākak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. periya maliyāḷikaḷ iṭattiṟkut takuntāṟpōl 7 nāṭkaḷiliruntu 11 nāṭkaḷ vari vilakku nāṭkaḷākak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. vilakku nāṭkaḷ muṭintatum, oru pārppaṉap purōkitari aḻittu vantu vīṭṭit tūymippaṭuttuvar.

makap pēṟu :

kuḻanti piṟanta paṉṉiraṇṭām nāḷilō allatu patiṉintām nāḷilō allatu patiṉāṟām nāḷilō vīṭṭit tūymip paṭuttit tāyi vīṭṭuk kaḻippar. āṉāl pacci maliyāḷikaḷ muppatām nāḷ variyil vilakku nāṭkaḷākak karutuvar. kuḻantikkup peyar cūṭṭuvataṟkeṉṟu, kuṟippiṭṭa entac caṭaṅkum kiṭiyātu. eppoḻutu peyar cūṭṭuvatu eṉṟa variyaṟiyum illi. kolli maliyāḷikaḷ kuḻanti piṟanta pattām nāḷilō allatu mūṉṟām tiṅkaḷilō peyariṭuvar. pacci maliyiluḷḷavarkaḷ mutal āṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum, cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷavarkaḷ mūṉṟām nāḷilum peyariṭuvar. peyariṭuvataṟku muṉpāka, entap peyarvippatu yeṉpatu paṟṟi avvūriluḷḷa pūcāriyik kalantu kēṭpar. cila caṭaṅkukaṭkup piṟaku pūcāri maruḷ koṇṭu āṭi, akkuḻantikku iṭavēṇṭiya peyarik kūṟuvāṉ. eppoḻutum kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyē kuḻantikaṭkup perumpālum iṭuvar. āṇāka iruntāl koṅkaṉ, vaṭamaṉ, cīraṅkaṉ, piṭavaṉ, kāḷi, aṟappaḷi eṉṟum, peṇṇāka iruntāl koṅkāyi, vaṭami, cīraṅki, piṭāri eṉṟum peyariṭuvar. kariyaṉ, veḷḷiyaṉ, kuṭṭiyaṉ, caṭiyaṉ, periya piyaṉ, ciṉṉap piyaṉ eṉak kāraṇap peyarkaḷum iṭuvatuṇṭu.

āṭi aṇikaḷ :

maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ iravikki aṇivatilli. vīṭṭilirukkumpōtum, vayalkaḷil vēli ceyyum pōtum uṭaliṉ mēṟpakuti tiṟantē irukkum. vēṟṟār muṉṉiliyil varumpōtum, cantikkuc cellumpōtum āṭiyiṉ muṉ tāṉiyit taḷartti mārpip pōrttu, iṭatu tōḷiṉ mēl koṇarntu mutukuppuṟam toṅka viṭuvārkaḷ. pacci maliyāḷikaḷ eppoḻutum vaṇṇa āṭikaḷiyē virumpuvar. pacci maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ tirumaṇattiṉpōtu maṭṭum veḷḷi niṟamāṉa kūṟi (puttāṭi) aṇivar. maṟṟa nēraṅkaḷil cikappu allatu karuppu āṭikaḷiyē aṇivar. āṉāl paḷic ceṉṟirukkum mañcaḷ, ārañcu, pacci niṟap puṭavikaḷiṉmītu avarkaṭku viruppamatikam. āṇkaḷ aṇiyum āṭi kōvaṇam oṉṟutāṉ. āṉāl atil kūṭa vaṇṇatti virumpuvar. kolli maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ iṭatu pakkamum. pacci maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ valatu pakkamum mūkkuk kuttik koḷvar. periya maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ mūkkē kuttik koḷvatilli. pacci niṟamāṉatum, irattac civappu niṟamāṉatumāṉa kaṇṇāṭikaḷ patitta kātu vaḷiyaṅkaḷi aṇivar.

iṟutic caṭaṅku :

maliyāḷikaḷ potuvāka, piṇaṅkaḷip putikkum paḻakka muṭiyavarkaḷ. āṉāl kālarā, toḻunōy mutaliyavaṟṟāl pīṭikkappaṭṭu iṟantavarkaḷuṭiya piṇaṅkaḷukkup 'pāl teḷikkum caṭaṅku' kiṭiyātu. maṟṟac caṭaṅkukaḷellām camaveḷiyiluḷḷavaṟṟip pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. piṇattit tūkkic cellum pāṭiyi avarkaḷum 'tēr' eṉṟē kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tīṭṭu nāṭkaḷ iṭattiṟku iṭam māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pacci maliyil ittīṭṭu nāṭkaḷ oru tiṅkaḷ vari nīṭikkum. ūttaṅkari vaṭṭattiluḷḷa pacci maliyāḷikaḷ 10 nāṭkaḷum, periya maliyāḷikaḷ 12 allatu 15 nāṭkaḷum, kolli maliyāḷikaḷ 3 nāṭkaḷum tīṭṭākak karutuvar. iṟantavarkaḷuṭiya āvi cila nāṭkaḷ vīṭṭic cuṟṟi aliyum eṉṟu karutukiṟārkaḷ. atit tiruptip paṭuttuvataṟkākap pūcāri kūṟum muṟippaṭi kōḻi, āṭu, paṉṟi mutaliyavaṟṟip paliyiṭuvar. ōr iruppāṇiyiyō allatu muḷiyiyō putikuḻiyil piṇattiṉ talikku nērāka aṭittu, avvāviyi vīṭṭiṉ pakkam varāmal taṭuppar. iṟantupōṉa muṉṉōri vaḻipaṭuvataṟkāka avarkaḷ 'cirārttam' ceyvatilli. āṉāl tirumaṇam, makappēṟu, pūppeytal mutaliya nikaḻccikaḷ naṭipeṟum pōtum, viḻāk kālaṅkaḷilum, veḷḷik kiḻamikaḷilum iṟantōrukku vaḻipāṭu ceyyum vaḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu. ivvāṟu vaṇaṅkum muṉṉōrkaḷi 'vīṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷ' eṉṟum, 'paṭṭavaṉ' eṉṟum ivarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. maliyāḷikaḷ taṅkaḷ illaṅkaḷi mikavum puṉitamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷākak karutukiṉṟaṉar. pārppaṉap purōkitarikkūṭa, ceruppuk kālōṭu ivarkaḷ vīṭṭiṟkuḷ aṉumatippatilli. camaya vāḻvu : maliyāḷikaḷ civaperumāṉiyum tirumāliyum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. nāḷtōṟum tirunīṟu aṇiyum vaḻakkattik kaṭip piṭikkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl cila kuṟippiṭṭa camaya vaḻipāṭṭiṉ pōtu maṭṭum tirumaṇ (nāmam) cārttik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. taṅkaḷuṭiya kōyilkaḷil taṅkaḷ iṉattāriyē arccakarkaḷāka amarttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. maliyāḷikaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṟku urimi peṟṟa perun teyvam tirumāliṉ avatāramāṉa karirāmaṉ. avaṉ kōyilkoṇṭu eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum iṭaṅkaḷil talimiyāṉatu periya kalrāyaṉ maliyilirukkum 'mēl nāṭṭi'c cārnta 'kōyil putūr' ākum. tammampaṭṭiyilum karirāmaṉukkuk kōyil uṇṭu. cērvarāyaṉ malimītuḷḷa karaṭiyūrilum ikkaṭavuḷukkuk kōyil eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu uruvattil kōyil putūrilirukkum kōyilip pōṉṟē uḷḷatu cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷōr aṅku eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum kaṭavuḷik 'kariya perumāḷ' eṉṟē aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. cēlattiṟku aṇmiyil oru kuṉṟiṉmēl kariyaperumāḷ kōyil uḷḷatu, akkuṉṟu 'kariyaperumāḷ mali' eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

kōyil putūrilirukkum karirāmaṉ kōyilil paramacivaṉ, pārvati, tirumāl, ilakkumi, vināyakaṉ ākiyōriṉ paṭimaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa, avaṟṟōṭu paṉṉiraṇṭu naṭukaṟkaḷum veṇ puḷḷi cārttappaṭṭu, iraṇṭu varicikaḷāka niṟkiṉṟaṉa. kōyiliṉ vāyilic caṅku cakkaraṅkaḷum, nāmamum, tirumāl, irāmaṉ uruvaṅkaḷum, nāṉku karuṭaṉum, nāṉku nantiyum aḻaku ceykiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru caṉikkiḻamiyum, ak kōyilil vaḻipāṭu nikaḻkiṉṟatu. āṇṭu tōṟum māci mātattil oru tērt tiruviḻāvum aṅku naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. ak kōyiṟ pūcāri pulāl uṇṇak kūṭātu. uyirp pali naṭipeṟum nikaḻccikaḷil paṅku koḷḷak kūṭātu. pulāl uṇpōriṉ uṭaṉamarntu uṇṇak kūṭātu. kari rāmaṉ kōyilil uyirkaḷip paliyiṭum vaḻakkam eppoḻutum kiṭiyātu. attakiya nikaḻccikaḷil paṅku koṇṭavarkaḷ, mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷ kaṭanta piṟakutāṉ kōyilukkuḷ nuḻiya vēṇṭum. maliyāḷi āṇkaḷum, peṇkaḷum ak kōyilukkuc ceṉṟu muṭi eṭukkum vaḻakkatti mēṟkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.

kolli malic carivil vēlappūr nāṭṭil oru civaṉ kōyil uḷḷatu. aṅku eḻuntaruḷiyiruk kum iṟivaṉ 'aṟappaḷīcuvaraṉ' eṉṟu aḻikkap paṭukiṟāṉ. maṟṟa maliyāḷikaḷ kōyilip pōlallāmal, iṅkup pārppaṉak kurukkaḷ arccakarāka amarttappaṭṭirukkiṟār. iv viṟivaṉmītu ampala vāṇak kavirāyar eṉpavar oru catakam pāṭiyuḷḷār, (catam eṉṟāl nūṟu. catakam eṉṟāl nūṟu pāṭalkaḷik koṇṭa nūl eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum). aṟappaḷīcuvara catakattiluḷḷa nūṟu pāṭalkaḷum "aṉutiṉamum maṉatil niṉi tarucaturakirivaḷar aṟappaḷīsvara tēvaṉē“ eṉṟa aṭikoṇṭu muṭikiṉṟamiyāl kolli malikkuc 'catura kiri' eṉṟa peyarum vaḻaṅkiyatu pōlum. 'teṇṇīrp poṉṉi tirik kiyāl aṭivaruṭap paḷḷi koḷḷum tiruvaraṅkap perumāṉi'yum ivarkaḷ paktiyōṭu vaṇaṅku kiṉṟaṉar.

maliyāḷikaḷ vāḻum ciṟṟūrkaḷil uḷḷa perumāḷ kōyilkaḷil tirumāl vaḻipāṭu pala māṟupāṭukaḷōṭu citintu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ak kōyilkaḷil naṭipeṟuvatu uṇmiyil tirumāl vaḻipāṭutāṉā eṉa aiyuṟa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. tirumāl vaḻipāṭṭiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta palipīṭam (shrine) cila kōyilkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvatilli, potuvāka ik kōyilkaḷil uyirp pali naṭipeṟuvatilli. iṅkup paṇipuriyum pūcāri (maliyāḷi)yum pulāl uṇṇātavaṉākavē irukkiṟāṉ.

nāmakkal kolli maliyiluḷḷa kuki nāṭṭil ittakiya kōyil oṉṟu uḷḷatu. ak kōyilil vīṟṟirukkum iṟivaṉip 'pēyp perumāḷ' eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. iṅku caṉikkiḻamikaḷilēyē pūci naṭipeṟukiṟatu.

kolli maliyāḷikaḷ 'araṅkaṭṭappaṉ' eṉṟa oru kaṭavuḷiyum vaḻipaṭukiṟārkaḷ. 'araṅka civaṉ' eṉṟa vēṟu peyarum ak kaṭavuḷukku uṇṭu. maliyāḷikaḷiṉ kula mutalvarāṉa mūṉṟu cakōtararkaḷum vaṇaṅkiya kula teyvamē araṅkaṭṭappaṉ eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum karirāmaṉiṉ vēṟṟava tāramākavum ik kaṭavuḷik karutukiṉṟaṉar. kuṇṭūr nāṭṭilum araṅkaṭṭappaṉ kōyil uḷḷatu. ikkōyilkaḷil pārppaṉak kurukkaḷē paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar. iṅku naṭipeṟum vaḻipāṭu maṟṟa civaṉ kōyil vaḻipāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ciṟitu māṟik kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mutalil taṇṇīrilum piṟaku pālilum, mūṉṟāvatāka nalleṇṇeyilum iṟivaṉukku apiṭēkam naṭipeṟukiṟatu. piṟaku puttāṭi aṇivittu, iṟivaṉiṉ tirumēṉiyil cantaṉattiyum, kuṅkumattiyum pūcukiṉṟaṉar. piṟaku tūpam kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. tīpaṅkaḷiyum koḷutti vikkiṉṟaṉar, oru vāḻiyiliyi iṟivaṉ tirumuṉṉāl virittu, atil poṅkalip paṭikkiṉṟaṉar. vaḻakkamāṉa mantiraṅkaḷik kūṟi, cūṭattik koḷutti vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. uyirp pali iṅkuk kiṭiyātu. poṅkal, pāl, carkkari, paḻam mutaliyaṉavum civa vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya vēṟu poruḷkaḷumē iṅkup paṭikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, araṅka civaṉukku aṭimip paṇipuriyum oru ciṟu teyvamum maliyāḷikaḷāl vaṇaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. akkaṭavuḷukku 'araṅka cēvakaṉ' eṉṟu peyar. araṅka cēvakaṉ kōyilil paṇipuriyum pūcāri pārppaṉaralla; maliyāḷiyē. araṅka cēvakaṉukku āṭu, kōḻikaḷ mutalil paliyiṭappaṭṭaṉavāka aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ippoḻutu ap paḻakkam illi.

vināyakar vaḻipāṭu camaveḷiyil naṭappatip pōṉṟē maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyilum naṭipeṟukiṟatu. ceṅkuttāṉa oru kalli naṭṭu, mēlē kūriyillāmal ik kōyilkaḷ amikkap peṟum. cila kōyilkaḷ cuṟṟuc cuvar illāmalum amikkap peṟum. ittakiya kōyilkaḷ cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷa mēlūrilum, kākampāṭiyilum uḷḷaṉa. vināyakar vaḻipāṭṭiṟku ēṟṟa nāḷ tiṅkaṭkiḻamiyē. murukaṉ vaḻipāṭum maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē uṇṭu. piṟakari nāṭṭiluḷḷa kantacāmi kōyil pukaḻ peṟṟatākum. paṅkuṉi uttarattiṉpōtu, ik kaṭavuḷukku viḻā eṭukkiṉṟaṉar. umiyiṉ vaṭivamāṉa kāmāṭciyip pala iṭaṅkaḷil kōyil kaṭṭi vaḻipaṭukiṉṟaṉar. pala iṭaṅkaḷil tarumarācar kōyil iruppatāl pāṇṭavar vaḻipāṭum maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyil iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.

maliyāḷikaḷ vaṇaṅkum ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷ (Demi Gods) kaṇakkilaṭaṅkātavi. caktiyiṉ avatāraṅḷāṉa kāḷi, piṭāri, māri mutaliya teyvaṅkaṭku eṅku pārttālum kōyilkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ayyaṉārukkum kōyil uṇṭu. cittiri allatu vikācit tiṅkaḷil eṭappuli nāṭṭil kāḷi tēvikkut tēr viḻā naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. caṉikkiḻami, kāḷi tēviyiṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṟkukanta nāḷākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. piṭāri eṉṟa teyvam, periya piṭāri, ciṉṉap piṭāri, cokkap piṭāri, putup piṭāri, karum piṭāri, kārak kāṭṭup piṭāri, maluṅkup piṭāri eṉap pala aṭi moḻikaḷōṭu maliyāḷikaḷāl aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu piṭārikkuriya vaḻipāṭṭu nāḷum, āṇṭu viḻāvum nāṭṭukku nāṭu māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷukkāṉa viḻākkaḷil ciṟappāṉatu māriyammaṉ tiruviḻāvākum. ivviḻā vacanta kālattil (ti, māci, paṅkuṉi) muḻu nilā nāṭkaḷil koṇṭāṭap paṭukiṟatu. cevvāykkiḻamiyum, veḷḷikkiḻamiyum māriyammaṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṟkukanta nāṭkaḷ. nācciyammaṉ, poṅkalāyi, koṅkalāyi, poṉṉāyi eṉpavi vēṟu pala kuṟippiṭattakka peṇ teyvaṅkaḷ. mēla nācci, koṭikari nācci, ariya nācci, iḷiya nācci, eḻukari nācci eṉap pala peyarkaḷōṭu nācci kāṭciyaḷikkiṟāḷ. avaḷ vaḻipāṭṭukkukanta nāḷ viyāḻakkiḻamiyākum. vaḻipaṭumpōtu nācciyār kōyilil niṟinta amiti nilaval vēṇṭum. vaḻipāṭṭiṉ iṟutiyil paṭiyal (piracātam) ellārukkum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. vēṭarkaḷik kākkum kula teyvamāka nācci karutappaṭukiṟāḷ. kolli maliyil ivaḷukku niṟiyak kōyilkaḷuṇṭu, naṭuvaṇṇaṉi maṇanta vēṭacciyiṭamiruntu, nācciyammaṉ vaḻipāṭu maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē paraviṟṟu eṉpar. poṅkalāyi, kucakkuḻip poṅkalāyi, mayilāttip poṅkalāyi, taṇṇip pāḻip poṅkalāyi, vēlarāyaṉ poṅkalāyi, mūlikkāṭṭup poṅkalāyi, paṇikkaṉ kāṭṭup poṅkalāyi, pēyk kāṭṭup poṅkalāyi eṉap pala aṭi moḻikaḷōṭu, poṅkalāyi eṉṉum ciṟu teyvam maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē kōyil koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟāḷ.

ayyaṉār, periya āṇṭavaṉ, āṇṭiyappaṉ, nampiyāṇṭāṉ, caṭiyaṉ, veṭṭukkāraṉ, māci maliyaṉ, uruḷiyaṉ eṉa vēṟupala ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. āṉāl karuppaṉār vaḻipāṭu maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ovvoru ūrilum karuppaṉārukkuk kōyiluṇṭu. paṉṟippali karuppaṉārukku viruppamāṉa oṉṟu. kaṉṉiyammāḷ eṉṟa peṇ teyvam karuppaṉārōṭu toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

potuvākac ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷukku, muṟiyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ kiṭiyā, tiṟanta veḷiyilō allatu kūriyillāta cuvarkaḷukku naṭuvilō ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ amikkappeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṟippākak karuppaṉār kōyil ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum ivvāṟē amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maliyāḷikaḷē ikkōyilkaḷiṉ pūcārikaḷāka uḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷit 'tācaṉ' eṉṟum 'āṇṭi' eṉṟum aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. ikkōyiṟ paṇi avarkaḷukkup parampari urimiyuṭiyatu. orē pūcāri pala kōyilkaḷilum paṇipurivatuṇṭu. talippākiyum, nīṇṭa muṭiyum ivarkaḷi maṟṟa maliyāḷikaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. vāḻnāḷ muḻutumō allatu kuṟippiṭṭa cila nāṭkaḷō ivarkaḷ pulāl uṇavi otukki viṭukiṉṟaṉar. maliyāḷikaḷiṉ mukkiyamāṉa peruviḻākkaḷ poṅkal, tīpāvaḷi, āṭippatiṉeṭṭu ākiya mūṉṟumē. avaṟṟil poṅkal tirunāḷ mikavum ciṟanta muṟiyil koṇṭāṭappaṭukiṟatu. māṭṭup poṅkalaṉṟu maliyāḷikaḷ kūṭṭamākac cērntu koṇṭu vēṭṭikkuc (Hunting Excursion) celvatuṇṭu . aṉṟu naṭipeṟum erutāṭṭam (Bull Dance) kaṇṭu makiḻattakkatu.

vēṟu cila paḻakkaṅkaḷ :

maliyāḷikaḷ potuvāka varaku, tiṉi, arici ākiyavaṟṟic camittu uṇkiṉṟaṉar. paṉṟi iṟicciyum avarkaḷuṭiya uṇavil mukkiya iṭampeṟukiṟatu. cakkimukkik kalli irumputtuṇṭāl aṭittu neruppuṇṭākkum paḻakkam iṉṉum pacci maliyāḷikaḷiṭam nilavi varukiṟatu. ovvoru paṭṭiyilum iruvar allatu mūvarē ikkaruviyi vittirukkiṉṟaṉar. neruppu uṇṭākkuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭum tuṇip poruḷāṉa pañci oru tōṟ piyil potintu vittuḷḷaṉar. vaṇṇāṉ, nāvitaṉ, maruttuvacci ākiyōriṉ paṇiyi avarkaḷilēyē oru cilar mēṟkoṇṭu ceykiṉṟaṉar. mukkiyamāṉa ceytikaḷit tuṭumpu koṭṭi ellāp paṭṭikaḷilum aṟivikkum vēliyi aṅkuḷḷa aricaṉaṅkaḷ (paṟiyar) ceykiṉṟaṉar. vayalkaḷil vēliceyvataṟkum, vēṭṭiyil tuṇi purivataṟkum aricaṉaṅkaḷiyē amarttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. kālnaṭikaḷil ētāvatoṉṟu iṟantuviṭṭāl, maliyāḷikaḷyārum ataṉ arukil kūṭac cellamāṭṭārkaḷ ; atit toṭavumāṭṭārkaḷ. arukilirukkum aricaṉaṅkaḷukkut terivittu eṭuttukkoṇṭu cellumāṟu kūṟuvārkaḷ. āṉāl ētēṉum vipattukkuḷḷāki aṭipaṭṭuc cākum niliyilirukkum kāl naṭikaḷit taṅkaḷiṭam kūlivēli ceypavarkaḷukku viṟṟuviṭuvārkaḷ. ataṟku īṭākac ciṟutoki peṟṟukkoḷvar. maliyāḷikaḷil cilar ciṟanta māṭṭu vittiyarkaḷāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ māṭṭiṉ oṭinta elumpi mika virivil kūṭumāṟu ceyyum āṟṟal mikkavarkaḷ, pacumāṭṭiṉ tōli avarkaḷ toṭuvatu kūṭak kiṭiyātu. uḻavut toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭum poruḷkaḷiyum attōliṉāl ceyvatilli. iṟantupōṉa pacuviṉ tōlikkūṭat tāṅkaḷ urimiyākkik koḷḷa virumpuvatilli. appiṇatti eṭuttuc cellum aricaṉaṅkaḷukkē koṭuttu viṭuvar. maliyāḷikaḷum peṇkaḷum mikutiyākap pukip piṭikkum paḻakkamuṭiyavarkaḷ.

kuṟiñci nilaṅkaḷil vāḻum im maliyāḷikaḷ palvēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷāl camaveḷiyiluḷḷa makkaḷōṭu toṭarpiṉṟi vāḻntu vantaṉar. malipaṭu poruḷkaḷik koṇṭē taṅkaḷ iṉṟiyamiyāta tēvikaḷit tīrttukkoṇṭu vāḻntu vantaṉar, muṉṉēṟṟattil ivarkaḷ atika akkaṟi kāṭṭavilli. āṉāl nam nāṭṭu araciyalār, ivarkaḷuṭiya muṉṉēṟṟattilum ippoḻutu karuttuc celuttukiṉṟaṉar. nalla malippātikaḷ kolli malimītu ippōtu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṭiyiruppukkāṉa vīṭukaḷum, kalvivacatikkāṉa paḷḷikaḷum ippoḻutu niṟiyak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maruntu vacatikaḷum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum cila tiṅkaḷkaḷil untu vaṇṭikaḷum immaliyiṉ mēl viṭappaṭum. paḻamiyāṉa vaḻakkaṅkaḷiṉ piṭiyiliruntu ivarkaḷ ippōtu nīṅki varukiṉṟaṉar.





↑ cēlattil ivar peyarāl iṉṟum oru vīti uḷḷatu.

patippurimi aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉittup patippurimikaḷiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

ēṣiyaṉ piriṇṭars,

ceṉṉi-5.





﻿muṉṉuri

“oru coṟṟāeṭar eḻutuvataṟku nūṟu nūlkaḷ paṭittēṉ. oru varuṇaṉi eḻutuvataṟku nūṟu kal cuṟṟup payaṇam ceytēṉ” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭāṉ āṅkilac collēruḻavaṉ mekālē. itip paṭittavuṭaṉ eṉṉiyaṟiyāmal oruvaki uḷḷakkiḷarcci peṟṟēṉ. ‘nāmum ēṉ tamiḻakattic cuṟṟivarakkūṭātu?’ eṉṟa eṇṇam eṉ uḷḷattil ēṟpaṭṭatu. mutaṉ mutalāka iyaṟki eḻilmikka tamiḻakattu malikaḷic cuṟṟip pārkkavēṇṭum eṉṟu muṭivuceytēṉ. inta muṭivu eṉ uḷḷattil karukkoṇṭa poḻutu, ippayaṇatti ōr iṉpap payaṇamākavē mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉittēṉ. āṉāl puṟappaṭum nērattil eṉ uḷḷam māṟiviṭṭatu. ippayaṇattip payaṉuḷḷa vaḻiyil celaviṭṭāl eṉṉa eṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ. ataṉ viḷivē innūl. āṉāl mutalil eṉ uḷḷattil karukkoṇṭa kuṟikkōḷ ﻿muṟṟup peṟāmal pōyviṭṭatu. varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷiyum, puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷiyum tēṭittirinta kāraṇattāl iyaṟki eḻilil eṉ uḷḷatti muḻukka muḻukkat tōyaviṭṭu, iṉpam nukara muṭiyāmal pōyviṭṭatu. akkuṟiyi niṟivu ceyya, nāṉ mīṇṭumorumuṟi tamiḻakattu malikaḷic cuṟṟittāṉ ākavēṇṭum.

innūl veḷivarap pala naṇparkaḷ eṉakkup pērutavi purintaṉar. avarkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavar cēlam māvaṭṭa viḷampara atikāriyum, eṉ keḻutaki naṇparumāṉa tiru. T. M. kāḷiyappā, M. A. avarkaḷ. ciṟanta mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷi avar eṉakku aritiṉ muyaṉṟu tēṭittantār. nīlakirik kāṭukaḷiṉ uṭpakutiyil nuḻintu pala ceytikaḷi aṟivataṟkum, tōṭarkaḷ vāḻum toṭṭapeṭṭā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu avarkaḷ paṇpāṭṭi aṟivataṟkum eṉakkup peruntuṇi purinta paṭakā iḷiñaṉāṉa ‘liṅkamūrtti’yi eṉṉāl iṅkuk kuṟippiṭāmal irukka muṭiyavilli. avviḷiñaṉ eṉṉik kutiri mītu amartti, eḷitil ceṉṟaṭiya muṭiyāta iṭaṅkaṭkellām iṭṭuc ceṉṟu, pala ariya ceytikaḷi aṟivataṟkup peruntuṇi purintāṉ.

innūl ciṟanta muṟiyil acciṭappaṭṭu uṅkaḷ kiyil vaṇṇamalarāy, virintiruppataṟkuk kāraṇamāṉavar, matippiṟkuriya tiru. paḻaṉiyappā avarkaḷē aṉṉārkku eṉ naṉṟi.

murukucuntaram

cēlam

1-12-61





﻿ 1. kuṟiñci

maṅkiyariṉ cirippoliyōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭṭu maliyiliruntu iḻintuvarum kuṟṟāla nīrvīḻcciyil mūḻkiyapōtum, utakamaṇṭalattic cuṟṟip paraviyuḷḷa kaṇkavarum iḷa marakkāṭṭil kavaliyiṉṟic cuṟṟit tirintapōtum, aṇiyiḻi makaḷir āṭavar puṭicūḻak kōṭikkāṉal ēriyil tōṇiyūrnta kāṭciyi nēril kaṇṭapōtum, puraṭcik kaviñaṉ tamiḻakatti vāḻttip pāṭiya teḷḷu tamiḻppaṇ, teṉṟal ivarntu eṉ cevippaṟiyil mōtiyatu.

“puṉaliṭi mūḻkip poḻiliṭi ulavi

poṉṉār iḻiyum tukilum pūṇṭu

kaṉimoḻi pēci illaṟam nāṭum

kātal mātar makiḻuṟum nāṭu!”





ippāṭal, ettaṉi muṟi paṭittālum teviṭṭāmal tittikkum kollittēṉ. “maṅki orutti tarum cukamum eṅkaḷ māttamiḻukku īṭāmō?” eṉṟa maṟattamiḻaṉ kūṟṟukkup poruttamāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu! iṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ iyaṟki vaḷatti eḻil nalattōṭu kūṭṭi, nam kaṇṇetirē koṇṭu vantu niṟuttum paṭappiṭippu aṉpoṭu iyinta kātal vāḻvi aṇipeṟap potintu vitta ﻿alaṅkārap peṭṭakam ! paṇṭit tamiḻ vāḻvit teriyak kāṭṭum paṇṇamippu !

ikkoñcu tamiḻp pāṭṭil eṉ uḷḷattik kuḻiyaviṭṭu, tamiḻakattu malikaḷil iraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ pittaṉippōl cuṟṟiyalintēṉ. cilampil cirittu, mēkaliyil miṉṉi, caṅkappoḻilil tavaḻntu viḷiyāṭum centamiḻk kumariyōṭu uṟavukoṇṭa eṉ kaviti uḷḷam, kapilarum paraṇarum, avviyum ātimantiyum vāḻnta caṅka kālatti nōkkic ciṟakaṭittup paṟantu ceṉṟatu. iṉṟiya viññāṉa nākarikam eṉ uḷḷattiviṭṭu nīṅkiyatu. īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa eṉṉarum tamiḻ nākarikam eṉ itayattil kuṭippukuntatu. nāṟpuṟac cuvarkaḷukkuḷ iruntu varivariyākap paṭitta kuṟiñcik kaliyiṉ uṇmip poruḷi nēril kaṇṭu uṇarntēṉ. kuṟiñcippāṭṭiṉ virivuriyi iṅku kūrntu kaṟṟēṉ. akanāṉūṟṟuppāṭalkaḷi iṅku āḻntu payiṉṟēṉ. paṇṭit tamiḻkkuṭiyiṉ kuṟiñci vāḻvu eṉ uḷḷattil niḻalāṭiyatu. accaṅka kālak kaṉavilēyē mūḻki eṉṟeṉṟum iruntuviṭṭāl eṉṉa eṉṟu eṉ uḷḷam ēṅkiyatu.

veḷḷiyar vāṇipattiṟkāka vantu iṟaṅkiya pōtutāṉ nākarikam eṉṉum carakkiyum taṅkaḷ kappaliliruntu tamiḻakattil koṭṭiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇum vaḻakkam cila mētāvikaḷiṭiyē iṉṟum iruntu varukiṟatu. kuḷir nāṭṭil vāḻnta veḷḷitturimārkaḷ tamiḻakattuk kotikkum veyilukku āṟṟāmal utakikkum, kōṭikkāṉalukkum ōṭiṉar. āṅkila nākarikattic cemmaṟiyāṭṭip pōl piṉpaṟṟiya nam nāṭṭuc celvarkaḷum, veḷḷiyariṉ vālippiṭittukkoṇṭu kuṟiñci nakaraṅ (Hill Stations) kaḷukku ōṭiṉar. kōṭiyil ﻿kuṟiñci nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻvati nākarikam eṉṟum karutiṉar. mali vāḻkkiyikkūṭa veḷi nāṭṭār tām namakkuk kaṟṟuk koṭuttatākak karutiṉar. pāvam! nam ilakkiyattil parantu kiṭakkum kuṟiñcit tiṇi avarkaḷ kaṇṇil paṭuvatilli.

ulakil vaḻaṅkum vaḷarcciyuṟṟa moḻikaḷilellām eḻuttu, col, yāppu, aṇi eṉṟa nāṉkiṟkum ilakkaṇam uṇṭu. āṉāl vāḻkkikkum ilakkaṇam amittu ataṉpaṭi vāḻnta perumi, paravicūḻa ipparanta ulakil tamiḻarukkēyaṉṟi vēṟu yārukkum kiṭiyātu. avvāḻkki ilakkaṇatti akam, puṟam eṉat tamiḻar iru kūṟākkiṉar. avvilakkaṇattiṉ aṭippaṭiyilēyē tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷellām taḻittu vaḷarntaṉa.

aka vāḻkki kātal vāḻkkiyākum. otta kātal vayappaṭṭa ōriḷiñaṉum kumariyum, tuytta iṉpatti iṉṉateṉa veḷiyil kūṟa iyalumō? kaṟṟaṟinta kātal moḻiyiyum, kātalaṉpāl uṟṟaṟinta iṉpa uṇarviyum akattil potintu vittu, eṇṇi eṇṇi iṉpaveṟi koḷvāḷē tavira entap peṇṇum veḷiyil kūṟamāṭṭāḷ. koñcumoḻik kōtiyiṭam payiṉṟa kātal viḷiyāṭṭi, neñcil niṟutti niṉivit tēṉākki vāḻvāṉē tavira, enta āṭavaṉum veḷikkūṟāṉ. ivvāṟu akattōṭaminta aṉpuṭi vāḻkki ‘akam’ eṉappaṭṭatu. avvāṟaṉṟi, tam vāḻvil tuytta iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷip palarum aṟiya veḷippaṭak kūṟum takutiyuṭiyatu puṟam eṉappaṭṭatu. akam eṉṟāl iṉpatti maṭṭum kuṟippataṉṟu. tuṉpamum kāmattic cārntu nikaḻumāyiṉ atu akattilum, piṟa cārpu paṟṟit tōṉṟumāyiṉ atu puṟatti﻿lum koḷḷappaṭum. maṟṟum īki, vīram, yāvum puṟam eṉappaṭum.

tamiḻ makkaḷ nilatti aivakiyākap pirittaṉar. kuṟiñci, pāli, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉpaṉavē avi. maliyum mali cārnta iṭamum kuṟiñci eṉavum, vayalum vayal cārnta iṭamum marutam eṉavum, kaṭalum kaṭal cārnta iṭamum neytal eṉavum, kāṭum kāṭu cārnta iṭamum mulli eṉavum kuṟikkap peṟum. ākiyiṉāltāṉ nilavulaki ‘nāṉilam’ eṉṟa peyarāl tamiḻp periyōr kuṟittup pōntaṉar. pālikkut taṉi nilappakuppuk kiṭiyātu. kuṟiñciyum mulliyum kāla vēṟṟumiyāl māṟic curamātalē pāliyeṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. innilaṅkaḷil nilipeṟṟa poruḷkaḷi mutaṟporuḷ, karupporuḷ, uripporuḷ eṉa mūvakip paṭuttiṉar.

mutaṟporuḷ nilam kālam eṉa iruvakippaṭum. kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya perumpoḻutu kūtir kālamum muṉpaṉik kālamum ākum. ciṟupoḻutu yāmam. karupporuḷ eṉpatu antanta nilattiṟkuriya teyvam, uyarntōr, tāḻntōr, paṟavi, vilaṅku, ūr, nīr, pū, maram, uṇavu, paṟi, yāḻ, paṇ, toḻil mutaliyaṉa. kuṟiñcikkuriya teyvam murukaṉ. uyarntōr poruppaṉ, veṟpaṉ, cilampaṉ, koṭicci. tāḻntōr kuṟavar, kāṉavar, kuṟattiyar. paṟavikaḷ, kiḷi, mayil. vilaṅku, paṉṟi, puli, karaṭi, yāṉi, ciṅkam. ūr ciṟukuṭi, kuṟicci. nīr, aruvinīr, cuṉinīr. pū, vēṅkippū, kuṟiñcippū, kuvaḷippū, kāntaṭpū. maram cantaṉam, tēkku, akil, vēṅki, timicu, acōkam, nākam, mūṅkil. uṇavu malinel, mūṅkilarici, tiṉi, tēṉ, paḻam. paṟi, murukiyam, toṇṭakappaṟi. yāḻ kuṟiñci yāḻ. ﻿paṇ kuṟiñcippaṇ. toḻil veṟiyāṭal, malinel vitittal, tiṉikāttal, kiḷikaṭital, tēṉaḻit teṭuttal, kiḻaṅku kiṇṭiyeṭuttal, aruvi nīrāṭal mutaliyaṉa.

kuṟiñcikkuriya uripporuḷkaḷ, puṇartalum, puṇartal nimittamum ākum. iyaṟkip puṇarcci yum iṭantalippāṭum, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamum, pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭamum, iravuk kuṟikkaṇ etirppāṭum, pakaṟ kuṟikkaṇ etirppāṭum puṇartal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum, talivaṉ tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟum pakutiyum, appōtu tōḻi kūṟuvaṉavum, kuṟi nērtalum, maṟuttalum puṇartal nimittamākum.

kuṟiñci eṉṟa ippeyar annilattil pūkkum malarpaṟṟi vantatākum. kuṟiñcippū nam nāṭṭil mikavum arukik kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu pallāṇṭukaḷukku orumuṟiyē pūkkum taṉmiyatu. aḻakum maṇamum mikkatu. itu malarum kālatti muṉkūṭṭiyē aṟintu mēli nāṭṭiṉarum, nam nāṭṭil ārvamuḷḷa palarum atu pūkkum iṭattil ceṉṟu kūṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṟutiyāka 1959-ām āṇṭu, kuṟiñci malar kōṭikkāṉalil pūttatu. icceytiyip pattirikikaḷ viḷamparappaṭuttiṉa.

nāṉilaṅkaḷil kuṟiñciyē ciṟantatu; vaṟṟāta vaḷappamuṭiyatu. ataṉāltāṉ vippu muṟiyil kuṟiñciyi mutalil vittuk kūṟiṉar ilakkaṇa nūlār. maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷippōl kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ uḻikka vēṇṭiyatilli. ivarkaḷatu vāḻkkikkup pōtumāṉa vaḷam iyaṟkiyākavē maṇṭik kiṭakkiṟatu. eppoḻutum iṭiyaṟāta maḻi peytu koṇṭiruppatāl, nīr vaḷattiṟku iṅku kuṟivilli. eṅku pārttālum aruvikaḷum, ciṟṟāṟukaḷum cuḻittu ōṭiya vaṇṇamirukkum. tiṉiyi vitittuviṭṭāl tāṉāka viḷiyum. aruvi nīriṉ vaḷattāl malinel viḷikiṟatu. mūṅkilariciyum kuṟiñcivāḻ makkaḷukku uṇavāka amikiṟatu. tēṉum ivarkaḷukkuc ciṟanta uṇavākum. tiṉi māvit tēṉil picintu kāṉavar viruppōṭu uṇpar. kāṭukaḷil uḷḷa tēṉkūṭṭi aḻittut tēṉeṭuppatu ivarkaḷukkuc ciṟanta poḻutu pōkku. kāṉavar tēṉeṭuppatil peru viruppuṭiyavar eṉpatik kuṟiñcik kali ilakkiya nayattōṭu kūṟic cellukiṟatu.

malināṭṭut talivaṉ oruvaṉ, kātaliyik kāṇavarāmal kālantāḻttukiṟāṉ. avaṉ pirivip poṟuttal āṟṟāta kātali, mikavum uḷḷam nintu vāṭukiṟāḷ. avaḷ tuṉpattik kaṇṭa tōḻi, “aṉpuṭit tōḻi! uṉ kātalaṉ uyarkuṭiyil piṟantavaṉ. avaṉ malināṭu vaḷappam mikkatu. avaṉ nāṭṭil, tiṉik kolliyil amitta paraṇmītu amarntu kāval puriyum kaṉi moḻippāviyar, tam kūntaliṉ īratti ularttik koḷvataṟkākap pukikkum akiṟpuki, vāṉil ceṉṟu paravukiṟatu. appukiyuḷ putiyuṇṭa viṇmati oḷi maṟittu maliyucciyi aṭikiṟatu. ammaliyiṉ aṭukkattil tēṉ kūṭukaḷ malintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tēṉ kūṭukaḷiyē kaṇṭu paḻakiya kāṉavar kaṇkaḷ, pukiyuṇṭa muḻumatiyi maliyucciyil kaṇṭatum, atiyum oru tēṉ kūṭu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. akkūṭṭi aṭivataṟkāka maliyucciyil ēṇiyic cārtti ēṟukiṉṟaṉar. attakiya ceḻippumikka malināṭṭut talivaṉ uṉṉik kāṇak kaṭṭāyam varuvāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟittēṟṟukiṟāḷ. ﻿

appāṭal piṉ varumāṟu :

“avaṉuntāṉ, ēṉal itaṉattu akiṟpuki uṇṭu iyaṅkum

vāṉ ūr matiyam varicēriṉ, avvarit

tēṉiṉ iṟāleṉa ēṇiiḻit tirukkum

kāṉakal nāṭaṉ makaṉ.”



aruvi nīriṉ vaḷattāl viḷinta malinelli avarkaḷ kuṟṟum ceyti ilakkiyattil cuvipaṭak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. muṟṟi maṇam vīcum cantaṉamarattāl ural ceyvārkaḷ. muttu utirumaḷavu mutircci peṟṟu, nīṇṭu vaḷarnta yāṉiyiṉ kompi ulakkiyākap payaṉpaṭuttuvārkaḷ. muṟam pōl akaṉṟirukkum cēmpiṉ iliyil malinelli vārik koṇṭu varuvārkaḷ. ati uralil koṭṭi makaḷir iruvar ulakkiyāl māṟi māṟik kuṟṟuvar. kuṟṟum uḻippi maṟappataṟkāka iruvarum iṉimiyākap pāṭuvar. appāṭal kēṭpataṟku iṉimiyāṉatu. atu vaḷḷippāṭṭu eṉappaṭum. ulaka kippāṭṭu, akaviṉam eṉṟum atu kūṟappaṭum. appāṭṭik kēḷuṅkaḷ :

“akaviṉam pāṭuvām tōḻi ! amarkkaṇ

nakimoḻi nallavar nāṇum nilipōl

takikoṇṭa ēṉaluḷ tāḻkural urīi

mukivaḷar cāntural muttār maruppiṉ

vakicāl ulakki vayiṉvayiṉ occip

pakiyil nōyceytāṉ payamali ētti

akaviṉam pāṭuvām nām.”



ivvāṟu kuṟṟiya ariciyi uliyiṭṭuc camittu viruntōṭu uṇpar.

mā, palā, vāḻi ākiya mukkaṉiyum tamiḻakattu malikaḷil ceḻittu vaḷarum. avvaḷam kalittokiyil mika iṉimiyākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. tiṉikkolliyi iravil kāval puriyum kāṉavaṉ oruvaṉ, yāṉiyiṉ aṭiyōci kēṭṭuk kavaṇkal vīcukiṟāṉ. iravu nēramākiyāl yāṉiyi nōkki eṟiyappaṭṭa kal kuṟitavaṟi vēṟu pakkam celkiṟatu. akkal vēṅki naṟumalaric citaṟi, āciṉippalaviṉ paḻutta kaṉiyip piḷantu, tēṉkūṭṭi uṭittu, māviṉ pū, kāy ākiya kulikaḷic citaṟi, māviṟku arukil vaḷarntirukkum vāḻiyiṉ maṭalik kiḻittu, iṟutiyāka ataṟku arukē irukkum cevvērp palaviṉ tīṅkaṉiyit tuḷittu uṭceṉṟu taṅkukiṟatām. eṉṉē malināṭṭiṉ vaḷam !

“iṭiumiḻpu iraṅkiya iravupeyal nāṭunāḷ

koṭiviṭupu iruḷiya miṉṉuccey viḷakkattup

piṭiyoṭu mēyum ceypuṉ yāṉi

aṭiotuṅku iyakkam kēṭṭa kāṉavaṉ

neṭuvari āciṉip paṇavi ēṟik

kaṭuvicik kavaṇiyil kalki viṭutaliṉ

iṟuvari vēṅkiyiṉ oḷvī citaṟi

āciṉi meṉpaḻam aḷintavi utirāt

tēṉcey iṟāal tuḷipaṭap pōki

naṟuvaṭi māviṉ pintuṇar uḻakkik

kuliyuṭi vāḻik koḻumaṭal kiḻiyāp

palaviṉ paḻattuḷ taṅkum mali”



ivvāṟu iyaṟki vaḷam ceḻittuk kuluṅkum malināṭṭil vāḻum makkaḷukkuk kavali ētu? iṉṉici pāṭi vāṉil tiriyum vāṉampāṭipōl iṉpa vāḻvu vāḻntaṉar malivāḻ makkaḷ. cuṉi nīril paṭintu viḷiyāṭi, cōliyil āṭippāṭip poḻutik kaḻittaṉar. kuṟiñcit tiṇikkuriya oḻukkam puṇarcci. annila makkaḷiṉ kātal vāḻvu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. puṉal viḷiyāṭṭil iṉpaṅ kaṇṭa oru malināṭṭut taliviyiṉ kātal aṉupavatti avaḷ tōḻi kūṟukiṟāḷ. ﻿

kāṭṭāṟṟil putuveḷḷam varukiṟatu. atil āṭi makiḻat tōḻiyarōṭu cellukiṟāḷ talivi. putuppuṉalil pāyntu nīnti viḷiyāṭukiṟāḷ. neṭunēram puṉalil viḷiyāṭiyatāl avaḷ kālkaḷ cōrntuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. tāmari malarpōlum oḷi vīcum avaḷuṭiya kaṇkaḷum cōrntuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṟṟu veḷḷam avaḷit taṉṉōṭu aṭittuk koṇṭu cellukiṟatu. appōtu avvaḻiyē vanta kaṭṭiḷaṅ kāḷiyoruvaṉ, taliviyiṉ tuṉpa niliyik kaṇṭu āṟṟil pāykiṟāṉ. taṉ mārpil aṇintuḷḷa puṉṉimalarmāli nīril aliyumāṟu nīntic ceṉṟu avaḷippaṟṟit taṉ mārpu avaḷuṭiya mārpōṭu poruntumāṟu aṇittuk koṇṭu vantu karicērttuk kāppāṟṟukiṟāṉ. appāṭal piṉvarumāṟu:

“kāmar kaṭumpuṉal kalantu emmōṭu āṭuvāḷ

tāmarik kaṇputittu añcittaḷarntu ataṉōṭu oḻukalāl

nīḷnāka naṟum taṇtār tayaṅkappāyntu aruḷiṉāl

pūṇ ākam uṟattaḻīip pōtantāṉ akaṉakalam

varumuli puṇarntaṉa eṉpataṉāl eṉtōḻi

arumaḻi taralvēṇṭil tarukiṟkum perumiyaḷē!”





iṉpa aṉupavam ! ittakiya aṉupavam, vāykkumāṉāl, nāḷtōṟum peṇkaḷ kāṭṭāṟṟil viḻattayaṅkuvārō! maṟṟāeru kuṟiñci nilattalivi ūcalāṭi makiḻnta ciṟappi avaḷ vāyilākavē kēṭpōm. talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟukiṟāḷ taṉ kātal iṉpatti.

“ tōḻi! nam tiṉikkollikku arukilirukkum ūcalil amarntu orunāḷ āṭikkoṇṭiruntēṉ. appōtu ōrāṇaḻakaṉ avvaḻiyē vantāṉ. avaṉ kaṭṭaḻaku eṉ kaṇkaḷi īrttatu. avaṉ, kāṇāmal ﻿eṉṉik kaṭikkaṇṇāl kaṇṭāṉ. “aṉparē ! ciṟitu nēram inta ūcali āṭṭuvīrō?” eṉṟu aṉpuṭaṉ kēṭṭēṉ. avaṉum ati etir nōkkittāṉ kāttiruntāṉ. “aṉpē ! uṉ viruppam pōl nāṉ ūcali āṭṭukiṟēṉ!” eṉṟu tēṉic collākkit tittikkak kūṟiṉāṉ. taṉ valiya kikaḷāl eṉ ūcalippaṟṟi āṭṭiṉāṉ. avaṉ āṭṭa nāṉ āṭiṉēṉ. iṭiyil kinaḻuvi vīḻpavaḷippōl avaṉ aṉpuk karattil vīḻntēṉ. uṇmiyil kinaḻuvavilli. poyyāka naṭittēṉ. āṉāl akkāḷiyō, uṇmi eṉṟu nampiṉāṉ. eṉṉi virinteṭuttu mārpil aṇittuk koṇṭāṉ. avaṉ akaṉṟa mārpil ciṟitu nēram elliyaṟṟa iṉpaṅ kaṇṭēṉ. āṉāl kaṇ viḻikka virumpavilli. akkāḷiyō nallaṉ. naṟpaṇpukaḷ vāykkappeṟṟavaṉ. kaṇviḻittāl, nāṉ taṉṉuṇarvu peṟṟuviṭṭatāka eṇṇi eṉṉi viṭuttu “illattukku ēku eṉ kaṇṇē!” eṉṟu kaṉi moḻiyāl kūṟiviṭuvāṉ. piṉ avaṉ mārpil tuyilum iṉpam eḷitil kiṭṭumā? eṉavē kaṇ tiṟakkāmal avaṉ kaṭimārpil māliyāyk kiṭantēṉ.” appāṭal varumāṟu:

“.................................... ēṉal

iṉakkiḷi yāmkaṭintu ōmpum puṉattayal

ūcal ūrntāṭa oruñāṉṟu vantāṉi

‘aiya ciṟiteṉṉi ūkki’ eṉakkūṟat

‘tiyāl ! naṉṟu’ eṉṟu avaṉūkkak kinekiḻpu

poyyāka vīḻntēṉ avaṉmārpil : vāyāc cettu

oyyeṉa āṅkē eṭuttaṉaṉ koṇṭāṉmēl :

meyyaṟiyā tēṉpōl kiṭantēṉmaṉ ; āyiṭi

meyyaṟintu ēṟṟeḻuvē ṉāyiṉ oyyeṉa

‘oṇkuḻāy ! celka’ eṉakkūṟi viṭumpaṇpiṉ

aṅkaṇ uṭiyaṉ avaṉ.”





﻿ kuṟiñcinilak kōti kūṟum kātal aṉupavam uḷḷattik koḷḷi iṉpattil cāykkiṟatu. poyyum vaḻuvum, pōṭṭiyum poṟāmiyum malinta innākarikam aḻintu, iyaṟkinalam tikaḻum avveḻil vāḻvu pūkkātā? eṉṟu uḷḷam ēkkam koḷkiṟatu.

mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu, paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ malivaḷatti aṇu aṇuvākac cuvittu iṉpamārntaṉar. ivvuṇmiyi aṟiyāc cilar, veḷḷiyarip pārttuttāṉ, nām malivāḻvik kaṟṟuk koṇṭōm eṉpatu evvaḷavu aṟivīṉam.

iruntālum iṟantālum āyiram poṉ peṟum yāṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattu malikaḷil kūṭṭam kūṭṭamākat tirikiṉṟaṉa. melliyalāriṉ viḻiyaḻaki veṉṟu, puḷḷimāṉ kūṭṭam tuḷḷi viḷiyāṭukiṉṟatu. kaṉṉiyariṉ cāyalik kavarnta vaṇṇamayilkaḷ, tam paṉṉiṟak kalāpatti virittuk kāṇpōriṉ eṇṇattik koḷḷi koḷkiṉṟaṉa. aruntik kaḷitīra aruvinīr! kuṭintu viḷiyāṭak kuḷirnta cuṉinīr! koṉṟi nikar kūntalil cūṭi makiḻak kōṭṭuppū, koṭippū! āṭi makiḻa acintāṭum ūcal! pāṭikkaḷi koḷḷap pāṭṭaminta kuṟiñcippaṇ! uṇṭu makiḻa uyarnta kaṉi vakikaḷ! nilattil kiḻaṅku! iṟicci uṇavukku, vaṉattil vilaṅku! kāṭcikku iyaṟki eḻilmikka neṭuvari! kātal māṭcikkuk kaṉimoḻiyār ! kuṭittu makiḻa, maliccāralil vaḻiyum komput tēṉ! cuvittu makiḻap pāviyariṉ palāccuḷi utaṭukaḷil vaḻiyum muttattēṉ! iṉpamāka vāḻa maṉitaṉukku iṉṉum eṉṉa vēṇṭum? eṉavētāṉ paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ malivāḻkkiyi virumpi ēṟṟaṉar. ﻿

makkaḷ vāḻkkiyē maliyiltāṉ tōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu cila aṟiñarkaḷiṉ koḷki. makkaḷ nākarikam maliyiltāṉ tōṉṟiyateṉṟum, aḷavik karuvikaḷum, icikkaruvikaḷum, vil mutaliya pōrk karuvikaḷum, maruttuvam, kaṭavuḷuṇmi, ici, aracu mutaliya kalikaḷum maliyilēyē mutaṉmutalil kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum tamiḻt teṉṟal tiru. vi. ka. kūṟukiṟār. malivāḻvu, makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa kāraṇattiṉālēyē tamiḻ makkaḷ kōvilkaḷi malikaḷiṉ mītu eḻuppiṉar eṉṟum kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

ittakiya peruñ ciṟappukkaḷ vāynta malikaḷ parantum, uyarntum, nīṇṭum tamiḻakameṅkaṇum niṟintuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vaḷam, avaṟṟil iḻitarum āṟukaḷ, avaṟṟil putintu kiṭakkum kaṉipporuḷkaḷ, vāḻum makkaḷ, avarkaḷ vāḻkki, toḻil vaḷam, aḻaku, maraṅkaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ ākiya ellāc ceytikaḷiyum innūl ārāyntu kūṟukiṟatu. ﻿2. cērvarāyaṉ mali

peyarkkāraṇam

‘cilam’ eṉṟa vaṭa collukku mali eṉpatu poruḷ. viṭṭuviṭṭuc citaṟik kiṭakkum malit toṭariṉ pakutikaḷ cēlam māvaṭṭattil niṟintirukkiṉṟaṉa. kolli malikaḷ, kalrāyaṉ malikaḷ, cērvarāyaṉ malikaḷ, pacci malikaḷ, kañcamali, pōtamali eṉpaṉa avikaḷil kuṟippiṭat takkavi. ivvāṟu malināṭāka viḷaṅkuvatālēyē, cilam eṉṟa col maruvi cēlam eṉṟāyiṟṟu eṉpar cilar. iccēlam māvaṭṭam koṅkunāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyākum. koṅku nāṭu cēranāṭṭiṟkum, cōḻanāṭṭiṟkum naṭu elli. cēra cōḻa maṉṉarkaḷil yār valimi ōṅkukiṟatō, avar kikku māṟi māṟi ūcalāṭikkoṇṭiruntatu ikkoṅkunāṭu. iruntālum cōḻariviṭac cēra maṉṉarkaḷum, avar vaḻi vanta atiyar kuṭiyiṉarumē neṭuṅkālam iṅku aracōcci vantaṉar. ‘perumali viṭarakat tarumicik koṇṭa, ciṟiyili nellit tīṅkaṉi kuṟiyātu, ātal taṉṉakattaṭakkic cātal nīṅka’ auvik kaḷittup perumpukaḻ koṇṭavaṉum, kaṭiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷil oruvaṉumākiya atikamāṉ vāḻnta takaṭūr nāṭu, cēlam māvaṭṭattut tarumapurik kōṭṭamākum. aṅku iṉṟuḷḷa ‘atamaṅ kōṭṭi’yē paṇṭiya kaṭiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷil oruvaṉāṉa atikamāṉ vāḻnta ‘atikaṉ kōṭṭi’ yākum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻakattil vāḻnta peṇpāṟ pulavarkaḷil tali ciṟantavarum, atikamāṉāl peritum pārāṭṭip pōṟṟappaṭṭavaru﻿māṉa avviyiṉ peyarit tāṅkikkoṇṭu, kalrāyaṉ malittoṭaril ‘avvi mali’ eṉṟa oṉṟu iṉṟumuḷatu. ivvāṟu cērar kuṭiyiṉarkku urimi peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya immāvaṭṭam paṇṭi nāḷil ‘cēralam’ eṉṟu vaḻaṅki, iṉṟu cēlamāka māṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟum, cērarācaṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭirunta mali ‘cērarācaṉ mali’ eṉṟa peyarkoṇṭu viḷaṅki, iṉṟu cērvarāyaṉ maliyākiviṭṭatu eṉṟum koḷvatu ēṟpuṭittām. cēlam māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa malikaḷil uyarvilum, vaḷattilum ciṟantavi cērvarāyaṉ malikaḷ.

amippu

mallāpuram malittoṭarukkum mañcavāṭik kaṇavāykkum iṭiyil immalikaḷ parantu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. immalit toṭar patiṉēḻu kal nīḷamum paṉṉiraṇṭu kal akalamum uṭiyatu. itaṉ parappu 100 catura mil ākum. ittoṭariṉ teṉ carivu ceṅkuttāṉatu. kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 4000 aṭiyiliruntu 4800 aṭivari uyaramuṭiyatu. iccarivil kuṇṭūr, tappakkāṭu eṉṟa ciṟṟūrkaḷ irukkum iṭamum, paḻamali uyarntu cellum iṭamumē camaveḷikaḷām. āṉāl vaṭacarivu ceṅkuttāṉatalla. atu parantu ciṟitu ciṟitākat tāḻntu cellukiṟatu.

immalit toṭarāṉatu naṭuvil ōṭum vāṇiyāṟṟiṉ paḷḷattākkāl iraṇṭu pirivākap pirikkappaṭukiṟatu. ivviraṇṭu pirivukaḷum amippil vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kiḻakkuppakuti maḻi nīrāl arikkappaṭṭ āṟṟuppaṭukikaḷik koṇṭatu. mēṟkuppakuti paruttu uyarnta malikaḷik koṇṭatu. kiḻakkup pakutiyil taliccōli, māṟamaṅkalam eṉṟa ﻿iraṇṭu pīṭapūmikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. appīṭapūmikaḷi iṇikkum iṭinilattil kōṭṭaṉcētu eṉṟa ciṟṟūr amintuḷḷatu. mēṟkup pakutiyi mēlum iru pakutikaḷākap pirikkalām. oṉṟu vāṇiyāṟṟiṟkum kāṭiyāmpaṭṭi āṟṟiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa pakuti. maṟṟāeru pakuti oḻuṅkaṟṟa pīṭapūmi. ippakutiyiltāṉ ērkkāṭu amintuḷḷatu. ippīṭapūmi vaṭakkiluḷḷa canniyācimali eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum ṭaḵp mali (Duffs Hill-5231') yōṭu muṭivuṟukiṉṟatu. itaṟku mēṟkup pakkattil tāḻvāṉa maṟṟāeru mali tali nīṭṭikkoṇṭirukkiṟatu. ammaliyiṉ mēluḷḷa pīṭapūmi, kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 2800-liruntu 2900 aṭi uyaramuṭiyatu. ataṉmītu tāṉ maliyāḷikaḷ vāḻum koṇṭiyaṉūr, cōṉappāṭi eṉṟa ūrkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

maṟṟāeru pakuti pala maliccikaraṅkaḷi iṇikkum toṭar oṉṟik koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. attoṭariṉ mēṟkil nākalūr pīṭapūmiyum, kiḻakkil pacci malip (Green Hill) pīṭapūmiyum uḷḷaṉa. it toṭarukku arukil kuṟippiṭattakka vēṟupala cikaraṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avi cērvarāyaṉ cikaram (5,342') piḷēṉ ḵpil cikaram (5,410'), pālamaṭi cikaram (5,370') kāvēri cikaram (5,086') eṉpaṉa. pulivāri cikaratti (4,576') nōkki iṭatupuṟam cellum toṭarum, vāṇiyāṟṟiṉ paḷḷattākki nōkki valatupuṟam cellum toṭarum kāvēri cikarattil iraṇṭākap pirikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

nākalūr pīṭapūmiyiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷum anēkamākak kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 4000 aṭikkuk kuṟintē irukiṉṟaṉa. ippīṭapūmiyiliruntu nōkkiṉāl vēppāṭip paḷḷattākku naṉṟākat teṉpaṭum. vēppāṭip paḷḷattākkiṟku mēṟkil ﻿ērimalit toṭar uḷḷatu. paccimalip pīṭapūmiyiliruntu pārppōrukku vāṇiyāṟṟiṉ paḷḷattākkum, hātārṉ cikaram (Hawthorne cliff—4,899') tēṉ cikaram (Honey Rocks 4,533) mutaliya aḻakiya uccikaḷum teṉpaṭum. tēṉ cikarattiṟku etiril orukal tolivil, kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 2,490' uyarattil vāṇiyāṟu iḻintu celkiṟatu.

āṟukaḷ

cērvarāyaṉ malikaḷiṉ teṉ carivu mikavum ceṅkuttāka uyarntiruppatāl iṅku āṟukaḷ ēṟpaṭuvaṟku vāyppilli. ākiyāl vaṭacarivilēyē pala ciṟṟāṟukaḷ tōṉṟi ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. ic ciṟṟāṟukaḷil eppoḻutum nīr iruppatilli. maḻikkālaṅkaḷiltāṉ nīrp perukkik kāṇa muṭiyum (1) toppūr āṟu (2) carapaṅka nati (3) vāṇiyāṟu eṉpaviyē immalikaḷil tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.

toppūr āṟu : toppūr āṟṟiṟku vēppāṭi āṟu eṉṟa vēṟu peyarum uṇṭu. itu cērvarāyaṉ malikaḷil muluvi eṉṟa iṭattil tōṉṟi vaṭakiḻakku nōkki mallāpuram malippāti cellum vaḻiyāka ōṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉ paḷḷattākkil vēppāṭi eṉṟa ciṟṟūr uḷḷatu. avvūriṉ peyari ivvāṟu ēṟṟuk koṇṭatu. mallāpurattiṟku arukil ivvāṟu mēṟku nōkkit tirumpi ōṭi, cōḻappāṭi eṉṟa iṭattil kāviriyōṭu kalakkiṟatu.
