maliyāḷikaḷ im mēṉarīka muṟiyi mikavum tīviramākak kaṭip piṭikkiṉṟaṉar. cila camayaṅkaḷil im muṟi viparītamākakkūṭat tōṉṟum. urimip peṇṇāṉavaḷ kula vaḻakkappaṭi avaḷukkeṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa maṇamakaṉiviṭa mikavum vayatil mutirntavaḷāka iruppatum uṇṭu. nāṉkintu vayatuṭiya iḷiñarkaḷukkup paruva meytiya maṅkiyarit tirumaṇam ceytu vippārkaḷ. appōtu maṇamakaṉuṭiya tanti, tāṉē poṟuppēṟṟuk koṇṭu vamcavirutti ceyvatuṇṭu. taṉ makaṉ vayatukku vantatum ap poṟuppi avaṉiṭam viṭṭu viṭuvatu tantiyiṉ kaṭami. poṟuppēṟṟuk koṇṭa makaṉ, taṉ tantiyiṉ vaḻiyit tāṉum kaṭip piṭippāṉ. ōr iḷam maṇamakaṉukkut tanti illi eṉṟālō, allatu tanti iruntum avaṉ marumakaḷiṉpāl akkaṟi koḷḷātavaṉāka iruntālō, tantiyiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantavaṉō allatu neruṅkiya uṟaviṉaṉ oruvaṉō ip poṟuppi ēṟṟuk koḷḷa aḻikkappaṭuvatu vaḻakkam. iv vaḻakkattippaṟṟi maliyāḷikaḷ ippoḻutu kuṟivu paṭa eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. iti ōraḷavu etirttum varukiṉṟaṉar. muṉ nāṭkaḷil itu peru vaḻakkāka ivarkaḷiṭam paraviyiruntatu.

tirumaṇam :

maliyāḷikaḷiṉ tirumaṇam camaveḷiyiluḷḷōriṉ tirumaṇattiṉiṉṟum ciṟitē māṟupaṭukiṟatu. tirumaṇattiṟkumuṉ 'veṟṟili pākkup piṭittal' eṉṟa oru paḻakkamuṇṭu. iti vaṭamoḻiyil 'niccayatārttam' eṉpar. tirumaṇa oppantamē ic caṭaṅku. ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ muṉṉiliyil peṇ vīṭṭārum, māppiḷḷi vīṭṭārumkūṭi iv voppantatti muṭippar. iru vīṭṭārum vevvēṟu ūrkaḷic cārntavarāka iruppiṉ avviraṇṭu ūrkaḷiṉ ūrk kavuṇṭarkaḷum avviṭattilkūṭi iti naṭatti vippar. paṭṭakkāraṉiṉ aṉumati peṟṟē itic ceyya vēṇṭum.

maṇap peṇṇukku viliyākap paricam vāṅkum vaḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu. atu avaravarkaḷiṉ takutikkēṟpak kuṟittō kūṭṭiyō peṟappaṭum, piḷḷi vīṭṭār paṇamākavum, poruḷkaḷākavum iti aḷippar. ūttaṅkari vaṭṭattiluḷḷa pacci maliyāḷikaḷiṉ peṇṇukkup paricamāka nāṉku kaṇṭakam tāṉiyamum, nāṉku pakōṭā ( rū. 14)kkaḷum, kaṉṟōṭu oru pacuvum koṭukka vēṇṭum. maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa maliyāḷikaḷ rū. 10-liruntu rū. 50 vari paricam koṭukkiṉṟaṉar. maṇac caṭaṅkukaḷi naṭatti vikkum ūrkkavuṇṭar mutaliyavarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭaṇamāka rū. 10-8-0 aḷikka vēṇṭum. āṉāl cila ēḻikaḷāl ip pūrāt tokiyiyum koṭukka muṭiyātu. appōtu it tokiyiṉ oru pakutiyi maṭṭum koṭuppar. mītiyip paṭṭakkāraṉ koṭuttu viṭuvāṉ. āṉāl pala tavaṇikaḷil paṭṭakkāraṉukku ap paṇattik koṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭum. ikkaṭaṉ āṇṭuk kaṇakkāka nīṇṭu celvatumuṇṭu. peṟṟōr tam tirumaṇattiṉpōtu paṭṭa ik kaṭaṉi, makkaḷ tōṉṟip paṭṭakkāraṉukkut tīrppatuṇṭām. oru kiḻavaṉ kumariyit tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷa nēriṭṭāl, niṟiyap paricam koṭukka vēṇṭum.

pacci maliyil vāḻvōr maṇamakkaḷukku ñāyiṟṟukkiḻami yaṉṟu naluṅku vippar. putaṉ kiḻamiyaṉṟu iru vīṭṭiṉ muṉṉum pantalkaḷ pōṭappaṭum. viyāḻakkiḻami peṇ vīṭṭil tiru maṇam naṭipeṟum. potuvāka maliyāḷikaḷiṉ tirumaṇam māppiḷḷi vīṭṭilēyē naṭipeṟum. peṇ vīṭṭilum, māppiḷḷi vīṭṭilum mūṉṟu putup pāttiraṅkaḷi oṉṟiṉmēl oṉṟu aṭukkik kalacampōl vittu, putaṉkiḻami iravu vināyakar kōyilukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvar. appōtu tāmpūlam ellārukkum vaḻaṅkappaṭa vēṇṭum. im mariyāti mikavum kaṇṭippōṭu etirpārkkappaṭukiṟatu. it tāmpūlattil periya turi aintu paṅkum, maṟṟa turikaḷ ovvoruvarum nāṉku paṅkum, mantirikaḷ ovvoruvarum mūṉṟu paṅkum, kuṭṭa kavuṇṭaṉ iraṇṭu paṅkum, mūppaṉ oru paṅkum peṟuvatu vaḻakkam. ic caṭaṅku muṭintatum maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷukkuk kūṟi paricaḷippatu vaḻakkam. itu karuppuk kariyiṭṭa veḷḷic cēliyākavō allatu civappuc cēliyākavō irukkum. itaṉuṭiya nīḷam 12 muḻattiliruntu 17 muḻam uṭiyatākavum, akalam iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu muḻam uṭiyatākavum irukkum, āṉāl periya maliyāḷikaḷ maṭṭum 3 allatu 4 muḻamuḷḷa kūṟi aḷippatu vaḻakkam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kolli maliyil maṇap peṇ viṭiyaṟ kālattil ēḻu maṇikku muṉpāka maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭiṟku aḻittuc cellappaṭukiṟāḷ. tirumaṇac caṭaṅkukaḷ aṅku naṭipeṟum.

maṇamakaṉ tāliyi maṇamakaḷiṉ kaḻuttil vittatum, piṉṉāl niṉṟu koṇṭirukkum ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ atik kaṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. piṟaku maṇamakaṉiṉ kiyip peṇṇiṉ kiyil vittu, ūrk kavuṇṭaṉ tāri vārttuk koṭuppāṉ. āṉāl periya maliyāḷikaḷiṉ vaḻakkam itaṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭatu. 'kaṇiyāṉ' eṉpavaṉ maṇamakaḷiṉ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvāṉ. maṇamakaḷiṉ kalavikkuk kaṇiyāṉ mutal urimi peṟṟavaṉākak karutappaṭukiṟāṉ. (kēraḷattil taṭakkum tāli kaṭṭuk kaliyāṇaṅkaḷil 'maṇavāḷaṉ' eṉpavaṉ 'kaṇiyāṉ' ceyyum vēlikaḷic ceykiṟāṉ). ip paḻakkam maṟṟa maliyāḷikaḷāl muṟi keṭṭatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.



maṇavilakkum maṟumaṇamum :

periya maliyāḷikaḷ maṇavilakku {divorce) muṟiyi aṉumatippatilli. āṉāl kolli maliyāḷikaḷiṭam maṇavilakkuc ceytu. koḷḷum paḻakkam uṇṭu. āṉāl maṇavilakkiṉ pōtu ellāk kuḻantikaḷiyum (vēṟu kaṇavaṉukkup piṟantavikaḷāy iruntālum cari) kaṇavaṉiṭam oppaṭittu viṭavēṇṭum.

pacci maliyāḷikaḷ rū 25 oṟupput toki (Fine) yākap peṟṟuk koṇṭu maṇavilakku aḷippar. itu camaveḷiyiliruntu kaṟṟuk koṇṭa paḻakkam. oru pacci maliyāḷi maṇavilakkup peṟavirumpiṉāl atik kuruviṉ muṉṉiliyil kūṟa vēṇṭum. maṇa vilakkiṟku aṟikuṟiyāka oru vikkōl tuṇṭiyō allatu marattuṇṭiyō, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyiṭam koṭuppāṉ. maṇavilakkup peṟṟa peṇ muṉṉāḷ kaṇavaṉ iṟanta piṟakē maṟumaṇam ceytukoḷḷa aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟāḷ. kimpeṇ maṇam {Widow remarriage) ellā maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyilēyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. kolli maliyāḷikaḷ, kaṇavaṉiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāṉi maṇakka entak kimpeṇṇiyum aṉumatippatilli. pacci maliyāḷikaḷiṭam ik kaṭṭuppāṭu kiṭiyātu.

kolli maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē kimpeṇ maṇam naṭantāl maṇap peṇṇum maṇamakaṉum etiretiril muḻantāḷiṭṭu uṭkārntu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. naṭuvil oru tiriyiṭappaṭum. maṇamakaṉ tāliyit tiriyiṉaṭiyil koṇṭuvantu peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil vippāṉ. ūrkkavuṇṭaṉ tāliyik kaṭṭiya piṟakutāṉ maṇamakaṉ peṇṇiṉ mukattip pārkka muṭiyum. maṟumaṇattiṉ pōtu, kim peṇṇāṉavaḷ taṉ mutal kaṇavaṉukkup piṟanta kuḻantikaḷi, avaṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāriṭamō allatu neruṅkiya uṟaviṉariṭamō oppaṭittuviṭa vēṇṭum. tantiyiṉ cottukkaḷellām ak kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭṭā ceyyappaṭum. allatu muṉ kūṭṭiyē paṭṭā ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. ac cottukkaḷi iraṇṭām kaṇavaṉ aṉupavippatit taṭukkavē im muṟiyik kiyāḷukiṉṟaṉar.

pūppeytal :

oru peṇ pūppeytiyatum, avaḷi vīṭṭiliruntu vilakkit taṉiyiṭattil vippatu maliyāḷikaḷiṉ vaḻakkam. 30 nāṭkaḷ vilakku nāṭkaḷākak (Pollutionperiod) karutappaṭum. āṉāl camaveḷiyiluḷḷa makkaḷip pārttu ivarkaḷum ippōtu annāṭkaḷik kuṟittuk koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. pacci maliyāḷikaḷ oru peṇ pūppeytiyati aṟintatum ūrukku veḷiyiluḷḷa oru kuṭiciyil aintu nāṭkaḷ vittiruppar. āṟām nāḷ ap peṇṇi naṉṉīrāṭṭi vīṭṭiṟku aḻittu varuvar. aṭutta muppatu nāṭkaḷ vilakku nāṭkaḷākak karutappaṭum. annāṭkaḷ kaḻiyum variyilum ūr makkaḷ yārum av vīṭṭiṟkuḷ cella māṭṭārkaḷ. anta muppatu nāṭkaḷil peṇṇāṉavaḷ nāḷ tōṟum taliyil nīrpeytu kuḷikka vēṇṭum. vāram oru muṟi vīṭṭit tūymippaṭutta vēṇṭum. ūttaṅkariyiluḷḷa pacci maliyāḷikaḷ 12 nāḷ vilakkivikkiṉṟaṉar. kolli maliyāḷikaḷil cilar 30 nāṭkaḷum, vēṟu cilar 15 nāṭkaḷum vilakku nāṭkaḷākak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. periya maliyāḷikaḷ iṭattiṟkut takuntāṟpōl 7 nāṭkaḷiliruntu 11 nāṭkaḷ vari vilakku nāṭkaḷākak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. vilakku nāṭkaḷ muṭintatum, oru pārppaṉap purōkitari aḻittu vantu vīṭṭit tūymippaṭuttuvar.

makap pēṟu :

kuḻanti piṟanta paṉṉiraṇṭām nāḷilō allatu patiṉintām nāḷilō allatu patiṉāṟām nāḷilō vīṭṭit tūymip paṭuttit tāyi vīṭṭuk kaḻippar. āṉāl pacci maliyāḷikaḷ muppatām nāḷ variyil vilakku nāṭkaḷākak karutuvar. kuḻantikkup peyar cūṭṭuvataṟkeṉṟu, kuṟippiṭṭa entac caṭaṅkum kiṭiyātu. eppoḻutu peyar cūṭṭuvatu eṉṟa variyaṟiyum illi. kolli maliyāḷikaḷ kuḻanti piṟanta pattām nāḷilō allatu mūṉṟām tiṅkaḷilō peyariṭuvar. pacci maliyiluḷḷavarkaḷ mutal āṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum, cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷavarkaḷ mūṉṟām nāḷilum peyariṭuvar. peyariṭuvataṟku muṉpāka, entap peyarvippatu yeṉpatu paṟṟi avvūriluḷḷa pūcāriyik kalantu kēṭpar. cila caṭaṅkukaṭkup piṟaku pūcāri maruḷ koṇṭu āṭi, akkuḻantikku iṭavēṇṭiya peyarik kūṟuvāṉ. eppoḻutum kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyē kuḻantikaṭkup perumpālum iṭuvar. āṇāka iruntāl koṅkaṉ, vaṭamaṉ, cīraṅkaṉ, piṭavaṉ, kāḷi, aṟappaḷi eṉṟum, peṇṇāka iruntāl koṅkāyi, vaṭami, cīraṅki, piṭāri eṉṟum peyariṭuvar. kariyaṉ, veḷḷiyaṉ, kuṭṭiyaṉ, caṭiyaṉ, periya piyaṉ, ciṉṉap piyaṉ eṉak kāraṇap peyarkaḷum iṭuvatuṇṭu.

āṭi aṇikaḷ :

maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ iravikki aṇivatilli. vīṭṭilirukkumpōtum, vayalkaḷil vēli ceyyum pōtum uṭaliṉ mēṟpakuti tiṟantē irukkum. vēṟṟār muṉṉiliyil varumpōtum, cantikkuc cellumpōtum āṭiyiṉ muṉ tāṉiyit taḷartti mārpip pōrttu, iṭatu tōḷiṉ mēl koṇarntu mutukuppuṟam toṅka viṭuvārkaḷ. pacci maliyāḷikaḷ eppoḻutum vaṇṇa āṭikaḷiyē virumpuvar. pacci maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ tirumaṇattiṉpōtu maṭṭum veḷḷi niṟamāṉa kūṟi (puttāṭi) aṇivar. maṟṟa nēraṅkaḷil cikappu allatu karuppu āṭikaḷiyē aṇivar. āṉāl paḷic ceṉṟirukkum mañcaḷ, ārañcu, pacci niṟap puṭavikaḷiṉmītu avarkaṭku viruppamatikam. āṇkaḷ aṇiyum āṭi kōvaṇam oṉṟutāṉ. āṉāl atil kūṭa vaṇṇatti virumpuvar. kolli maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ iṭatu pakkamum. pacci maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ valatu pakkamum mūkkuk kuttik koḷvar. periya maliyāḷip peṇkaḷ mūkkē kuttik koḷvatilli. pacci niṟamāṉatum, irattac civappu niṟamāṉatumāṉa kaṇṇāṭikaḷ patitta kātu vaḷiyaṅkaḷi aṇivar.

iṟutic caṭaṅku :

maliyāḷikaḷ potuvāka, piṇaṅkaḷip putikkum paḻakka muṭiyavarkaḷ. āṉāl kālarā, toḻunōy mutaliyavaṟṟāl pīṭikkappaṭṭu iṟantavarkaḷuṭiya piṇaṅkaḷukkup 'pāl teḷikkum caṭaṅku' kiṭiyātu. maṟṟac caṭaṅkukaḷellām camaveḷiyiluḷḷavaṟṟip pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. piṇattit tūkkic cellum pāṭiyi avarkaḷum 'tēr' eṉṟē kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tīṭṭu nāṭkaḷ iṭattiṟku iṭam māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pacci maliyil ittīṭṭu nāṭkaḷ oru tiṅkaḷ vari nīṭikkum. ūttaṅkari vaṭṭattiluḷḷa pacci maliyāḷikaḷ 10 nāṭkaḷum, periya maliyāḷikaḷ 12 allatu 15 nāṭkaḷum, kolli maliyāḷikaḷ 3 nāṭkaḷum tīṭṭākak karutuvar. iṟantavarkaḷuṭiya āvi cila nāṭkaḷ vīṭṭic cuṟṟi aliyum eṉṟu karutukiṟārkaḷ. atit tiruptip paṭuttuvataṟkākap pūcāri kūṟum muṟippaṭi kōḻi, āṭu, paṉṟi mutaliyavaṟṟip paliyiṭuvar. ōr iruppāṇiyiyō allatu muḷiyiyō putikuḻiyil piṇattiṉ talikku nērāka aṭittu, avvāviyi vīṭṭiṉ pakkam varāmal taṭuppar. iṟantupōṉa muṉṉōri vaḻipaṭuvataṟkāka avarkaḷ 'cirārttam' ceyvatilli. āṉāl tirumaṇam, makappēṟu, pūppeytal mutaliya nikaḻccikaḷ naṭipeṟum pōtum, viḻāk kālaṅkaḷilum, veḷḷik kiḻamikaḷilum iṟantōrukku vaḻipāṭu ceyyum vaḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu. ivvāṟu vaṇaṅkum muṉṉōrkaḷi 'vīṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷ' eṉṟum, 'paṭṭavaṉ' eṉṟum ivarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. maliyāḷikaḷ taṅkaḷ illaṅkaḷi mikavum puṉitamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷākak karutukiṉṟaṉar. pārppaṉap purōkitarikkūṭa, ceruppuk kālōṭu ivarkaḷ vīṭṭiṟkuḷ aṉumatippatilli. camaya vāḻvu : maliyāḷikaḷ civaperumāṉiyum tirumāliyum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. nāḷtōṟum tirunīṟu aṇiyum vaḻakkattik kaṭip piṭikkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl cila kuṟippiṭṭa camaya vaḻipāṭṭiṉ pōtu maṭṭum tirumaṇ (nāmam) cārttik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. taṅkaḷuṭiya kōyilkaḷil taṅkaḷ iṉattāriyē arccakarkaḷāka amarttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. maliyāḷikaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṟku urimi peṟṟa perun teyvam tirumāliṉ avatāramāṉa karirāmaṉ. avaṉ kōyilkoṇṭu eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum iṭaṅkaḷil talimiyāṉatu periya kalrāyaṉ maliyilirukkum 'mēl nāṭṭi'c cārnta 'kōyil putūr' ākum. tammampaṭṭiyilum karirāmaṉukkuk kōyil uṇṭu. cērvarāyaṉ malimītuḷḷa karaṭiyūrilum ikkaṭavuḷukkuk kōyil eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu uruvattil kōyil putūrilirukkum kōyilip pōṉṟē uḷḷatu cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷōr aṅku eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum kaṭavuḷik 'kariya perumāḷ' eṉṟē aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. cēlattiṟku aṇmiyil oru kuṉṟiṉmēl kariyaperumāḷ kōyil uḷḷatu, akkuṉṟu 'kariyaperumāḷ mali' eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

kōyil putūrilirukkum karirāmaṉ kōyilil paramacivaṉ, pārvati, tirumāl, ilakkumi, vināyakaṉ ākiyōriṉ paṭimaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa, avaṟṟōṭu paṉṉiraṇṭu naṭukaṟkaḷum veṇ puḷḷi cārttappaṭṭu, iraṇṭu varicikaḷāka niṟkiṉṟaṉa. kōyiliṉ vāyilic caṅku cakkaraṅkaḷum, nāmamum, tirumāl, irāmaṉ uruvaṅkaḷum, nāṉku karuṭaṉum, nāṉku nantiyum aḻaku ceykiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru caṉikkiḻamiyum, ak kōyilil vaḻipāṭu nikaḻkiṉṟatu. āṇṭu tōṟum māci mātattil oru tērt tiruviḻāvum aṅku naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. ak kōyiṟ pūcāri pulāl uṇṇak kūṭātu. uyirp pali naṭipeṟum nikaḻccikaḷil paṅku koḷḷak kūṭātu. pulāl uṇpōriṉ uṭaṉamarntu uṇṇak kūṭātu. kari rāmaṉ kōyilil uyirkaḷip paliyiṭum vaḻakkam eppoḻutum kiṭiyātu. attakiya nikaḻccikaḷil paṅku koṇṭavarkaḷ, mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷ kaṭanta piṟakutāṉ kōyilukkuḷ nuḻiya vēṇṭum. maliyāḷi āṇkaḷum, peṇkaḷum ak kōyilukkuc ceṉṟu muṭi eṭukkum vaḻakkatti mēṟkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.

kolli malic carivil vēlappūr nāṭṭil oru civaṉ kōyil uḷḷatu. aṅku eḻuntaruḷiyiruk kum iṟivaṉ 'aṟappaḷīcuvaraṉ' eṉṟu aḻikkap paṭukiṟāṉ. maṟṟa maliyāḷikaḷ kōyilip pōlallāmal, iṅkup pārppaṉak kurukkaḷ arccakarāka amarttappaṭṭirukkiṟār. iv viṟivaṉmītu ampala vāṇak kavirāyar eṉpavar oru catakam pāṭiyuḷḷār, (catam eṉṟāl nūṟu. catakam eṉṟāl nūṟu pāṭalkaḷik koṇṭa nūl eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum). aṟappaḷīcuvara catakattiluḷḷa nūṟu pāṭalkaḷum "aṉutiṉamum maṉatil niṉi tarucaturakirivaḷar aṟappaḷīsvara tēvaṉē“ eṉṟa aṭikoṇṭu muṭikiṉṟamiyāl kolli malikkuc 'catura kiri' eṉṟa peyarum vaḻaṅkiyatu pōlum. 'teṇṇīrp poṉṉi tirik kiyāl aṭivaruṭap paḷḷi koḷḷum tiruvaraṅkap perumāṉi'yum ivarkaḷ paktiyōṭu vaṇaṅku kiṉṟaṉar.

maliyāḷikaḷ vāḻum ciṟṟūrkaḷil uḷḷa perumāḷ kōyilkaḷil tirumāl vaḻipāṭu pala māṟupāṭukaḷōṭu citintu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ak kōyilkaḷil naṭipeṟuvatu uṇmiyil tirumāl vaḻipāṭutāṉā eṉa aiyuṟa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. tirumāl vaḻipāṭṭiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta palipīṭam (shrine) cila kōyilkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvatilli, potuvāka ik kōyilkaḷil uyirp pali naṭipeṟuvatilli. iṅkup paṇipuriyum pūcāri (maliyāḷi)yum pulāl uṇṇātavaṉākavē irukkiṟāṉ.

nāmakkal kolli maliyiluḷḷa kuki nāṭṭil ittakiya kōyil oṉṟu uḷḷatu. ak kōyilil vīṟṟirukkum iṟivaṉip 'pēyp perumāḷ' eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. iṅku caṉikkiḻamikaḷilēyē pūci naṭipeṟukiṟatu.

kolli maliyāḷikaḷ 'araṅkaṭṭappaṉ' eṉṟa oru kaṭavuḷiyum vaḻipaṭukiṟārkaḷ. 'araṅka civaṉ' eṉṟa vēṟu peyarum ak kaṭavuḷukku uṇṭu. maliyāḷikaḷiṉ kula mutalvarāṉa mūṉṟu cakōtararkaḷum vaṇaṅkiya kula teyvamē araṅkaṭṭappaṉ eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum karirāmaṉiṉ vēṟṟava tāramākavum ik kaṭavuḷik karutukiṉṟaṉar. kuṇṭūr nāṭṭilum araṅkaṭṭappaṉ kōyil uḷḷatu. ikkōyilkaḷil pārppaṉak kurukkaḷē paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar. iṅku naṭipeṟum vaḻipāṭu maṟṟa civaṉ kōyil vaḻipāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ciṟitu māṟik kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mutalil taṇṇīrilum piṟaku pālilum, mūṉṟāvatāka nalleṇṇeyilum iṟivaṉukku apiṭēkam naṭipeṟukiṟatu. piṟaku puttāṭi aṇivittu, iṟivaṉiṉ tirumēṉiyil cantaṉattiyum, kuṅkumattiyum pūcukiṉṟaṉar. piṟaku tūpam kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. tīpaṅkaḷiyum koḷutti vikkiṉṟaṉar, oru vāḻiyiliyi iṟivaṉ tirumuṉṉāl virittu, atil poṅkalip paṭikkiṉṟaṉar. vaḻakkamāṉa mantiraṅkaḷik kūṟi, cūṭattik koḷutti vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. uyirp pali iṅkuk kiṭiyātu. poṅkal, pāl, carkkari, paḻam mutaliyaṉavum civa vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya vēṟu poruḷkaḷumē iṅkup paṭikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, araṅka civaṉukku aṭimip paṇipuriyum oru ciṟu teyvamum maliyāḷikaḷāl vaṇaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. akkaṭavuḷukku 'araṅka cēvakaṉ' eṉṟu peyar. araṅka cēvakaṉ kōyilil paṇipuriyum pūcāri pārppaṉaralla; maliyāḷiyē. araṅka cēvakaṉukku āṭu, kōḻikaḷ mutalil paliyiṭappaṭṭaṉavāka aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ippoḻutu ap paḻakkam illi.

vināyakar vaḻipāṭu camaveḷiyil naṭappatip pōṉṟē maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyilum naṭipeṟukiṟatu. ceṅkuttāṉa oru kalli naṭṭu, mēlē kūriyillāmal ik kōyilkaḷ amikkap peṟum. cila kōyilkaḷ cuṟṟuc cuvar illāmalum amikkap peṟum. ittakiya kōyilkaḷ cērvarāyaṉ maliyiluḷḷa mēlūrilum, kākampāṭiyilum uḷḷaṉa. vināyakar vaḻipāṭṭiṟku ēṟṟa nāḷ tiṅkaṭkiḻamiyē. murukaṉ vaḻipāṭum maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē uṇṭu. piṟakari nāṭṭiluḷḷa kantacāmi kōyil pukaḻ peṟṟatākum. paṅkuṉi uttarattiṉpōtu, ik kaṭavuḷukku viḻā eṭukkiṉṟaṉar. umiyiṉ vaṭivamāṉa kāmāṭciyip pala iṭaṅkaḷil kōyil kaṭṭi vaḻipaṭukiṉṟaṉar. pala iṭaṅkaḷil tarumarācar kōyil iruppatāl pāṇṭavar vaḻipāṭum maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyil iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.

maliyāḷikaḷ vaṇaṅkum ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷ (Demi Gods) kaṇakkilaṭaṅkātavi. caktiyiṉ avatāraṅḷāṉa kāḷi, piṭāri, māri mutaliya teyvaṅkaṭku eṅku pārttālum kōyilkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ayyaṉārukkum kōyil uṇṭu. cittiri allatu vikācit tiṅkaḷil eṭappuli nāṭṭil kāḷi tēvikkut tēr viḻā naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. caṉikkiḻami, kāḷi tēviyiṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṟkukanta nāḷākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. piṭāri eṉṟa teyvam, periya piṭāri, ciṉṉap piṭāri, cokkap piṭāri, putup piṭāri, karum piṭāri, kārak kāṭṭup piṭāri, maluṅkup piṭāri eṉap pala aṭi moḻikaḷōṭu maliyāḷikaḷāl aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu piṭārikkuriya vaḻipāṭṭu nāḷum, āṇṭu viḻāvum nāṭṭukku nāṭu māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷukkāṉa viḻākkaḷil ciṟappāṉatu māriyammaṉ tiruviḻāvākum. ivviḻā vacanta kālattil (ti, māci, paṅkuṉi) muḻu nilā nāṭkaḷil koṇṭāṭap paṭukiṟatu. cevvāykkiḻamiyum, veḷḷikkiḻamiyum māriyammaṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṟkukanta nāṭkaḷ. nācciyammaṉ, poṅkalāyi, koṅkalāyi, poṉṉāyi eṉpavi vēṟu pala kuṟippiṭattakka peṇ teyvaṅkaḷ. mēla nācci, koṭikari nācci, ariya nācci, iḷiya nācci, eḻukari nācci eṉap pala peyarkaḷōṭu nācci kāṭciyaḷikkiṟāḷ. avaḷ vaḻipāṭṭukkukanta nāḷ viyāḻakkiḻamiyākum. vaḻipaṭumpōtu nācciyār kōyilil niṟinta amiti nilaval vēṇṭum. vaḻipāṭṭiṉ iṟutiyil paṭiyal (piracātam) ellārukkum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. vēṭarkaḷik kākkum kula teyvamāka nācci karutappaṭukiṟāḷ. kolli maliyil ivaḷukku niṟiyak kōyilkaḷuṇṭu, naṭuvaṇṇaṉi maṇanta vēṭacciyiṭamiruntu, nācciyammaṉ vaḻipāṭu maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē paraviṟṟu eṉpar. poṅkalāyi, kucakkuḻip poṅkalāyi, mayilāttip poṅkalāyi, taṇṇip pāḻip poṅkalāyi, vēlarāyaṉ poṅkalāyi, mūlikkāṭṭup poṅkalāyi, paṇikkaṉ kāṭṭup poṅkalāyi, pēyk kāṭṭup poṅkalāyi eṉap pala aṭi moḻikaḷōṭu, poṅkalāyi eṉṉum ciṟu teyvam maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē kōyil koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟāḷ.

ayyaṉār, periya āṇṭavaṉ, āṇṭiyappaṉ, nampiyāṇṭāṉ, caṭiyaṉ, veṭṭukkāraṉ, māci maliyaṉ, uruḷiyaṉ eṉa vēṟupala ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. āṉāl karuppaṉār vaḻipāṭu maliyāḷikaḷiṭiyē mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ovvoru ūrilum karuppaṉārukkuk kōyiluṇṭu. paṉṟippali karuppaṉārukku viruppamāṉa oṉṟu. kaṉṉiyammāḷ eṉṟa peṇ teyvam karuppaṉārōṭu toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

potuvākac ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷukku, muṟiyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ kiṭiyā, tiṟanta veḷiyilō allatu kūriyillāta cuvarkaḷukku naṭuvilō ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ amikkappeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṟippākak karuppaṉār kōyil ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum ivvāṟē amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maliyāḷikaḷē ikkōyilkaḷiṉ pūcārikaḷāka uḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷit 'tācaṉ' eṉṟum 'āṇṭi' eṉṟum aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. ikkōyiṟ paṇi avarkaḷukkup parampari urimiyuṭiyatu. orē pūcāri pala kōyilkaḷilum paṇipurivatuṇṭu. talippākiyum, nīṇṭa muṭiyum ivarkaḷi maṟṟa maliyāḷikaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. vāḻnāḷ muḻutumō allatu kuṟippiṭṭa cila nāṭkaḷō ivarkaḷ pulāl uṇavi otukki viṭukiṉṟaṉar. maliyāḷikaḷiṉ mukkiyamāṉa peruviḻākkaḷ poṅkal, tīpāvaḷi, āṭippatiṉeṭṭu ākiya mūṉṟumē. avaṟṟil poṅkal tirunāḷ mikavum ciṟanta muṟiyil koṇṭāṭappaṭukiṟatu. māṭṭup poṅkalaṉṟu maliyāḷikaḷ kūṭṭamākac cērntu koṇṭu vēṭṭikkuc (Hunting Excursion) celvatuṇṭu . aṉṟu naṭipeṟum erutāṭṭam (Bull Dance) kaṇṭu makiḻattakkatu.

vēṟu cila paḻakkaṅkaḷ :

maliyāḷikaḷ potuvāka varaku, tiṉi, arici ākiyavaṟṟic camittu uṇkiṉṟaṉar. paṉṟi iṟicciyum avarkaḷuṭiya uṇavil mukkiya iṭampeṟukiṟatu. cakkimukkik kalli irumputtuṇṭāl aṭittu neruppuṇṭākkum paḻakkam iṉṉum pacci maliyāḷikaḷiṭam nilavi varukiṟatu. ovvoru paṭṭiyilum iruvar allatu mūvarē ikkaruviyi vittirukkiṉṟaṉar. neruppu uṇṭākkuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭum tuṇip poruḷāṉa pañci oru tōṟ piyil potintu vittuḷḷaṉar. vaṇṇāṉ, nāvitaṉ, maruttuvacci ākiyōriṉ paṇiyi avarkaḷilēyē oru cilar mēṟkoṇṭu ceykiṉṟaṉar. mukkiyamāṉa ceytikaḷit tuṭumpu koṭṭi ellāp paṭṭikaḷilum aṟivikkum vēliyi aṅkuḷḷa aricaṉaṅkaḷ (paṟiyar) ceykiṉṟaṉar. vayalkaḷil vēliceyvataṟkum, vēṭṭiyil tuṇi purivataṟkum aricaṉaṅkaḷiyē amarttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. kālnaṭikaḷil ētāvatoṉṟu iṟantuviṭṭāl, maliyāḷikaḷyārum ataṉ arukil kūṭac cellamāṭṭārkaḷ ; atit toṭavumāṭṭārkaḷ. arukilirukkum aricaṉaṅkaḷukkut terivittu eṭuttukkoṇṭu cellumāṟu kūṟuvārkaḷ. āṉāl ētēṉum vipattukkuḷḷāki aṭipaṭṭuc cākum niliyilirukkum kāl naṭikaḷit taṅkaḷiṭam kūlivēli ceypavarkaḷukku viṟṟuviṭuvārkaḷ. ataṟku īṭākac ciṟutoki peṟṟukkoḷvar. maliyāḷikaḷil cilar ciṟanta māṭṭu vittiyarkaḷāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ māṭṭiṉ oṭinta elumpi mika virivil kūṭumāṟu ceyyum āṟṟal mikkavarkaḷ, pacumāṭṭiṉ tōli avarkaḷ toṭuvatu kūṭak kiṭiyātu. uḻavut toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭum poruḷkaḷiyum attōliṉāl ceyvatilli. iṟantupōṉa pacuviṉ tōlikkūṭat tāṅkaḷ urimiyākkik koḷḷa virumpuvatilli. appiṇatti eṭuttuc cellum aricaṉaṅkaḷukkē koṭuttu viṭuvar. maliyāḷikaḷum peṇkaḷum mikutiyākap pukip piṭikkum paḻakkamuṭiyavarkaḷ.

kuṟiñci nilaṅkaḷil vāḻum im maliyāḷikaḷ palvēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷāl camaveḷiyiluḷḷa makkaḷōṭu toṭarpiṉṟi vāḻntu vantaṉar. malipaṭu poruḷkaḷik koṇṭē taṅkaḷ iṉṟiyamiyāta tēvikaḷit tīrttukkoṇṭu vāḻntu vantaṉar, muṉṉēṟṟattil ivarkaḷ atika akkaṟi kāṭṭavilli. āṉāl nam nāṭṭu araciyalār, ivarkaḷuṭiya muṉṉēṟṟattilum ippoḻutu karuttuc celuttukiṉṟaṉar. nalla malippātikaḷ kolli malimītu ippōtu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṭiyiruppukkāṉa vīṭukaḷum, kalvivacatikkāṉa paḷḷikaḷum ippoḻutu niṟiyak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maruntu vacatikaḷum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum cila tiṅkaḷkaḷil untu vaṇṭikaḷum immaliyiṉ mēl viṭappaṭum. paḻamiyāṉa vaḻakkaṅkaḷiṉ piṭiyiliruntu ivarkaḷ ippōtu nīṅki varukiṉṟaṉar.





4. nīlakiri malikaḷ

peyark kāraṇam :

nīlakiri eṉṟāl nīla niṟamāṉa mali eṉṟu poruḷ. nīlakiri eṉṟa peyar 800 āṇṭukaḷāka vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. nīlakirikku aṇmiyiluḷḷa camaveḷiyil niṉṟu mēṟkut toṭarcci malikaḷiṉūṭē nimirntu niṟkum itik kāṇumpōtu, nīla niṟamāṉa paṭalam immaliyip pōrttiruppatu pōl tōṉṟum. ākiyiṉālēyē iti nīlakiri eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. nīlakiri maliyil nīla niṟamāṉa (Violet) oru pū eṅkum niṟintu malarntirukkiṟatu. ammalariṉālēyē nīla niṟamāṉa paṭalam tōṉṟuvatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. annīlappū nīlakiri maliyiṉ veḷippuṟattil pūppatilli. maliyiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa pīṭa pūmikaḷilēyē pūkkiṟatu. ākiyiṉāl maliyic cuṟṟi ēṟpaṭum nīla niṟappaṭalam ammalarkaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭatalla eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu.

amippu :

nīlakiri mali, mēlē maṭṭamāṉa oru mēciyip pōl amintirukkiṟatu. itaṉ uyaram ellāp pakutikaḷilum ēṟakkuṟiya orē aḷavuṭiyatāka uḷḷatu. nīlakiri mali 35 kal nīḷamum 20 kal akalamum carācari 6500 aṭi uyaramumuṭiyatu. mēṟkut toṭarcci malikaḷum kiḻakkut toṭarcci malikaḷum cērum iṭattil itu amintuḷḷatu. immaliyil oru caturamil kūṭac camanilattik kāṇa muṭiyātu. eṅku pārttālum mēṭum paḷḷaṅkaḷumē teṉpaṭum, itu camaveḷiyiliruntu ceṅkuttāka uyarntuḷḷatu. immaliyiṉ mēṟkuc carivilum, avuṭṭarlāṉi paḷḷattākkiṟku (Ouchterlony valley) mēlum, teṉcarivilum, cuvarkaḷip pōṉṟa, nūṟṟuk - kaṇakkāṉa aṭikaḷ uyaramuḷḷa pāṟikaḷ niṟintuḷḷaṉa. maraṅkaḷkūṭa vēr ūṉṟa muṭiyāta aḷavu avikaḷ ceṅkuttāka uḷḷaṉa. maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa carivukaḷilellām, aṭarntakāṭukaḷ niṟintuḷḷaṉa.

nīlakiri maliyāṉatu naṭuvil teṉvaṭalākac cellum uyarnta oru toṭarāl kiḻakkup pakutiyākavum mēṟkup pakutiyākavum pirikkappaṭukiṟatu. attoṭaril uḷḷa uyarnta cikaram toṭṭapeṭṭā ākum. toṭṭapeṭṭā eṉṟāl 'perumali' eṉṟu poruḷ. iccikaram utakamaṇṭalattiṟkuk kiḻakkē amintuḷḷatu. itaṉ uyaram kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 8,640 aṭi. nam nāṭṭil imayamalikkut teṟkiluḷḷa malikaḷil, tiruvāṅkūriluḷḷa āṉi muṭi cikarattiṟku (8837 aṭi) aṭuttāṟpōl uyaramāṉatu toṭṭapeṭṭā cikaramtāṉ. ic cikarattiṉ mēl, muṉpu vāṉāyvukkūṭam amikkappaṭṭiruntatu. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu toṭṭapeṭṭāviṉ aḻakip pūraṇamāka aṉupavikka muṭiyātu. kiḻakkup pakutiyilō allatu mēṟkup pakutiyilō irukkum toliviluḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu ic cikarattik kāṇiṉ mikavum aḻakākat tōṉṟum.

toṭṭapeṭṭā maliyiṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyilum teṟkup pakutiyilum uḷḷa pīṭapūmikaḷ perumpālum kūṉūr kōṭṭattilēyē amintuḷḷaṉa. aṅku vāḻum paṭakarkaḷ appīṭa pūmikaḷil payirt toḻil ceytu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ aṅkirunta kāṭukaḷil perumpakutiyi aḻittu viṭṭaṉar. eṅku pārttālum cenniṟa ōṭukavitta paṭakarkaḷiṉ kuṭicikaḷaṭaṅkiya ciṟṟūrkaḷē tōṉṟum. ippaṭakarkaḷ muṉ kālattil ōrāṇṭu payiriṭṭa iṭattil, aṭutta āṇṭu payiriṭuvatilli. eṉavē taṅkaḷ payirt toḻilukku vēṇṭiya putiya putiya nilattiṟkākak kāṭukaḷiṉ perumpakutiyi aḻittu viṭṭaṉar. toṭṭapeṭṭā cikarattiṉ ceṅkuttāṉa carivukaḷ, payirt toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭāta kāraṇattāl paṭakarkaḷiṉ aḻivu vēliyiṉiṉṟum tappi, niṟinta kāṭukaḷip peṟṟup pēraḻakōṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

toṭṭapeṭṭāviṟku mēṟkil uḷḷa pakutikaḷil paṭakar vāḻvatilli. paṭakarkaḷ vāḻāta iṭattil payirttoḻil illi eṉṟu collalām. avviṭaṅkaḷil tōṭarkaḷ vāḻum ciṟu kuṭicikaḷ teṉpaṭum. eṅku pārttālum ciṟitum peritumāṉa pacumiyāṉa kuṉṟukaḷ teṉpaṭum. ak kuṉṟukaḷil pala vaṇṇaṅkaḷiyuṭiya malarkaḷ pūttuk kuluṅkum. ikkuṉṟukaḷukku naṭuvil nīrōṭikaḷum ciṟṟāṟukaḷum ōṭi, ivikaḷit taṉittaṉiyē pirikkiṉṟaṉa. avvōṭikaḷi aṭuttāṟpōl puṟṟarikaḷ, paranta iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ niṟintirukkum. ikkāṭukaḷi 'ṣōlā' (Shola) eṉṟu aḻippar. iṭiyiṭiyē mēli nāṭṭiṉarukkuc contamāṉa pūṅkākkaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. appūṅkākkaḷil pacumiyāṉa ilikaḷukku naṭuvil peritāka malarntirukkum cenniṟa rōjā malarkaḷ tam taliyi acittuc cirikkum.

toṭṭapeṭṭāvaṉṟi nīlakiri maliyil vēṟu pala cikaraṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. paṉivīḻ cikaram (Snowdown 8,299'), kiḷap cikaram {Club Hill 8,030'), elk cikaram {Elk Hill 80,90'), tēvaṣōlā cikaram (The devine wood Hill 7,417') kulakkampi cikaram (5,601') kūṉūrpeṭṭā cikaram (6,894'), ralliyā cikaram (Rallia Hill 7,325') timmaṭṭi cikaram (6,903'), ekupā cikaram (Hecuba Hill 7793'), kaṭṭakkāṭu cikaram (7933'), kulkuṭi cikaram (8,002'), nīlakiri cikaram (8,118'), mūkaṟutti cikaram (8,380'), tērpeṭṭā cikaram (8,304'), kolipeṭṭā cikaram (8,182') eṉpavi kuṟippiṭattakkavi.

aruvikaḷum nīrvīḻccikaḷum :

nīlakiriyiṉ mītuḷḷa pīṭapūmi uyarvum tāḻvumāṉa kuṉṟukaḷiyuṭiyatu eṉṟu paṭittōm. iraṇṭu kuṉṟukaḷukku naṭuviluḷḷa ovvōr iṭi veḷiyilum ōr aruvi ōṭikkoṇṭirukkum. ivvāṟu ōṭum aruvikaḷ pala oṉṟu cērntu pēraruviyāka māṟum. appēraruvi vaṭakkiluḷḷa mōyāṟṟilāvatu, teṟkiluḷḷa pavāṉiyāṟṟilāvatu viḻum. nīlakiriyiṉ vaṭapakutiyil tōṉṟum cīkūr āṟāṉatu cīkūr malikaḷiṉ pakkamāka ōṭi mōyāṟṟil viḻukiṟatu. ivvāṟu utakamaṇṭala ērikku mēṟpuṟattiluḷḷa carivil tōṉṟi, ēriyil kuḷittuk kiḷampi, 'caṇṭi nullā' aruviyōṭu cērkiṟatu. avvāṟu cērnta tum 170 aṭi uyarattiliruntu vīḻntu 'kālakaṭṭi' nīr vīḻcciyi uṇṭākkukiṟatu. in nīrvīḻcci cīkūr malimēl amintuḷḷa kālakaṭṭi taṅkalmaṉi (Travellers' bangalow) yiṉ etirē uḷḷatu.

nīlakiriyiṉ vaṭakiḻakkiliruntu tōṉṟi ōṭivarum mutukāṭu aruvi, ārañcup paḷḷattākku eṉṟa iṭattil oru nīrvīḻcciyi uṇṭākkukiṟatu. ivviṭam iyaṟki vaṉappil īṭum eṭuppum aṟṟatu. ivviṭattil ārañcup paḻaṅkaḷ niṟiya viḷivatāl ip peyar peṟṟatu pōlum. ip paḷḷattākkiṟkuḷ mutal mutal nuḻinta ōr āṅkilak kāṭṭilākā atikāri, ataṉ aḻakil īṭupaṭṭut tammi maṟantār. "ārañcup paḷḷattākku! aḻakāṉa peyar! ip peyarilēyē oruvitac cuvi tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. vaṇṇa malarkaḷ pūttuk kuluṅkum kuḷir niḻal cūḻnta ivviṭattil nuḻintatum, eṉ mutumiyuḷḷattil kūṭap puttuṇarcci piṟakkiṟatu. eṉ uḷḷatti itaṉ aḻakil paṟikoṭuttu viṭṭut tēṭukiṟēṉ. amiti, putumi ākiya iraṇṭiyum nāṉ iṅku kāṇkiṟēṉ. iṉpatti nāṭum iḷaṅ kātalar kiyoṭu kiyiṇittuk koṇṭu, kaṉintu kuluṅkum paḻaṅkaḷic cuvittukkoṇṭu, maṇaṅ kamaḻum malarkaḷip paṟittu nukarntukoṇṭu meṉṉaṭi pōṭṭu metuvāka ulavuvataṟku ivviṭam mikavum ēṟṟatu“ eṉṟu kūṟi makiḻkiṟār.

kōtakiri (Kotagiri)yiliruntu katātakallā (Gathada Halla) eṉṟa ōrāṟu teṟku nōkki ōṭi varukiṟatu. atu 250 aṭi uyarattiliruntu vīḻntu ceyiṇṭ kātariṉ nīrvīḻcciyi uṇṭākkukiṟatu. ceyiṇṭ kātariṉ eṉṟa ammiyār tiruvāḷar em. ṭi. kākparṉ eṉṟa veḷḷiyariṉ maṉivi. ivvammiyārum, ivar kaṇavarum mutaṉ mutalākak kōtakiriyil kuṭi pukunta veḷḷiyaril kuṟippiṭat takkavarkaḷ. ivviruvariṉ camātikaḷ kōtakiriyilēyē uḷḷaṉa. ivvammiyāriṉ peyar innīr vīḻccikku iṭappaṭṭatu. kūṉūrukku arukiluḷḷa ṭāḵpiṉ mūkki (Dolphin's nose)liruntu kāṇpōrkkuk kātariṉ nīrvīḻcci kaṇkoḷḷāp pēraḻakōṭu kāṭci tarum.

kūṉūr malikkarukiluḷḷa paṭukiyil tōṉṟi, kūṉūr, velliṅṭaṉ ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka ōṭi varukiṟatu. kūṉūr āṟu. ivvāṟu 'kāṭṭēri nīrvīḻcci'yi ēṟpaṭuttukiṟatu. in nīr vīḻcciyiliruntu eṭukkappaṭum miṉṉāṟṟal aravaṅ kāṭṭiluḷḷa paṭikkalat toḻiṟcāli (Cordite factory)yi iyakkukiṟatu. in nīrvīḻcciyiṉ uyaram 180 aṭi. kūṉūr, utakamaṇṭala vācikaḷ ivviṭattiṟku aṭikkaṭi iṉpac celavi mēṟkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.

kulakkampi malikku arukiluḷḷa iṭattil pāyntu varum kulakkampi āṟu oru nīrviḻcciyi uṇṭākkukiṟatu. ivvāṟu mikavum ciṟiyatu. āṉāl itaṉāl uṇṭākkappaṭum nīr vīḻcciyō, nīlakiri maliyilēyē uyaramāṉatu. cumār 400 aṭi uyarattiliruntu itu vīḻkiṟatu. kulakkampi āṟṟukku mēṟkil kuntā āṟu kuṟukiya paṭukki vaḻiyāka ōṭi varukiṟatu. ivvāṟu kuntā maliyi, nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyiliruntu taṉiyākap pirikkiṟatu. utakamaṇṭalattiṟkut teṉmēṟkiluḷḷa nañca nāṭu paḷḷattākku, emarālṭu paḷḷattākku ākiyavaṟṟil ōṭi varum ciṟṟaruvikaḷiyum oṉṟu cērttukkoṇṭu kuntā maliyiliruntu ōṭi varukiṟatu. ivvāṟṟiṉ kuṟukkē amikkappaṭṭirukkum nīr miṉcārat tiṭṭam mikavum periyatu. itil uṇṭākum miṉṉāṟṟal pala toḻiṟcālikaḷi ōṭṭuvataṟkum viḷakkerikkavum payaṉpaṭukiṟatu.

nīlakiriyiṉ teṉmēṟku mūliyiliruntu 'pilli tātā kallā' eṉṟa aḻakiya ciṟṟāṟu ōṭi varukiṟatu. itu avalañci maliyiṉ mēṟkuc carivil pāykiṟatu. pavāṉi yāṟṟiṉ mukkiyat tuṇiyāṟukaḷil ituvum oṉṟu.

nīlakiriyiṉ mēṟkuk kōṭiyiliruntu ellāvaṟṟiṉum periya āṟākiya pikkārā tōṉṟukiṟatu. mūkkaṟutti maliyiṉ carivil ivvāṟu tōṉṟi, kiḻakkiluḷḷa kirūr maṇṭu, pārcaṉ ākiya paḷḷattākkukaḷiliruntu ōṭi varum aruvikaḷiṉ nīri peṟṟuk koṇṭu, pikkārā taṅkal maṉikku etirē ōṭi varukiṟatu. piṟaku kuṟukiya oru paḷḷattākkil pukuntu iraṇṭu periya nīrvīḻccikaḷāka muṟiyē 180 aṭi uyarattiliruntum, 200 aṭi uyarattiliruntum viḻukiṟatu. kūṭalūr - micūr pātiyil nāṉku kallukku appāl varumpōtē nīr viḻum oli nam kātil viḻum. aṅkiruntu kāṇpōrukku, pīṭapūmiyākiya peruñ cuvaril, paṉik kaṭṭiyāl ceytu cārttappaṭṭa uyarnta ēṇikaḷ pōl iv vīḻccikaḷ tōṉṟum.

piṟaku pikkārā āṟu metuvāka iṟaṅki vayanāṭṭuk kāṭukaḷil nuḻintu, maṟupaṭiyum kiḻakkil tirumpic ceṉṟu kūṭalūr - micūr pātikkarukiluḷḷa tippakkāṭu eṉṟa iṭattil maṟupaṭiyum oru nīrvīḻcciyi uṇṭākkukiṟatu.

pikkārā āṟṟit tōṭarkaḷ puṇṇiya āṟākak karutukiṉṟaṉar. karuvuṟṟa tōṭar kulap peṇkaḷ iv vāṟṟik kaṭakkat tuṇiyamāṭṭārkaḷ. āṇkaḷkūṭa ivvāṟṟu nīri etaṟkum payaṉpaṭuttuvatilli. mikavum iṉṟiyamiyāta nēraṅkaḷil ivvāṟṟik kaṭantu cella nēriṭṭāltāṉ nīrit toṭuvārkaḷ. āṟṟik kaṭantatum, āṟṟi nōkkit tirumpi vaṇaṅkiviṭṭuttāṉ celvar. pikkārā taṅkal maṉikku arukiluḷḷa pālattiṉ mēl ēṟi āṟṟik kaṭantāl kūṭa, ataṟku mariyāti celuttāmal tōṭarkaḷ cellamāṭṭārkaḷ. pikkārā nīr vīḻcci miṉcārat tiṭṭam mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

kāṭukaḷ

intiya nāṭṭiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷ, nāṭṭiṉ vivacāyap poruḷātāra muṉṉēṟṟattil perum paṅku vakikkiṉṟaṉa. nam nāṭṭil 2.81 laṭcam catura mil parappuḷḷa kāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. nam nāṭṭiṉ mottapparappil 100kku 222 viḻukkāṭu ivi uḷḷaṉa. mattiyap piratēcam, assām, tiripuram, kēraḷam, micūr ākiya mānilaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷ malipaṭu celvattiṟkup peyar peṟṟavi. tiripurattiṉ mottap parappil 100kku 60 paṅkum, assāmil 100kku 44 paṅkum, kāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ik kāṭukaḷiṉāl kiṭṭattaṭṭa 10 laṭcam pērukku vēli kiṭikkiṟatu. intiya nāṭṭuk kāṭukaḷ pōtiya aḷavu celva vaḷattip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. uyarnta rakamāṉa maraṅkaḷ, ivaṟṟil niṟiyak kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. nam nāṭṭu irayilvē ilākā, perumpālum kāṭṭu maraṅkaḷiyē nampi vaḷarntirukkiṟatu. kākitam, nūl cuṟṟum kaṭṭi (bobin), oṭṭup palaki {plywood), neruppup peṭṭi mutaliyaṉa tayārikkum toḻilkaḷukkuk kāṭṭu maraṅkaḷiyē nāṭa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ eri poruḷ (fuel) kāṭukaḷiliruntē peṟappaṭukiṟatu. tēkku, karuṅkāli, cāl tītar, cir, cīcam mutaliya viliyuyarnta payaṉmaraṅkaḷ kāṭukaḷiliruntē peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maruntukaḷ ceyyap payaṉpaṭum mūlikikaḷukku nām kāṭukaḷiyē tēṭic cella vēṇṭum.

nam nāṭṭiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷ avvav viṭaṅkaḷiṉ taṭpa veppa nilikaḷukkēṟpap pala tiṟappaṭṭavikaḷāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. mikka maḻi peyyum iṭaṅkaḷil eṉṟum pacumi māṟāta kāṭukaḷ vaḷarum. naṭuttaramāṉa maḻiyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil uyarnta rakamāṉa maraṅkaḷ vaḷarum. teṉṉāṭṭut tēkkum, vaṭanāṭṭuc cāl maramum ittakiya kāṭukaḷilēyē viḷikiṉṟaṉa. mitamāṉa maḻiyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil muḷ maraṅkaḷum, ceṭikaḷum niṟinta vaṟaṇṭa kāṭukaḷ vaḷarum.

nīlakiri malimītuḷḷa kāṭukaḷi nāṉku vakiyākap pirikkalām. aviyāvaṉa : (1) pacumi māṟākkāṭukaḷ (Sholas), (2) eri poruḷukkākavē āstirēliya maraṅkaḷi vaḷarkkum ceyaṟkik kāṭukaḷ, (3) vaṭa carivilum mōyāṟṟup paḷḷattākkilum uḷḷa aḻikkappaṭum kāṭukaḷ (decidous forests), (4) vaya nāṭṭiluḷḷa aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ.

mutal vakiyic cārnta pacumi māṟāk kāṭukaḷ nīlakiriyiṉ ucciyiluḷḷa pīṭapūmikaḷil uḷḷaṉa, poruḷātārak kaṇkoṇṭu pārkkumpōtu ik kāṭukaḷellām payaṉaṟṟavi. ik kāṭukaḷil vaḷarum maraṅkaḷellām metuvāka vaḷarak kūṭiyavi. im maraṅkaḷ muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭiya oru nūṟṟāṇṭu cellum. mēlum ivikaḷiṉiṉṟum veṭṭi eṭukkappaṭum viṭṭaṅkaḷ (timber) mikac ciṟiyavi; vilimatippaṟṟavi. iyaṟki aḻakukkum, maliyil nīr vaṟṟāmaliruppataṟkum ik kāṭukaḷ payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

karu maram (black wood), tītar, piṉ mutaliya maraṅkaḷ āstirēliya iṉattic cārntavi. im maraṅkaḷ āṟu allatu ēḻu āṇṭukaḷilēyē virivāka vaḷarntu muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭiyak kūṭiyavi. im maraṅkaḷ aḷikkum viṭṭaṅkaḷ uṟutiyāṉavi ; payaṉ mikkavi; vili matippuṭiyavi. ivi nam nāṭṭiṉ niṟinta eriporuḷ tēviyiyum niṟivu ceykiṉṟaṉa.

piṉṉiru vakik kāṭukaḷil cumār muppattintu vakiyāṉa payaṉuḷḷa maraṅkaḷ aṭarntu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil mikavum mukkiyamāṉavi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭattakkavi veḷḷi citār, cāṭiṉvuṭ, pūvā, vēṅki, āccā, iruḷ, karuṅkāli, maṭṭi, veṇ tēkku, tēkku, nelli, cantaṉam eṉpavi.





vilaṅkukaḷ

yāṉi :

yāṉikaḷ nīlakiri maliyil ippoḻutu atikamilli. kōṭiyiṉ veppattit tāḷāmal, aṭi vārattiliruntu ivi kuntā pīṭapūmikkuk kūṭṭam kūṭṭamāka vantu viṭum. ippōtu cattiya maṅkala malikaḷilum, mēṭṭuppāḷiyattic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa kāṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. yāṉikaḷiṉ tolliyi oḻippataṟkāka ki. pi. 1840-ām āṇṭu kōvi māvaṭṭat taṇṭalarāka iruntavar ciṭṭakāṅkiliruntu vēṭṭikkārarkaḷi varavaḻittu, avikaḷip piṭikkumāṟu ceytār. avuṭṭarlāṉip paḷḷattākkilkūṭa yāṉikaḷ muṉpu niṟiyat tirintaṉa, aṅku kāḵpi payiriṭat toṭaṅkiyatum yāṉikaḷiṉ varuki oḻintatu. nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyiṉ vaṭapakutiyilum vaya nāṭṭilum ippoḻutu atikamāka uḷḷaṉa. pavāṉip paḷḷattākkilum yāṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ aṭikkaṭi teṉpaṭuvatuṇṭu.

puli :

nīlakiriyiṉ ucciyilum, tāḻnta pīṭapūmikaḷilum pulikaḷ cuṟṟit tirikiṉṟaṉa. mārcciliruntu jūṉ vari ivi aṭikkaṭi teṉpaṭum. kōṭi veppattukkum, kāṭṭut tīkkum añci ivi im mātaṅkaḷil ucciyiluḷḷa pīṭapūmikaḷukku vantuviṭum. vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷum, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa paṉiyarkaḷum pulikaḷi vali pōṭṭup piṭippatil vallavarkaḷ, ovvoru vīṭṭilum oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu valivāṉa valikaḷ eppoḻutum toṅkik koṇṭirukkum. taṅkaḷ ūrukku aṇmiyiluḷḷa kāṭṭil puli vantiruntāl, eppaṭiyum ivarkaḷukku acceyti terintuviṭum. uṭaṉē valikaḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭu kiḷampi viṭuvārkaḷ, puli vēṭṭikkup puṟappaṭuvataṟku muṉpākat taṅkaḷuṭiya kula teyvaṅkaḷukku vaḻipāṭu nikaḻttuvar. aṅkuḷḷa pūcārikaḷ maruḷ koṇṭu āṭi vēṭṭiyiṉ muṭivippaṟṟi muṉkūṭṭic colluvatum uṇṭu. puli paṭuttirukkum iṭattic cuṟṟip 'pa' vaṭivāka valiyi amippar, oru paṉiyaṉ marattiṉmēl ēṟik koṇṭu maṟṟavarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭaḷikaḷ piṟappippāṉ. kiyil 12 aṭi nīḷamuḷḷa kuttīṭṭikaḷōṭu puliyi nāṟpuṟamum vaḷittuk koḷvar, avvalikkuḷḷēyē ap puliyip pala nāḷ paṭṭiṉipōṭṭu, īṭṭiyāl kuttit tuṉpuṟuttuvar; iriccaliṭṭuk koṇṭum, āṭik koṇṭum, pāṭik koṇṭum puliyik kolluvārkaḷ.

ciṟuttiyum karaṭiyum :

ciṟuttikaḷ im maliyil ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. karuñ ciṟuttikaḷ pīṭapūmikaḷiviṭac camaveḷikaḷil atikamākat teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. karaṭikaḷum iṅku niṟiya uṇṭu. āṉāl avi carivukaḷilum tāḻvāṉa pīṭapūmikaḷilumē aṭikkaṭi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ārañcup paḷḷattākkil mutalil niṟiya iruntaṉaveṉṟu kāṭṭatikārikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

māṉ :

māṉiṉam iṅku niṟiya vāḻkiṟatu. kāṭu uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilellām māṉkaḷik kāṇalām. kuṟippākak kuntāp pīṭapūmiyiluḷḷa paḷḷattākkil ivi atikamākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēṭṭik kaḻakattārāl (Game Association) ik kāṭu pātukākkappaṭuvatāl, ayalār yārum aṉumati peṟṟalaṉṟi ik kāṭṭiṟkuḷ vēṭṭiyāṭac cella muṭiyātu. eṉavē māṉiṉam iṅku peruki vaḷarntuḷḷatu. iṅkuk kalimāṉkaḷ niṟiya uṇṭu. im māṉkaḷiṉ kompukaḷ vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa māṉkaḷiṉ kompukaḷippōl atika nīḷam vaḷarvatilli. iruntālum 42 aṅkula nīḷam irukkiṉṟaṉa. airōppāvilum ivi pōṉṟa tōṟṟamuṭiya māṉiṉam uṇṭu. avikaṭku elk (Elk) eṉṟu peyar, utakamaṇṭalattil vantu taṅkiya airōppiyar itiyum elk eṉṟē aḻikkat toṭaṅkiṉar. ivviṉam mikuntu kāṇappaṭum iṭatti elk mali (Elk Hill) eṉṟa peyarālēyē aḻittaṉar. puḷḷi māṉkaḷ uyarnta pīṭapūmi kaḷukku varuvatilli. avi maciṉikkuṭikkarukil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

kurikkum māṉ (Barking deer) eṉṟa ōriṉam iṅku irukkiṟatu. āṉāl nīlakiri malivācikaḷ itik 'kāṭṭāṭu' eṉṟa tavaṟaṉa peyarāl aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. mutaṉ mutalil nīlakiri malikku vanta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ itik kāṭṭāṭu eṉṟu kūṟiyatāl appeyarē nilittu viṭṭatu. ivikaḷum kali māṉkaḷip pōlavē, utakamaṇṭalattu vēṭṭi nāykaḷukku añcāta muraṭṭuttaṉam vāyntavi. nāṉku kompukaḷiyuṭiya māṉkaḷum iṅku irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl avi mikavum arukik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟiya eli māṉkaḷ (Mouse deer) carivukaḷiluḷḷa aṭarnta kāṭukaḷil uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl kuṟintē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. karumāṉ (antelope) kaḷum cīkūruk karukil kuṟinta aḷavil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.

kāṭṭerumi:

cila camayaṅkaḷil kāṭṭerumikaḷ cattiyamaṅkalam malikaḷiliruntu mēṭṭuppāḷiyattiṟ karukiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷukku varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl maciṉikkuṭik kāṭukaḷilum, vaya nāṭṭiṟku vaṭakkiluḷḷa mutumalik kāṭukaḷilum niṟiya uḷḷaṉa. kāṭṭil ēṟpaṭṭa oru koṭiya nōyiṉāl ivi tākkappaṭṭup perum pakuti aḻivuṟṟaṉa. ippōtu nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyil ivikaḷ illi eṉṟē collalām.

kāṭṭup paṉṟi :

nīlakiri maliyil vāḻum paṉṟikaḷ koḻuttup paruttu muraṭṭuttaṉattōṭu tiriyum. ivi payirkaḷi aṭikkaṭi aḻippatāl, ivikaḷi evviṭattilum kolluvataṟkaṉumati yuṇṭu. vēṭṭi nāykaḷ kūṭa ivikaḷip piṉ toṭara añcum, oru camayattil oru paṉṟi 13 nāykaḷit taṉ kōṭṭāl kuttik kāyappaṭutti viṭṭatām.

variyāṭu :

variyāṭu allatu kāṭṭāṭu (Ibex) intiya nāṭṭuk kāṭukaḷil maṭṭum kāṇappaṭum ōr iṉamākum. imayamaliyil kāṇappaṭum tār (Tarh) eṉṉum āṭu ivviṉattilēyē maṟṟoru pirivic cērntatākum. ceṉṉi mānilattiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷukkē urittāṉa iv variyāṭu, apicīṉiyāviluḷḷa uyarnta malikaḷit tavira ulakil vēṟu eṅkum kiṭiyātu. mutaṉ mutalāka nīlakiri malikku vanta airōppiyarkaḷukku ivvāṭu oru putumiyāka iruntatu. ēṉeṉṟāl avarkaḷ nāṭṭil itu pōṉṟa āṭu kiṭiyātu. ivvāṭu nīlakiri maliyilallāmal, mēṟkut toṭarcci maliyiṉ vēṟu pakuti kaḷāṉa paḻaṉi malik kāṭukaḷilum, āṉi malik kāṭukaḷilum niṟiya uḷḷaṉa. ēṉ? kumarimuṉi variyilum ivviṉam malintu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. nīlakirip pīṭapūmikaḷil itu cātāraṇamākak kāṇappaṭṭālum, kuntā malittoṭariṉ teṟkilum mēṟkilum uḷḷa mikavum ceṅkuttāṉa carivukaḷilēyē kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. iv vāṭukaḷiṉ koṭiya paki ciṟuttiyē. ivikaḷiṉ kompukaḷ 171 aṅkulam vari vaḷarum. pārppataṟku aḻakaṟṟavi eṉṟālum, vilimatippuḷḷavi. ēṉeṉṟāl ivikaḷ eḷitil kiṭippatilli. ivvāṭukaḷ tiriyum ceṅkuttup pāṟikaḷil yārum kāli ūṉṟakkūṭa muṭiyātu. 1875-ām āṇṭil oru variyāṭṭit turattic ceṉṟu carivil viḻuntu iṟanta puccer eṉṟa veḷḷiyariṉ uṭal ceyiṇṭ. sṭīḵpaṉ carccil putikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

kāṭṭu nāy :

maṟṟak kāṭukaḷil iruppatip pōṉṟē kāṭṭu nāy (wild dog) nīlakiri maliyiluḷḷa māṉiṉattiṟkup perum etiriyāka uḷḷatu. in nāykaḷ oṉṟu viṭṭa āṇṭukaḷil nīlakirik kāṭukaḷukku vantu viṭṭuc cellukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu ivikaḷ vantu celvataṉ kāraṇam puriyavilli . ki. pi. 1906-ām āṇṭil kuntāk kāṭukaḷil ivi niṟintirunta kāraṇattāl māṉiṉam avvāṇṭil arukiyē kāṇappaṭṭatu. kāṭṭil paravum nōykaḷ oru vitattil naṉmiyē purikiṉṟaṉa. in nāykaḷ eḷitil nōykkuḷḷāki iṟakkum taṉmiyuṭiyavi. ki. pi. 1893-ām āṇṭu cīkūrukku aṇmiyiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷil 9 nāykaḷ nōykku ilakkāki melintu iṟantu kiṭantaṉa. vayanāṭṭiluḷḷa nalla kōṭṭiyil 3 nāykaḷ iṟantu kiṭantaṉa.

kuraṅkukaḷ :

nīlakiri maliyil vāḻum kuraṅkukaḷ palatiṟappaṭṭavi. 'nīlakirilaṅkar' eṉṟu potuvākak kūṟappaṭum kuraṅkiṉam, pīṭapūmikaḷilum, pacumarak kāṭukaḷilum niṟiyak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉuṭiya uṭampil paṭṭuppōlak karumiyāṉa mayir niṟintirukkum. mukamum kaḻuttum cenniṟamāka irukkum. paḻat tōṭṭaṅkaḷukku itaṉāl peruṅkēṭu viḷivatuṇṭu. ākiyiṉāl anumāriṉ avatāram eṉpatik kūṭap poruṭpaṭuttāmal, ivaṟṟic cuṭṭuk koṉṟu viṭukiṉṟaṉar.

'ciṅkavāl kuraṅku' eṉṟa ōr iṉam iṅku uṇṭu. itu mēṟkup pakkattup pīṭapūmikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ uṭalil karumayir niṟintirukkum: itaṉuṭiya mukattic cuṟṟi veṇmi kalanta cemmayir vaṭṭamāka muḷittirukkum, ciṅkattiṟku iruppatippōl itaṉ vāl nuṉiyil mayir kuñcamāka muḷittirukkum.

vēṟu cila vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷ :

kiḻakkiluḷḷa tāḻnta carivukaḷil mūṉṟu vakiyāṉa vuvālkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟuttip pūṉi, kāṭṭup pūṉi, marap pūṉi (palm civet) eṉap pala vakiyāṉa pūṉikaḷ iṅku niṟiya irukkiṉṟaṉa. varikkaḻuttukkīri, ceṅkīri, paḻuppuk kīri eṉa mūṉṟu vitamāṉa kīrikaḷ iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēṟkuc carivil tāḻvāka uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil 'paṟakkum aṇil' eṉṟa oruvaki aṇil iṉam uṇṭu. itōṭu ēḻu vitamāṉa aṇilkaḷ nīlakirik kāṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi īḻam, jāvā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭum iṉaṅkaḷic cāntavi.

iṅkup palavaki elikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. puḷḷikaḷiyuṭiya eli iṉam oṉṟu iṅku uḷḷatu. ivvelikaḷ vayalkaḷukku aṭiyil nilattik kuṭintu payirkaḷiṉ vērik kaṭittu nācam ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. kāḵpic ceṭiyiṉ kiḷikaḷil ēṟip pūkkaḷit tiṉṟu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.



uṇavup payirkaḷ :

nīlakiri malikaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa payirt toḻili iru vakiyākap pirikkalām, nīlakiriyil vāḻum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāl naṭattappeṟum uṇavut tāṉiyap payirt, toḻil oruvaki, airōppiya, intiyat tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷāl naṭattappeṟum payirttoḻil maṟṟoru vaki, iṅku naṭipeṟum payirtoḻil muḻukka muḻukka maḻi nīriyum, aruvi nīriyumē nampi naṭipeṟukiṟatu. kiṇaṟu mutaliya ceyaṟkip pācaṉa muṟikaḷ iṅkuk kiṭiyā.

camaveḷiyil viḷivatip pōṉṟu iṅkuḷḷa pīṭapūmikaḷilum cāmiyum, rākiyum viḷikiṉṟaṉa. korali (Tiny millet) eṉṟoru tāṉiyamum iṅku viḷikiṟatu. itu kōtumiyil irupatil oru paṅku parumaṉē irukkum. iti ellāvitamāṉa nilaṅkaḷilum viḷivikkalām. mōcamāṉa vaḷamaṟṟa nilamāka iruntāl kūṭa avviṭattilum itu ceḻittu vaḷarum.

iṅku atikamāka viḷiyum kōtumiyāṉatu, camaveḷiyil viḷiyum kōtumiyiṉiṉṟum uruvattil māṟupaṭṭatu. itaṉmēl nīṇṭa vittuṟi pōrttukkoṇṭirukkum. iṭikkum pōtutāṉ ivvuṟi tāṉiyattiliruntu piriyum. ittāṉiyattip payir ceyyum paṭakarkaḷ, ivvāṟu vittuṟi kōtumiyip pōrttukkoṇṭiruppatu ciṟappu eṉṟu karutukiṟārkaḷ. ēṉeṉṟāl nīlakiri maliyil payirkaḷukku mikavum cētam viḷivikkum pūccikaḷiṭamiruntu, atu tāṉiyattik kāppatākac collukiṟārkaḷ, moṭṭik kōtumi eṉṟa vēṟoru tāṉiyamum iṅku viḷikiṟatu. itu airōppiya vakiyic cārntatu. callivaṉ eṉṟa airōppiyar iti mutaṉ mutalāka nīlakiriyil payiriṭṭup parappiṉār. itu vittuṟiyuṭaṉ kūṭiyirāta kāraṇattāl moṭṭik kōtumi (Naked wheat) eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.

iṅku viḷiyum pārliyil palavaki uṇṭu. avikaḷil mikavum ciṟantatu 'akki kāñci' eṉpatākum. 'paṭakar kāñci' eṉpatu, paṭakarkaḷāl nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷākap payiriṭappaṭṭu vanta nāṭṭup pārli. turikkañci (Gentlemen's barlie) eṉṟa oṉṟu uṇṭu. itu callivaṉ turiyāl iṅkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭatāl ippeyar peṟṟatu. āṉāl nāṭṭuk kāñciyiviṭa itu tarattil kuṟintatu. haṉīvel eṉṟa airōppiyar iṅkilāntiliruntum, skāṭlāntiliruntum uyarnta rakamāṉa pārliyi varavaḻittu, aravaṅkāṭṭiṟku arukilirunta paṭakarkaḷiṭam koṭuttup payiriṭumāṟu kūṟiṉār. avarkaḷ ciṟitu tayaṅkavē, viḷiyum tāṉiyatti atikavili koṭuttu vāṅkikkoḷvatāka vākkuṟutiyaḷittār. avarkaḷum koñca nāḷ payiriṭṭaṉar. maṟṟa pārlikaḷiviṭa itaṟku atikat taḻi uram tēvippaṭṭatu. maṟṟavarkaḷippōl nāṭṭup pārliyip payiriṭuvati viṭṭuviṭṭut tāṅkaḷ maṭṭum putu mātiriyāṉa tāṉiyattip payiriṭuvati avarkaḷ virumpavilli. ākiyiṉāl ammuyaṟci kiviṭappaṭṭatu.

uruḷik kiḻaṅku :

iṅku viḷiyum uruḷik kiḻaṅku perum aḷavil, parmā, ilaṅki mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭukiṉṟatu, āṉāl mēli nāṭṭu uruḷik kiḻaṅkukaḷip pōl avvaḷavu uyarntatalla. airōppiyat tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷ cīmik kiḻaṅkukaḷiṉ vitikaḷik koṇṭuvantu payirttoḻil ceyyum paṭakarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉarkaḷ. cīmik kiḻaṅkip payiriṭumpōtu nalla kaṇkāṇippu vēṇṭum; pāttikaṭṭa vēṇṭum ; kaḷi piṭuṅka vēṇṭum. pūcci puḻukkaḷ viḻāmal pārttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. paṭakarkaḷ pōtiya kavaṉam celuttātatāl appayir mutalil cariyāka viḷiyavilli. āṉal ippoḻutu nalla muṟiyil payiriṭukiṟārkaḷ.

ōṭs :

uruḷik kiḻaṅkiṟku aṭuttāṟpōl ōṭs oru ciṟanta payir, āṉāl iṅkuk kuṟinta aḷavē viḷikiṟatu. jārj oks eṉṟa airōppiyar, pāṭṉā āstirēliyā, niyūcīlantu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil iruntu ciṟanta vitikaḷit taruvittu iṅkup parappiṉar.

amarnāt

iṅku amarnāt eṉṟāeru tāṉiyamum viḷikiṟatu. itu ciṟṟūrkaḷi aṭuttuḷḷa vaḷamāṉa nilaṅkaḷilēyē payirākiṟatu. itic conta upayōkattiṟkē payiriṭṭuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. viṟpaṉikkup payiriṭuvatilli. itu kōtumiyil nāṟpatil oru paṅku parumaṉuḷḷa, ciṟiya veṇmiyāṉa tāṉiyam. iti māvāka aṟittu atiliruntu uṇavup paṇṭam ceykiṉṟaṉar. itaṉuṭiya ilikaḷik kūṭac camittu uṇkiṉṟaṉar.

kāy kaṟikaḷ

cīmik kāykaṟikaḷ iṅku niṟiyap payir ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kēraṭ, ṭarṉip, takkāḷi, pārciṉip, muṭṭik kōcu, kuvāliḵ piḷavar, pīṭrūṭ, rēṭicas, leṭṭuces, ruparp, ḵpreñcu avari, akaṉṟa avari, veḷḷari, cileri eṉpavi paṭakarkaḷāl niṟiyap payiriṭappaṭṭu nakaraṅkaḷukku aṉuppap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl camaveḷiyāṉa peṅkaḷūrilirukkum tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷōṭu, vāṇipat tuṟiyil ivarkaḷāl pōṭṭiyiṭa muṭiyavilli. mali mītu ivikaḷ viḷivatāl camaveḷiyiluḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu cella niṟiyap poruṭ celavu ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.

nel nīlakiri maliyiṉ oru pakutiyāṉa vayaṉāṭṭil kolli maliyip pōṉṟu nel payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu. atōṭu cāmiyum irākiyum uyarnta mēṭṭu nilaṅkaḷil payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nel tāḻvāṉa pīṭa pūmikaḷil payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu. maliyiliruntu ōṭi varum aruvikaḷ nel vayalkaḷil pāykiṉṟaṉa.

vaṇikap payirkaḷ (Commercial Crops)

nīlakiri maliyil payirttoḻil ceyyappaṭum motta nilapparappil 36% kāḵpit tōṭṭaṅkaḷ. tēyilit tōṭṭam 10-6%. ciṉkōṉā 4-6%. uṇavut tāṉiyaṅkaḷum, paḻattōṭṭaṅkaḷum, kāykaṟit tōṭṭaṅkaḷum mīti nilattil uḷḷaṉa.

kāḵpi

ulakil viḷiyum mottak kāḵpiyil ¾ paṅku pirēcil nāṭṭil viḷikiṟatu. mītik kāl paṅku jāvāvilum, teṉṉintiyāvilum vēṟu cila nāṭukaḷilum viḷikiṟatu. teṉṉintiyāvil viḷiyum kāḵpi, ulakil viḷiyum mottak kāḵpiyil 1 paṅkutāṉ. teṉṉintiyāviṉ motta viḷiccalil pātippaṅku micūr malikaḷilum, maṟupāti ceṉṉi , kuṭaku, tiruvāṅkūr mānilaṅkaḷilum payirākiṉṟaṉa.



1795- ām āṇṭu kolōṉal rīṭ eṉṟa peyar koṇṭa cēlam māvaṭṭat taṇṭalar, tiruppattūril kāḵpi payiriṭṭuc cōtaṉi nikaḻttiṉār. āṉāl am muyaṟci veṟṟiyaḷikkavilli. ṭākṭar puccāṉaṉ eṉpavar ki. pi. 1801-ām āṇṭu telliccēriyil kāḵpip payir naṉṟāka viḷivatik kaṇṭār. ki. pi. 1828-il piruṉ eṉpavar añcarikaṇṭiyiliruntu kāḵpip payirkaḷik koṇṭuvantu maliyāḷa nāṭṭic cērnta vaya nāṭṭil payiriṭṭār. āṉāl 1839-ām āṇṭu variyil aṅku appayirttoḻil ciṟappaṭiyavilli. kūṉūril vāḻnta ṭācaṉ eṉṟa airōppiyar 1838-ām āṇṭu nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyiluḷḷa kālakaṭṭi eṉṟa iṭattil kāḵpip payirkaḷi naṭṭuc cōtaṉi nikaḻttiṉār. accōtaṉi ōraḷavu veṟṟikaramāka muṭintatu. uṭaṉē avuṭṭar lāṉip paḷḷattākkil kāḵpit tōṭṭaṅkaḷ uruppeṟat toṭaṅkiṉa, ki. pi. 1866-67-ām āṇṭukaḷil appaḷḷattil 13,500 ēkar nilam kāḵpit tōṭṭamāka māṟiyatu. 35,000 rāttal kāḵpik koṭṭi aṅkiruntu ēṟṟumatiyāyiṟṟu. nīlakiri maliyiṉ kiḻakku, teṟku, vaṭakiḻakkuc carivukaḷ kāḵpi viḷivataṟku ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu nīlakiri maliyiluḷḷa kāḵpit tōṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ parappu 50,000 ēkarukku mēl irukkum. uyarnta rakak kāḵpiyāṉa arēpikā tāṉ iṅku viḷikiṟatu.

tēyili

intiyāviliruntu ēṟṟumatiyākum tēyiliyil teṉṉintiyāviṉ paṅku mika mikak kuṟivu. atuvum nīlakiriyil viḷiyum tēyiliyiṉ aḷavu motta intiyat tēyiliyil 1% tāṉ. nīlakiriyiluḷḷa tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷ mutaṉ mutalil tēyiliyi virumpip payiriṭṭataṟkuc cila kāraṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. kāḵpiyip pōl tēyili cīkkirattil nōyiṉāl tākkappaṭuvatilli. pūcci puḻukkaḷum kāḵpic ceṭikku aḻivu viḷivippatip pōl tēyilikku viḷivippatilli. ētēṉum ōrāṇṭil kāḵpiyiṉ viḷiccal piṟa nāṭukaḷil mikutiyāka iruntāl, uṭaṉē nam nāṭṭil vili atikamākak kuṟintu viṭum. ataṉāl nam nāṭṭut tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷukkup perum aḷavu poruḷ iḻappu ēṟpaṭum. ivi pōṉṟa tollikaḷ tēyilit tōṭṭakkārarkaḷukku ēṟpaṭuvatilli. perumpālum kāḵpip payirt toḻilil tōlvi kaṇṭavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ kāḵpit tōṭṭaṅkaḷi aḻittuviṭṭut tēyili payiriṭṭaṉar.

nīlakiri maliyiṉ tēyilip payirttoḻil varalāṟu ki. pi. 1833-ām āṇṭiliruntu tuvaṅkukiṟatu. aṅku vāḻnta kirusṭī (Christie) eṉpavar, kūṉūrukkarukil tēyiliyip pōl cuviyuṭiya oruvakic ceṭi niṟiya muḷittiruppatik kaṇṭār. immaliyilum tēyiliyip payiriṭṭāl eṉṉa eṉṟa eṇṇam avarukku ēṟpaṭṭatu. uṭaṉē uyarnta rakamāṉa tēyilic ceṭikaḷic cīṉa nāṭṭiliruntu taruvikka ēṟpāṭu ceytār. āṉāl avikaḷ nīlakiri vantu cērvataṟku muṉpē kirusṭī iṟantu viṭṭār. ciṉāviliruntu taruvikkappaṭṭa ceṭikaḷ nīlakiri maliyiṉ pala pakutiyilirunta makkaḷukkum payiriṭuvataṟkākak koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.

ki. pi. 1835-ām āṇṭu intiya nāṭṭiṉ kavarṉar jeṉaral, oru kuḻuvi ēṟpaṭutti nīlakiri, kuṭaku, micūr mutaliya malikaḷil tēyili payiriṭa muṭiyumā eṉpati aṟivataṟkākak kalkattāviliruntu aṉuppi vittār. cīṉāviliruntu nalla vitikaḷi varavaḻittuc cōtaṉit tōṭṭaṅkaḷil payiriṭṭaṉar. nīlakirikku aṉuppappaṭṭa ceṭikaḷik 'kēti' cōtaṉit tōṭṭattil payiriṭṭaṉar. āṉāl aṭutta āṇṭilēyē ic cōtaṉikaḷ kiviṭappaṭṭuk 'kēti' tōṭṭamum atilirunta kaṭṭiṭamum pāṇṭiccēri kavarṉarukku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. tiruvāḷar em. perrāṭeṭ (M. Perrottet) eṉṟa ḵpireñcut tāvara ārāycciyāḷar at tōṭṭattiṟku vantirunta poḻutu, cōtaṉik kuḻikaḷil naṭappaṭṭirunta tēyilic ceṭikaḷip pārttār. ac ceṭikaḷ pātiyaḷavu pūmikkuḷ putittu naṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivvāṟu naṭappaṭṭataṟkuk kāraṇam, tōṭṭa vēlikkāraṉiṉ aṟiyāmiyē eṉpati uṇarntu, avikaḷiyellām piṭuṅki maṟupaṭiyum ēṟṟa muṟiyil naṭṭu vaḷarttār. 1838-ām āṇṭu akṭōpar tiṅkaḷ ac ceṭikaḷ nāṉkaṭi uyarattiṟku vaḷarntu, ceḻippāṉa ilikaḷit taḷirttaṉa. pūkkaḷum, kāykaḷum, paḻaṅkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. uṭaṉē ic ceytiyippaṟṟi oru kaṭṭuri varintu, pattirikiyil veḷiyiṭṭār. ik kaṭṭuri ceṉṉi mānilattiluḷḷa ellāt tōṭṭakkārarkaḷiṉ kavaṉattiyum kavarnta ki. pi: 1840-ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar callivaṉ kēti tōṭṭattilum pillikal tōṭṭattilum viḷinta tēyiliyiṉ mātirikaḷi, ceṉṉiyiluḷḷa 'payirt toḻil vaḷarccik kaḻakat'tiṟku aṉuppi vittār. avaṟṟiliruntu iṟakkiya tēnīrik kuṭittu makiḻnta avarkaḷellām avaṟṟiṉ cuviyip pārāṭṭiṉar.

piṟaku kūṉūril vāḻnta tiruvāḷar māṉ (Mr. Man) eṉṟa veḷḷiyar ki. pi. 1854-il cīṉāviliruntu uyarnta rakamāṉa ceṭikaḷi varavaḻittu, pala iṉṉalkaḷukkiṭiyē avvūrukkarukiluḷḷa ōriṭattit tērnteṭuttup payiriṭṭār. ippōtu avviṭam. 'kūṉūr tēyilit tōṭṭam' (Coonoor Tea Estate) eṉṟa peyarāl aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. ki. pi. 1856 il māṉ avarkaḷāl payiriṭappaṭṭa tēyili mikavum ciṟantateṉṟu ilaṇṭaṉ tēyili viyāpārikaḷāl pārāṭṭappaṭṭatu. āṉāl tam viḷiccali atikappaṭutta avar virumpavilli. kāraṇam payiriṭum nilattiṟkākak kāṭukaḷi aḻippatu avarukku mikavum tolliyāṉa vēliyākappaṭṭatu.

ṭākṭar kḷek hārṉ (Dr, Cleghorn) eṉpavar, tēyili payiriṭuvatil nalla aṉupavamuḷḷa cila cīṉa uḻavarkaḷi vaṭa intiyāviliruntu nīlakirikkut taruvittu utavumāṟu ceṉṉi kavarṉarukku vēṇṭu kōḷ viṭuttār. āṉāl avaruṭiya vēṇṭukōḷ araciyalārāl maṟukkappaṭṭatu. piṟaku tiru. rē (Mr. Rae) eṉpār cōlūruk karukil araciyalāriṭamiruntu nilattip peṟṟut tēyilit tōṭṭatti niṟuviṉār. atu ippoḻutu 'ṭaṉcēṇṭal tōṭṭam' (Dunsandle Estate) eṉṟa peyarāl aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. kōtakiriyil oru tōṭṭamum, utaka maṇṭalattil 'pelmāṇṭ' (Belmont) eṉṟa tōṭṭamum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.

ki. pi. 1863--64 ām āṇṭukaḷil ceṉṉi kavarṉarāka irunta villiyam ṭeṉṉicaṉ eṉpavar vaṭamēṟku mānilaṅkaḷiliruntu tēyili payiriṭuvatil nalla payiṟcipeṟṟa tōṭṭa vēlikkārarkaḷi varavaḻittu nīlakirikku aṉuppit tēyilip payirt toḻil vaḷarcciyil ciṟitu akkaṟi kāṭṭiṉār. atōṭu uyarnta vitikaḷit taruvittum vaḻaṅkiṉār . ki. pi. 1869 ām āṇṭu ēṟakkuṟiya 300 ēkar nilam tēyilit tōṭṭaṅkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭiruntatu. avvāṇṭu naṭanta payirt toḻil kaṇkāṭciyil 18 tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷ taṅkaḷ tōṭṭattil viḷin tirunta tēyilikaḷip pārvikku vittiruntaṉar.



piṟaku paṭippaṭiyākat tēyilip payirttoḻil vaḷarntu nalla niliyaṭintatu. payirāṉa tēyiliyi viṟpataṟku vēṇṭiya ēṟpāṭukaḷellām ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. taṟpōtu, nīlakirit tēyili iṅkilāntu nāṭṭiṟkup perum aḷavu ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭukiṟatu.

nīlakiriyil viḷiyum tēyili mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. aviyāvaṉa (1) tūymiyāṉa cīṉat tēyili. it tēyili mikavum uṟutiyāṉatāka irukkum (2) assāmil perum aḷavu viḷiyum nāṭṭut tēyili. itu 25 allatu 30 aṭi uyaram vaḷarum taṉmiyuṭiyatu. (3) cīṉa iṉattiyum, nāṭṭu iṉattiyum kalantu uṇṭākkappaṭṭa tēyili (Hybrid). itu cīṉat tēyiliyippōl irumaṭaṅku ilikaḷik koṇṭiruppatōṭu, ataṉ uṟutiyiyum peṟṟirukkiṟatu.

ciṉkōṉā

nīlakiri maliyil payiriṭappaṭum ciṉkōṉā maraṅkaḷ mukkiyamākak koyṉā maruntu ceyvataṟkum vēṟucila maruntukaḷ ceyvataṟkum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṉkōṉāviṉ paṭṭiyiliruntu eṭukkappaṭum koyṉā, malēriyā nōykku arumaruntāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ippōtu utakamaṇṭala vaṭṭattil 4000 ēkar nilattil ciṉkōṉā payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu. it tōṭṭaṅkaḷil perumpakuti araciyalārukkuc contamāṉatu. itaṟkeṉṟu oru taṉi ilākā (Chincona Department)vē araciyalārāl naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. mikavum malivāṉa viliyil koyṉāvit tayārittu nāṭṭu makkaḷukkuk koṭukka vēṇṭum eṉpatē ivvilākā viṉ nōkkam.

ciṉkōṉā maram teṉṉamerikkak kāṭukaḷukkē urittāṉa payir, aṅkiruntu ulakiṉ maṟṟa pakutikaḷukku ippayir paraviyatu. itu ulakil paravuvataṟku muṉpu teṉṉamerikkac cevvintiyarkaḷukku itaṉ payaṉ teriyumā eṉpatu aiyappāṭē. koyṉā (Quinine) eṉṟa collāṉatu cevvintiya moḻic collāṉa kviṉā-kviṉā (Quina-Quina) eṉpatiliruntu peṟappaṭṭatākum. kviṉā eṉṟāl paṭṭi eṉṟu poruḷ.

ki. pi. 1640 il 'perū' nāṭṭiṉ aracappiratinitiyāka irunta airōppiyariṉ maṉiviyāṉa ciṉkōṉ cīmāṭṭi (Countess of Chinchon)yum, avaruṭiya yūta naṇparkaḷumē ciṉkōṉā maram ulakil paravuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ. ki. pi. 1846-kku mēl tāṉ immaram airōppāvil payiriṭappaṭṭatu. iti nam nāṭṭukkuk koṇṭuvarap perumuyaṟci eṭuttukkoṇṭavar tiruvāḷar ṭalhuci pirapu. ciṉkōṉāc ceṭikaḷi intiyāviṟkuk koṇṭu varuvataṟkāka avar ki. pi. 1852 il oru kuḻuvit teṉṉamerikkāviṟku aṉuppiṉār. āṉāl avarkaḷ koṇṭuvanta ciṉkōṉāk kaṉṟukaḷ varum vaḻiyilēyē karukiviṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu pala taṭavi eṭuttukkoṇṭa muyaṟcikaḷ vīṇāyiṉa.

ciṉkōṉā payiriṭuvatil payiṟci peṟṟa tiruvāḷar mārkkam (Mr. Markham) eṉpavar ki. pi. 1860 ām āṇṭu akṭōpar tiṅkaḷ nīlakirikku vantār. āṉāl avar varuvataṟku muṉpāka, utaka maṇṭalattiluḷḷa aracāṅkat tōṭṭattiṉ pātukāvalarāka irunta ṭapḷ yū. ji. mekkalvar (W. G. Mclvor) eṉpār toṭṭa poṭṭāviṟkarukiluḷḷa paḷḷattākkukaḷil ōr iṭattit tērnteṭuttuc ciṉ kōṉā payiriṭa ārampittār. tiru. mārkkam avviṭattip pārviyiṭṭār. avviṭam ōraḷavu takutiyāṉatē eṉṟālum, pōtiya īramum veppamum illi eṉṟu karuti pīṭapūmiyiṉ mēṟku elliyiluḷḷa naṭuvaṭṭam eṉṉum iṭattiṟkarukil ciṉkōṉā payiriṭat toṭaṅkiṉār. piṟaku pala iṭaṅkaḷil it tōṭṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟat toṭaṅki vaḷarcciyuṟṟaṉa. ippoḻutu ippayirttoḻil kāḵpikku aṭuttāṟpōl varuvāy taruvatāka uḷḷatu.

irappar :

kāḵpi, tēyili pōṉṟu irappar mikunta varuvāy aḷippatāka nīlakiriyil illi . ki. pi. 1882-ām āṇṭu irappar vayanāṭṭilum kōtakiriyilum mutaṉ mutalākap payiriṭappaṭṭatu. kuraṅkukaḷālum, paṉṟikaḷālum tōṭṭakkārarkaḷiṉ kavaṉak kuṟivālum irappar payirttoḻil appoḻutu veṟṟikaramāka naṭipeṟavilli. cērvarāyaṉ maliyil irappar payiriṭutali veṟṟikaramākac ceytu kāṇpittut taṅkappatakkam peṟṟa nikkalcaṉippaṟṟi muṉpē kūṟiṉōm. avar cērvarāyaṉ maliyiliruntu irappar ceṭikaḷik koṇṭu vantu kōtakiriyil tamakkuc contamāyirunta kiḷeṉparṉ tōṭṭattil payiriṭṭār, ataṉ piṟaku pala tōṭṭakkārarkaḷ nīlakiriyil kuṟinta aḷavu irappar payiriṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. ki. pi. 1902-ām āṇṭu ēṟakkuṟiya 1200 ēkar nilaṅkaḷ irappar tōṭṭaṅkaḷāka māṟiṉa. teṉṉintiyāvil kocci, āṉimali, cērvarāyaṉ mali, paḻaṉimali, maliyāḷam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil irappar tōṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyil kuṟippiṭattakkatu kiḷaṉrāk irappar kampaṉi (glem Rock Rubber Company). itu paṇṭalūruk karukil uḷḷatu.

paḻavakikaḷ :

airōppiyarkaḷ nīlakiriyil kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅkiyatum, mēl nāṭṭup paḻavakikaḷi aṅku niṟiyap payiriṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. araciyalār cōtaṉit tōṭṭaṅkaḷi niṟuvi, uyarntarakap paḻaṅkaḷip payiriṭuvataṟkāṉa vaḻivakikaḷi ārāyntaṉar. ittoḻil ippoḻutu mikkavaruvāy aḷikkum oṉṟāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. āppiḷ, pēri, cīmimātuḷi (Quince), cīmik kovvi (peach), tirāṭci , peruntirāṭci (Apricot), pḷam tirāṭci , {plum), cerri, kūsperri, muḷḷi (Rasp-berry), sṭrā perri, malperri, atti (fig), koyyā, ārañcu, elumicci , nekṭariṉ, percimmaṉ, cerimāyar mutaliya paḻaṅkaḷ niṟiya viḷikiṉṟaṉa.

moḻikaḷ :

kōvittamiḻum, kēraḷattu maliyāḷamum, micūrk kaṉṉaṭamum oṉṟu cērum iṭattil nīlakiri maliyāṉatu uḷḷatu. ākiyāl im mūṉṟu moḻikaḷiṉ citivukaḷē {dialects) immali vāḻ makkaḷāl pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ, paṭakā, maliyāḷam, teluṅku, kuṟumpā, intustāṉi, āṅkilam ākiyavi iṅku vaḻaṅkum mukkiya moḻikaḷ. paṭakā citinta kaṉṉaṭam. iṅkuvāḻum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāl pēcappaṭum tutam (Toda language) mutaliya moḻikaḷ yāvum kālṭu velliṉ kūṟṟuppaṭi, tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉattic cārntavi.

camayam :

intu, pārci, iculām, kiṟittavam ākiya nāṉku camayaṅkaḷē immaliyil vāḻum makkaḷāl kaṭippiṭittu oḻukap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. rōmaṉ kētalik, carc āḵp iṅkilāntu, amekariṉ micaṉ, ciṉāṉā micaṉ, pācil lutaraṉ micaṉ ākiyakiṟittava neṟiyiṉar iṅkut tattamakkuriya kōyilkaḷi amittuk koṇṭu, camaya vaḷarcciyil īṭupaṭṭirukkiṟaṉar.



makakḷ

tamiḻ vēḷāḷar, tamiḻp paṟiyar, teluṅkar, paṭakar, kōtar, tōṭar, iruḷar, kuṟumpar vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭimār, paṉiyar, ciṟupāṉmi veḷḷiyar ākiyōrē nīlakiri malimītu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.

paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ :

nīlakiri maliyil vāḻum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ. mēli nāṭṭiliruntum, intiya nāṭṭiṉ pala pakutikaḷiliruntum, paḻaṅkuṭi ārāycci (Anthropology) kkākap palar iṅku vantu kūṭiya vaṇṇamirukkiṉṟaṉar. intiya nāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟum enta nūlum, tōṭarkaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭāmaliruppatilli. tōṭarkaḷit tavira, nīlakiriyil vāḻum maṟṟa iṉattavarellām ōraḷavu nākarikam peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl tōṭarkaḷ maṭṭum iyaṟkiyōṭu paṭinta vāḻkkiyilēyē iṉpam kāṇkiṉṟaṉar.

paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷippaṟṟi, ellā nāṭukaḷilum pēcap paṭukiṟatu. viññāṉa nākarittiṉ aṭippaṭiyil iyaṅkum nakara vāḻviviṭa, kāṭukaḷilum malikaḷilum kavaliyaṟṟu vāḻum iyaṟki vāḻvē uyarnta teṉṟum pēcappaṭukiṟatu. paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷukku aṇukkuṇṭip paṟṟiyō, nīrvaḷik kuṇṭip paṟṟiyō, paṟakkum kuṇṭip paṟṟiyō teriyātu , kavali kiṭiyātu. araciyal, poruḷiyal, camūkaviyal, toḻil vaḷarcci, kalvi ākiyavaṟṟip paṟṟiyum avarkaḷ kavalip paṭuvatilli, nāḷi uṇavukku eṉṉa ceyvatu? eṉṟu kavaliyōṭu kaṇtuyilac cellum iḻinili avarkaḷiṭam illi. iyaṟki aḷikkum vaḷattip pakuttuṇṭu potu uṭami vāḻvu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭita nēru, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷip paṟṟip piṉvarumāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār:-



"camaveḷiyil vāḻum nākarika makkaḷiṭattil kāṇa muṭiyāta pala arum paṇpukaḷi nāṉ avarkaḷiṭam kāṇkiṟēṉ. ap paṇpukaḷ eṉṉuḷḷattip peritum kavarkiṉṟaṉa. 'paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ veṟiyuṇarcci mikkavarkaḷ ; kolikkañcāk koṭiyavarkaḷ ; ciṉaṅkoṇṭa poḻutu piṟar cirattik koyyac ciṟitum tayaṅkātavarkaḷ' eṉṟellām kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl ic ceyalkaḷ ellām, camaveḷiyil vāḻum nākarika maṉitaṉ ceyyum akkiramaṅkaḷi viṭak koṭumiyil kuṟintaviyē! piṟaruṭiya uḷḷatti ēṟi mitittu nacukkuvativiṭat taliyi veṭṭuvatu evvaḷavō mēl eṉṟu nāṉ karutukiṟēṉ.

"avarkaḷ oḻuṅkiṟkum kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟkum uṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ. pārāḷu maṉṟamum caṭṭa capiyum avarkaḷukku illi. āṉāl jaṉanāyakatti nammiviṭa nalla muṟiyil uṇarntavarkaḷ. āṭiyum pāṭiyum vāḻkkiyi iṉpattōṭu vāḻak kaṟṟavarkaḷ. āṉāl nāmō, nākarikattiṉ ucciyil vīṟṟiruppatākap perumi pēcikkoṇṭu, piṟari iḻitturippavarkaḷ ; piṟar ākkaṅkaṇṭu poṟāmippaṭupavarkaḷ, ittakiya nākarikattiyā nām paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷukku aḷikka vēṇṭum? oruvaṉi oruvaṉ iḻittup pēcuvatik kaliyākak koṇṭu vāḻum nākarika camutāyattiviṭṭu vilaki, oru paḻaṅkuṭi makaṉākak kāṭukaḷil tirivatip peritum virumpukiṟēṉ," eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṟār.

paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷippaṟṟi iraṇṭu vitak koḷkikaḷ ulakil ulavukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷik kaṇkaḷukkum, karuttukkum viruntaḷikkum kāṭcip poruḷākavum, ārāyccikkuriya karupporuḷākavum appaṭiyē kāṭukaḷil viṭṭuvittirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu oru koḷki. camaveḷiyil paraviyirukkum viññāṉa nākarikatti valiyap pukutti, avarkaḷiyum nam vaḻikkuk koṇṭu varavēṇṭum eṉpatu maṟṟoru koḷki. ki. pi. 1936-ām āṇṭu airōppiya nāṭṭuc caṭṭacapi oṉṟil, "paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷi ārāycciyāḷarukkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil, poruṭkāṭci cālikaḷil ēṉ aṭittu vikka lākātu?” eṉṟu ōr uṟuppiṉar kēṭṭār. ac ceyal maṉitat taṉmikkup puṟampāṉatu eṉṟu vēṟu pala uṟuppiṉarkaḷāl kaṇṭikkappaṭṭuk kiviṭappaṭṭatu.

patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil iṅkilāntil vāḻnta perum pulavaṉāṉa jāṉcaṉum, avaṉ naṇpaṉ pāsvellum, 'paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ, paṇpaṭṭa makkaḷ ākiya iruvar vāḻkkiyil etu uyarntatu?' eṉpatu paṟṟik kāracāramāṉa uriyāṭal nikaḻtti yirukkiṉṟaṉar. ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟa nāṭakap pulavarāṉa cēkspiyarkūṭa, tām eḻutiyuḷḷa puyal (Tempest) eṉṟa nāṭakattil kālipaṉ, pirāsp perō, sṭīḵpeṉō. ṭiriṅkulō, antōṇiyō, ālaṉcō eṉṟa pāttiraṅkaḷiṉ mūlam paḻaṅkuṭi vāḻkkiyiyum nākarika vāḻkkiyiyum viḷakkik koṇṭu cellukiṟār. kālipaṉip paṟṟit tiṟaṉāyvāḷar (critics) kuṟippiṭumpōtu, “avaṉ oru maṭiyaṉ ; veṟi koṇṭavaṉ. tīṅkiḻikkum paṇpu avaṉ uḷḷattil ūṟiyatu. avaṉip paṇpaṭṭavaṉāka ākkap pirāspperō evvaḷavō muyaṉṟāṉ, am muyaṟci kālipaṉ uḷḷattil poṟāmi, taṉ tāḻnta nili kaṇṭu atirupti, mirāntāviṉ mēl capalam, etirppuṇarcci, kuṭiveṟi ākiya tīppaṇpukaḷiyē kiḷappiviṭṭatu' eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.



āṉāl cēkspiyar ak kāṭṭu maṉitaṉiṭamirunta ariya paṇpukaḷiyum pulappaṭuttukiṟār. kālipaṉ icippiriyaṉ; kāṭṭu moḻiyil kaviti pāṭum pulavaṉ. cila camayaṅkaḷil aḻakuṇarcciyōṭum pēcukiṟāṉ. āṉāl nākarikattiṉ piratinitikaḷāka at tīvil pukuntavarkaḷ - avaṉiviṭa uḷḷattāl iḻintavarkaḷ, sṭīḵpaṉōvum, ṭiriṅkulōvum kuṭikārak kōmāḷikaḷ, antōṇiyō vañcakaṉ. ālaṉcōviṉ vāḻkki kaṟipaṭintatu. in nākarikarkaḷ, paḻaṅkuṭi makaṉāṉa vālipaṉik kaḷ veṟiyaṉākkip piṟaku naṉṟi keṭṭavaṉāka māṟṟiṉar.

patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta rūcō, "paḻami vāḻvē paṇpuṭi vāḻvu; avviyaṟki vāḻvi nōkkic ceṉṟāl tāṉ etirkāla ulakam amitiyōṭum iṉpattōṭum vāḻa muṭiyum“ eṉṟa koḷkiyip parappa ōr iyakkamē (Back to Nature Movement) naṭattiṉār. ivvāṟu paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷip paṟṟic cuviyāṉa pēccukkaḷ paṉṉip paṉṉip pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

paṭakar :

nīlakiriyil vāḻum veḷḷiyar ivarkaḷip parkar (Burgher) eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. vaṭakkiluḷḷa micūriliruntu vantatāl ivarkaḷ vaṭakar eṉṟu peyar peṟṟaṉar eṉṟum, appeyarē paṭakar eṉat tirintatu eṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. micūr nāṭṭil ēṟpaṭṭa pañcam kāraṇamākavum, araciyalil koṇṭa karuttu vēṟupāṭṭiṉ kāraṇamākavum, camutāyak koṭumi kāraṇamākavum, micūril vāḻum liṅkāyattāriṭam koṇṭa camayappūcal kāraṇamākavum ivarkaḷ iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiṉarākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu, tōṭarkaḷiṭiyē vaḻaṅkum paramparik katikaḷil paṭakarip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ etuvum illāta kāraṇattāl, ivarkaḷ tōṭarkaḷukkuppiṉ iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu.

paṭakarkaḷ uḻavut toḻilil vallavarkaḷ. nīlakiriyil uḷḷa payirttoḻil muḻukka muḻukkap paṭakarkaḷiṉ uḻippiyē nampi naṭipeṟukiṟatu. nīlakiriyiṉ kiḻakkuppātiyil ivarkaḷ niṟiya vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. kuntā pīṭa pūmiyil, tōṭṭak kūlikaḷāka niṟiya pēr paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar. cilar taṅkaḷukkurimiyāṉa nilaṅkaḷil payirt toḻil purintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. tōṭṭaṅkaḷil uḻuvatit tavira maṟṟa ellā vēlikaḷiyum peṇkaḷē ceytu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. nakaraṅkaḷil kūlivēli ceyvataṟkāka āṇkaḷ niṟiyapēr varukiṉṟaṉar. paṭakarkaḷ cilar koṭṭukkārarākavum (Artisans), taccarākavum, nāvitarākavum, vaṇṇārākavum taṅkaḷ iṉattārkkup paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar. koraliyum, cāmiyum ivarkaḷuṭiya mukkiya uṇavup poruḷ. uṇavup poruḷ payiriṭṭatu pōka eñciya nilaṅkaḷil uruḷikkiḻaṅku, kucci vaḷḷikkiḻaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟip payiriṭṭu vilikku viṟkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ parampariyākap parama ēḻikaḷ. airōppiyar varukikkup piṟaku, ivarkaḷuṭiya vāḻkkittaram paṭippaṭiyāka uyarntu varukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ iyaṟkiyilēyē añcum iyalpuṭivarkaḷ. pēcum poḻutu mikavum paṇivōṭum aṭakkattōṭum pēcukiṉṟaṉar. iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiya kālattiliruntu tōṭarkaḷukku añci, avarkaḷukku aṭaṅkiyavarkaḷākavē vāḻntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. ippoḻutu kūṭat taṅkaḷ viḷiccaliṉ orupakutiyi āṇṭutōṟum avarkaṭku vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar.

uruvam

nīlakiri malimēl ceṉṟavuṭaṉ eḷitil paṭakarkaḷi aṟintu koḷḷalām. tirumpiya pakkamellām avarkaḷuṭiya koccik kaṉṉaṭam nam kātit tuḷikkum. ciṟiya uruvamum, olliyāṉa uṭaṟkaṭṭum, mā niṟamāṉa mēṉiyum uṭiyavarkaḷ. āṇkaḷ vaṇṇak kariyiṭṭa veḷḷāṭiyi iṭuppil uṭuttuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. mēlē cokkāyaṇintu, ataṟku mēl taṭitta kōṭṭu (waist coat) aṇi kiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ mārpi mūṭumpaṭi akkuḷic cuṟṟik kuṟukalāṉa mēlāṭi yaṇikiṉṟaṉar. kīḻē cēli uṭuttuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. taliyil eppoḻutum oru tuṇiyic cuṟṟik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum eppōtum malarnta mukattuṭaṉ kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉar.

pirivukaḷ

paṭakarkaḷ āṟu piriviṉarākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. uṭiyār, āruvā, atikāri, kaṉakar, paṭakar, toriyar eṉpaviyē appirivukaḷ. uṭiyār piriviṉar maṟṟa aivariyumviṭa uyarntavarkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟu kūṭa micūr nāṭṭil vāḻum celvarkaḷ uṭiyār eṉṟa peyarāl taṅkaḷi aḻittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. micūr maṉṉar kūṭa uṭiyār kulattic cērntavar eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. toriyar maṟṟa ellāp piriviṉariyumviṭat tāḻntavarkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟap piriviṉarkku ivarkaḷ aṭimi vēli ceytu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. āruvā eṉṉum piriviṉar maṟṟiyōrkkup pārppaṉat toḻil purintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. paṭakarkaḷ micūriliruntu iṅkuk kuṭi pukunta poḻutu, uṭaṉvanta pārppaṉarkaḷē ippiriviṉar eṉṟu. karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.

pūppeytal

oru peṇ pūppeytiyatum, vīṭṭiliruntu vilakki, avaḷi oru taṉikkuṭiciyil paṭakarkaḷ vittuviṭuvārkaḷ. aṭutta uvānāḷ (peḷarṇami) vari ap peṇ akkuṭiciyilēyē irukkavēṇṭum. maṟṟa vīṭṭup peṇkaḷellām nāḷtōṟum akkuṭicikku māvu koṇṭu ceṉṟu, uṇavu camittu oruṅkiruntu uṇpar. uvā nāḷaṉṟu vīṭṭukkaḻikkum caṭaṅku naṭipeṟum. aṉṟu appeṇṇi nīril kuḷippāṭṭuvar. puttāṭi aṇivittu vīṭṭiṟku veḷiyil uṭkāra vippar. vāṉattil muḻu nilā tōṉṟiyatum, aintu peṇkaḷ kūṭi appeṇṇi vīṭṭiṟkuḷ aḻittuc celvar. tāy makaḷi varavēṟṟu, "nalla vīṭṭiyum, aṉpuḷḷa kaṇavaṉiyum āṟṟalmikka makaṉiyum peṟuvāyāka“ eṉṟu vāḻttuvāḷ. uḷḷē camittu vikkappaṭṭa uṇavi appeṇṇukkup paṭippar. avvuṇavil ciṟitaḷavu uṇṭu, mītiyi aṇṭi vīṭukaḷiluḷḷa mutiya peṇkaḷiṭam eṭuttucceṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvāḷ. akkiḻaviyarum, tāy muṉpu vāḻttiyatu pōlavē vāḻttuvar.

tirumaṇam

paṭakarkaḷ vīṭṭil oru peṇ piṟantavuṭaṉ, 10 rūpāy paricamāka vāṅkikkoṇṭu, yārāvatu ōr uṟaviṉarukku akkuḻantiyi marumakaḷāka viṟṟu viṭuvatu vaḻakkam. paruvam eytum variyilum ap peṇ tāy vīṭṭilēyē iruppāḷ. piṟaku kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭiṟku aṉuppiviṭuvārkaḷ. ciṟitu kālattukku muṉ variyil paṭakarkaḷiṭiyē kātal maṇamtāṉ peru vaḻakkāka iruntatu. uṭiyār piriviṉar maṭṭum kātal maṇatti ātarippatilli. ōr iḷiñaṉ oru peṇṇi virumpukiṟāṉeṉṟāl, uṭaṉē taṉ peṟṟāēriṉ opputalōṭu cila paricup poruḷkaḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭu appeṇṇiṉ vīṭṭukkuc celluvāṉ. appeṇṇiṉ peṟṟāērukku ap paricup poruḷkaḷi vaḻaṅkuvāṉ. piṟaku cila nāḷ avarkaḷ vīṭṭilēyē taṅki, appeṇṇōṭu uḷaṅkalantu paḻakuvāṉ. entavitak kaṭṭuppāṭum avarkaḷukkuk kiṭiyātu. kaṭiciyil iruvarum oruvariyoruvar virumpi maṉamotta kātal koṇṭāl tirumaṇam naṭipeṟum. āṉāl im muṟi cila viparītamāṉa muṭivukaḷiyum koṇṭuvantu viṭukiṟatu. cila iḷiñarkaḷ immuṟiyit taṅkaḷ poḻutupōkkiṟkuriya karuviyākak karutat toṭaṅkiṉar. "poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar, aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa“ eṉṟa tolkāppiyac caṭṭam iṅkum amulākkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvaḻakkam ciṟitu ciṟitākat tēyntu varukiṟatu. caṭṭa varampiṟ kuṭpaṭṭa tirumaṇam malarkiṟatu.

tirumaṇam eppoḻutum, maṇamakaṉ illattilēyē naṭipeṟukiṟatu. caṭaṅkukaḷ atikamāka oṉṟum illi. maṇappeṇṇik kuṭattil nīr koṇṭuvarac colluvārkaḷ, avvāṟu ceyvataṉ mūlam, appeṇ kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭup poṟuppi ēṟṟuk koḷvatākap poruḷām. accaṭaṅku muṭintatum maṇappeṇ, māppiḷḷi vīṭṭiluḷḷa ellāriyum vaṇaṅkavēṇṭum. kōtarkaḷiṉ ici muḻaṅka ellāruṭiya muṉṉiliyilum peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil maṇamakaṉ tālikaṭṭuvāṉ. paricappaṇam māppiḷḷi vīṭṭāriṉ caktikkut takuntapaṭi koṭukkappaṭum. 200 rūpāy variyil paricappaṇam koṭuppatuṇṭu. ‘kūṟippaṇam' eṉṟu oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokiyi māppiḷḷi vīṭṭār peṇṇukkaḷippatu vaḻakkam.

maṇavilakku : paṭakarkaḷiṭiyē maṇavilakku atikamākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭār tirumaṇattiṉpōtu koṭutta paricattokiyiyum, kūṟippaṇattiyum tiruppik koṭuttuviṭṭāl, eppoḻutum maṇavilakkup peṟṟukkoḷḷal eḷitu. oru peṇ karuvuṟṟavaḷāka iruntāl, pañcāyattār muṉṉiliyil ēḻām mātac caṭaṅki (cīmantam) nikaḻttiya piṟakē, maṇavilakku aḷikkappaṭum. piṟakkum kuḻanti kaṇavaṉukkē uriyatu. oru paṭakar kulappeṇ vāḻkkiyil tiruptiyum, amitiyum koḷḷum variyilum ettaṉi muṟi vēṇṭumāṉālum maṇavilakkup peṟṟukkoḷḷalām. itaṉāl peṇkaḷiṉ oḻukkam cīrkeṭukiṟatu eṉpatu uṇmiyē. paṭakarkaḷ atip poruṭpaṭuttuvatilli. āṉāl oru peṇ vēṟu cātiyiṉariṭam toṭarpu koṇṭāl, acceyal mikavum iḻintatākavum, peruṅ kuṟṟattiṉpāṟ paṭṭatākavum karutappaṭukiṟatu. mikavum kaṭumiyāka appeṇ taṇṭikkappaṭukiṟāḷ. cila camayaṅkaḷil koli kūṭac ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉar.

iṟutic caṭaṅku :

paṭakarkaḷiṉ iṟuticcaṭaṅku nikaḻccikaḷ mikavum cuviyāṉavi. iṟakkum taruvāyil iruppavaṉ vāyil ‘vīrarāyappaṇam' eṉṟa ciṟiya taṅka nāṇayattip pōṭṭu nīrūṟṟi viḻuṅkumāṟu ceyvar. ip paṇattiṉ matippu nāṉkaṇātāṉ. yārēṉum oru paṭakaṉ iṟantavuṭaṉ acceytiyi oru toriyaṉ mūlamāka aṇṭiyiluḷḷa ciṟṟūrkaḷukkut teriyappaṭuttuvar. toriyaṉ ic ceytiyip piṟariṭattil kūṟumpōtu, talippākiyik kaḻaṟṟiviṭṭup paṇivōṭu kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. piṇattit tiṟanta veḷiyil oru kaṭṭilil kiṭattuvar. erumi oṉṟip piṇatticcuṟṟi mūṉṟu muṟi valamāka iḻuttuvaruvar. piṟaku piṇattiṉ kiyit tūkki erumiyiṉ kompiṉmēl vippar. pāṭi tēr pōl alaṅkarikkappaṭukiṟatu. celvarkaḷāka iruntāl pala māṭikaḷōṭu alaṅkāramākat tēr ceyvar. tērip potuvākat tuṇikaḷālēyē alaṅkarippar. piṇattiṟkup puttāṭi aṇivittut tēril kiṭattuvar. piṇattiṉ neṟṟiyil iraṇṭu veḷḷip paṇaṅkaḷip patittu vippar. iṟantavaṉuṭiya cuṟṟattārkaḷ piṇattic cuṟṟi niṉṟu kūccaliṭṭu aḻuvārkaḷ. piṇattiṟku iṟuti vaṇakkam celuttuvārkaḷ. āṇkaḷellām arikki mēluṭiyum, putumātiriyāṉa talippākiyum aṇintukoṇṭu kōtarkaḷiṉ iṉṉicik kēṟpa āṭuvārkaḷ. erumik kompiṉāl amikkappaṭṭa piṭiyōṭu kūṭiya arivāḷ, ciṟu kōṭari, pullāṅkuḻal, ūṉṟu kōl ākiya poruḷkaḷi ācāri ceytu koṇṭu vantu, piṇattiṉarukil tēr mītu vippāṉ. aṭutta ulakil avaṉukkup payaṉpaṭuvataṟkāka akkaruvikaḷ vikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉapōlum. cuṭukāṭṭukkuc ceṉṟatum tēri aḻittuviṭuvar. iṟantavaṉ maṉivi taṉ nakikaḷik kaḻaṟṟip piṇattiṟkarukil vittuviṭṭut tirumpuvāḷ. oru mutiyōṉ. iṟantavaṉ taliyarukil niṉṟu avaṉ ceyta pāvaṅkaḷippaṟṟi nīṇṭa oru pāṭṭup pāṭuvāṉ. appāṭṭup piṉvarumāṟu :

āṇṭiyiṉ cāvi aṟiyak kēṭpīr!

māṇṭa kaṉṟiṉ māttali yōṭu

pūṇṭa ivaṉpavam pūṇṭōṭu oḻika;

maṇtiṇi ñāla vāḻvati viṭṭu

viṇ taṉil vāḻa virikiṟāṉ tēril,

viyakat tiṉilavaṉ ceytavi ellām

pāvam pāvam tīrāp pāvam!

peṟṟōr ceyta perumpā vattiyē

maṟṟavar tamakku muṟṟavē ceytāṉ ;

muṉṉōr ceyta muṭivilpā vattiyē

piṉṉōṉ ivaṉum pēṇiyē ceytāṉ ; iṉattavar ceyta iḻiceyal ellām

maṉattiṉil koṇṭu maṭṭiṉṟic ceytāṉ; poyyum vaḻuvum kiyaka vippu :





ceyyiṉ elli tiruṭiṉāṉ

ivaṉē. eḷiyōr tammiyum ēḻiyar tammiyum nalivē purintu naṭuṅkac ceytāṉ ;

aṇṭiyar nilattil maṇṭiya payirik

kaṇṭatum uḷattil koṇṭaṉaṉ poṟāmi ; iṟutic caṭaṅkil iyaṟṟiya kaṉṟik

karutic ceṉṟē kiyakam koṇṭāṉ ;

mēlō riṭattil kōḷaṉāyc ceṉṟē

piṟarpaḻi tūṟṟum pētiyāy vāḻntāṉ ; pāmpik koṉṟāṉ : pacuvik koṉṟāṉ ;

.....﻿....﻿....﻿.....

ippaṭi yāka ivaṉpavam nīḷum;

muṉṉūṟu pālam muṟṟavē ceytāṉ ;

paliyiṭap paṭumik kaṉṟiṉ uyirōṭu

vaḷiyiṉil puḻutiyāy ivaṉpavam tolika ; kaṉṟiṉ kālik kiyiṉil paṟṟi

maṉṟil āṭum maṅki pākaṉ

niṉṟiv vulakam kākkum

naṉṟuṭi patiyē ceṉṟivaṉ cērka!





pāṭal muṭintatum ōr erumik kaṉṟip piṇattiṉarukil niṟuttik kōṭariyiṉ piṉpuṟattāl tākkik kolluvar. iṟantavaṉ ceyta pāvaṅkaḷiyellām, paliyiṭappaṭṭa akkaṉṟu ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟatām. ivaṉ taṉ pāvaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum nīṅkic civaṉulaki aṭikiṉṟāṉām, yūtarkaḷiṭam paṇṭik kālattil ittakiya nampikki iruntatāka viviliya nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu. yūtarkaḷ iṟantavaṉ pāvatti āṭṭiṉ (Scape goat) taliyil ēṟṟik koṉṟaṉar. nīlakiri maliyil veḷḷiyarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiyatum, ip paḻakkattit taṭuttu niṟuttiviṭṭaṉar. ippaḻakkam ippoḻutu anēkamāka illi eṉṟu kūṟalām.

paṭakarkaḷ piṇatti erippatum uṇṭu ; putippatum uṇṭu. putikkappaṭṭa piṇattiṟkē 'pāl teḷittal' eṉṉum caṭaṅku naṭipeṟum, erikkap paṭṭa piṇattiṉ elumpukaḷip poṟukki, avikaḷip pōṭuvataṟkeṉṟu ovvōr ūrilum tōṇṭappaṭṭuḷḷa kuḻiyil pōṭṭuviṭṭut tirumpuvārkaḷ. uṭiyār piriviṉar maṭṭum, camaveḷiyil vāḻum liṅkāyattārkaḷippōl uṭkāra vittup putippar.

camaya vāḻvu :

paṭakarkaḷ mikavum paḻami virumpikaḷ, intu camayak koḷkikaḷil aciyāta nampikki koṇṭavarkaḷ. civaperumāṉtāṉ avarkaḷukkut taliyāya kaṭavuḷ. makāliṅkacāmi, mātēcuvaraṉ, tirumāl mutaliya vēṟu kaṭavuḷarkaḷiyum avarkaḷ vaṇaṅkuvatuṇṭu. mēṭṭuppāḷiyattiṟkarukil araṅkacāmi maliyiluḷḷa araṅkacāmit teyvattiyum aṭikkaṭi ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvārkaḷ. ivikaḷaṉṟi vēṟupala ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷiyum vaṇaṅkukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil karirāyaṉum, hēti (hētammā)yum kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ. hētammāḷukkup pala iṭaṅkaḷilkōyilkaḷ eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. taṉ kaṇavaṉ iṟanta poḻutu uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟi māṇṭa kāraṇattāl, paṭakarkaḷ ivaḷip perumatippōṭu pōṟṟi vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. hētammā kōyilukku etiril tiruviḻākkālaṅkaḷil 'tīmitittal' naṭipeṟum. hētammā vaḻipāṭum, oruvakip pattiṉi vaḻipāṭutāṉ. ōṅkāḷiyammaṉ, turōpatiyammaṉ, māriyammaṉ ākiya pattiṉit teyvaṅkaḷiṉ tiruviḻākkaḷil 'tīmitittal' naṭipeṟuvatip pōṉṟatē ituvum. (āṉāl tamiḻakattiṉ vīra pattiṉit teyvamāṉa kaṇṇakikku viḻā eṭuppati maṭṭum nām maṟantu viṭṭōm)

vēṟu ceytikaḷ :

nīlakiri malimītu vāḻum makkaḷil mikavum mukkiyamāṉavarkaḷ paṭakarkaḷē. ippōtu ēṟakkuṟiya 50,000 pēr nīlakiriyil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuṭiya tiruviḻākkaḷil kaṇṭu makiḻtaṟkuriya ciṟappuvāyntatu, aṟuvaṭik kālaṅkaḷil naṭipeṟum 'irā viruntu' (Fire feast) eṉṉum viḻāvākum. nalla viḷiccal ēṟpaṭa vēṇṭi, iṟivaṉit tiruptippaṭuttuvataṟkāka ivviḻā koṇṭāṭappaṭukiṟatu. paṭakarkaḷ icippiriyarkaḷ. avarkaḷ ici taṉit taṉmi vāyntatu. palavita iṉṉēci payappatu. avarkaḷ ici paṇpaṭṭatalla eṉṟālum, toliviliruntu kēṭpōrukku, avarkaḷuṭiya cevvōṭu pōṭṭa kuṭicikaḷum, avaṟṟic cūḻntuḷḷa eḻilmikka kāṭukaḷum, akkāṭukaḷil malarntuḷḷa kaṇṇik kavarum vaṇṇa malarkaḷum niṉivil tōṉṟāmalirukka muṭiyātu.

paṭakarkaḷ ippōtu paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷākak karutappaṭum niliyil illi. nākarika makkaḷāka māṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. mēli nāṭṭiṉarip pōl kāluṟiyum, mūṭuceruppum, kōṭṭum, toppiyum aṇintu kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉar. kalvi vaḷarcciyum avarkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. periya tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷākavum, vaṇikarkaḷākavum palar uḷḷaṉar. ovvoru paṭakar kuṭumpattukkum kuṟintatu aintu ēkkar nilam uḷḷatu. uḻavukkālam tavira maṟṟa nāṭkaḷil kūlikaḷākap paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar. periya ilaṭcātipatikaḷ kūṭa oru cilar ivarkaḷiṭiyē uḷḷaṉar. paṭakarkaḷiṭamiruntu nilaṅkaḷi maṟṟiyōr vāṅkuvati aracāṅkam caṭṭa pūrvamākat taṭuttirukkiṟatu. paḻamiyiṉ piṭippiliruntum, mūṭappaḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiliruntum ivarkaḷ mīṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.

tōṭar : nīlakiri maliyil vāḻum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷil mikavum toṉmiyāṉavarkaḷ tōṭarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ iyaṟki eḻil mikka nāṭṭuppuṟa vāḻvil (Arcadian life) ūṟip pōṉavarkaḷ. maṟṟiya makkaḷ kaṭippiṭittoḻukum. nākarikatti virumpātavarkaḷ. erumik kūṭṭattit tavira vēṟu etiyum celvamākak karutāta mēyccalkārarkaḷ. ulakap peruṅ kavi cēkspiyar eḻutiyuḷḷa iṉpiyal nāṭakaṅkaḷāṉa 'virumpiya vaṇṇamē ' (As YouLikeIt) 'naṭu vēṉiṟkaṉavu' (Mid Summer Nights' Dream) eṉṟa iraṇṭilum cittarittuk kāṭṭappaṭum iyaṟki vāḻviṉ eḻil nalattik kāṇa virumpuvōr tōṭar vāḻum toṭṭapeṭṭāviṟkuc ceṉṟāl kāṇalām. puṉaliṭi mūḻkip poḻiliṭi ulavi, kavaliyeṉṟāl eṉṉaveṉṟu aṟiyāmal iyaṟkiyōṭu iyaṟkiyāka oṉṟivāḻum avar kaḷ vāḻ vikkāṇpōr, tāṅkaḷum iyaṟkivāḻ makkaḷāka (Pastoral Nomads) ēṉ māṟiviṭakkūṭātu? eṉṟu eṇṇāmal irukkamāṭṭārkaḷ. ilakkiya uṇarvuṭiyōr aṅkuc ceṉṟārkaḷāṉāl, caṅka kālak kuṟiñcittiṇi avarkaḷ kaṇmuṉ niḻalāṭum.

tōṭarkaḷ perumitamum, añcāmiyum, kavarcciyūṭṭum uṭaṟkaṭṭum vāykkappeṟṟavarkaḷ. veḷḷiyarkaḷiṭam añcāmiyōṭu uriyāṭuvārkaḷ. āṉāl cōmpēṟikaḷ, erumi mēyppatit tavira, vēṟu enta vēliyum ceyya virumpātavarkaḷ. nīlakiri mali taṅkaḷukkup parampari urimiyuṭiyateṉṟu karutupavarkaḷ. mutalil kuṭiyēṟiyapōtu, paṭakarkaḷ ivarkaḷukku añciyavarkaḷākavē vāḻntaṉar. nilaṅkaḷip payiriṭṭu viḷinta tāṉiyattiṉ oru pakutiyik kappam vaḻaṅkuvatupōl tōṭarkaḷukkup paṭakarkaḷ vaḻaṅkivantaṉar. iṉṟum appaḻakkam iruntu koṇṭu varukiṟatu.

tōṭarkaḷiṉ toki nāḷukku nāḷ kuṟintu koṇṭu varukiṟatu. itaṟkup palakāraṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. nōyiṉāl tuṉpuṟṟu niṟiyappēr maṭikiṉṟaṉar. ivviṉap peṇkaḷil malaṭikaḷ atikam. peṇ kuḻanti piṟantatum koṉṟuviṭum paḻakkam (Female Infanticide) ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu . orupeṇ pala āṇkaḷit tirumaṇam ceytu, koḷḷum paḻakka (Polyandry)mum, pala kātalarkaḷi ēṟṟukkoḷḷum paḻakkamum ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu . ivvaḷavu taṭikaḷi vittukkoṇṭu, avarkaḷuṭiya iṉam evvāṟu peruka muṭiyum? nīlakiri maliyil mottam 1000 pērkaḷē vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ eṇṇikkiyil kuṟintavarkaḷāka iruntālum paḻaṅkuṭi ārāycciyāḷariṉ kavaṉatti ivarkaḷ īrkkum aḷavukku, intiya nāṭṭil uḷḷa entap paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉarum īrppatilli. nīlakiri malimītu ivarkaḷ koṇṭirunta taṉiyurimi piṟarāl koñcaṅ koñcamākap paṟikkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. poruḷātārattilum, uḷḷamakiḻcciyilum muṉṉiviṭac caṟṟuk kuṟintavar kaḷākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. intiya ūḻiyarkaḻaka (Servents of India Society)mum, ceṉṉi aracāṅkamum ivarkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkut tuṇipurikiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷuṭiya kuḻantikaḷukkuk kalvi vacati aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. maruttuva utaviyum, poruḷutaviyum ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu cōmpali nīkki orucilar tōṭṭak kūlikaḷākap paṇi yāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.

tōṟṟam :

ivarkaḷuṭiya tōṟṟattiyum, uṭalamippukaḷiyum paṟṟi ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ palatiṟappaṭṭa ceytikaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuṭiya mūkku rōmāṉiya iṉattāruṭiyatip pōṉṟatu; mukac cāyal kirēkka iṉattic cārntatu. eṉavē ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ iv viṉattāri rōmāṉiyarkaḷōṭum, kirēkkarkaḷōṭum toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nīṇṭa mēlāṭi pōrttukkoṇṭiruppatāl ivarkaḷ īpru (Hebrews) iṉattic cārntavarkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu cilar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. camaveḷi makkaḷāl turattappaṭṭu, im malikaḷil tañcam pukunta citiyar iṉattārē ivarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvōrum uṇṭu. tōṭarkaḷiṉ camayac caṭaṅkukaḷil pāṭappaṭum pāṭalkaḷi ārāynta kirēkka nāṭṭu ārāycciyāḷarāṉa ec. ec, piriṉs pīṭṭar (H. H. Prince Peter) eṉpār, cumēriyarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa kaṭavuḷariṉ peyarkaḷum, ak kaṭavuḷarkaḷip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷum, ivarkaḷuṭiya kaṭavuḷarkaḷiyum, ceytikaḷiyum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. eṉavē tōṭarkaḷ cumēriya iṉattic cārntavarkaḷ eṉṟu avar eṇṇukiṟār.

tōṭarkula āṇmakaṉiṉ carācari uyaram 5 aṭi 7 aṅkulam. peṇkaḷiṉ uyaram 5 aṭi 1 aṅkulam. camaveḷiyiluḷḷa makkaḷiviṭa aḻakiya niṟattip peṟṟirukkiṟārkaḷ. vaṭitteṭutta cili pōṉṟa uṭaṟkaṭṭuṭiyavarkaḷ. āṇkaḷ taliyil aṭarttiyāṉa mayirip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭiya kūntali veṇṇeyiṭṭu nīvi, uruṇṭiyāṉa kucciyil curuḷkaḷākac cuṟṟip pakkaṅkaḷil toṅka viṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum mottamāṉa pōrviyoṉṟu maṭṭum pōrttukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ap pōrvi veṇmi niṟamāṉatu. ōraṅkaḷil civappum nīlamum kalanta vaṇṇak karikaḷiyuṭiyatu. ip pōrviyip 'puṭkuli' eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ip pōrviyik kaḻuttiliruntu pātaṅkaḷ variyil pōrttuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. āṇkaḷ kōvaṇam aṇikiṉṟaṉar.



āṭavarkaḷ uṭaluramum, perumitamāṉa tōṟṟamum, nuṇṇaṟivum, uṟcākamum, calippaṟṟa taṉmiyum, kūriya pārviyum, piṟari aṭakkiyāḷum nampikkiyum iyaṟkiyākavē peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷ mayiri veṭṭuvatumilli; cirippatumilli. eppōtum caṭāmuṭiyōṭu kāṭciyaḷippārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ aḻakiya tōṟṟamuṭiyavarkaḷ. āṉāl aṟivil kuṟintavarkaḷ. oḻukkattip paṟṟik kavalippaṭātavarkaḷ, pacci kuttikkoḷḷum paḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu.

vīṭukaḷ :

tōṭarkaḷ vāḻum ciṟṟūrukku 'maṇṭu' eṉṟu peyar. ovvoru maṇṭuvilum nāṉku allatu aintu vīṭukaḷē uḷḷaṉa. iv vīṭukaḷ ari vaṭṭa vaṭi vamāṉavi; pīppāyi nīḷa vākkil kuṟukkāka aṟuttuk kavittu vittāṟpōl tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. marap palakikaḷālum, caṭṭaṅkaḷālum iv vīṭṭi amittu, mēlē puṟkaḷiyum, taḻikaḷiyum pōṭṭuk kūri vēykiṟārkaḷ. vīṭṭiṟku muṉṉāl maṭṭum orē oru vaḻiyuṇṭu. atu kuṟukalāṉatu ; iraṇṭaṭi catura aḷavuḷḷatu. vīṭṭiṟkuḷ celvōr kuḻan tikaḷip pōl nāṉku kālil tavaḻntukoṇṭē cella vēṇṭum. vīṭṭiṟkuḷ paṭuttuṟaṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu mēṭikaḷ amikkappaṭṭirukkum. kāṟṟum katiravaṉ oḷiyum uḷḷē maruntukkukkūṭak kiṭiyā. vīṭṭic cuṟṟi nāṟpuṟac cuvar amintirukkum. erumikaḷ uḷḷē nuḻivataṟkeṉṟu taṉi vaḻiyoṉṟu accuvaril irukkum.

moḻi :

tōṭarkaḷ pēcum moḻi, aṅkuḷḷa maṟṟa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭirukkiṟatu. avarkaḷuṭiya moḻi 'tutam' eṉṟa peyarāl aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. tirāviṭa moḻiyārāycci vallunarāṉa kālṭuvel turimakaṉār, tiruntāta tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil itiyum cērttirukkiṟār. maṟṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiviṭat tutam tamiḻōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koṇṭiruppatāka moḻiyārāycci vallunarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. im muṭiviliruntu ōruṇmi maṭṭum pulaṉākiṟatu. atāvatu tōṭarkaḷ tirāviṭa iṉattic cārntavarkaḷ eṉpatē. tōṭarkaḷil tārttar (Tarthar), tīvali (Teivali) eṉṟa iru piriviṉar uṇṭu . ivarkaḷ vevvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ.

aṉṟāṭa vēli :

tōṭarkaḷ payirt toḻil ceyvatilli. kāliyil eḻuntatum, kaṭṭi viralkaḷi mūkkiṟketiril vittu, maṟṟa viralkaḷi akala virittup pariti vaṇakkam ceyvārkaḷ. piṟaku erumit toḻuvattiṟkuc ceṉṟu pāl kaṟappārkaḷ. mutal nāḷ piriyiṭṭu uṟinta tayirik kaṭivārkaḷ. uṇavu uṇṭapiṉ erumikaḷi ōṭṭikkoṇṭu celvārkaḷ. nāḷ muḻuvatum erumi mēyttukkoṇṭu cōmpalākap poḻutu pōkkuvar. māli nērattil viḷakku ēṟṟi ati vaṇaṅkuvar. erumikaḷit toḻuvattil aṭitta piṉ uṇṭu uṟaṅkuvar. pāl kaṟattal, erumikaḷik kaṇkāṇittal mutaliya paṇikaḷi āṇkaḷ mēṟkoḷvar. vīṭṭu vēlikaḷip peṇkaḷ ceyvar.

camaya vāḻvu :

katirōṉ vaṇakkattiliruntu, ivarkaḷ iyaṟkiyik kaṭavuḷākak koḷḷum vaḻakkamuṭiyavarkaḷ eṉpatu pulaṉākiṟatu. atōṭu avarkaḷukkē urittāṉa vēṟupala kaṭavuḷkaḷiyum vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. tōṭarkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya mukkiyamāṉa kaṭavuḷ āṉ (On) ākum. āṉiṉ tanti piti (Pithi). āṉukku oru cōtariyum uṇṭu. avaḷ peyar tīkirci (Teikirzi). piti mikavum paḻamiyāṉa kaṭavuḷ. pititāṉ tōṭarkaḷiyum, avarkaḷuṭiya celvamāṉa erumikaḷiyum paṭittār eṉṟu avarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl piti ippōtu ivvulakil illiyām, iṟantōr vāḻum ulakiṉ talivarāka irukkiṟārām. tōṭar iṉattiṉ mutal tōṟṟattippaṟṟi avarkaḷiṭiyē oru kati vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.

pitiyum avar maṉiviyum, oru nāḷ kuntā maliyiṉ uccikkuc ceṉṟārkaḷām. oru nīṇṭa irumpuc caṭṭatti avvucciyiṉ mēl kuṟukkāka vittu, accaṭṭattiṉ oru nuṉiyil pitiyum, maṟṟoru nuṉiyil avar maṉiviyum niṉṟu koṇṭārkaḷām. piti annuṉiyiliruntavaṇṇam pūmiyiliruntu 1800 erumikaḷi mēlē koṇarntārām. pitiyiṉ maṉivi, maṟṟoru nuṉiyiliruntukoṇṭu 1600 erumikaḷik koṇarntārām. piti koṇṭuvanta erumikaḷē, ippoḻutu tōṭarkaḷiṭiyē uḷḷa puṉita erumikaḷiṉ (Sacredbuffaloes) muṉṉōrkaḷ. pūmiyiliruntu pitiyiṉāl koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa kaṭicip puṉita erumiyiṉ vālip piṭittukkoṇṭu tōṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōṉ vantāṉ. avaṉ taṉ mārpu elumpukaḷil oṉṟi eṭuttu, mutal tōṭarkulap peṇṇi uruvākkiṉāṉ. orunāḷ pitiyiṉ makaṉāṉa āṉ taṇṇīril mūḻki iṟantuviṭṭāṉām. makaṉiṉ pirivit tāḷamuṭiyāta piti, tāmum iṟantōr vāḻum ulakiṟkuc ceṉṟu, avvulakiṉ talimip poṟuppi ēṟṟukkoṇṭu, aṅkēyē irukkiṟārām. pitikkup piṟaku avar makaḷāṉa tīkirci tōṭarik kākkum paṇiyi mēṟkoṇṭu, avarkaḷiṉ matippiṟkuriya kaṭavuḷāka vīṟṟirukkiṟāḷ. uṇmiyilēyē ikkatiyum, ik katiyil varum kaṭavuḷar peyarkaḷum kirēkka, cumēriya pāṇiyilēyē amintuḷḷaṉa. ik katiyiliruntum, ivarkaḷuṭiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiliruntum, ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷukkeṉa oru taṉic camayattiyum, kaṭavuḷarkaḷiyum koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu.
