cila camayaṅkaḷil tōṭarkaḷ intu camayak kaṭavuḷarkaḷiyum vaḻipaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ vaṇaṅkum intu camayak kaṭavuḷkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkatu 'nañcaṉ' eṉpatākum. piḷḷippēṟṟukkāka maṭṭum, ikkaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. puṉita mantiyiṉ kuru, mantiravāti, maruttuvaṉ ākiya ovvoruvarukkum taṉittaṉik kaṭavuḷuṇṭu. kurumārkaḷiṉ caṭaṅkukaḷil kūṟappaṭum mantiram maliyāḷa moḻiyilēyē amintuḷḷatu.

kōyilum puṉita mantiyum :

tōṭarkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ avarkaḷuṭiya kuṭicikaḷip pōlavē kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷuṭiya kuṭicik kūṭṭattiliruntu, ciṟitu tūrattil ik kōyilkaḷ amintirukkiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ kōyilukkuḷ aṉumatikkappaṭuvatilli. taṅkaḷip pōlavē kaṭavuḷum erumikaḷi mēyttukkoṇṭu maliyucciyil vāḻvatākat tōṭarkaḷ eṇṇukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl maṉitarkaḷ kaṇṇil teṉpaṭuvatilliyām. taṅkaḷ mantiyiliruntu, kōyilukkuc cila erumikaḷi viṭṭuviṭukiṟārkaḷ. avvāṟu viṭum erumikaḷip 'puṉita erumikaḷ' eṉṟu avarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ, avikaḷ kaṭavuḷukkuc conta māṉavi eṉṟu karutukiṟārkaḷ. ippuṉita erumikaḷik kaṇkāṇikka oru kuru amarttappaṭukiṟāṉ. avaṉi ellārum pālōl (Palol) eṉṟu aḻik kiṉṟaṉar. pālōl tāṉ puṉita erumikaḷukkum tōṭar kaḷukkum iṭiyiliruntu paṇipuripavaṉāka. {Intermediary) viḷaṅkukiṟāṉ. avaṉukkut tuṇiyāka oru paṇiyāḷaṉ uṇṭu. avaṉik 'kalṭamok' (Kaltmokh) eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar.

tōṭariṉ kōyil iru pakutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. muṟpakuti pālōliṉ iruppiṭamākap payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. uṭpakuti kaṭavuḷiṉ iruppiṭam. kōyililuḷḷa erumippaṇṇikku 'ṭi' (Ti) eṉṟu peyar. ittakiya paṇṇi cīkūr (Sigur) cikarattiṟkarukil uḷḷatu. utakamaṇṭalattiluḷḷa airōppiyarkaḷ itit tōṭar kōyil (Toda Cathedral) eṉṟē aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. puṉita erumikaḷiliruntu kiṭikkum varuvāy kōyil vaḻipāṭṭiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu. kōyiliṉ muṟpakutiyil pālōl uṟaṅkuvatu vaḻakkam. intuk kōyilkaḷil karuvaṟi (mūlattāṉam) evvaḷavu puṉitamākak karutappaṭukiṟatō, atu pōla itaṉ uṭpakuti tōṭarkaḷāl karutappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉuḷ pālōlit tavira vēṟu yārum nuḻiyak kūṭātu. puṉitap paṇṇiyil paṇipuripavarkaḷ kūṭa uḷḷē aṉumatikkappaṭuvatilli. puṉita erumikaḷiṉ pālik kaṟattal mutaliya paṇikaḷikkūṭap pālōl tāṉ. nēraṭiyākac ceyvatu vaḻakkam, kōyilukkuḷ irukkum pōtu pālōl orē oru laṅkōṭutāṉ aṇivatu vaḻakkam. pālōlaṇiyum pōrvi paḻuppu allatu karuppu niṟam uṭiyatāka irukkum. kōyil vaḻipāṭṭiṟkum, puṉita erumikaḷiṉ upayōkattiṟkum oru nīrōṭi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum. ataṉ nīri vēṟu yārum payaṉ paṭuttakkūṭātu. pālōl etirpārāvitamāka, vēṟu ētēṉum kuṭiciyil uṟaṅka nēriṭṭāl, tariyiyum, uṟaṅkum mēṭiyiyum tavira, ak kuṭiciyiluḷḷa vēṟu epporuḷiyum toṭuvatu kiṭiyātu. periya kōyilil uḷḷa pālōl mikavum puṉitamāṉavaṉākak karutappaṭukiṟāṉ. cantikkuc celluvatum, peṇṇōṭu uṭaltoṭarpu koḷvatum, piṟarōṭu paḻakuvatum avaṉāl vilakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tiṅkaḷ, viyāḻaṉ ākiya iraṇṭu nāṭkaḷil maṭṭum piṟar avaṉi neruṅka aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl ikkaṭṭuppāṭukaḷellām ippoḻutu taḷarntu varukiṉṟaṉa. oru kuru puṉita mantikaḷiṉ poṟuppi ēṟṟukkoḷvataṟku muṉpākac cila caṭaṅkukaḷ naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kōyilukkeṉṟu otukkappaṭṭa puṉita aruviyil kuḷittu, ataṉ nīri uṭkoḷvatu accaṭaṅkukaḷil kuṟippiṭattakka oṉṟākum.

uṇavu :

tōṭarkaḷ potuvāka marakkaṟi uṇavē uṇkiṉṟaṉar. pālum tayirum avarkaḷuṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ. caṭaṅkukaḷil erumik kaṉṟukaḷip paliyiṭumpōtu maṭṭum avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi uṇkiṉṟaṉar. vēṭṭiyil māṉ kiṭittāl, atiyum viruppattōṭu uṇkiṉṟaṉar. matu aruntuvatil, ivarkaḷ peru viruppam uṭiyavarkaḷ.

paliyiṭutal :

erumik kaṉṟukaḷip paliyiṭutal ivarkaḷiṭam peru vaḻakkākap paraviyiruntatu. kaṭavuḷit tiruptip paṭuttuvataṟkāka āṇṭutōṟum ovvoru maṇṭuvilum ōr erumik kaṉṟum, ovvoru 'ṭi'yilum iraṇṭu kaṉṟukaḷum paliyiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. palavita nuṇukkamāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷ appōtu naṭipeṟum. caṭaṅkukaḷellām muṭintapiṟaku paliyiṭappaṭṭa kaṉṟiṉ ūṉi ellārum uṇpar. pāvaṅkaḷip pōkkikkoḷvataṟkum, tuyaraṅkaḷit tīrttukkoḷvataṟkumkūṭa, ivarkaḷ paliyiṭum vaḻakkatti mēṟkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. tēṉum, paḻaṅkaḷum malic carivil perukavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkāka āṇṭutōṟum akṭōpar tiṅkaḷil pālōlum avaṉ tuṇivaṉum neruppu mūṭṭi vēḷvi ceyvatu vaḻakkam. araciyalāriṉ taliyīṭṭāl, paliyiṭum paḻakkam kuṟintu varukiṉṟatu.

pilli cūṉiyam :

pilli cūṉiyam ellāt tōṭarkaḷukkum ōraḷavu teriyum. āṉāl oru cilarukku naṉṟākat teriyum. mēṉmēlum terintu koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟa āci ellārukkum uṇṭu. ikkali parampariyākap payilappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. piṟa iṉattār itaṉāl tāṉ tōṭarkaḷik kaṇṭu añcukiṟārkaḷ. ivvaccattiṉālēyē āṇṭutōṟum naṉkoṭiyāka ivarkaḷukkut tāṉiyam vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. maṉita mayiril aintu kallikkaṭṭi, mantira uccāṭaṉam ceytu, avaṟṟit tuṇiyil kaṭṭi, etiriyiṉ vīṭṭuk kūriyil ceruki viṭuvatu vaḻakkam. allatu avaṉ vāḻum maṇṭuviṟkarukiluḷḷa ṣōlāvil putittu viṭuvatu vaḻakkam.

kuḻantik koli :

tōṭarkaḷiṉ toki nāḷukku nāḷ arukik koṇṭu varuvataṟkup peṇ kuḻantikkoli mukkiyakkāraṇam ākum eṉṟu muṉpē kuṟippiṭṭēṉ. peṇ kuḻantikaḷiṉ mītu avarkaḷ koṇṭa veṟuppiṟkuriya uṇmiyāṉa kāraṇam viḷaṅkavilli. peṇkuḻanti piṟantatum, erumit toḻuvattiṉ vāyilil atik kiṭattiviṭuvārkaḷ. malarntu maṇam vīcavēṇṭiya avviḷamokku, erumikaḷiṉ kālil mitipaṭṭu maṭikiṟatu. ki. pi. 1820- il utakamaṇṭalattil vāḻnta callivaṉ eṉṟa airōppiyar ippaḻakkatti kiviṭumāṟu tōṭarkaḷukku aṟivuṟuttiṉār. ki. pi. 1856-ām āṇṭil kōvi māvaṭṭattiṉ taṇṭalarāka iruntavar oru putiya caṭṭam iyaṟṟiṉār. ataṉpaṭi tōṭar vīṭṭil piṟanta peṇ kuḻantiyip peṟṟōr koṇṭuvantu āṇṭutōṟum nēril kāṭṭiṉāl, ataṉ vaḷarccikkākak kuṟippiṭṭa oru tokiyik koṭuppatākak kūṟiṉār. ippoḻutukūṭa ic caṭṭam amulil uḷḷatu. itaṉāl peṇ kuḻantik kolic ceyal ōraḷavu kuṟintateṉalām. āṉāl muḻutum nīṅkavilli. tōṭarkaḷiṉ icceyalāl peṇkaḷ toki āṇkaḷiṉ tokiyiviṭa ep poḻutum kuṟintē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

utakamaṇṭalattiṟ karukil vāḻum tōṭarkula makaḷir kātaṟ ceyalkaḷil kitērntavarkaḷ. nakaril vāḻum maṟṟa iṉa āṇkaḷōṭu koṇṭa toṭarpāl, ōraḷavu avarkaḷ makkaḷ toki atikarittatu eṉalām. āṉāl appaṭip piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷ tōṭarkaḷiṉ uṭaṟpaṇpukaḷiyē peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. vēṟṟu iṉap paṇpukaḷip peṟṟup piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷ mikavum kuṟivu.

pala kaṇavar muṟiyum potu maṉivi muṟiyum :

tōṭarkaḷiṉ kātal vāḻkkiyum, illaṟamum cuviyāṉavi, kaṟpippaṟṟi avarkaḷ atikamākak kavalippaṭuvatilli. kalācāra varalāṟṟiṉpaṭi, kaṟpu muṟiyāṉatu, kālattiṟkuk kālam nāṭṭukku nāṭu māṟupaṭṭukkoṇṭu vantirukkiṟatu. oru nāṭṭuk kaṟpumuṟi, maṟṟoru nāṭṭiṉarukku putumiyākat tōṉṟum. paṇpaṭāta makkaḷiṉattilum kaṟpik kāṇa muṭiyātu; ellōrum kalvi kaṟṟu aṟivu ārāycciyil mikuntirukkum iṉattāriṭattilum kaṟpik kāṇa muṭiyātu. muṉṉavar aṟiyāmiyiṉālum, piṉṉavar alaṭciyattālum kaṟpik kikaḻuvi viṭukiṉṟaṉar. kīḻi nāṭṭiṉariṭiyē kaṟpu ōraḷavu kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟkuṭpaṭṭirukkiṟatu. nākarikattil (vaḷḷuvar kūṟum nākarikamalla) mēmpaṭṭavarkaḷāka viḷaṅkum mēṉāṭṭiṉariṭiyē, kaṟpu atika matippup peṟuvatilli. pireñcu nāṭṭil kaṟpu mikavum iḻinta niliyil uḷḷatu. amerikka nāṭṭil 10 nimiṭattiṟkoru untuvaṇṭit tiruṭṭum, 40 nimiṭattiṟkoru kaṟpaḻippum naṭi, peṟuvatākak kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. iṅkilāntu nāṭṭil vipacāram atikamilli, ruciya nāṭṭil, vipacārattic caṭṭapūrvamākat taṭuttuk kaṭuntaṇṭaṉiyaḷippatāl, kaṟpu aṅkuk kākkappaṭukiṟatu. paṭiyāta makkaḷ pāvattiṟkañci, iḻi ceyal purivatilli. vaṟumi tākkumpōtu maṭṭum, vēṟu vaḻiyiṉṟik kuṟṟam ceykiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl mikavum paṭitta makkaḷukkup pāvattil nampikki kiṭiyātu. kaṟpi iḻappatik kuṟṟamākavum avarkaḷ karutuvatilli, etirkālattil taṉi maṉiviyurimi eṉṟa nilimāṟi, atilum potuvuṭimik koḷki ēṟpaṭalām eṉpatu ulakap pēraṟiñarāṉa irassaliṉ karuttākum.

tōṭarkaḷ kaṟpip paṟṟik kavalippaṭāmaliruppataṟku iraṇṭu kāraṇaṅkaḷuṇṭu. mutalāvatu kāraṇam aṟiyāmi. ovvoru tōṭarkula āṇukkum, ovvoru peṇ kiṭikkāmaliruppatu iraṇṭāvatu kāraṇam. peṇkaḷiṉ paṟṟāk kuṟiyiṉāl, ovvoru peṇṇum pala āṭavarik kaṇavarāka ēṟṟukkoḷḷum vaḻakkatti -(Polyandry}k koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷōṭu amiyāmal, vēṟucila āṭavarik kātalarkaḷākavum oru peṇ ēṟṟuk koḷvatuṇṭu. kaṇavarkaḷum ip paḻakkatti aṉumatikkiṉṟaṉar. ākiyiṉāl avarkaḷ aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭa kātalarkaḷ (recognised lovers) eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.

pala āṭavarkaḷ cērntu pala maṉiviyarip potuvākak koḷḷum paḻakkamum ivarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu. oru kuṭumpattil pala cakōtararkaḷiruntāl, avarkaḷuṭiya maṉiviyar, ellārukkum urimiyuṭiyavarkaḷ. ippaḻakkam pala maṉivi muṟiyōṭu (Polygamy) ōraḷavu toṭarpuṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl ippoḻutu oru kuṭumpattiluḷḷa ovvoru cakōtaraṉum taṉakkeṉa oruttiyi maṉiviyāka urimiyākkik koḷkiṟāṉ. āṉāl kaṭṭuppāṭiṉṟit taṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāriṉ maṉiviyariṭam toṭarpukoḷḷa avaṉukku urimiyuṇṭu. ic ceyaliṉāl varampiṟkuṭpaṭṭa maṉivi muṟi (Monogamy) ōraḷavu talikāṭṭukiṟatu.

oru peṇṇukkuriya kaṇavarkaḷilō allatu kātalarkaḷilō, yārēṉum oruvaṉ, appeṇ irukkum kuṭicikkuḷ nuḻiyumpōtu, oru pūvi vāyilil cerukiviṭṭuc celvatu vaḻakkam. appūvik kāṇum maṟṟa kaṇavaṉmārō, allatu kātalarkaḷō appōtu kuṭicikkuḷ nuḻiyak kūṭātu. mēṟ kūṟiya paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷellām, camaveḷiyiluḷḷa makkaḷ toṭarpāl ippōtu kuṟintuvarukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl muṟṟilum maṟiyavilli,

tirumaṇam :

tārttār, tīvali eṉṟa irupiriviṉar tōṭarkaḷiṭiyē uṇṭu eṉak kaṇṭōm. avarkaḷiṭiyē maṇat toṭarpu kiṭiyātu. āṉāl ōriṉap peṇ maṟṟōriṉattāṉik kātalaṉāka ēṟṟukkoḷḷalām. oru peṇ kuḻanti piṟantatum, mūṉṟu allatu nāṉkāvatu vayatil, atit tāymāmaṉ makaṉukkō, atti makaṉukkō , tirumaṇam ceytu koṭuppatu vaḻakkam. māppiḷḷi vīṭṭār peṇ vīṭṭārukku aṭikkaṭi paricukaḷ vaḻaṅkuvataṉ mūlam tirumaṇatti uṟuti ceytu koḷkiṉṟaṉar, peṇ 15 allatu 16 vayatu variyil peṟṟōr vīṭṭilēyē iruppatu vaḻakkam. oru peṇ paruvam aṭiyum niliyil irukkumpōtu, vēṟṟukkulattāṉ oruvaṉi aḻittu appeṇṇōṭu ōr iravu muḻuvatum taṉi aṟiyil irukkumāṟu ceyvar. itu 'paruvap peṇṇiṉ kaṟpaḻippuc caṭaṅku' {ceremonial defloration of nubile girls) eṉappaṭum. iccaṭaṅku mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatākak karutappaṭṭatu. iccaṭaṅku naṭipeṟāmal orupeṇ paruvameytuvatu, avamāṉamākak karutappaṭṭatu. āṉāl ippaḻakkam ippoḻutu kiviṭappaṭṭatu.

maṇa vilakku :

tōṭarkaḷ kaṭippiṭit toḻukum maṉivi muṟikaḷāl maṇamuṟivum, maṟumaṇamum aṭikkaṭi ēṟpaṭum eṉpatil aiyamilli. maṇavilakkup peṟa virumpuvōr cila erumikaḷi oṟuppukkaṭṭaṇamākac (Fine) celuttiviṭṭu eḷitil peṟalām. ākiyiṉāl vēṇṭumeṉṟē cila āṭavarkaḷ, āṭi māṟṟuvatu pōl maṉiviyari māṟṟik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar, oru peṇ taṉ vāḻkkiyil aintu muṟikūṭa maṇa vilakkup peṟuvatuṇṭu.

piḷḷippēṟu :

tōṭar kulappeṇ karuvuṟṟa aintāvatu tiṅkaḷil, pala caṭaṅkukaḷ nikaḻttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ vāḻum ‘maṇṭu'viliruntu ciṟitu tolivil oru kuṭici amittu, atil appeṇṇik kuṭiyēṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. oru kayiṟṟiṉ nuṉiyil tīyip paṟṟavittu, attīyiṉāl taṉ iṭuppiṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷiyum appeṇṇiyē cuṭṭuk koḷḷac colkiṉṟaṉar. oru tiṅkaḷ akkuṭiciyil iruntapiṟaku, pala caṭaṅkukaḷōṭu appeṇ vīṭṭiṟkaḻikkappaṭukiṟāḷ.

ēḻāvatu tiṅkaḷil maṟupaṭiyum oru caṭaṅku naṭipeṟukiṟatu. iccaṭaṅku mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu. itaṟku 'villampuc caṭaṅku' eṉṟu peyar. karuvuṟṟa peṇṇum, vayiṟṟil uḷḷa kuḻantikaḷukkuriya tanti eṉṟu avaḷāl kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa kaṇavaṉum, maṇṭuviṟkarukiluḷḷa ṣōlāviṟkuc celluvārkaḷ. kaṇavaṉ marattiliruntu kucciyi oṭittu mukkōṇamāka vippāṉ. ataṉ naṭuvil oru viḷakku ēṟṟappaṭum. piṟaku kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum vēṟoru kucciyi oṭittu vil ceyvārkaḷ. oru pulliṉāl ampu ceyvārkaḷ. piṟaku tam uṟaviṉari vaṇaṅkuvārkaḷ, villik kiyilēntiya vaṇṇam appeṇ eriyum viḷakki orumaṇi nēramō, aṇiyum variyilō pārttukkoṇṭirukka vēṇṭum.

piṟaku ammaṇṭuviluḷḷa ellārum, avviṭattilēyē uṇavu camittu uṇṭuviṭṭu, avviravi avarkaḷōṭu kaḻippar. oru peṇ mutal taṭaviyākak karuvuṟum pōtō, putiya oru kaṇavaṉi ak kuḻantiyiṉ tantiyākat tērnteṭukkum pōtō iccaṭaṅku naṭipeṟum. tirumaṇamillāmalō, kuḻantiyillāmalō orupeṇ iṟantāl, cuṭukāṭṭil tirumaṇac caṭaṅkum, villampuc caṭaṅkum naṭi peṟum. kuḻanti piṟantavuṭaṉ appeṇ maṟupaṭiyum maṇṭuviṟku veḷiyiluḷḷa akkuṭicikku aṉuppappaṭukiṟāḷ. akkuṭiciyil mūṉṟu vāraṅkaḷō oru tiṅkaḷō avaḷ irukka vēṇṭum. kuḻantiyiṉ mukatti mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷkaḷ muṭiyum vari yārum pārkkak kūṭātu. ākiyiṉāl oru tuṇi koṇṭu ataṉ mukatti mūṭiyē vippārkaḷ. kuḻantikku muṭi yeṭukkumpōtu peyariṭuvārkaḷ.

cottu muṟi :

oru peṇṇukkup piṟakkum kuḻanti ēḻāvatu mātac caṭaṅkiṉpōtu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa kaṇavaṉukkē urimiyāṉatu. avaṉuṭiya cottellām ak kuḻantiyiyē cārum. vēṟoruvar kuḻantiyit tatteṭukkum vaḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭam illi. peṇkaḷukkuc cotturimi kiṭiyātu. iccotturimi naṭavaṭikkikaḷik kaṇkāṇippataṟku amarttappaṭṭavari mōṇikar (Monigar) eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl tōṭariṭiyē irukkum pañcāyattuc capi, mōṇikariviṭac celvākku mikuntatāka uḷḷatu. appañcāyattiṉ tīrppiyē ellārum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.

iṟutic caṭaṅku :

tōṭarkaḷiṉ iṟutic caṭaṅku mūṉṟu pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pacci iṟutic caṭaṅku (Green Funeral), kāynta iṟutic caṭaṅku (Dry Funeral) kaṭici iṟuticcaṭaṅku (Last Funeral) eṉpaviyē appirivukaḷ. mutaṉ mutalāka iṟantupōṉavaṉi āṭi aṇikaḷāl alaṅkarittu, oru kuṭiciyil koṇṭupōy vippārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ iṇi iṇiyāka oruvar neṟṟiyōṭu maṟṟoruvar muṭṭik koṇṭu kūccaliṭṭu aḻuvārkaḷ. appoḻutu puṉita erumikaḷil iraṇṭip paliyiṭa vēṇṭum. ataṟkākap puṉita erumikaḷi ṣōlāvil ōṭṭi accuṟuttuvārkaḷ. avi maruṇṭu ciṉaṅkoṇṭu ōṭum. appoḻutu palapēr cērntu avaṟṟit turattip piṭippārkaḷ. ammuyaṟciyil pala pērukkuk kaṭumiyāṉa kāyam ēṟpaṭuvatuṇṭu. kaṭiciyāka iraṇṭu erumikaḷip piṭittup piṇattiṟkarukil koṇṭuvaruvārkaḷ. avaṟṟiṉ kompukaḷil neyyip pūcuvārkaḷ. piṟaku kōṭariyiṉ piṉpuṟattāl taliyil tākki, erumikaḷik kolluvārkaḷ. iṟantavar āṇāka iruntāl, paliyiṭappaṭṭa erumikaḷip piṇattiṉ taliyarukil, valatukip puṟamākak kiṭattuvar. peṇṇāka iruntāl, piṇattiṉ kālkaḷi erumikaḷiṉ talimītu tūkki vippar. aṅkirukkum āṭavarkaḷellārum iṟantukiṭakkum erumikaḷiṉ kompukaḷit toṭṭu, avikaṭku vaṇakkam celuttuvar. piṟaku piṇam pāṭiyilēṟṟappaṭṭu iṭukāṭṭukku eṭuttuc cellappaṭum. piṇattōṭu uṇavu, āṭi, aṇi, paṇam, pukiyili mutaliyaṉavum aṭutta ulakil payaṉpaṭuttuvataṟkāka vittaṉuppappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉ piṟaku piṇattic citiyil vittut tīyiṭuvārkaḷ. viliyuyarnta poruḷkaḷiyum, piṇattiṉ taliyiliruntu ciṟitu mayiriyum eṭuttukkoḷvārkaḷ, piṇam erikkappaṭṭa piṟaku, citaṟiya ataṉ maṇṭiyōṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyiyum eṭuttukkoḷvārkaḷ. ittuṭaṉ pacci iṟutic caṭaṅku muṭivuṟukiṟatu.

mēṟkūṟiya mūṭṭiyi oru kuṭiciyil koṇṭupōy vippārkaḷ. mutalil evvāṟu peṇkaḷ aḻutārkaḷō, atē pōl iṉṟum aḻuvārkaḷ. erumik kaṉṟukaḷ paliyiṭappaṭum. palakaṟikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa oru taṭiyi uyarttip piṭittukkoṇṭu, ak kuṭiciyic cuṟṟi oruvaṉ āṭi varuvāṉ. kaṭiciyāka ellārum uṇavu uṇṭu, tattam vīṭukaḷukkup pirintu celvar. itaṟkuk kāynta iṟutic caṭaṅku eṉṟu peyar.



maṟunāḷ kāliyil kaṭici iṟutic caṭaṅku toṭaṅkum, kuṭiciyiliruntu am mūṭṭiyi eṭuttukkoṇṭu ceṉṟu vaṭṭamāka amikkappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷukku naṭuvil oru kuḻi tōṇṭi, atil vippārkaḷ. avviṭattiṟku ' 'ācāram' (Azaram) eṉṟu peyar. am mūṭṭiyiyum, uṇavup poruḷōṭu kūṭiya marat taṭṭiyum vittu oṉṟāka erippārkaḷ. piṟaku ac cāmpalik kuḻikkuḷḷēyē vittu mūṭiviṭuvārkaḷ. piṟaku mūṉṟu muṟi maṇiyaṭikkappaṭum. putiya maṭpāṇṭamoṉṟu uṭikkappaṭum. ittuṭaṉ cāccaṭaṅkukaḷ muṭivuṟukiṉṟaṉa. cilar ic caṭaṅkukaḷic cila tiṅkaḷkaḷ kaḻittukkūṭac ceyvatuṇṭu.

tōṭarkaḷ yār?

intiya nāṭṭilēyē mikavum kavarcciyūṭṭum paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉarākiya it tōṭarkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷikkoṇṭu, ivarkaḷ yārāyirukkalām? eṅkiruntu vantirukkalām ? eṉṟa viṉākkaḷukku viṭi kāṇa muyalukiṉṟaṉar. tiruvāḷar rivers (Mr. Rivers) eṉpār, kēraḷattu maliyāḷa makkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭōṭu, ivarkaḷ paṇpāṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koṇṭiruppati viḷakkap pala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟār. oru peṇ pala kaṇavari ēṟṟukkoḷḷum muṟi kaṭanta aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vari maliyāḷattil nilavi vantatu. am maṇa muṟi, 'campanta maṇa muṟi' eṉṟu maliyāḷa makkaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭu vantatu. tirumaṇattiṉpōtu naṭakkum. kūṟic caṭaṅku, iru iṉattāriṭiyilum mukkiya māṉatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. tirumaṇamākāmal oru peṇ iṟantuviṭṭāl, putuppuṭavi vittu erittalum, maṇattiṟkuriya caṭaṅkukaḷip piṇattiṟkuc ceytalum. iru iṉattāriṭiyilēyum nilavivarum paḻakkaṅkaḷākum. palakaṟikaḷāl oru kaḻiyi alaṅkarittu, piṇam vikkappaṭṭa kuṭiciyic cuṟṟi āṭum paḻakkam maliyāḷa makkaḷukkē uriyatu. tōṭarkaḷum ap paḻakkatti mēṟkoṇṭu oḻukukiṉṟaṉar. iṟantupōṉavaṉuṭiya uyir mēṟku nōkkic (maliyāḷam irukkum tici nōkki) celluvatākat tōṭarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. tōṭarkulak kurumārkaḷ, camayac caṭaṅkukaḷiṉpōtu, maliyāḷa mantiraṅkaḷiyē kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tōṭarkaḷiṉ uruva amippukaḷ maliyāḷattiluḷḷa nampūtirikaḷiyum, nāyarkaḷiyumē perumpālum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. tōṭarkaḷ nīṇṭa kālattiṟku muṉ (cumār 800 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ) maliyāḷattiliruntu iṅku vantirukka vēṇṭum. ākiyiṉāl tāṉ tōṭar moḻi tamiḻ moḻiyōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koṇṭuḷḷatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, paṇṭiya maliyāḷam tamiḻēyaṉṟō?

piṟa ceytikaḷ :

koṉṉamarā eṉṟa iṭattil tōṭarkaḷukkuriya kōyil oṉṟuḷatu. uyaramāṉa vaṭivatti uṭiyatu. ' piṅkar pōsṭ'ṭiṟkut tirumpum vaḻiyil itik kāṇalām. kāṭṭerumikaḷik kaṇṭāl, ellōrum añci naṭuṅkuvārkaḷ. āṉāl avikaḷiṉ arukil accamillāmal ceṉṟu, pāl kaṟakkum. kaliyit tōṭarkaḷ naṉku aṟivārkaḷ.

kōtar :

kōtar nīlakiri malimītu 1500 pēr vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. kōtakiriyiluḷḷa 6 ciṟṟūrkaḷum, kūṭalūrukkarukiluḷḷa oru ciṟṟūrum, ivarkaḷuṭiya vāḻviṭaṅkaḷām. ivarkaḷuṭiya vīṭukaḷ aḻakaṟṟavi ; uruvattāl periyavi. ovvoru vīṭṭiṉ muṉ pakutiyilum tāḻvāram amintirukkum. kaṟṟūṇkaḷ vīṭṭil nāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. āṉāl cittira vēlippāṭu. avaṟṟil atikamirukkātu. vīṭukaḷ perumpālum kūriyiṉāl vēyappaṭṭavi.

kōtarkaḷ tūymiyaṟṟa uṭaliṉar. avarkaḷ aṇiyum āṭikaḷ mikavum aḻukkaṭintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻarippōl vēṭṭiyē ariyil uṭukkiṉṟaṉar. kōtarkaḷi eḷitil aṟintukoḷḷalām. paṭakarkaḷiviṭak kaṟutta niṟamuṭivarkaḷ ; naṭuvil vakiṭu eṭuttup piṉṉāl kuṭumi muṭintirukkiṉṟaṉar. nākarikam vaḷarntirukkum ik kālattilum kūṭa, iṟanta vilaṅkukaḷiṉ ūṉi mukkiya uṇavākak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuṭiya kaṭavuḷākiya kāmaṭṭarāyaṉ (Kamattarayan) taṉ uṭaliliruntu vaḻinta mūṉṟu tuḷi viyarviyiliruntu tōṭarkaḷiyum, kuṟumparkaḷiyum, kōtarkaḷiyum paṭittāṉām. atōṭu, tōṭarkaḷ pāl, tayir, veṇṇey ākiyavaṟṟiyum, kuṟumparkaḷ iṟicciyiyum, kōtarkaḷ aḻukiya iṟicciyiyum uṇṇa vēṇṭumeṉṟu āṇiyiṭṭāṉām. avvāṇiyiṉ paṭiyē tāṅkaḷ aḻukiya iṟicciyi uṇpatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iv viḻinta uṇavu, avarkaḷ uṭal nilikku ēṟṟatāka amintuviṭṭatu. kōtarkaḷ parutta, uṟutiyāṉa uṭaṟkaṭṭuṭiyavarkaḷ.

kōtarkaḷ micūr nāṭṭuk kuṉṟukaḷiliruntu iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ pēcum moḻi tiruntāk kaṉṉaṭam. taccarākavum, kammiyarākavum, cakkiliyarākavum, kollarākavum piṟa iṉattārukkuk kōtarkaḷ paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar. ivviṉap peṇkaḷ maṭpāṇṭam ceytu piṟa iṉattārukku vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ic cēvikku īṭāka maṟṟa varkaḷiṭamiruntu tāṉiyam, erumikaḷ, iṟanta mirukaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟip peṟukiṉṟaṉar. aṟuvaṭik kālattil oru kuṟippiṭṭa aḷavu tāṉiyam paṭakarkaḷāl āṇṭutōṟum ivarkaṭku vaḻaṅkappaṭum. paṭakarkaḷip pōṉṟu ivarkaḷum āṇṭutōṟum tōṭarkaḷukku naṉkoṭit tāṉiyam vaḻaṅkuvatu vaḻakkam. ataṟkuk 'kuṭu' (Gudu) eṉṟu peyar. paṭakarkaḷiṭiyē mūṉṟu pirivukaḷuṇṭu. ip piriviṉar taṅkaḷukkuḷ maṇat toṭarpu koḷvatilli. kōtarkaḷ iciñarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ nīlakiri maliyiluḷḷa ellā iṉattārkkum, tirumaṇak kālaṅkaḷilum, viḻāk kālaṅkaḷilum, icip paṇi purikiṉṟaṉar.

tirumaṇam :

tirumaṇam peṟṟōrkaḷiṉ icivuṭaṉēyē naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. maṇamakaṉ māmaṉāriṉ vīṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu, vaṇakkam celuttip paricup poruḷkaḷi aḷittuviṭṭut tirumpuvataṉ mūlam tirumaṇam niccayikkappaṭukiṟatu. tirumaṇattiṟkuriya caṭaṅkukaḷ atikamāka oṉṟumilli. paricappaṇattik koṭutta piṟaku, maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷit taṉ vīṭṭiṟku aḻittuk koṇṭu varuvāṉ. ataṉ piṟaku viruntu oṉṟu naṭipeṟum. ittuṭaṉ tirumaṇam muṭivaṭikiṟatu. mutal maṉivikkup piḷḷippēṟu illi yeṉṟāltāṉ, āṇkaḷ iraṇṭāvatu maṇam ceytu koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.

maṇavilakku :

maṇavilakku kōtarkaḷiṭiyē atikamāka naṭipeṟuvatilli. poṟuppaṟṟa taṉmiyum, kuṭiveṟiyum, cōmpalum, oḻukkak kuṟivum uṭiya peṇṇē maṇavilakkiṟkuriyavaḷākak karutappaṭukiṟāḷ. maṇavilakku ūriluḷḷa periyavarkaḷ muṉṉiliyil naṭipeṟum. mikavum cikkalāṉa vaḻakkāka iruntāl, kōtarkaḷ vāḻum ēḻu ūrkaḷilumuḷḷa periyōrkaḷaṭaṅkiya pañcāyattāl ārāyappaṭṭu maṇavilakku aḷikkappaṭum.

piḷḷippēṟu :

piḷḷip pēṟṟukkākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum kuṭici, iru aṟikaḷākat taṭukkappaṭṭirukkum. ōr aṟi maruttuvattiṟkuriya maṉiyākavum, maṟṟoṉṟu piḷḷippēṟṟuk kālattil taṅkuvataṟkuriya iṭamākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum. kuḻanti piṟantatum, tāy aṭutta uvā nāḷ vari ak kuṭiciyilēyē taṅkavēṇṭum. uvā nāḷaṉṟu ap peṇṇi vīṭṭukkaḻippar. aṉṟu ellārukkum oru viruntu naṭipeṟum. ūrttalivaṉ kuḻantikkup peyariṭuvāṉ. mutaṟ kuḻanti āṇāka irun tāl, ataṟkuk 'kāmaṭṭaṉ' eṉṟu taṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷ peyari iṭuvar. peṇkaḷukku ‘māṭi' eṉṟa peyarē atikamāka vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. māṭi eṉpatu avarkaḷ vaḻipaṭum oru ciṟu teyvattiṉ peyar.

camaya vāḻvu :

kōtarkaḷ vaḻipaṭum kaṭavuḷaril talimi peṟṟavaṉ kāmaṭṭarāyaṉ. kōtarkaḷiṉ camayakuru 'tēvāti' eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. camaya kuruviṉ patavi parampari urimiyuṭiyatu. kōyil paṇi puriyum pūcārikaḷik kōtarkaḷ tērnteṭukkiṉṟaṉar. tōṭarkulap pūcārikaḷukkiruppatu pōṉṟu, taṉiyuṭiyō, vēṟu kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷō ivarkaḷukkuk kiṭiyā. maṟṟa makkaḷip pōlat tirumaṇam ceytu koṇṭu, kuṭumpa vāḻkkiyil īṭupaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. kāmaṭṭarāyaṉ maṉiviyiṉ peyar 'kāciki.' kōtarkaḷ vāḻum ovvōr ūrilum ivarkaḷukkuk kōyil uṇṭu. kāmaṭṭarāyaṉukku āṇṭutōṟum peru viḻā oṉṟu naṭipeṟum. taṅkaḷ vāḻvil naṉmi peruka vēṇṭum eṉṟu iṟivaṉi vēṇṭi avviḻāvik koṇṭāṭuvar. jaṉavarit tiṅkaḷil uvā nāḷ kaḻintu, mutal tiṅkaṭkiḻamiyaṉṟu iv viḻā tuvaṅkum. piṟaku patiṉintu nāṭkaḷ toṭarntu naṭipeṟum. ip patiṉintu nāṭkaḷiyum, ovvōr āṇṭilum ōyvu nāṭkaḷāka ivarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. it tiruviḻāviṉ pōtu, āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum oṉṟākak kūṭi āṭum āṭṭam mikavum āpācamāṉatu.

arukil vāḻum paṭakarkaḷil mukkiyamāṉavarkaḷik kōtarkaḷ ivviḻāviṟku aḻippatu vaḻakkam. ivarkaḷuṭiya aḻippi ēṟṟukkoṇṭu varāviṭṭāl, acceyal perum avamāṉattiṟkuriyatākak kōtarkaḷāl karutappaṭum. avvāṟu vantu viḻāvil kalantu koḷḷāta paṭakarkaḷiṉ nilaṅkaḷil yārum paṇi purivatilli. vēṟu entac caṭaṅkukaḷilum kalantu koḷvatilli. kāmaṭṭarāyaṉ viḻā naṭipeṟum appatiṉintu nāṭkaḷum, kōyiliṉ muṉṉāl mūṭṭiya tī aṇiyāmal erintukoṇṭē irukkum. kōyiliṉ paḻiya kūriyi nīkkiviṭṭu, mūṅkiliṉāl putiya kūri vēyvārkaḷ. iṟivaṉukkuc ciṟappāṉa āṭikaḷum, talippākiyum aṇivittu alaṅkāram ceyvar. tiruviḻāk kālaṅkaḷil ellārum cērntu vēṭṭikkuc celvārkaḷ. tēvāti ōr irumput tuṇṭi neruppil pōṭṭup paḻukkak kāyccuvāṉ. pūcāri avvirumput tuṇṭic cuttiyāl aṭippāṉ. ivvāṟākap palavitac caṭaṅkukaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa.

iṟuticcaṭaṅku :

iṟakkum niliyil iruppavariṉ vāyil paṭakarkaḷip pōṉṟu vīrarāyap paṇattip pōṭṭu nīr ūṟṟum paḻakkam ivarkaḷiṭamum uṇṭu, piṇattiṉ kikaḷi mārpiṉ mēl kiṭattip peru viralkaḷi nūliṉāl kaṭṭiviṭuvārkaḷ. iṟantavariṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷ, piṇattiṟku iṟuti vaṇakkam celuttuvar. marattiṉāl pāṭiceytu, atit tuṇiyāl alaṅkarippar. alaṅkaritta appāṭiyi vīṭṭiṉ muṉṉāl vippar. uṟaviṉar ellārum aḻuvar. maṟṟiyōr icikkēṟpa āṭuvar. ōr erumiyip paliyiṭṭu, ataṉ ūṉi ellārum paṅkiṭṭuk koḷvārkaḷ. piṇatti alaṅkarittu ataṉ neṟṟiyil kācip patippārkaḷ, piṟaku irumpiṉāl ceyta ciṟu karuvikaḷiyum, arici, pukiyili, aṉṟāṭa uṇavup poruḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum piṇattōṭu pāṭiyil vippārkaḷ. aḻukiyum, āṭalum, matuvaruntalum palamaṇi nēram naṭipeṟum. kaṭiciyākap pāṭiyi iṭukāṭu nōkki eṭuttuc celluvar, iṟantavaṉ maṉivi, taṉ nakikaḷi iṭukāṭṭil kaḷintu, iṟuti vaṇakkam celuttuvāḷ, piṇattic citiyil eṭuttuvitta piṟaku, pāṭiyi aḻittu viṭuvar. piṇam erinta piṟaku, nīrūṟṟic citiyi naṉittu, elumpukaḷiyellām poṟukki oru kuḻiyil putittu mūṭi, ataṉmēl naṭukal nāṭṭuvar.

tōṭarkaḷ evvāṟu kāynta iṟutic caṭaṅku ceykiṟarkaḷō, atē pōl ticampar tiṅkaḷil kōtarkaḷum ceykiṟārkaḷ. iṟantu pōṉavariṉ vīṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉāl, caṭaṅkukaḷ naṭipeṟuvataṟku eṭṭu nāḷ muṉpiruntē tiṉamum āṭalpāṭal naṭi peṟum. kuṟippiṭṭa nāḷil uṟaviṉarkaḷ ellārum cērntu, erumiyiṉ maṇṭiyōṭṭit tuṇiyil oru mūṭṭiyākak kaṭṭip pāṭiyiṉ mēl vittu vaṇaṅkuvārkaḷ. aṉṟu piṇattōṭu vittavikaḷip pōṉṟē, iṉṟum pukiyili, arici, uṇavup poruḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum palakaṟikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa oru taṭiyiyum am mūṭṭiyōṭu iṭukāṭṭil vittu erippārkaḷ. apporuḷkaḷellām erinta piṟaku, citiyi muṉ pōlavē nīr ūṟṟi naṉippārkaḷ. ellārum avviravi iṭu kāṭṭilēyē kaḻippar. ittuṭaṉ kōtarkaḷiṉ iṟuticcaṭaṅku muṭivuṟukiṟatu.

piṟa ceytikaḷ :

tōṭarkaḷiṭiyē iruppatu pōṉṟu oru peṇ pala kaṇavari ēṟṟukkoḷḷum muṟi kōtarkaḷiṭiyē illiyeṉṟālum, ataṉiṉṟum ciṟitu māṟupaṭṭa orumuṟi ivarkaḷiṭiyē nilavi varukiṉṟatu. oru kuṭumpattil pala cakōtararkaḷ iruntāl, oruvaṉ maṟṟavaṉuṭiya maṉiviyōṭu entavita vēṟupāṭumiṉṟit toṭarpu vittuk koḷvatu vaḻakkam. iti avarkaḷ kuṟṟamākak karutuvatilli. itaṉ kāraṇamākap poṟāmiyō, pūcalō ēṟpaṭuvatilli. kōtarkaḷiṭiyē uṭaṉ piṟanta oṟṟumi mikavum atikam. avarkaḷuṭiya camūka, poruḷiyal muṟikaḷellām, cōtara oṟṟumiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyilēyē amintuḷḷaṉa. 'uṭaṉ piṟantār illātavaṉ kiyiḷittavaṉ' eṉṟa oru paḻamoḻi avarkaḷiṭiyē nilavukiṟatu. matu aruntuvatilum, apiṉi tiṉpatilum ivarkaḷukkup peruviruppam uṇṭu.

kuṟumpar :

ivarkaḷik kuṟumar eṉṟum, kuṟupar eṉṟum vēṟu peyarkaḷāl aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyiluḷḷa ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, vaya nāṭṭilum vāḻkiṟārkaḷ. immakkaḷ kuṟumpar, ūrkkuṟumpar, jēṉ kuṟumpar eṉa mūṉṟu piriviṉarākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyil kuṟumparkaḷum, nelliyāḷatti (Nellialam)c cuṟṟi ūrkkuṟumparkaḷum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. jēṉ kuṟumparkaḷ, 'ṣōlā nāyakkar'kaḷ eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ vaya nāṭṭilum, kuṟippāka matumalip pakutiyilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.

ēḻu, eṭṭu, oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil, kāñciyit talinakarākak koṇṭu, pallavar eṉṟa iṉattār tamiḻakattic ciṟappāka āṇṭu vantaṉar. avarkaḷukkum kuṟumparkaḷ eṉṟa vēṟu peyaruṇṭu. māmallapuram. ciṟṟaṉṉavācal, kuṭumiyāṉ mali, nāmakkal, tirucci ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kuṭikōyilkaḷum, kāñci nakariluḷḷa peruṅkōyilkaḷum avarkaḷuṭiya, kaliyiyum nākarikattiyum paṟicāṟṟiya vaṇṇam iṉṟum kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattil peruñcōḻa (Imperial Cholas)riṉ āṭci tamiḻakattil ōṅkiyapōtu, pallavarkaḷiṉ pēraracu citintu, cōḻap pēraracōṭu oṉṟiviṭṭatu. cōḻariṭam tōlviyuṟṟa pallavarkaḷ, nīlakiri maliyil ōṭi oḷintaṉareṉṟum, avar vaḻi vantōrē iṉṟuḷḷa kuṟumpar eṉṟum cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. immuṭivu ēṟṟukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatāka illi. ēṉeṉṟāl pallavarkaḷ nākarikamum, kaliyuṇarvum mikutiyākak koṇṭavarkaḷ; vaṭa moḻiyiyō, ataṉ cārpu moḻiyiyō tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavarkaḷ; vaṭamoḻik kalikaḷi vaḷarttavarkaḷ. āṉāl nīlakirik kuṟumparkaḷ nākarikamum kaliyuṇarvum aṟṟavarkaḷ. tirāviṭa moḻiyit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavarkaḷ. pallavarkaḷ poṉṉiṟa mēṉiyum, aḻakiya tōṟṟamum vāykkap peṟṟavarkaḷ. āṉāl kuṟumparkaḷō, kaṟutta uṭalum, aḻakaṟṟa tōṟṟamum koṇṭavarkaḷ. pallavarkaḷ cōḻa nāṭṭi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiya kālam pattām nūṟṟāṇṭu. tōṭarkaḷiṉ varukikkup piṉ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻittē kuṟumparkaḷ nīlakiri maliyil kuṭiyēṟiṉar. eṉavē, nīlakirik kuṟumparkaḷ pallavarkaḷāka irukka muṭiyātu.

ūrkkuṟumparit tavira maṟṟa iru iṉattārum kūccam koḷḷum iyalpiṉar. āṉāl kōḻikaḷalla. veḷḷiyarik kaṇṭāl muṉpellām ōṭi oḷintu koḷvārkaḷ. kuṟumpar vāḻum ciṟṟūr 'mōṭṭā' eṉṟum, 'kampi' eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. ippoḻutu 'kulakkampi, mañcak kampi' eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟumpar vāḻvatilli. āṉāl mutalil avviṭaṅkaḷilum vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati appeyarkaḷāl aṟiyalām. ovvoru kampiyilum, cumār paṉṉiraṇṭu kuṭicikaḷ irukkum. ikkuṭicikaḷ maṇṇiṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭup puṟkaḷāl kūri vēyappaṭṭavi.

tōṟṟam :

kuṟumparkaḷ karuniṟamum, kuḷḷamāṉa uruvamum, oṟṟi nāṭi uṭalum peṟṟu, tōṟṟattil iruḷarkaḷippōl viḷaṅkukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ taliyiluḷḷa mayir taṭippākavum curuṇṭum irukkum; taliyic cuṟṟip putar pōlap paravi muḷittirukkum, peṇkaḷ orē oru tuṇiyi āṭiyāka aṇikiṉṟaṉar. mārpi mūṭumpaṭiyāka akkuḷic cuṟṟi attuṇiyi muṭintu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. muḻantāḷ variyil attuṇi kīḻē maṟittukkoṇṭirukkiṟatu.

moḻi ;

kuṟumparkaḷ pēcum moḻi kaṉṉaṭattōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyateṉṟum, citinta kaṉṉaṭamē ammoḻi eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl tirāviṭa moḻiyārāycci vallunarāṉa kālṭuvel ammoḻiyic citinta tamiḻ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.



toḻil :

kuṟumparkaḷ tōṭarkaḷippōl mēyccalkārarkaḷumalla; paṭarkaḷip pōl nilakkiḻārkaḷumalla, ivarkaḷ kōtarkaḷip pōl ēḻikaḷ. piṟa iṉattārkkup paṇipurintē vāḻpavarkaḷ, tōṭarkaḷukku ivarkaḷ añcuvatumilli; maṟṟavarkaḷip pōl 'kuṭu' koṭuppatumilli. kōtarkaḷōṭu cērntu maṟṟiyōrkku icippaṇi purintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mantira vittiyil ivarkaḷ mikavum vallavarkaḷ. itaṉālēyē ivarkaḷik kaṇṭu maṟṟa iṉattār mikavum añcukiṉṟaṉar. 'paṭakar' vāḻum ovvoru ciṟṟūrilum, avarkaḷukkut tuṇipurivataṟkākak kuṟumparkaḷi amarttik koḷḷuvatuṇṭu. nilatti mutaṉ mutalāka uḻum caṭaṅkiyum, mutal vitiyi vitikkum caṭaṅkiyum, muṟṟiya payiri mutaṉ mutalāka aṟukkum caṭaṅkiyum nikaḻttivikkap paṭakarkaḷāl ivarkaḷ aḻikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. paṭakarkaḷiṉ nilattil paṇṇiyāṭkaḷākavum kuṟumparkaḷ paṇipurikiṉṟaṉar, kāl naṭikaḷukkum, payirkaḷukkum ēṟpaṭum nōyit tīrppataṟkup paṭakarkaḷ ivarkaḷiyē nāṭavēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. ippōtu kuṟumparkaḷiṭam paṭakarkaḷ koṇṭirukkum accam ciṟitu kuṟintuviṭṭatu eṉalām. paṇṭikkālattil paṭakarkaḷ koṇṭirunta accattip paṟṟi tiru. meṭs (Mr. metz) eṉṟa airōppiyar kuṟippiṭum pōtu, “oru paṭakaṉ taṉiyāka oru kuṟumpaṉik kāṭṭiṟkuḷ etirpārātavitamākak kāṇa nēriṭṭāl, añci naṭuṅki appoḻutē uyirviṭṭuviṭuvāṉ" eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.

jēṉ kuṟumparkaḷ mali iṭukkukaḷil uḷḷa malit tēṉīyiṉ kūṭṭi aḻittut tēṉ eṭuppatil vallavarkaḷ, iti ivarkaḷ toḻilākavum koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. jēṉu eṉṟāl kaṉṉaṭattil tēṉ eṉṟu poruḷ. ākiyiṉāl ikkuṟumparkaḷ taṅkaḷit “tēṉeṭukkum kāṭṭut talivarkaḷ" (jēṉu koyyō ṣōlā nāyakā) eṉṟu aḻittuk koḷkiṟārkaḷ.

aka vāḻkki :

kuṟumpar kulattil oru peṇ paruvamaṭintatum, appeṇṇiṉ mūtta cakōtaraṉ putitāka oru kuṭiciyi amippatu vaḻakkam. atil appeṇṇik koṇṭupōy vippārkaḷ. uṟaviṉarkaḷ nāḷ tōṟum kuṭicikkuc ceṉṟu appeṇṇip pārppārkaḷ. paruvamaṭinta pattām nāḷ appeṇṇik kuḷippāṭṭi vīṭṭiṟ kaḻippārkaḷ. atti makaḷē perumpālum maṇattiṟkuriyavaḷākat tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṟāḷ. tirumaṇam peṟṟōriṉ icivuṭaṉēyē naṭipeṟum. maṇamakaḷiṉ tāyār peṇṇukkut tāliyum, maṇamakaṉukkup puttāṭiyum aḷippāḷ. maṇamakaṉiṉ peṟṟōr peṇṇukkuk kūṟippuṭaviyum, pittaḷiyiṉāl ceyta mōtiraṅkaḷum vaḷiyalkaḷum aḷippar. maṇap peṇṇiṉ tāy tāliyik kiyileṭuttu makaḷiṉ kaḻuttil kaṭṭuvāḷ. piṟaku ellārum makiḻcciyuṭaṉ viruntuṇpar. viruntiṉpōtu maṇamakkaḷukku orē uṇkalattil uṇavu parimāṟappaṭum. uṇaviṉ oru pakutiyi maṇamakaṉ maṇap peṇṇukkum maṇappeṇ maṇamakaṉukkum ūṭṭa vēṇṭum. viruntu muṭintatum maṇamakkaḷit taṉiyaṟiyil viṭṭuviṭṭu ellārum pirintu celvar.

maṇavilakku kuṟumparkaḷiṭiyē aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟatu. āṇukkē maṇavilakkuk kōra urimiyuṇṭu; peṇṇukkuk kiṭiyātu. maṟumaṇamum, kimpeṇ maṇamum ivarkaḷiṭiyē naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. im maṇaṅkaḷil peṇvīṭṭārē māppiḷḷi tēṭavēṇṭum. maṟumaṇattiṟku enta vitac caṭaṅkum kiṭiyātu. peṟṟōr icivu kiṭittavuṭaṉ, taṉik kuṭittaṉam ceyya vēṇṭiyatu tāṉ.

iṟutic caṭaṅku :

kuṟumparkaḷiṉ iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷum paṭakarkaḷiṉ caṭaṅkukaḷiyē ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷum vīrarāyap paṇatti iṟappavaṉ vāyil pōṭṭu nīr ūṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. tēramittu atil piṇattik kiṭatti iṭukāṭṭiṟku eṭuttuccellukiṉṟaṉar. mikavum vayatu mutirntavarkaḷi erittuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟavarkaḷi uṭkārnta niliyil putittu naṭukal nāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. ēḻām nāḷ iṟantavariṉ kuṭumpattic cērnta ellārum iṭukāṭṭiṟkuc celvar. oru mutiyavar kuḻipaṟittu atil oru pulli naṭuvār. mūṅkiliṉāl ceyta oru kalattil nīri nirappi, atil iraṇṭu coṭṭu viḷakkeṇṇey viṭuvārkaḷ. ivviraṇṭu coṭṭu viḷakkeṇṇeyum tolivil vilakiyiruntāl, tīccakuṉam eṉṟu karuti, vēṟu entac caṭaṅkum ceyyamāṭṭārkaḷ. oṉṟu cērntāl naṟcakuṉamākak karuti, annīrik kīḻē koṭṭi viṭuvārkaḷ. piṟaku putuppāṉi oṉṟu vāṅkip 'pillālā' kōyilil vippārkaḷ, uṟaviṉarkaḷ yārēṉum camātiyik kaṭantu cella nēriṭṭāl veṟṟili, pākku, pukiyili mutaliyavaṟṟi vittu vaṇaṅkiviṭṭuc celvar. iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ muṭiyum vari, maḻi kālattil camātiyiṉ mēl pantal pōṭuvārkaḷ!

piṟa ceytikaḷ :

kuṟumparkaḷ kallāttā (peṇ teyvam), airu pilli, kāṭupillālā eṉṟa mūṉṟu teyvaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. airupilliyum, kāṭupillālāvum maliyāḷattiliruntu vayanāṭṭil kuṭipukunta teyvaṅkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nīlakiriyiluḷḷa maṟṟa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiviṭa, jēṉ kuṟumparkaḷum mantirattil vallavarkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ niṉittapōtu kāṭṭāṉikaḷi aḻikkum āṟṟal mikkavarkaḷeṉṟum, cila marmamāṉa mūlikikaḷi vīcip pāṟikaḷikkūṭap poṭi ceytu viṭukiṟārkaḷ eṉṟum kati kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. camūkac caṭṭaṅkaḷi amul naṭatta ovvoru ūrilum oru pañcāyattu uḷḷatu. ap pañcāyattiṉ talivar 'ecamāṉ' eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟār. tōṭarkaḷum paṭakarkaḷum oṉṟu cērntu kuṟumparkaḷukku aṭikkaṭi tolli koṭuppatuṇṭu. kuṟumparkaḷ vāḻum ūrkaḷil pukuntu, kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāka avarkaḷik koṉṟuviṭuvārkaḷ. it takiya paṭukolikaḷ, ki. pi. 1824, 1835, 1875, 1882, 1900 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil naṭipeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṟumparkaḷ ciṟanta villāḷikaḷ. kōṭṭiyam maṉṉarukkum āṅkilēyarukkum pōr ēṟpaṭṭa pōtu, kōṭṭiya maṉṉariṉ cārpiliruntu kuṟumparkaḷ vīrappōr purintaṉar.

vayanāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷ :

nīlakiri maliyiluḷḷa vaya nāṭṭu vaṭṭattil vāḻpavarkaḷ āliyāl, ivarkaḷ vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ, ivarkaḷ toḻilāl uḻavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ maliyāḷa moḻi pēcuvatōṭu, maliyāḷattiluḷḷa marumakkaḷ tāya muṟiyiyum kaṭippiṭittu oḻukukiṉṟaṉar. vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ kōvi māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tārāpurattiliruntu nīlakiri malimītu kuṭipukunta veḷḷāḷac ceṭṭiyarē āvar. eṉavē ivarkaḷ iṉattāl tamiḻar, vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷiṭiyē iraṇṭuvitat tirumaṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. mutalvakit tirumaṇattiṉpaṭi, peṇṇāṉavaḷ kaṇavaṉiṭam toṭarpu (marital relations} koḷḷuvāḷ ; āṉāl kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu vāḻamāṭṭāḷ. maṟṟoru vakit tirumaṇam ‘mālik kalyāṇam' eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉpaṭi maṉivi kaṇavaṉōṭu vāḻa aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟāḷ. marumakkaḷ tāya muṟiyē it tirumaṇa vēṟupāṭukaḷukkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu collalām. ivarkaḷ vaya nāṭṭup pīṭapūmiyil, iḻintuvarum nīraruvikaḷiṉ nīrippāycci nel viḷivikkiṉṟaṉar. atōṭu puṉceyt tāṉiyaṅkaḷiyum viḷivikkiṉṟaṉar. vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷiṉ camutāyattil aintu kuṭumpaṅkaḷ talimipeṟṟa kuṭumpaṅkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkuṭumpattāriṉ āṇivaḻi ellārum oḻukukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ ciṟanta uḻippāḷikaḷ ; kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟ kaṭaṅkiyavarkaḷ ; amitiyāṉa vāḻkkiyi uṭiyavarkaḷ ; vēṭṭiyil viruppamuṭiyavarkaḷ; pulikaḷi vali pōṭṭup piṭittu, īṭṭiyāl kuttik kollum vēṭṭi muṟiyil kitērntavarkaḷ. vayanāṭṭuc ceṭṭiyaril palar nilakkiḻārkaḷāka iruntālum, perumpālōr ēḻikaḷ. cultāṉ pēṭṭariyilirukkum māppiḷḷikaḷiṭam ellārum kaṭaṉpaṭṭu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. (māppiḷḷikaḷeṉpōr maliyāḷattu muslīmkaḷ, arēpiyāviliruntu maliyāḷakkaṭaṟkariyil kuṭiyēṟi aṅkēyē nilittuviṭṭavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ cēra nāṭṭut tamiḻp peṇkaḷi maṇantu, namakku māppiḷḷikaḷāki viṭṭaṉar pōlum. vaya nāṭṭil periya vaṭṭikkaṭikkārarkaḷāka ivarkaḷ viḷaṅkukiṟārkaḷ). vaya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭikaḷ peruṅ kuṭiyarkaḷ.

piṟa ceytikaḷ :

nīlakiriyil paṉiyar, iruḷar eṉṟu vēṟucila paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉarum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ ellārum maṟṟa iṉattārkkup paṇipurintu varukiṉṟaṉar. kuṟumparkaḷ iruḷarkaḷik kaṇṭu mikavum añcuvārkaḷ. kāraṇam ivarkaḷum periya mantiravātikaḷ eṉpatē. iruḷar kulattic cērnta peṇ, veḷiyil vēlikkākac cellumpōtu, peṇ pulikaḷit taṉ kuḻantikaḷukkuk kāvalāka vittuviṭṭuc celluvāḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

malikaḷil vāḻum ip paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ nalaṉukkāka, mattiya aracāṅkamum, ceṉṉi aracāṅkamum niṟiyap poruṭ celavu ceykiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ kalvikkāka rū. 10.67 ilaṭcam otukkiyuḷḷaṉar. mutal aintāṇṭut tiṭṭattiṉpaṭi māṇavarkaḷ taṅkip paṭikkak kūṭiya 9 paḷḷikaḷum, 11 cātāraṇap paḷḷikaḷum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭattil, 16 paḷḷikaḷum tuvakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivikaḷaṉṟi 4 taṅkum viṭutikaḷum naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

malivāḻ makkaḷil perumpālōr uḻavuta toḻil ceyya nilam illātavarkaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉar. orucilar tām uḻavut toḻilil īṭupaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. uḻavut toḻil ceyvataṟkum, uḻavuk karuvikaḷ vāṅkuvataṟkum avarkaḷiṭam paṇavacati kiṭiyātu. ākavē uḻavu māṭukaḷ, uḻavuk karuvikaḷ, vitikaḷ mutaliyaṉavāṅkuvataṟkāka ivarkaḷukku māṉiyaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu rū 5.8 ilaṭcam otukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

cila malivāḻ makkaḷ kūṭimuṭital, taccuvēli mutaliya ciṟu kittoḻilkaḷic ceytal ākiyavaṟṟil vallavarkaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉar. it tuṟiyil avarkaḷukku utaviyaḷippataṟkāka rū 2.75 ilaṭcam otukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyiluḷḷa kōtakiriyil orupayiṟci uṟpatti niliyam tuvakkavum, cila kuṭicit toḻilkaḷit tuvakkavum inta niti payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum.

malivāḻ makkaḷ nalla kāṟṟuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻntuvanta pōtilum, nalvāḻvu naṭattavilli. malēriyāk kāyccaliṉāl ivarkaḷ kaṭumiyākat tākkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl palar āṇṭutōṟum iṟantuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. nīlakiri maliyiluḷḷa tōṭarkaḷ tōlnōyiṉāl pīṭikkappaṭṭu āṇṭutōṟum makkaḷ tokiyil kuṟintuvantaṉar. nīlakiri māvaṭṭap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷukkāka oru naṭamāṭum maruttuvamaṉi {mobile dispensary) iyaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. malivāḻ makkaḷ irukkum ellāp pakutikaḷukkum immaruttuvamaṉi ceṉṟu paṇipurivataṉ mūlam tōṭarkaḷiṭiyē paravivanta tōlnōy aṟavē nīṅkiviṭṭatu. koyiṉā maruntu ellārukkum vaḻaṅkuvataṉ mūlam malēriyāvum kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ taṇṇīr niṟiyak kiṭikkak kūṭiya malippakutikaḷil vāḻnta pōtilum, tūymiyāṉa kuṭi taṇṇīr kiṭikkāmal varuntukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē ellā māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilumuḷḷa malivāḻ makkaḷukkuk kuṭi taṇṇīr vacatikaḷ ceyvataṟkāka iraṇṭāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭattil rū 80,000 otukkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

malivāḻ makkaḷ ciṟukuṭicikaḷil kuṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. akkuṭicikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷum taṅkamuṭiyātapaṭi mikavum kēvalamāṉa niliyil uḷḷaṉa. nalla kuṭiyiruppukaḷ illāmal avarkaḷ nalattuṭaṉ vāḻvatu eppaṭi? ākavē vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭa rū 5.53 ilaṭcam otukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru vīṭṭiṟkum rū 900 celaviṭa muṭivu ceytuḷḷaṉar.



iraṇṭāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭattil nīlakiri, cēlam māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa malikaḷiṉmītu cāli vacatikaḷip perukkuvataṟkāka rū. 37,000 celaviṭappaṭum. āṭikaḷ mutaliya itara celaviṉaṅkaḷukkāka rū 77,000 otukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittiṭṭaṅkaḷellām nalla muṟiyil amulākkappaṭṭāl, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ vāḻvu ciṟappaṭiyum eṉpatil aiyamilli.

kuṭiyēṟṟam :

mutaṉ mutalākak ki. pi. 1602 ām āṇṭil oru pōrttukīciyap pātiriyār, nīlakiri maliyik kāṇpataṟkāka vantār. avar peyar ḵperrīri (Ferreiri). nīlakiriyil vāḻum tōṭarkaḷōṭu uriyāṭiyatōṭu amiyāmal, avarkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷukkup pala paricilkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉār. mikavum toṉmiyāṉa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa tōṭarkaḷ vāḻum ceyti, appoḻututāṉ camaveḷiyil vāḻum makkaḷukkut terintatu.

ḵperrīrikkup piṟaku nīlakiri maliyi ārāyvataṟku, 200āṇṭukaḷ variyil yārum muyaṟci eṭuttuk koḷḷavilli. ṭākṭar puccāṉaṉ (Dr. Buchanan) eṉpavar, intiya araciyalāriṉ āṇiyi mēṟkoṇṭu, micūr nāṭṭilum ataṟku aṇmiyilum uḷḷa malikaḷi, vāṇipa nōkkōṭum, payirt toḻil nōkkōṭum ārāyvataṟkāka vantār. nīlakiri maliyiyum cuṟṟip pārttār. oru nāḷil evvāṟu ati ārāyamuṭiyum? nīlakiri maliyip paṟṟi avar koṭutta kuṟippukaḷ payaṉaṟṟavi ; mēṟpōkkāṉavi. ṭākṭar puccāṉaṉ ceṉṟu tirumpiyapiṉ, 12 āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu nīlakiri maliyi viññāṉa nōkkōṭu ārāyvataṟkāka tiru. kīs (Keys) eṉṟa ōr ārāycciyāḷariyum, avarukkut tuṇiyāka makmakāṉ (Macmahon) eṉṟa oruvariyum kōvi māvaṭṭat taṇṭalar aṉuppivittār. āṉāl avarkaḷ aḷitta kuṟippukaḷ conta ārāycciyiṉāl eḻuntavikaḷalla. piṟar vāyilākak kēṭṭaṟintaviyē. eṉavē avarkaḷuṭiya ārāycciyum payaṉaṟṟatāka oḻintatu.

āṟu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintapiṉṉar, kōvi māvaṭṭattiṉ taṇṭalarākap paṇiyāṟṟiyavarum, nīlakirimaliyiṉ varalāṟṟil mukkiya iṭam peṟṟavarumāṉa tiruvāḷar jāṉ callivaṉ eṉpār, ārāycci viruppum, viṭā muyaṟciyum koṇṭa iruvari nīlakiri malikku aṉuppiṉār. avarkaḷ ārāyntu veḷiyiṭṭa karuttukkaḷ taṇṭalariṉ karutti īrttaṉa. avarum nīlakirikkuc ceṉṟār. ceṉṟatōṭu amiyāmal, oru nalla iṭattit tērnteṭuttu, kōṭiyil taṅkuvataṟkuriya kuḷirmaṉi yoṉṟi eḻuppuvataṟkāṉa muyaṟcikaḷil īṭupaṭṭār. nīlakirimaliyiṉ mītu kuṭiyēṟiya mutal airōppiyar ivarē.

tiruvāḷar jāṉ callivaṉ nīlakiri malikkuc ceṉṟa āṇṭu ki. pi. 1819 ākum. nīlakiri maliyi uṇmiyil ārāyntu piṟarukku veḷiyiṭṭavar ivarē eṉil, atu mikiyākātu. ippōtu utaka maṇṭalam amintirukkumiṭatti tiru. callivaṉ mutalil kaṇṭatum, avviṭattiṉ iyaṟkiyaḻakum. taṭpa veppa niliyum avar uḷḷatti vekuvākak kavarntaṉa. uṭaṉē, avviṭam oru kuṟiñci nakaram amippataṟkuriya ciṟanta takutikaḷiyellām peṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉṟa ceytiyi araciyalārukku aṟivittār. ki. pi. 1823-ām āṇṭu tām taṅkiyiruppataṟkāka ōraḻakiya maṉiyi eḻuppiṉār. atu ippoḻutu 'kalmaṉi' (Stone House) eṉṟa peyarāl aḻik ppaṭukiṟatu. utaka maṇṭalattiluḷḷa canti veḷiyiṉarukil, uyaramāṉa iṭattil cila māṟutalkaḷip peṟṟu iṉṟum kalmaṉi kaṇṇikkavarum tōṟṟattōṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

ki. pi. 1824 ām āṇṭiliruntu airōppiyarkaḷ perum aḷavil nīlakiri mali mītu kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅkiṉar. kōṭikkālaṅkaḷil, ceṉṉi mānila āḷunarum (governor) iṅku vantu taṅkat toṭaṅkiṉār. āḷunar āṇṭutōṟum vantu taṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu araciyalār ōraḻakiya māḷiki eḻuppiṉar. ammāḷiki mutalil ' 'araciyal maṉi' (Government House) eṉṟa peyarāl aḻikkappaṭṭatu. ippoḻutu ati 'nārvuṭ maṉi' (Norwood House) eṉṟu ellārum aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. pakkiṅkām pirapu (Duke of Buckingham) ceṉṉi mānila āḷunarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya pōtu, araciyal maṉiyiṉ vaḷarccikkup peritum pāṭupaṭṭār. ammāḷikiyic cuṟṟi aḻakāṉa tōṭṭattiyum amittār. māḷikiyilum tōṭṭattilum paṉipurivōriṉ aluvalkaḷi aṭikkaṭi kaṇkāṇittār. ivaruṭiya iṭiyaṟāta taliyīṭṭi appaṇiyāḷarkaḷ periya tolliyākak kūṭak karutiṉar. ōyvu nāṭkaḷil eḷiya uṭiyaṇintu cuṟṟi varuvār pirapu. orunāḷ kāliyil, mukac cavaram kūṭac ceytu koḷḷāmal, alaṅkōlamāṉa tōṟṟattōṭu tōṭṭattic cuṟṟi vantār. ōr airōppiya vēlikkāraṉ pātti amittukkoṇṭiruntāṉ. pirapu avaṉarukil ceṉṟu, "ēṉappā, ippāttiyi ippaṭi amittāl naṉṟāyirukku mallavā? ic ceṭikaḷi ēṉ ippaṭi naṭak kūṭātu?" eṉṟu viṉaviṉār. av vēlikkāraṉ pirapuvi attakiya tōṟṟattil itaṟku muṉ pārttatu kiṭiyātu. vēṟu, yārō vaḻippōkkar eṉṟu eṇṇiṉāṉ. avaṉ avari nimirntu kūṭap pārkkavumilli ; avaruṭiya viṉākkaḷic ceviyil vāṅkik koḷḷavumilli. pirapu mēlum mēlum viṉavavē, avaṉ poṟumi yiḻantāṉ. uṭaṉē tali nimirntu, "ēṉayyā! uṉakku vēṟu vēliyilliyā? um vaḻiyip pārttukkoṇṭu pōm. pakkiṅkām pirapu eṉṟa eṇṇam pōlirukkiṟatu!" eṉṟu ciṉanturittāṉ. uṭaṉē pirapu taṉṉi maṟantu cirittār. vēlikkāraṉ avari uṭaṉē yāreṉṟu uṇarntu koṇṭāṉ. taṉ ceyalukkāka varunti maṉṉippuk kēṭṭukkoṇṭāṉ.

araciyal maṉikkuc caṟṟu mēlē, kāṭṭilākā atikāri oruvarāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kuṭici yoṉṟuḷḷatu. araciyal maṉiyil taṅkiyirunta āḷunariṉ kuḻantikaḷ aṅkuc ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭuvatu vaḻakkam. ippoḻutu atu iṉpa viruntu (Picnic) uṇṇa mikavum ēṟṟa iṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

utakamaṇṭalam

peyark kāraṇam :

utaka maṇṭalam eṉṟu innakarukkup peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu paṟṟi oru cevi vaḻic ceyti ulavu kiṉṟatu. 'maṇṭu' eṉṟa col tōṭar vāḻum ciṟṟūrik kuṟikkum eṉṟu muṉpē kuṟippiṭṭēṉ. tiruvāḷar callivaṉ kaṭṭiya kalmaṉi amintirukkum iṭam, muṉpu oru maṇṭuvāka iruntatām. ovvoru maṇṭuvilum vaḻakkamāka nāṉkintu kuṭicikaḷ irukkum. āṉāl inta maṇṭuvil orē oru kuṭicitāṉ iruntatām. pārtki (Parth-Kai) eṉṟa oru vayatu mutirnta tōṭar kulac celvaṉ ak kuṭiciyil taṉ kuṭumpattārōṭu vāḻntu vantāṉ, celvarkaḷāka irukkum tōṭar vīṭṭiṉ etirē ciṟitu tolivil oru kal naṭappaṭṭirukkum. ataṉmēl taṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷukku uṇavu paṭippatu avarkaḷ vaḻakkam.

tiruvāḷar callivaṉ mutaṉ mutalāka nīlakiri malikku vantapoḻutu, pārtkiyik kaṇṭu pēciṉār. pārtki kal nāṭṭappaṭṭirunta avviṭattik kāṭṭi, “jellōkō e maṇṭu" eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉām. “kal nāṭṭappaṭirukkum ic ciṟṟūri nī vittuk koḷ” eṉpatu attoṭariṉ poruḷ. 'jel lōkō' eṉṟa toṭar moḻic col, taṉiyāka niṟkum oru kalliyō, allatu kal nāṭṭappaṭṭa ciṟṟūriyō kuṟippiṭum. ac colliṉ tamiḻ moḻi peyarppu, oṟṟikkal maṇṭu eṉpatākum. oṟṟikkal maṇṭu eṉṟa peyar ottikkal maṇṭu eṉa māṟi, ippoḻutu 'uṭakamaṇṭ' (Ootacamund) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

vēṟu cilar ippeyarip paṭakarkaḷōṭu toṭarpu paṭuttukiṉṟaṉar, paṭakar, vaṭakkiluḷḷa micūr nāṭṭiliruntu vantatāl vaṭakar eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar eṉpati muṉpē kuṟippiṭṭēṉ, vaṭakar vantu kuṭiyēṟiya avviṭam • vaṭakamaṇṭu' eṉṟu tōṭarkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭatām, piṟaku appeyar ‘vaṭṭayāka maṇṭu,' ' 'vaṭṭaca maṇṭu' eṉap palavitamāka māṟi, ippoḻutu uṭakamaṇṭ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatākavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu cilar maṟṟoru kāraṇamum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ikkāraṇam ēṟṟuk koḷḷak kūṭiyatākavum uḷḷatu. 'utakamaṇṭalam' eṉpatē ātiyiliruntu ivvūrukku vaḻaṅkum peyar eṉpatu ak koḷki, utakamaṇṭalam eṉṟāl paṭakar moḻiyil 'eppōtum maḻi peytu koṇṭirukkumiṭam' eṉpatu poruḷ.



vaḻi :

utakamaṇṭalatti iru vaḻikaḷil vantaṭiyalām. micūriliruntu malivaḻip pātiyil 100 kal untu vaṇṭiyiṉ mūlam ceṉṟu utaka maṇṭalatti aṭiyalām. kōviyiliruntu mēṭṭup pāḷiyam vaḻiyākavum ceṉṟaṭiyalām. micūriliruntu varum vaḻi kaṉṉaṭak kāṭṭil (Kanara forest) pukuntu varukiṟatu. ivvaḻi aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷik koṇṭatu; uḷḷattiṟku iṉpattip payappatu. kōviyiliruntu utaka maṇṭalam cella untu vaṇṭikaḷum niṟiya uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl pukivaṇṭiyil ēṟi, mēṭṭup pāḷiyam vaḻiyākac ceṉṟāl nīlakiriyiṉ malivaḷatti nalla muṟiyil kaṇṭu iṉpuṟalām. pukivaṇṭic celavu nam poṟumiyi aḷavukku mīṟic cōtittuviṭum. puki vaṇṭi mikavum metuvākac cellum. mēlum iṭiyiluḷḷa puki vaṇṭi niliyaṅkaḷil aṭikkaṭi niṉṟu viṭum. maṟṟa puki vaṇṭikaḷukkum nīlakiri malimēl cellum puki vaṇṭikaḷukkum oru vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. ivvaṇṭikaḷil iyaṅki (engine) muṉṉāl poruttappaṭuvatilli, piṉṉiruntu vaṇṭiyit taḷḷukiṟatu. ivvaṇṭiyiluḷḷa mutal peṭṭi tiṟanta pakkaṅkaḷiyuṭiya tāka irukkum. ākiyiṉāl ippeṭṭiyil iṭam piṭikka ellāp pirayāṇikaḷum pōṭṭiyiṭuvar. ēṉeṉṟāl appeṭṭiyil uṭkārntiruppōr maliccarivukaḷil uḷḷa iyaṟkik kāṭcikaḷi naṉku. pārttu makiḻa muṭiyum.

vaḷarcci :

araciyal maṉi (Government House) kaṭṭappaṭṭatum, cutēca maṉṉarkaḷum celvantarkaḷum utakamaṇṭalattil periya periya māḷikikaḷi eḻuppat toṭaṅkiṉar. ippōtu ūṭṭiyil ēṟakkuṟiya 5000 vīṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iv vīṭukaḷellām vaṇṇam pūcappaṭṭup pēraḻakuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

innakariṉ makkaḷ toki perukikkoṇṭē iruppatālum, putiya putiya illaṅkaḷ tōṉṟik koṇṭē iruppatālum nakariṉ vacatikaḷiyum perukka vēṇṭiya nili ēṟpaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1826-ām āṇṭu , mutal añcal maṉi {Post-office) tōṟṟu vikkappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1829-ām āṇṭu mutal maruttuvamaṉi kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1863-ām āṇṭu nakarāṭci maṉṟam ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. taṇṭalar aluvalakam ki. pi. 1866-ām āṇṭu niṟuvappaṭṭatu. 1878-ām āṇṭu 40,000 nūlkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru nūlakam ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa mikavum ciṟanta nūlakaṅkaḷil ituvum oṉṟākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. cevvāykkiḻami tōṟum aṅkuc canti kūṭukiṟatu. utakamaṇṭalattil vāḻum āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum aṅkuk kūṭi, oru vārattiṟku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷi vāṅkic cellum kāṭci uṇmiyilēyē uḷḷak kiḷarcciyūṭṭum taṉmiyatu. 'cārmiṅ kirās' (Charming Cross) eṉpatu utakamaṇṭalattiṉ naṭuviṭam. ammuṉiyil nāṉku puṟamum paravic coriyum ceyaṟki nīr ūṟṟu (Flora Fountin) oṉṟu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kārkālattil avvūṟṟu kaḷinaṭam puriyum. avviṭattic cuṟṟi aḻakiya kaṭikaḷ variciyāka uḷḷaṉa.

makkaḷ toki perukiyatum pala kaḻakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiṉa. ki. pi. 1841-ām āṇṭu ūṭṭik kaḻakam (Ooty Club) tōṉṟiyatu. ataṉ uṟuppiṉarkaḷellām veḷḷiyarē. tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷukkākavē ik kaḻakam amikkappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1869-ām āṇṭu jimkāṉā kaḻakam (Gymkhana Club) tōṉṟiyatu. atu ippoḻutu ūṭṭi kuḻippantāṇṭak kaḻakam (Golf Club) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ūṭṭiyiluḷḷa vēṭṭiyar kaḻakam (Hunt Club) ippoḻutu ciṟanta muṟiyil uḷḷatu. ivikaḷēyaṉṟi vēṟu cila kaḻakaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil kuṟippiṭat takkatu lāli niṟuvaṉam (Lawly Institute) eṉpatākum, kōṭik kālattil maṭṭip pantāṭṭap (Tennis Championship) pōṭṭiyi itu ciṟanta muṟiyil naṭatti vikkiṟatu.

kōṭiyil intiya nāṭṭiṉ pala pakutikaḷiliruntum makkaḷ peruntiraḷāka iṅku vantu kūṭukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē avarkaḷuṭiya vacatikkākap pala ciṟanta uṇavu viṭutikaḷum, taṅkal maṉikaḷum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kōṭiyil taṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu vīṭukaḷ kuṭik kūlikku viṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

ciṟappu :

nīlakiri maliyiṉ parappu 900 catura mil ākum. itaṉmītu amintuḷḷa kuṟiñci nakaramāṉa utakamaṇṭalam iyaṟkiyaḻakil īṭu iṇiyaṟṟatu. car ci. pi. irāmacāmi aiyar oru camayam, “ulakilēyē mikavum aḻakiya kuṟiñci nakaram utakamaṇṭalam" eṉṟu pārāṭṭip pēciṉār. 'kuṟiñci nakaraṅkaḷiṉ araci' (Queen of Hill stations) eṉṟa ciṟappup peyarukku utakamaṇṭalam takutiyuṭiyatu. ekkālattum itu taṉ ciṟappuk kuṉṟāmal viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ēpral toṭaṅki jūṉ tiṅkaḷ muṭiyum vari itippōl makiḻcciyum malarcciyum tāṇṭavamāṭum iṭam etiyum intiya nāṭṭil kāṇa muṭiyātu. intiya nāṭṭiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷiliruntum, iṉpatti nāṭuvōr iṅku vantu kuvikiṉṟaṉar. viruntuṇṭum, vēṭṭiyāṭiyum, āṭal nikaḻttiyum, kutirip pantayattil paṅku koṇṭum, kuḻippantu maṭṭip pantāṭṭap pōṭṭikaḷik kaṇṭu kaḻittum, mīṉ piṭittum, malikaḷil cuṟṟit tirintum, ēriyil tōṇiyūrntum, pacumpuṟṟariyil paṭuttup puraṇṭum, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷik kaṇṭu kaḷittum, kuṟiñciyaḻakil uḷḷattip paṟikoṭuttum poḻuti iṉpamākak kaḻikkiṉṟaṉar. utakamaṇṭalattiṉ taṭpa veppanili iḷiñar, mutiyōr, nōyvāyppaṭṭōr ākiya ellārukkum ēṟṟatāka amintuḷḷatu. innakaril kār kālam eṉṟu taṉiyāka oru kālam kiṭiyātu. āṇṭu muḻuvatum ciṟu tūṟalkaḷāka maḻi tūṟikkoṇṭē irukkum. āṇṭukku 30 mutal 40 aṅkulam vari maḻi peykiṟatu. innakariṉ veppam 60°-kku mēl uyarvatumilli. 50°-kkuk kīḻk kuṟivatum illi .

cikaraṅkaḷ :

mutalilēyē cikaraṅkaḷip paṟṟip potuvākak kuṟippiṭṭēṉ. avikaḷil mukkiyamāṉavaṟṟiyum aḻakiyavaṟṟiyum paṟṟic ciṟitu kuṟippiṭuvōm. utakamaṇṭalattic cuṟṟit toṭṭapeṭṭā, elk, paṉivīḻcikaram, ḵperṉ, keyraṉ eṉṟa aḻakiya cikaraṅkaḷ vāṉaḷāvi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. toṭṭapeṭṭā : toṭṭapeṭṭā eṉṟāl perumali eṉpatu poruḷ. itaṉuṭiya uyaram 8,640 aṭi, nīlakirip pīṭapūmiyil amintuḷḷa mika uyarnta cikaram itu tāṉ, iccikaratti eḷitil aṭiyalām. mūṉṟil iraṇṭu paṅku tūratti untuvaṇṭiyil kaṭantuviṭalām. mītippakutiyi naṭantē ēṟic cella vēṇṭum. utakamaṇṭalattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa cārmiṅ kirāsiliruntu, tār pōṭappaṭṭa pāti kōtakirikkuc cellukiṟatu. appātiyil nāṉku kal tolivu ceṉṟu piṟaku 13-kkal malimītu ēṟa vēṇṭum. mutal nāṉku kal tūramuḷḷa pāti vaḷintu vaḷintu cellukiṟatu. vaṇṭiyi ōṭṭic celluvōr mikavum eccarikkiyōṭu cella vēṇṭum.

toṭṭapeṭṭā ucciyil vāṉāyvukkūṭamoṉṟu ki. pi. 1846-ām āṇṭu niṟuvappaṭṭatu. āṉāl ki. pi. 1859-il utakamaṇṭalattiṟku oṉpatu kal tolivil uḷḷa irāṇuvattāriṉ taṅkal iṭamāṉa velliṅṭaṉukku itu māṟṟappaṭṭatu. cila āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu maṟupaṭiyum toṭṭapeṭṭāviṟkē māṟṟappaṭṭatu. āṉāl itu ippoḻutu payaṉpaṭum niliyil illi. toṭṭapeṭṭāviṉ ucciyiliruntu kāṇpōr kaṇkaḷukku utakamaṇṭalattiṉ muḻuk kāṭciyum pēraḻakōṭu teṉpaṭum. atōṭu kōvi māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa ellā malikaḷum teṉpaṭum.

paṉivīḻ cikaram :

toṭṭapeṭṭāviṟku aṭuttāṟpōl mikavum uyariya cikaram paṉivīḻcikaram (Snow down} eṉpatākum. itaṉ uyaram 8,299 aṭi. toṭṭapeṭṭāvippōl eḷitāka itaṉmītu ēṟa muṭiyātu. ēṟuvataṟkut tolliyāka iruntālum, uyarnta maraṅkaḷaṭarnta vaḻikaḷūṭē ēṟic cellumpōtu uḷḷattiṟku iṉpamāka irukkum. speṉcar mali mītu amintuḷḷa ceyiṇṭ sṭīḵpaṉ carcciṟkup piṉṉāl irukkum merlimaṇṭ pātiyil iraṇṭu kal tolivu ceṉṟāl, ivvucciyi aṭiyalām. ivvaḻiyum maraṅkaḷaṭarntatu.



elk cikaram :

elk cikaram 8000 aṭikaḷukku mēl uyaramuḷḷatu. itaṉmītu pala kōyilkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivvucciyiṉ pakkattil ōr aḻakiya kōyil kuṭiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṅkuḷḷa pūcāri mālitōṟum oru viḷakkēṟṟi vikkiṟār. ūṭṭiyiṉ eppakutiyiliruntu pārttālum avviḷakku naṉṟākat teriyum.

keyraṉ cikaram :

ellāvaṟṟiyumviṭa mikavum aḻakiyatu keyraṉ cikaram (Cairn Hill). maṟṟa ellāc cikaraṅkaḷiyum viṭa itu maraṅkaḷaṭarntatu. nalla vēḷiyāka, maṟṟa cikaraṅkaḷil maraṅkaḷ aḻikkappaṭṭāṟpōl itil aḻikkappaṭavilli . itaṉ uyaram 6,583 aṭi. paccik kampaḷam parappiṉāṟ pōṉṟa pulveḷikaḷ iccikarattil eṅkum paravik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa maraṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil mēṭiyiṭṭāṟpōl pāci paṭarntuḷḷatu. ataṉmītu ilakkiya uṇarvuḷḷa yārēṉum taṉimiyil amarntiruntārkaḷāṉāl, jōkaṉ sṭrās (Johan Struse) eṉṟa mēli nāṭṭuk kaviñaṉiṉ uḷḷattil, amaratvam peṟṟa icip pāṭal uruvākumāṟu tūṇṭiya, viyaṉṉāviṉ iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ niṉivil tōṉṟum, keyraṉ cikaram utakiyiliruntu caṟṟuttolivil uḷḷatu. nakarattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu, ēriyic cuṟṟic cellum pukivaṇṭip pātiyōramākac ceṉṟōmāṉāl, tūṇkaḷ pōl uyarntu niṟkum pāṟikaḷik kāṇalām. avviṭattiliruntu oru kal tolivu mikavum mōcamāṉa pāti amintuḷḷatu, atik kaṭantuviṭṭāl, keyraṉ maliyiṉ iḷamarak kāṭṭiṟkuḷ nuḻiyalām.



mūkkaṟutti cikaram :

mūkkaṟutti cikaram (Mukerti Peak) tōṭarkaḷāl puṉitamāṉatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. iccikarattiṟku appāl cuvarkkattiṉ vāyil iruppatāka avarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ. ic cikarattōṭu toṭarpuṭiya iraṇṭu katikaḷ tōṭarkaḷiṭiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tōṭarkaḷiṭiyē peṇ kuḻantikaḷik koli ceyyum paḻakkam iruntatākak kuṟippiṭṭōm. koli ceyya vēṇṭiya kuḻantikaḷi iṅkuk koṇarntu eṟintu koṉṟu viṭuvatuṇṭām, ākiyiṉāl entap peṇṇiyum tōṭarkaḷ iccikarattiṉ pakkamākac cella viṭuvatilli. ik kaṭṭuppāṭṭi mīṟi oru peṇ iṅku vantuviṭṭāḷām. itiyaṟinta tōṭarkaḷ, ap peṇṇiṉ mūkki aṟuttut taṇṭittārkaḷām. taṇṭaṉi peṟṟa ap peṇ eppaṭiyō immalic cikaratti aṭintu maṟintuviṭṭaṭāḷām. iṉṟukūṭa ap peṇṇi oru ciṟu teyvamākat tōṭarkaḷ vaṇaṅku kiṉṟaṉar.

ic cikarattōṭu toṭarpuṭiya maṟṟoru kati irāvaṇaṉip paṟṟiyatākum. tōṭarkaḷ irāvaṇaṉukku mariyāti kāṭṭāmal, irāmaṉiṭattil atika aṉpu celuttiṉārkaḷām. ataṉāl ciṉaṅkoṇṭa ilaṅki maṉṉaṉ oru ki maṇṇi eṭuttuk kāṟṟil vīciṉāṉām. ammaṇ koṭiya kirumikaḷāka māṟit tōṭarkaḷuṭiya kālnaṭikaḷiyum, vīṭukaḷiyum pīṭittut tuṉpuṟuttiyatōṭu, avarkaḷiyum nōy koḷḷumāṟu ceytu koṭumi purintatām. iṉṟukūṭa akkirumikaḷāl tuṉpuṟuvatākat tōṭarkaḷ nampukiṟārkaḷ. iti uṇarnta irāmaṉ icceyalukkup paḻivāṅka eṇṇi irāvaṇaṉ taṅkiyāṉa cūrppanakiyiṉ mūkki aṟuttu, ellārukkum teriyumpaṭi ic cikarattil patittu vittāṉām. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷālēyē iccikaram ip peyar peṟṟatākat tōṭarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

kuṉṟu veḷikaḷ :

utakamaṇṭalattic cuṟṟip paranta kuṉṟu veḷikaḷ (downs) amintuḷḷaṉa. vēṟu entak kuṟiñci nakarattilum iṅkiruppaṉa pōṉṟu paranta veḷikaḷik kāṇa muṭiyātu. untuvaṇṭikaḷil ēṟi ullācamākac cuṟṟit tiriya virumpuvōr, iṇiyaṟṟa iṉpam kāṇa ivveḷikaḷ peruntuṇi purikiṉṟaṉa. utakamaṇṭalattic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa pātikaḷ amippilum, aḻakilum īṭaṟṟavi, vēṟeṅkum ittakiya malivaḻip pātikaḷik kāṇa muṭiyātu. makāpalēcuvaram (Mahabaleshwar), māteraṉ (Matheran) ākiya nakaraṅkaḷil iruppatupōl, utakiyic cuṟṟi uyarnta malikaḷum, kuṟukiya paḷḷattākkukaḷum kiṭiyā. micūriliruntu kūṭalūr vaḻiyāka utaki cellum pāti. aḻakiya veḷikaḷiṭiyē pukuntu cellukiṟatu. ivvaḻic celvōr eytum iṉpam eṭuttiyampaṟpālatō?

ivvaḻi tāḻnta kuṉṟukaḷil ēṟi iṟaṅkic cellukiṟatu. aṅkup paravik kāṇappaṭum pacum pulveḷikaḷ, iṅkilāntu nāṭṭil mikka aḻakōṭu viḷaṅkum, yārkṣir pulveḷikaḷi (Yorkshire Moors) niṉivūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivviṭam kuḻip pantāṭṭattiṟku mikavum ēṟṟa iṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. iṅkuḷḷa kuḻippantāṭṭa mitāṉam (Golf Course) intiya nāṭṭilēyē mikavum aḻakiyatu. ivveḷikaḷil amintuḷḷa iḷamarakkāṭukaḷil cempuli (leopards) kaḷum, ciṟutti (Panthers) kaḷum, variyāṭukaḷum niṟiya uḷḷaṉa. ivikaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭuvatu, utaki vāḻ makkaḷukkuc ciṟanta poḻutu pōkkāka uḷḷatu. āṇṭutōṟum cepṭampar tiṅkaḷiṉpōtu ivviṭaṅkaḷil eṅkup pārttālum pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟum. aṭuttaṭuttu varum kuṉṟukaḷiṭiyē, vaḷintu vaḷintu cellum pātikaḷil kavaliyiṉṟi naṭantu tirivatum īṭaṟṟa iṉpamē.

tiru. veṉlāk pirapu (Lord Wenlock) ceṉṉi mānila āḷunarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya pōtu, ivveḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkup peritum pāṭupaṭṭār. ākiyiṉālēyē ivveḷikaḷ avaruṭiya peyari muṉṉē koṇṭu 'veṉlāk veḷikaḷ' (Wenlock Downs) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. utakamaṇṭalattiluḷḷa ellā mukkiya uṇavu viṭutikaḷiliruntum iv veḷikaḷi eḷitil ceṉṟaṭiyalām. uṇavu viṭutikaḷiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu 1 allatu 1½kkal, vesṭpari pātiyil cella vēṇṭum. utakiyiliruntu kiḷampuvōr pātiyi aṭintu ḵpiṅkar pōsṭ (Finger Post) ṭi nōkkic ceṉṟāl ivveḷikaḷic cīkkiram aṭiyalām, ki viralkaḷi virittu vittāṟpōl aintu pātikaḷ pirintu celvatāl ivviṭam ḵpiṅkar pōsṭ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu.

ēri :

utaka maṇṭalattil amintuḷḷa ēri ciṟiyatu. itu ceyaṟki ēri eṉpati ellārum aṟivar. āṉāl itik kāṇpōr iyaṟkiyāka amintuḷḷa eḻil mikka ēri eṉṟē eṇṇuvar. itaṉ cuṟṟaḷavu iraṇṭu kal irukkum. mutaṉ mutalāka ivvēriyiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattil akkaṟi kāṭṭiyavar tiruvāḷar callivaṉ turi. ivvēriyiṉ nīrip payirt toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭutta mutalil ellārum eṇṇiṉar. ciṟitu cīrtiruttappaṭṭatum itu kaṇ kavarum vaṉappōṭu kāṭciyaḷittatāl, aḻakukkākavē itip payaṉ paṭuttap piṉṉar muṭivu ceytaṉar. ki. pi. 1823-ām āṇṭiliruntu, itu pala māṟutalkaḷukkuṭpaṭṭup pala vaḻikaḷilum muṉṉēṟiyatu. ki. pi. 1896-liruntu 1899 variyil rū.20,000 itaṟkuc celaviṭappaṭṭatu. ippōtu ivvēriyic cuṟṟi maraṅkaḷaṭarnta kāṭu oṉṟuḷḷatu. amitiyāṉa aḻaku avviṭattil kuṭi koṇṭirukkiṟatu. utaki makkaḷ ivvēriyil tōṇiyūrntum, mīṉ piṭittum iṉpamākap poḻutik kaḻikkiṉṟaṉar. ēriyiṉ etirk kariyil perṉ mali toṭaṅkukiṟatu. ēriyic cuṟṟi naṭantu ceṉṟāl, nam uḷḷattiṟkum uṭalukkum oru vitap puttuṇarcci ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. ēriyiṉ kariyōramākak kaṭivītiyi nōkki naṭantu ceṉṟāl hōparṭ pūṅkā (Hobert Park) viṉ elliyi aṭiyalām. avviṭattil kutirip pantaya mitāṉamum pārviyāḷar iruppiṭamum (Pevilian) amintuḷḷaṉa. kutirip pantaya vīrarkaḷiyum, poḻutu pōkkiṟkākak kutiri mītum, kōvēṟu kaḻuti mītum cavāri ceyvōriyum aṅkuk kāṇalām. akkāṭci mikavum vēṭikkiyāṉatāka irukkum.

iṉpac celaviṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ (Excursions)

avalañci :

utakamaṇṭalattic cuṟṟi iṉpamākap poḻutu pōkkaṟkuriya pala iṭaṅkaḷum, kaṇṭu makiḻtaṟkuriya pala iṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. keyraṉ maliyil uḷḷa kāṭukaḷiṉ orupakuti paṉippaṭalattil maṟintu tōṉṟum, utaka maṇṭalattiliruntu 15-āvatu kallil ikkāṭci amintuḷḷatu. ivviṭattiṟku 'avalañci' eṉṟu peyar. avalañci (Avalanche) eṉṟa āṅkilac collukku, 'iḻintu varum paṉikkaṭṭi' eṉṟu peyar. ki. pi. 1823-ām āṇṭu, paṉiveḷḷam ivviṭattil ōṭivanta kāraṇat tāl itu ippeyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu ivviṭattiṟkup cellum vaḻi mikavum mōcamāṉatu. āṉāl ippātiyiṉ vaḷivukaḷil ceṟintu tōṉṟum iyaṟki vaḷam, kāṇpōr uḷḷatti viyappilum makiḻcciyilum āḻttum. ivvaḻiyil pala ciṟṟūrkaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. acciṟṟūrkaḷik kaṭantatum vaḻi iraṇṭākap pirintu celkiṟatu. oruvaḻi emarālṭu paḷḷattākki (Emerald Valley)ṟkuc cellukiṟatu. appaḷḷattākkil irāṇuvattiṉar taṅki, mātirip pōr (Mock battle) payilukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟōr vaḻiyil aintu kal ceṉṟāl avalañciyi aṭiyalām. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu 14 āvatu kallil avalañci āṟu ōṭukiṟatu. iyaṟkivaḷam ceṟinta accarivil, paḷiṅku pōl cuḻittu ōṭum avvāṟṟiṉ aḻakic coṟkaḷāl kūṟamuṭiyātu. avviṭattil amintirukkum pirayāṇikaḷiṉ taṅkal maṉi (Travellers' Bangalow)yiliruntu oru ciṟiya vaḻi mēlē cellukiṟatu. avvaḻiyāka mēlēṟi nōkkiṉāl orē aḷavāṉa uyarattōṭu 8300 aṭi nimirntu niṟkum cikaraṅkaḷik kāṇalām.

mīṉ paṇṇi :

pirayāṇikaḷ taṅkal maṉiyiliruntu orukal tolivil oru periya mīṉ paṇṇi (Trout Hatchery) amintuḷḷatu. ittakiya paṇṇi nīlakiriyiṉ mītu eṅkum kiṭiyātu. 'iṅku vaḷarkkappaṭumpaṭiyāṉa mīṉiṉ peyar 'ṭiravuṭ' eṉappaṭum. itu 'cāl maṉ' (Salmon) eṉṟa mīṉiṉattic cārntatu. āṉāl, ativiṭa uruvattil ciṟiyatu. ippaṇṇiyil aintu periya kuḷaṅkaḷil ōrāṇṭiliruntu mūṉṟāṇṭuvari vayatuṭiya mīṉkaḷ vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. navampar mutal ḵpepruvari variyilum uḷḷa kālamē immīṉiṉ vaḷarccikku ēṟṟa paruvamākum. 10,000 mīṉkaḷ ippaṇṇiyil vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. immīṉiṉ muṭṭikaḷ 29 nāṭkaḷil kaṇ viḻikkiṉṟaṉa,

piṟa iṭaṅkaḷ :

ippaṇṇik karukiluḷḷa kāṭu uḷ nuḻintu kāṇpataṟku ēṟṟatu. aḻakiya oru ciṟupāti kāṭṭiṉ uṭpakutiyil cellukiṟatu. appātiyil 9 kal tolivu ceṉṟāl kāṭṭilākāt talivariṉ māḷiki uḷḷatu. avviṭattiṟkup paṇṭakapal (Bantakapal) eṉṟu peyar.

utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu avalañci cellum vaḻiyil amintuḷḷa mūkkaṟutti, pikkārā cikaraṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavi. utaki ceṉṟuviṭṭu ivviraṇṭiyum kāṇāmal yārum varamāṭṭārkaḷ. iccikaraṅkaḷ amintuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil pala āṟukaḷum, aṇikaḷum, ērikaḷum pēraḻakuṭaṉ kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟaṉa.

utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu patiṉōrāvatu kallil amintuḷḷa pikkārā āṟṟiṉ pālattik kaṭantu ceṉṟōmāṉāl, ōr aṇi kaṭṭik koṇṭiruppatik kāṇalām. ivvaṇi pikkārā aṇikkaṭṭiṉ toṭarcci. itaṉ uyaram 150 aṭi.

ivvaḻiyilēyē mēlum aintu kalceṉṟu iṭatu puṟamākat tirumpiṉāl ōr aḻakiya pāti cellukiṟatu. avvaḻiyiṉ ovvoru tiruppattilum calacalattu ōṭum nīraruviyiyō, pacuṅ kuṉṟiyē. allatu ciṟu ciṟu kuṭicikaḷiyō kāṇalām. kkap kāṭukaḷil nuḻintāl tōṭariṉ vāḻviṭaṅkaḷik kāṇalām.

mūkkaṟuttiyil amintirukkum taṅkal māḷiki (Dak Bangalow) aḻakiyatākavum, vacatiyāṉa tākavum amintuḷḷatu. itu aṇik kaṭṭiliruntu orukal tolivil amintuḷḷatu. ivvaṇi ki. pi. 1835il iruntu ki. pi. 1938 vari kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. itu uruvattāl ciṟiyatu. āṉāl, itil iraṇṭu etirolikaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. mutal etiroli toṭaṅki ari nimiṭam kaḻittu iraṇṭāvatu etiroli tōṉṟukiṟatu. iṅkuḷḷa ēri pārppataṟkuc ciṟitākat tōṉṟum. āṉāl itu mūkkaṟutti maliyic cuṟṟip paraviyuḷḷatu. ivvēriyiṉ nāṉku pakkaṅkaḷilum niṟiyappēr mīṉpiṭittup poḻutik kaḻikkiṉṟaṉar. ivvēriyil tōṇiyūrntu ceṉṟē mūkkaṟuttiyiṉ aṭivāratti aṭiya muṭiyum. aṭi vārattiliruntu accikaram mikavum ceṅkuttāka uyarntu cellukiṟatu. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu pārppōrukku avvucci teḷivākat teriyum. iv vēriyic cuṟṟiyirukkum karaṭumuraṭāṉa pāti 12 kal nīḷamuṭiyatu. ippātiyil naṭantu ēriyi valam varutal peruveṟṟikkuriya ceyalākum.

kavarṉars ṣōlā {Governor's Shola) eṉṟa iḷamarakkāṭu kuṟippiṭattakka aḻakuvāyntatu. akkāṭṭiṉ iṭiyē niḻalaṭarnta maraṅkaḷiṉaṭiyil pukuntu cellum 5 kal nīḷamuḷḷa pāti, iṅku vāḻnta mānila āḷunarkaḷukku mikavum piṭittamāṉatu. kaṉṉamarā pāti eṉṟa maṟṟoru vaḻi 10 kal nīḷamuḷḷatu. kuṭi maraṅka (Umbrella trees) ḷiṭiyē ippāti pukuntu cellum kāṭci pēraḻaku āppatu. ippātiyiliruntu orukal tolivu ceṉṟāl iṉpa viruntu (Picnic) uṇpataṟkāṉa aḻakiya iṭam oṉṟuḷḷatu. avviṭattiliruntu mēkamūṭṭamaṟṟa nāḷil nōkkiṉāl, micūr nakaram naṉṟākat teriyum. kaṉṉamarāp pātiyiṉ muṭivil ceṅkuttāṉa cīkūr mali (Sigur ghat) toṭaṅkukiṟatu. attoṭar 3000 aṭikkuk kīḻ aṭarnta kāṭukaḷiṭiyē amintirukkum kālkaṭṭi nīr vīḻcci (Kalhatty falls) vari cellukiṟatu.

araciyalār payirt tōṭṭam :

utakamaṇṭalattil amintuḷḷa aracāṅkap payirt tōṭṭam (The Botanical Gardens), in nakara varalāṟṟil kuṟippiṭattakka oṉṟākum. ki. pi. 1850 ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar makkalvar (Mr. Mclver} eṉpavarāl ippayirt tōṭṭam niṟuvappaṭṭatu. mutalil itu 'kiyū tōṭṭam' (Kew Gardens) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkiyatu. ki.pi. 1950-ām āṇṭu it tōṭṭattiṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻā mikavum ciṟanta muṟiyil koṇṭāṭappaṭṭatu. mutaṉ mutalāka itu kāykaṟi payiriṭuvataṟkāka niṟuvappaṭṭatu. āṉāl itu ippoḻutu tāvarappayir ārāyccikkuriya iṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. iṅkut tāvara ārāyccik kaḻakam (Harticultural Society) ki. pi. 1847-ām āṇṭu potu makkaḷiṉ naṉkoṭiyik koṇṭu tuvakkappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1855-ām āṇṭu ittōṭṭam araciyalār kikku māṟiyatu. piṟaku itu palavita māṟutalkaḷiyum, viyakkattakka muṉṉēṟṟaṅkaḷiyum mēṟkoṇṭatu. taṟpōtu itu pala aṭukkukaḷāka amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. payaṉuḷḷa ceṭikaḷum, palavita malarc ceṭikaḷum ulakattiṉ pala pakutikaḷiliruntum koṇarappaṭṭu iṅkup payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippōtu ēṟakkuṟiya 650 vakiyāṉa ceṭi koṭikaḷum, maraṅkaḷum iṅku uḷḷaṉa. aḻakukkākavē vaḷarkkappaṭum maraṅkaḷ, muṭceṭikaḷ, āṇṭu muḻutum payaṉ tarattakka maraṅkaḷ, kiḻaṅkuc ceṭikaḷ, vēli amippataṟkup payaṉpaṭum paṭiyāṉa ceṭikaḷ, mūlikic ceṭikaḷ, nīrc ceṭikaḷ, maṇapporuḷ ceyyappayaṉpaṭum ceṭikaḷ, aḻaku vaḷivukaḷil paṭaraviṭum koṭikaḷ, rōjāc ceṭikaḷ eṉa aḷavaṟṟa tāvara vakikaḷ iṅku vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

pūk kaṇkāṭci :

āṇṭu tōṟum mēt tiṅkaḷil iṅku naṭi peṟum pūk kaṇkāṭci (Flower Show) mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ak kaṇkāṭciyil pūvōṭu kāykaṟi, paḻam mutaliyaṉavum vikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, nīlakiri maliyiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷiliruntum, camaveḷiyiliruntum makkaḷ akkaṇkāṭciyil vantu kūṭukiṉṟaṉar. nākarika makkaḷum, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum oṉṟucēra ivviḻā peritum tuṇipurikiṉṟatu. nākarikar paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiyum, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ nākarikariyum aṅkānta vāyōṭu pārttu viyappaṭivar. pūk kaṇkāṭci naṭipeṟum an nāḷilēyē paṟavikaḷiṉ kaṇkāṭci (Poultry Show) oṉṟum naṭipeṟum. nāyk kaṇkāṭcikkāka oru nāḷ appōtu otukkappaṭukiṟatu. utakamaṇṭalattil pala nāy vaḷarppu niliyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa: avviṭaṅkaḷiliruntum veḷiyūrkaḷiliruntum kāṭcikkāka niṟiya nāykaḷ aṅku vantu kūṭum. uyarnta iṉa nāykaḷil ciṟappāṉavaṟṟiyellām aṅkuk kāṇalām. pūṉikkuṭṭi aḷaviliruntu kaṉṟuk kuṭṭiyiṉ aḷavu variyuḷḷa nāykaḷ aṅku aṇi aṇiyāka irukkum.

vaṉa vilaṅkukaḷik kāṇa virumpuvōr, kūṭalūrukku appāl uḷḷa kāṭukaḷukkuc cellukiṉṟaṉar. ūṭṭiyiliruntu nāṟpatu kallukku appāl tāṉ vaṉavilaṅkukaḷik kāṇa iyalum. māliyil puṟappaṭṭu, cūriyaṉ malivāyil viḻuntatum yāṉi mēl ēṟik kāṭṭiṟkuḷ nuḻintāl, kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ kūṭṭattik kāṇalām. puli, kāṭṭerumi, māṉiṉam ākiyavi etirppaṭum.

paḷḷikaḷ :

utakamaṇṭalattil māṇavarkaḷ taṅkip payiluvataṟkēṟṟa nāṉku paḷḷikaḷ (boarding schools) uḷḷaṉa. pirīks niṉivuppaḷḷi (Breek's Memorial School), tiruccapi makaḷir uyar nilippaḷḷi (High School for Sisters of the Church), nācarēttu makaḷir paḷḷi (The Nazareth Convent), lāraṉs paḷḷi (The Lawrence School) eṉpaṉa avi. mēṟkūṟiya nāṉku paḷḷikaḷil mikavum ciṟappāṉatu vāraṉs paḷḷi. itu utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu nāṉku kal tolivil lavṭēl (Lovedale) eṉṟa iṭattil amintuḷḷatu. 'lavṭēl' eṉṟāl aṉpup paḷḷattākku' eṉṟu peyar. aṉpāl piṇikkappaṭṭup palkali payilum piñcuḷḷaṅkaḷiṉ kalik kūṭam amintirukkumiṭattiṟku ippeyar mikavum poruttamē. ivviṭattiṉ taṭpa veppa nili uṭal nalattiṟku mikavum ēṟṟatāka amintuḷḷatu.

ippaḷḷi ki. pi. 1858-ām āṇṭu cepṭampar tiṅkaḷ 6-ām nāḷ tuvakkappaṭṭatu. appōtu intiya nāṭṭup paṭiyiṉ taḷapati (Major General) yāka viḷaṅkiya car heṉṟi lāraṉs eṉpāriṉ peyar koṇṭu ippaḷḷi viḷaṅkukiṟatu. intiyap paṭiyil paṇiyāṟṟiya paṭivīrarkaḷ, ōyvupeṟṟa paṭivīrarkaḷ, ākiyōriṉ kuḻantikaḷ ciṟanta muṟiyil kalvi payiluvataṟkāka, nīlakiri maliyil uyariya oru paḷḷiyi niṟuva vēṇṭum eṉṟa karutti mutaṉ mutalāka veḷiyiṭṭavar avarē. ki. pi. 1949-ām āṇṭu variyil, paṭiyiṉar kuḻantikaḷ payilum paḷḷiyākavē itu iruntuvantatu. avvāṇṭu intiya araciyalār ippaḷḷiyiṉ poṟuppi ēṟṟukkoṇṭatum, ellāruṭiya kuḻantikaḷum payilum potuppaḷḷiyāka itu māṟṟappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.

ki. pi. 1949-kku muṉ āṅkilēyarukkē ēkapōka urimi peṟṟirunta ippaḷḷi, ippōtu intiya nāṭṭuc celvarkaḷukkē urimiyuṭiyatāka irukkiṟatu. iṅkilāntu nāṭṭil īṭaṉ {Eton) eṉṟa iṭattil uḷḷa paḷḷi, araciḷaṅkumararkaḷum, pirapukkaḷiṉ kuḻantikaḷum, māperum āli mutalāḷikaḷiṉ makkaḷum payilum paḷḷiyāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. appaḷḷi, varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟatu ; pala nūṟṟāṇṭu vaḷarcciyuṭiyatu; pala aṟiñarkaḷiyum, eḻuttāḷarkaḷiyum, kaviñarkaḷiyum uruvākkiyatu; pala ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷāl eṭuttuk kiyāḷappaṭṭatu. attakiya oru paḷḷi intiya nāṭṭil irukkiṟa teṉṟāl, atu lāreṉs paḷḷiyākattāṉ irukka muṭiyum. 'intiya nāṭṭiṉ īṭaṉ' eṉṟu iti aḻittālum takum. āṇṭu oṉṟukku ēṟakkuṟiya rū. 4000 iṅku payilum ovvoru māṇavaṉukkum celavākiṉṟaṉa. ippaḷḷiyil iṭam piṭippateṉpatu, avvaḷavu eḷitāṉa kāriyamalla. māṇavarkaḷic cērttuk koḷḷum atikāram ippaḷḷiyiṉ ceyaṟkuḻu (The Board of Administration) viṟkē uriyatu. intiya nāṭṭuk kalvit tuṟic ceyalāḷarē (The Secretary to the Government of India, Ministry of Education) ic ceyaṟkuḻuviṉ talivar. ippaḷḷi amintirukkum iṭattiṉ mottap parappu 700 ēkar. itu amintirukkum iṭattiṉ uyaram kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 7200 aṭi. ippaḷḷiyiṉ naṭuvil aḻakiya ēri oṉṟum amintuḷḷatu.



itu taṅkip payilum paḷḷi. itil 550 māṇavarkaḷē taṅkip payila iṭamuṇṭu. 5 vayatu mutal 9 vayatu vari uḷḷa iḷamāṇavar pirivu (Preparatory School), āṇkaḷ pirivu, peṇkaḷ pirivu eṉa ippaḷḷi muppirivukaḷik koṇṭatu. im muppiriviṉarkkum taṅkumiṭaṅkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavēyaṉṟi, āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum oṉṟākavē payilkiṉṟaṉar. kuṭumpakkali (Demestic Science) mutaliya peṇkaḷuk kurittāṉa kalikaḷum, poḻutu pōkkuk kalikaḷum taṉiyāka avarkaḷukkuk kaṟṟukkoṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru viṭutiyum oru kuṭumpam pōṉṟa muṟi (House ---System)yilēyē naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku mikavum ciṟanta āṅkilak kalvi payiṟṟappaṭukiṟatu. āṅkilattiṟ kaṭuttāṟpōl, inti kaṭṭāya pāṭamākak kaṟpikkappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku mēli nāṭṭu icikkaliyum, intiya icikkaliyum kaṟṟukkoṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. icik kaliyip pāṭamāka eṭuttukkoṇṭa ellā māṇavarkaḷum piyāṉō, vayaliṉ ākiya icik karuvikaḷi icikkak kaṟṟukkoṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. mēli nāṭṭu iciyil nalla tērcciyum, ārvamumuḷḷa māṇavarkaḷ ilaṇṭaṉ ṭiriṉiṭi kallūri icittērvukku aṉuppappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. maṭṭip pantu (Tennis), tuṭuppup pantu, (Cricket), kūṭip pantu (Basket ball), kālpantu (Foot ball), kip pantu (Volley ball), valippantu(Net ball), taṭippantu (Hockey) mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ iṅkuk kaṟṟuk koṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṉupavam vāynta ōrāciriyar kutiri ēṟṟamum māṇavarkaṭkuk kaṟṟukkoṭukkiṟār. āṉāl, ataṟkut tiṅkaḷ tōṟum rū. 20 taṉik kaṭṭaṇamākac celuttavēṇṭum. camayak kalviyum iṅkup payiṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.



māṇavarkaḷuṭiya uṭal nalatti nalla muṟiyil kaṇkāṇikka oru maruttuva maṉiyum uḷḷatu. ammaṉiyil payiṟci peṟṟa oru maruttuvar iruntu paṇipurikiṟār. 55 paṭukkikaḷ ammaruttuva maṉiyil uḷḷaṉa. ovvoru kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarcciyum karuttōṭu kavaṉikkappaṭukiṟatu. māṇavarkaḷiṉ eṭi, uyaram mutaliyaṉa yāvum tiṅkaḷtōṟum kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭup pativu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷ tōṟum cōtaṉiyiṭappaṭṭa cīriya uṇavē ellārukkum aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. maruttuvamaṉiyil oru māṇavaṉ 24 maṇi nērattiṟkumēl taṅkiyirukka nēriṭṭāl, ac ceytiyip paḷḷi nirvākikaḷ peṟṟōrukku aṟivittu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. maruttuva maṉiyil cikicci peṟum māṇavaṉiṉ uṭal nala muṉṉēṟṟam, vārantōṟum peṟṟōrkaḷukku aṟivikkappaṭum. māṇavaṉiṉ uṭal nalattip paṟṟi viṉavum peṟṟōrukku uṭaṉukkuṭaṉē muṭaṅkal mūlam akkaṟiyōṭu nilimi aṟivikkappaṭum. mikavum nōyvāyppaṭṭa māṇavarkaḷ utakamaṇṭalattiluḷḷa puṉita pārttalōmi maruttuvamaṉi {St. Bartholomews Hospital) yil cērkkappaṭuvar. appōtu ēṟpaṭum celaviṉattip peṟṟōrkaḷ ēṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. paḷḷi maruttuvamaṉiyil ceyyum cikiccikkup paṇam kiṭiyātu. eks-rē paṭam piṭippataṟkum, aṟuvi maruttuvattiṟkum, ṭāṉikkukaḷukkum, viliyuyarnta maruntukaḷukkumāṉa paṇattip peṟṟōrkaḷ aḷikka vēṇṭum. nīlakiri māvaṭṭa maruttuva atikāri, (District Medical Officer) ip paḷḷiyiṉ maruttuva ālōcakarāka eppoḻutum iruppār. ip paḷḷikkeṉṟu taṉippaṭṭa pal maruttuvar (Dental Surgeon) oruvar irukkiṟār. māṇavarkaḷuṭiya paṟkaḷiṉ nalam naṉku kavaṉikkappaṭukiṟatu. itaṟkeṉṟu āṇṭutōṟum rū. 5 kaṭṭaṇamākap peṟappaṭukiṟatu.



pattu vayatiṟkuṭpaṭṭa māṇavarkaḷiṉ piṟanta nāḷ viruntum iṅku naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. yārāvatu oru māṇavaṉiṉ peṟṟōr tam kuḻantiyiṉ piṟanta nāḷ viḻāvik koṇṭāṭa virumpiṉāl, piṟanta nāḷ varuvataṟkup patiṉintu nāṭkaḷukkumuṉ oru viṇṇappattōṭu rū. 10-m aṉuppi vikka vēṇṭum. vārattiṟku oru muṟiyāvatu peṟṟōrukku muṭaṅkal eḻuta vēṇṭumeṉpatu paḷḷiyiṉ caṭṭam, muṭaṅkal eḻuta muṭiyāta tuvakkappaḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukkāka vakuppu āciriyarkaḷē iraṇṭu vāraṅkaḷukkoru muṟi eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. muṭaṅkal tavaṟiviṭṭāl, atip paṟṟiya ceytiyip peṟṟōrkaḷ paḷḷi . nirvākikaḷukkut teriyappaṭutta vēṇṭum. paḷḷiyil aṇivataṟkeṉṟu taṉippaṭṭa uṭi (Uniform) ellā māṇavarkaḷukkum aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu.

aḻakuk kalikaḷāṉa ōviyam, ciṟpam, āṭal mutaliyaṉavum iṅkuc ciṟanta muṟiyil kaṟṟuk koṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟanta paṭip payiṟciyum, tuppākki cuṭum payiṟciyum iṅku aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaḷa.

kiruttavak kōyilkaḷ :

utakamaṇṭalattil ciṟanta mūṉṟu kiruttavak kōyilkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. puṉita sṭīḵpaṉ tirukkōyil (St. Stephen's Church), puṉita tāmacu kōyil (St. Thomas Church), kaṇṭal urōmaṉ kattōlikkat tirukkōyil (The Tiny Church of Kandal) eṉpaviyē avi. puṉita sṭīḵpaṉ tirukkōyil ki. pi. 1830-ām āṇṭil kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ik kōyiliṉ muṉ pakutiyil poruttappaṭṭirukkum marattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷivukaḷ kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavarum kalivaṉappuṭiyavi. ivi tippu cultāṉ māḷikiyiliruntu koṇarappaṭṭavi, utakamaṇṭalatti nīlakiri maliyiṉmītu tōṟṟuvitta tiruvāḷar callivaṉ avarkaḷuṭiya camātiyum, avaruṭiya kuṭumpattār camātikaḷum ik kōyililēyē uḷḷaṉa. ikkōyiliṉ maṇik kūṇṭu ki. pi. 1851-ām āṇṭilum, ālayamaṇi ki. pi. 1894-ām āṇṭilum amikkappaṭṭaṉa, puṉita tāmacu tirukkōyil ki. pi. 1867-ām āṇṭu tuvakkappaṭṭu ki. pi. 1870-ām āṇṭil muṭikkappaṭṭatu. ivviru kōyilkaḷum nakarattiṉ naṭuvilēyē amintuḷḷaṉa.

utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu iraṇṭu kal tolivil uḷḷa 'kaṇṭal' eṉṟa iṭattil amintuḷḷa kattōlikkar kōyil mikavum pukaḻ peṟṟatu. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu, ērikkari vaḻiyāka vaḷintu cellum micūrppātiyil ceṉṟāl, iṭatu puṟattil iyaṟkiyaḻaku koḻikkum oru mēṭṭu nilam teṉpaṭum. atuvē kaṇṭal tirukkōyilākum. akkōyilil kuṟippiṭattakka periya kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ etuvumilli. ammēṭṭiṉ ucciyil ēcu nātarōṭu kūṭiya oru ciluvi nāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. icciluvi pāriciluḷḷa kattōlikkat tiruccapiyāriṉ āṇiyāl ki. pi. 1933-il iṅku nāṭaṭappaṭṭatu. itu nāṭṭappaṭṭa cila āṇṭukaḷil ivviṭattiṉ pukaḻ nāṭeṅkum paravat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ āṇṭu tōṟum iṅkup puṉita yāttiri cellukiṉṟaṉar. ciluvi nāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa mēṭikkuc cellum vaḻi, mikka aḻakōṭu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. av vaḻiyil iṭiyiṭiyē pala taṅkumiṭaṅkaḷ kaṇ kavarum vaṉappōṭu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

ikkōyil amintuḷḷa iṭam kattōlikkak kiruttavarkaḷip putippataṟkāka araciyalāriṭamiruntu peṟappaṭṭatākum. camātikaḷellām nila maṭṭattiṟkuk kīḻē aṭukkaṭukkāka amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa, kōyiliṉ vaḷarccikkutaviya naṉkoṭiyāḷarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ caṭṭamiṭṭa takaṭukaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivviṭukāṭṭiṉ naṭuvil nāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum ciluvi, ēcu nātariṉ ciliyōṭu aḻakuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṟatu. accili veṇkalattāl āṉatu; ciṟpak kalivaḷam ceṟintatu.

icciluvi cakti vāyntatākavum, kēṭṭōrukkuk kēṭṭa varam nalkum peṟṟiyuṭiyatākavum, teyvat taṉmi mikkatākavum ellārālum karutappaṭukiṟatu. icciluvi paṟṟip pala katikaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. icciluviyil aṭikkappaṭṭirunta pittaḷi āṇikaḷi māṭu mēykkum ciṟuvarkaḷ oru nāḷ tiruṭik koṇṭu cella muyaṉṟaṉarām. āṉāl avikaḷip piṭuṅka muṭiyāmal vīṭu ceṉṟaṉar. vīṭu ceṉṟatum acciṟuvarkaḷ kaṭum nōyvāyppaṭṭu varuntiṉarām. uṇmiyaṟinta peṟṟōrkaḷ ciluviyiṉ muṉ maṇṭiyiṭṭu vaṇaṅkit tam ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ aṟiyāmiyi maṉṉikkumāṟu vēṇṭiṉar. piṟaku ac ciṟuvarkaḷ piḻitteḻuntaṉar. ki. pi. 1935-ām āṇṭu camaveḷiyil vāḻnta ēḻeṭṭup pērkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru kuṭumpam icciluviyik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkak kaṇṭalukku vantiruntatām. mikavum kuḷirāṉa ac camayattil pōrttuk koḷḷap pōrvikaḷ kūṭa koṇṭuvara villiyām. ciluvikketiriluḷḷa pantalil avarkaḷik kaṇṭa pātiriyār, kuḷiriṉ koṭumiyip paṟṟik kūṟi, takka pātukāppōṭu illāviṭṭāl iṟakka nēriṭum eṉṟukūṭac coṉ ṉārām. āṉāl ak kuṭumpattār pātiriyāri nōkki, "aiyarē! ēcu perumāṉ paṉiyil naṉintu kuḷirāl varuntumpōtum, nāṅkaḷ maṭṭum ēṉ pōrvi tēṭa vēṇṭum? ēcu nātariṉ kālaṭiyilēyē nāṅkaḷ eṭṭu nāṭkaḷ taṅkiyirukka muṭivu ceytirukkiṟōm,“ eṉṟu kūṟiṉārkaḷām. eṭṭu nāṭkaḷ iravu pakal ennēramum appantaliṉ aṭiyilēyē iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkiya vaṇṇam akkuṭumpattār kāttukkiṭantaṉarām. evvitat tuṉpamumiṉṟi eṭṭu nāṭkaḷiyum kaḻittuviṭṭu ūr tirumpiṉarām. ikkuṭumpattār intu camayattic cārntavarkaḷ eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. tamiḻakattu intukkaḷ paḻaṉiyāṇṭavaṉukkum, tiruppati vēṅkaṭappaṉukkum ' vēṇṭutali' mēṟkoḷvatupōl, kiruttavarkaḷ kaṇṭal ciluvikku vēṇṭutali mēṟkoḷḷu kiṉṟaṉar. perum perum celvarkaḷum, uyar niliyiluḷḷa cīmāṉkaḷum, paṭittavarkaḷum icciluviyiṉ makimiyit tam vāḻvil aṉupavittu aṟintatākap pattirikiyil aṭikkaṭi eḻutikkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar, icciluviyiṉ ciṟappukkuc cāṉṟākap putupputuk katikaḷ tōṉṟiyavaṇṇamirukkiṉṟaṉa.

nāḷ tōṟum icciluviyik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkap pirayāṇikaḷ vantukoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl veḷḷikkiḻamiyaṉṟu nōyvāyppaṭṭōrum, maṉṉippup peṟa virumpum pāvikaḷum, varam peṟa virumpum pakta kōṭikaḷum, kavaliyāl karuttiḻantu amitikāṇa virumpuvōrum iṅkut tiraḷākak kūṭukiṉṟaṉar. viḻā nāṭkaḷil kaṇṭal ciluviyit taricikka varum pirayāṇikaḷuk keṉṟu, taṉippaṭṭa pukivaṇṭikaḷum, untuvaṇṭikaḷum viṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nōyvāyppaṭṭōri, 'ṭōlikaḷil vittut tūkkik koṇṭuvarukiṉṟaṉar. koṭuṅkuḷiriyum poruṭpaṭuttāmal viṭiyaṟkāli mūṉṟu maṇikkē ellārum vantu kūṭukiṉṟaṉar.



kūṉūr :

nīlakiri malikku varum pirayāṇikaḷil perumpālōr iravil taṅkuvataṟku utakiyiviṭak kūṉūriyē ēṟṟa iṭamākat tērnteṭukkiṉṟaṉar. itu mitamāṉa kuḷiriyuṭiya iṭamākum. ivvūr amintirukkum iṭam kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 6000 aṭi uyara muḷḷatu. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu 12 kal ceṉṟāl ivvūri aṭiyalām. ivvaḻiyāṉatu keyṭi (Kaity) paḷḷattākkil pukuntu celkiṟatu. aḻaku koluvīṟṟirukkum ippaḷḷattākkil aṉāti viṭuti yoṉṟum amintuḷḷatu. ivvaḻic cellum pātiyil amintuḷḷa aravaṅkāṭṭil paṭikkalat toḻiṟcāli (Cordite factory) oṉṟu amintuḷḷatu. itu ki. pi. 1904-ām āṇṭu araciyalārāl iṅku niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ittoḻiṟcāli āṟu pirivukaḷik koṇṭatu. itik kāṇa virumpuvōr, intiyap paṭit tuṟic ceyalāḷa (The secretary, Ministry of Defence)riṭam muṉkūṭṭiyē aṉumati peṟṟirukkavēṇṭum.

velliṅṭaṉ :

aravaṅkāṭṭiliruntu mūṉṟu kal tolivu vantāl 'velliṅṭaṉ' eṉṟa paṭiyiṉar vāḻviṭattik (Military town) kāṇalām. paṭiyiṉar taṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu amikkappaṭṭa illaṅkaḷ varici variciyāka amintuḷḷaṉa. ki. pi. 1852-ām āṇṭu iṅkup pāṭi vīṭukaḷ amikkap peṟuvutaṟku muṉ ivviṭam 'jēkṭalā' (Jacktala) eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭatu. iṅku aḻakiya poḻutu pōkku maṉṟam (Club} oṉṟu amintuḷḷatu. iṅkuḷḷa maṭṭip pantāṭṭa (Tennis courts) mitāṉaṅkaḷil irāṇuva atikārikaḷum, avar tam maṉiviyarumē viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉar. velliṅṭaṉukkuḷ ellārum aṉumatikkappaṭuvatilli, uṟaviṉarkaḷō, naṇparkaḷō iruntāl aṅkuc ceṉṟu iṉpamākap poḻutik kaḻikkamuṭiyum. ovvoru caṉikkiḻami iravilum, iṅkuḷḷa maṉṟaṅkaḷil mēli nāṭṭu naṭaṉaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa, āṭavarum peṇṭirum iṇintu iṉṉicik kēṟpa āṭum akkaḷiyāṭṭam kāṇṭaṟkiṉiyatu.

kāṉiṅ cīmāṭṭiyiṉ irukki :

ki. pi. 1857-ām āṇṭu vaṭintiyāvil naṭanta cippāyk kalakattiṉpōtu, intiya nāṭṭu aracap piratinitiyāka vīṟṟirunta kāṉiṅ pirapuviṉ maṉiviyār teṉṉintiyāviṟku vantārkaḷ. appoḻutu ceṉṉi, peṅkaḷūr, nīlakiri ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil ciṟitu kālam taṅkiṉārkaḷ. cīkūr malittoṭariṉ vaḻiyāka nīlakiriyi aṭintārkaḷ. ūṭṭiyiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu 1858-ām āṇṭu ēpral tiṅkaḷ 7-ām nāḷ kūṉūri aṭintārkaḷ. cīmāṭṭiyum avarōṭuvanta pirayāṇak kuḻuviṉarum oru māḷikiyil taṅkiṉārkaḷ. ammāḷiki iṉṟu kiḷaṉviyū hōṭṭal (Glenview Hotel) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. atē māḷikiyil tāṉ kāṉiṅkiṟku muṉ aracap piratinitiyāka irunta ṭalhucipirapu 1855-ām āṇṭu mē mutal ākasṭu vari taṅkiyiruntār.

kūṉūriliruntu 6 kal tolivil kāṉiṅ cīmāṭṭiyiṉ irukki {Lady Cannig's seat) amin tuḷḷatu. ivviṭam ivvammiyārukku mikavum piṭittamāṉatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. nāḷtōṟum ivviṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟu avar amarntiruppārām. 'iraṇṭu uttamar' (Two Noble Lives) eṉṟa āṅkilap putiṉattil (Novel), ivvammiyār iṅku amarntu eḻutiya muṭaṅkalkaḷ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivviṭam aḻakiya oru kāṭṭiṉ naṭuvil amintuḷḷatu. ivviṭattiliruntu kāṇpōrukkuk kūṉūriṉ muḻut tōṟṟamum, pala tēyilit tōṭṭaṅkaḷum, aḻakōṭu kāṭciyaḷikkum. avaṟṟōṭu micūri āṇṭa tippucultāṉiṉ paḻiya kōṭṭi oṉṟum teṉpaṭum. pakivariṭamiruntu kippaṟṟiya kitikaḷi avviṭattiliruntu kīḻē taḷḷik koṉṟuviṭuvārkaḷām. mēṭṭuppāḷiyamum, avvūriliruntu maliyiṉmēl vaḷintu vaḷintu ēṟivarum pukivaṇṭip pātiyum, untuvaṇṭip pātiyum naṉṟākat teriyum. kūṉūriliruntu iv virukkikkuc cellum vaḻiyil. lēmps pāṟi (Lamb's Rock) eṉṟa ōr aḻakiya iṭamum uṇṭu.

cim pūṅkā :

kūṉūr nakariṉ naṭuvil amintuḷḷa cim pūṅkā (Sim's Park) mikavum aḻakāṉatu. utakamaṇṭalattiluḷḷa araciyalār payirt tōṭṭattiviṭa itu aḷavil ciṟiyateṉṟālum uḷḷattik kavarum taṉmiyatu. maliccarivil itu amintuḷḷatu. ulakiṉ pala pakutikaḷilum uḷḷa maraṅkaḷ iṅkup payiriṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṭukkaṭukkāṉa tiṭṭukaḷil varici variciyākap perumaraṅkaḷum, pala vaṇṇa malarc ceṭikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. ciṟiya ōṭikaḷ nāṟpuṟamum ōṭivantu pūṅkāviṉ naṭuviluḷḷa kuḷattil viḻukiṉṟaṉa. akkuḷattiṉmītu pala aḻakiya ciṟu pālaṅkaḷ amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippūṅkā tiruvāḷar je. ṭi. cim ci. es. ai. (I. D. Sim C. S. I) eṉpavarāl niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ākiyiṉālēyē ippūṅkā ivar peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ivar ceṉṉi mēl capiyiṉ uṟuppiṉarāka (Member of the Legislative Council) 1870 mutal 1875 vari paṇiyāṟṟiṉār. intiyāvil tām vāḻnta iṟuti nāṭkaḷik kūṉūrilēyē kaḻittār. appōtu ippūṅkāvi amittu ataṉ vaḷarccikkup peritum pāṭupaṭṭār.

pāsṭar niṟuvaṉam :

kūṉūril kuṟippiṭattakka oṉṟu pāsṭar niṟuvaṉam (Pasteur Institute) ākum. lūyi pāsṭari aṟiyātavarkaḷ ulakil yārum irukka muṭiyātu. viññāṉa ulakil avaruṭiya ārāyccikaḷ puraṭcikaramāṉa māṟutalkaḷic ceytaṉa. 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil, avar āyntu veḷiyiṭṭa kirumiyārāycci (Bacteriology)k koḷkikaḷ maruttuvat tuṟikkup perum payaṉ viḷittaṉa. eṉavē, ulakiṉ pala pakutiyilum kirumiyārāyccik kaḻakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. intiyāvilum pala kaḻakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ākrā kaḻakam 1892-lum, pampāyi luḷḷa āḵpkiṉ kaḻakam (Haffkine Institute) 1896-lum, kaculiyiluḷḷa pāsṭar kaḻakam 1900-lum, ceṉṉi aracar ārāyccik kaḻakam (King Institute) 1903-lum, kaculi naṭuvaṇ ārāyccik kaḻakam (Central Research Institute) 1906-lum, niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. 1902-ām āṇṭu veṟi nāyk kaṭiyiṉāl tākkuṇṭa āṅkilac ciṟumiyi, kaculikkuk koṇṭu cellak kāla tāmatam ēṟpaṭṭatāl, ap peṇ iṟantuviṭṭāḷ. ic ceyti teṉṉintiyāvilirunta āṅkila atikārikaḷiṉ kavaṉattik kavarntatu. ataṉ viḷivākavē kūṉūriluḷḷa pāsṭar niṟuvaṉam 1907-ām āṇṭu ēpral tiṅkaḷ 25-ām nāḷ (viyāḻakkiḻami) ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.

amerikkāvil vāḻnta tiruvāḷar heṉṟi ḵpips (Mr. Henry Phipps) eṉṟa kōṭīcuvarar, intiya nāṭṭiṉ maruttuvattuṟi muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkākap perun tokiyi naṉkoṭiyāka vaḻaṅkiṉār. appōtu intiya nāṭṭiṉ makkaḷ talivarāka (Governor General} viḷaṅkiya karcaṉ pirapu an naṉkoṭiyiṉ poṟuppāḷarāka iruntār. at tokiyiliruntu oru laṭcam rūpāyi, teṉṉāṭṭil pāsṭar kaḻakam niṟuvuvataṟkāka vaḻaṅkiṉār. appōtu ceṉṉi mānila āḷunarāka viḷaṅkiya āmptil pirapu (Lord Ampthill) ap paṇiyil atika akkaṟi kāṭṭiṉār. ceṉṉiyil ciṟanta maruttuva nipuṇarāka viḷaṅkiya ṭapiḷyu. ār. piruṉ, ai.em.es, (W. R. Brown, I.M.S.) eṉpāriyum, ji. āris (G. Harris) eṉṟa kaṭṭiṭa nipuṇariyum kūṉūrukku aṉuppi, oru ciṟanta iṭattit tērn teṭuttu, pāsṭar niṟuvaṉatti amikkumāṟu paṇittār. ippōtu, in niṟuvaṉam nalla muṟiyil vaḷarcci peṟṟu, pala iṉṟiyamiyāta ārāyccikaḷic ceytukoṇṭirukkiṟatu. veṟi nāyk kaṭiyiṉāl tākkuṇṭa āyirak kaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ iṅkuc ciṟanta cikicci peṟukiṉṟaṉar.

piṟa kāṭcikaḷ :

kūṉūril kāṇṭaṟkuriya vēṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. pulimali (Tiger's Hill), vākkars mali (Walker's Hill), purūklēṇṭs pāti (Brooklands Road) ākiyavi kuḷirnta niḻal tarum maraṅkaḷaṭarnta kāṭukaḷiṉ naṭuvil amintuḷḷaṉa. utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu, keyiṭṭi paḷḷattākku, aravaṅkāṭu, kūṉūr, kiḷaṉ mōrkaṉ tēyilit tōṭṭam, kāṭṭēri aṇi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu maṟupaṭiyum utakamaṇṭalatti aṭiyum cuṟṟuc celavu (Round trip) uḷḷattiṟku mikavum iṉpam payakkakkūṭiyatākum. kūṉūrukku 6 kal toliviliruntu aḻakiya kuntā malittoṭar toṭaṅkukiṟatu. aṭarnta kāṭukaḷiyuṭiya am malittoṭar, ārāyum iyalpūkkamuṭiyōrkku nalvāyppi nalkum. kūṉūril pala ciṟanta paḷḷikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. puṉita cūciyappar paḷḷi (St. Joseph's School) mikavum toṉmiyāṉatu.

kōtakiri :
