utakamaṇṭalattiliruntu 18-āvatu kallil kōtakiri eṉṟa ūr amintuḷḷatu. nīlakiri maṭantiyiṉ kātil oḷirum āṇimuttu eṉṟu itik kūṟalām. utakiyiliruntu toṭṭapeṭṭāviṟkuc cellum vaḻiyil nāṉku kal ceṉṟu iṭatu puṟamākac cellum ulōkap pāti (Metal Road) yil ceṉṟāl, kōtakiriyi aṭiyalām. iv vaḻiyiṉ oru puṟattil aḻakiya paḷḷattākkiyum, ciṟṟūrkaḷiyum kāṇalām. tummaṉaṭi, maṭittoṟi, kuṇṭamukki mutaliya ūrkaḷik kaṭantu cellum pōtu nāṭṭuppuṟa vāḻviṉ ciṟappu nam uḷḷattil teṉpaṭum. ivvūrkaḷik kaṭantatum vaḻi neṭukat tōṉṟum nīraruvikaḷum, iḷamarak kāṭukaḷum, pacum pul veḷikaḷum nammi mīḷā iṉpattilāḻttum. kuṟuki vaḷintu cellum pātikaḷum, avaṟṟil amintuḷḷa vaḷivukaḷil perumūccu viṭṭuk koṇṭu tiṇaṟi ēṟum untu vaṇṭikaḷum, kōtarkaḷiṉ nāṭṭuppuṟa vāḻvum, nam uḷḷatti viṭṭu nīṅkāmal nilittu, niṉikkuntōṟum iṉikkum. 15 kal kaṭantatum kaṭṭapuṭṭi eṉṟa ciṟṟūr uḷḷatu. avviṭattiliruntu mīṇṭum tār pōṭappaṭṭa pāti toṭaṅkukiṟatu. aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷiṭiyē mēlum mūṉṟu kal ceṉṟōmāṉāl, kōtakiriyi aṭiyalām. kōtakiriyiṉ kaṭivīti oru carivil amintuḷḷatu. av vītiyilēyē ūrāṇmik kaḻaka aluvalakamum, vēṟu cila aḻakiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum amintuḷḷaṉa.



5. paḻaṉi malikaḷ

peyark kāraṇam :

paḻaṉimali eṉṟu kuṟippiṭumpōtu, murukaṉ kōvil koṇṭu eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum karumaliyē ellōruṭiya uḷḷattilum tōṉṟum. āṉāl iṅkuk kuṟippiṭappaṭum paḻaṉi malikaḷ, paḻaṉi nakarukkut teṟkiluḷḷa malik kūṭṭamē ākum. vaṭa moḻiyil ivaṟṟi 'varāka kiri' eṉpar. tamiḻil itip paṉṟi mali eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar. im malikku ip peyar ēṟpaṭṭataṟkuk kāraṇamāka oru karṇa paramparik kati vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. im malimītu oru muṉivar tavam ceytukoṇṭiruntārām, av vaḻi vanta paṉṉiraṇṭu kuṟumpukkāra iḷiñarkaḷ am muṉivari ikaḻntu pēciṉārkaḷām. itaṉāl ciṉaṅkoṇṭa am muṉivar ap paṉṉiruvariyum paṉṟikaḷākap pōkumpaṭi capittārām. avarkaḷum paṉṟikaḷāka māṟi am malimītu cuṟṟit tirintārkaḷ . avarkaḷ pāl irakkam koṇṭa civaperumāṉ avarkaḷuṭiya paṉṟiyuruvi māṟṟi, pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ avikkaḷattil amiccarkaḷāka amarttiṉārām. ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyē im mali paṇṭi nāṭkaḷil paṉṟi mali eṉṟu peruvaḻakkāka aḻikkappaṭṭu vantatu eṉpar. paṉṟi maliyiṉ tiripē paḻaṉi mali eṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. pukaḻ mikka paḻaṉi nakarukku arukil iruppataṉāl, im mali paḻaṉi mali eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatākavum vēṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

amippu:

itu mēṟkut toṭarcci maliyiṉ oru pirivākum. im malikkut teṉ mēṟkē ēlakkāy mali (Cardamon Hills) amintuḷḷatu. am maliyiṉ kiḻakkē kampam paḷḷattākku amintuḷḷatu. paḻaṉi maliyiṉ nīḷam 40 kal, akalam 25 kal, itu kiḻakkup pakuti eṉṟum, mēṟkup pakuti eṉṟum iru pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ippirivukaḷi mēṟpaḻaṉi eṉṟum kīḻppaḻaṉi eṉṟum kuṟippiṭuvar. kīḻppaḻaṉi maliyil 3000 aṭiyiliruntu 5000 aṭivari uyaramuḷḷa pala cikaraṅkaḷ citaṟik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ic cikaraṅkaḷiṭiyē kuṟukiya pala paḷḷattākkukaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. aḻakiṉ iruppiṭamāka viḷaṅkum ippaḷḷattākkukaḷil aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumākap pala kukkirāmaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil kuṟippiṭattakkatum, periyatum 'paṇṇikkāṭu' eṉṟa ciṟṟūrām. itil 4000 makkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ic ciṟṟūrkaḷic cuṟṟi mā, palā, puḷiyaṉ, ārañcu, elumicci, cītaḷi (itu oruvakip pērelumicci, kaṭāraṅkāy eṉṟum kūṟuvar. Citron) mutaliya maraṅkaḷaṭarnta cōlikaḷ kaṇkavarum vaṉappōṭu viḷaṅkum. iṅku vāḻum makkaḷip 'paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ' (Hill tribes) eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli. ivarkaḷellām kōvi, maturi māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa camaveḷikaḷiliruntu paṇṭi nāṭkaḷil iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷē, ivarkaḷ tōṟṟattilum, naṭi uṭi pāvaṉikaḷilum, aṅka amippilum camaveḷiyil vāḻum makkaḷiliruntu vēṟupaṭṭavarkaḷākat teriyavilli. iṅku vāḻum 'kuṉṉuvar' (Kunnuvans) eṉṟa kulattārē perumpālum nilakkiḻārkaḷāka uḷḷaṉar. iṅku vāḻum ‘puliyar' eṉṉum iṉattār ivarkaḷukkup paṇipurintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. iṅku vāḻum teluṅkuc ceṭṭimārkaḷum, iculāmiyarkaḷum kuṉṉuvarkaḷiṉ nilattik koñcam koñcamākat taṅkaḷukku urimiyākkik koṇṭaṉar. ivviru iṉattārum ōraḷavu paṇam paṭittavarkaḷ, kuṉṉuvarukkum, puliyarukkum vēṇṭiyapōtu paṇaṅkoṭuttu avarkaḷik kaṭaṉkārarkaḷākki, piṟaku avarkaḷ nilaṅkaḷiyum paṟittuk koṇṭaṉar. ikkīḻp pakutiyāṉatu malēriyā nōykku iruppiṭam eṉṟu collalām. mārc mutal jūli vari iṅku vāḻum makkaḷ kāyccalāl tākkuṇṭu peritum varuntukiṉṟaṉar, āṉāl maṟṟa tiṅkaḷkaḷil kāyccal varātu eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu.

mēl paḻaṉimali 6000 aṭi mutal 8000 aṭi variyil uyarvuḷḷatu. paḻaṉi maliyil mikavum uyarnta cikaram vempāṭi ṣōlā mali eṉpatākum. itu maturi māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa ellā maliccikaraṅkaḷiyum viṭa uyarntatu eṉṟē collalām. itaṉ uyaram 8218 aṭi. kuṟiñci nakaramāṉa kōṭikkāṉal im mēṟpaḻaṉiyiṉ teṉpākattil amintuḷḷatu. kīḻppaḻaṉiyil paraviyuḷḷa aṭarnta kāṭukaḷi iṅkuk kāṇamuṭiyātu. niṟinta paḷḷattākkukaḷum iṅkuk kiṭiyā. veppa niliyum kuḷirntu tōṉṟum. payirt toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭāta nilam iṅku atikam. iṅkuḷḷa pīṭapūmikaḷil muraṭṭup pul muḷitta paranta veḷikaḷ (Downs) mikuntu tōṉṟum, oru cila paḷḷat tākkukaḷil makkaḷiṉ kuṭiyiruppukaḷic cuṟṟi utakamaṇṭalattil iruppavaṟṟip pōṉṟu iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ (Sholas) niṟintirukkum. ip paḷḷattākkukaḷit tavira maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa nilaṅkaḷellām, pul cetti aṭarnta melliya maṇpaṭalattāl mūṭappaṭṭirukkum. appaṭalattiṉaṭiyil, mañcaḷ niṟamāṉa kaḷimaṇ nilam amintirukkum. ak kaḷimaṇṇiṟ kaṭiyil paraviyuḷḷa kaṟpāṟikaḷ, eṅkup pārttālum tali nīṭṭikkoṇṭirukkum.

paḻaṉimali mītuḷḷa carivu vaṭakku nōkkip paṭippaṭiyāka iṟaṅkukiṟatu. āṉāl teṉpakutiyō mikavum ceṅkuttāṉa pāṟikaḷiyum carivukaḷiyum koṇṭatu. kampam paḷḷattākkiliruntu kāṇpōrukku accariviluḷḷa pāṟikaḷ periya cuvarkaḷ pōl kāṭciyaḷikkum. appāṟikaḷiṉiṭiyē perumperum paḷḷaṅkaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. aṭivārattiliruntu kāṇpōrukku akkāṭci viyappūṭṭum eṉpatil aiyamilli. vaṭa pakutiyil amintuḷḷa iruperum paḷḷattākkukaḷ appīṭapūmiyi iṭiyaṟuttuk koṇṭu vilpaṭṭi, pūmpāṟi ākiya ciṟṟūrkaḷ vari teṟku nōkkic cellukiṉṟaṉa. camaveḷiyiliruntu ivviru paḷḷattākkukaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka maliyucciyi vantaṭiyum iru kuṟukiya pātikaḷum kuṟippiṭattakkavi. im maliyōṭu neruṅkiya vāṇipat toṭarpu koṇṭa nakaram 'paḻaṉi' ākum. paḻaṉiyiliruntu puṟappaṭuvōr kāl naṭikaḷiṉ mītu potikaḷi ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ippātikaḷ mūlamākavē ucciyi aṭivar. paḻaṉiyiliruntu vilpaṭṭi cellum malivaḻip pāti aṭikkaṭi kuṟukiya paḷḷaṅkaḷāl iṭiyaṟukkappaṭuvatāl, kutirikaḷ ivvaḻiyākac cella muṭivatilli. āṉāl pūmpāṟikkuc cellum malivaḻippāti celluvataṟku eḷitāṉatu. periyakuḷattiliruntu kōṭikkāṉal cellum kutirip pāti (bridle path)' amikkappaṭṭatum, mēṟkūṟiya iru pātikaḷum kavaṉippāraṟṟuc cīr keṭṭup pōyiṉa. kutirip pātiyum lās pāti (Law's Ghat)yum, putiya āttūrppātiyum, kōṭikkāṉal kuṭiyiruppukaḷi iṇikkum pātikaḷumē paḻaṉi malimītu puḻakkattiliruntavi. ippōtu ōraḷavu cīrāṉa pātikaḷ amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ōr ūriliruntu maṟṟōr ūrukkuc ceyti aṟivikkum vēliyāṉatu, karaṭu muraṭāṉa malivaḻip pātikaḷiṉ mūlamum, kaṭappataṟkariya kāṭṭuvaḻikaḷiṉ mūlamumē. naṭipeṟṟaṉa.



immali mītu amintuḷḷa pūmpāṟip paḷḷattākku mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ip paḷḷattākkiṉ irupuṟac carivukaḷum cama aḷavuḷḷavi. ic carivukaḷil payirttoḻil ciṟanta muṟiyil naṭipeṟukiṟatu. eṅkup pārttālum aḻakiya iḷa marak kāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa, aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumākap piḷavupaṭṭa nilappakutikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cuṟṟilumuḷḷa nilaṅkaḷiṉ tāḻcciyiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa ciṟu ciṟu ceṅkuttāṉa mēṭukaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaḷḷat tākkiṉ ucciyil amintuḷḷa oru mēṭṭu nilattilēyē 'pūmpāṟi' amintuḷḷatu. itil ēṟakkuṟiya 2000 makkaḷē vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mēl paḻaṉi malimītu amintuḷḷa ciṟṟūrkaḷil itu mukkiyamāṉatu. immalimītu nilavari vacūlippataṟkāṉa aluvalakam, muṉpu iṅku tāṉ amintiruntatu. iṅku amintuḷḷa cuppiramaṇiyar kōvil mikavum pukaḻ vāyntatākum. viḻā nāṭkaḷil paḻaṉimaliyiṉ pala pakutiyiliruntum makkaḷ iṅku vantu kūṭu kiṉṟaṉar.

mēlpaḻaṉiyil amintuḷḷa vīṭukaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷ kuṟukkum neṭukkumākap poruttappaṭṭa mūṅkil kaḻikaḷiṉ mēl maṇ pūcappaṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kūri puṟkaḷāl vēyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ovvoru vīṭṭilum kaṉappu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅkuk kuṉṉuvar, kārakkāṭṭu veḷḷāḷar (kārkātta vēḷāḷar) eṉṟa iru iṉattārum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷ toki mikak kuṟivu. mēl paḻaṉi, kīḻppaḻaṉi ākiya ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷiṉ mottat toki 20,000.

maturi māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa malikaḷiṉiṭiyē īṭum eṭuppumaṟṟup paḻaṉimali perumitattōṭu nimirntu niṟkiṟatu. kāliñāyiṟṟup poṉṉoḷiyil pakkaṅkaḷiluḷḷa toṭarkaḷōṭu tali nimirntu niṟkum cikaraṅkaḷ, kāḷiyarkaḷ palar tam valimikka tōḷkaḷi uyarttikkoṇṭu niṟpatu pōl tōṉṟum. naṭuppakal nērattil maṟṟa malikaḷit taṉ cemmānta uyarattāl iruḷil āḻttiviṭṭu oḷiyuṭaṉ tikaḻum, māli nērattil katiravaṉiṉ cenniṟak kiraṇaṅkaḷip pōrttukkoṇṭu īṭillāp peruvaṉappōṭu tikaḻum. perumaḻi peytu ōynta camayattil itaṉ uyariya cikaraṅkaḷ mēkaṅkaḷākiya muṭiyit taliyil cumantukoṇṭu, peruñ ciṟappōṭu niṟkum pēraracippōl kāṭciyaḷikkum. itaṉ carivukaḷil iḻintu calacalattu ōṭi varum nīr aruvikaḷiṉ mītum, ekkāḷamiṭṭut tāvi varum nīr vīḻccikaḷiṉ mītum taṅkak katirōṉ taṉ taṇaṟ kuḻampip pūci makiḻumpōtu, pacumiyum nīlamum māṟi māṟic cuṭarviṭum.

āṟukaḷ :

maturi māvaṭṭattiṉ vaṭa pakutiyil uḷḷa paḻaṉi, tiṇṭukkal vaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa cemmaṇ nilattil cama tūraṅkaḷil pāyum nāṉku āṟukaḷum paḻaṉi maliyilēyē tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. koṭavaṉāṟu, naṅkāñci āṟu, nal taṅkiyāṟu, caṇmuka nati eṉpaviyē avi. innāṉkum kāviriyiṉ tuṇi natiyāṉa amarāvatiyil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. paḻaṉiyil perumaḻi peyta kālaṅkaḷil ivvāṟukaḷilum tiṭīreṉṟu veḷḷap perukku ēṟpaṭum. cila nāṭkaḷil ap perukkut taṇintu, ciṟṟōṭiyākak kāṭci tarum. innāṉku āṟukaḷil mikavum payaṉuṭiyatu caṇmuka natiyākum. vilpaṭṭi, pūm pāṟip paḷḷattākkukaḷil vaḻiyum nīrip peṟṟu itu ōṭi varukiṟatu. ippaḷḷattākkukaḷil amintuḷḷa āṟu pirivāṉa paṭukikaḷil iḻintu varum nīr oṉṟākakkūṭi ivvāṟṟi urukkoḷḷumāṟu ceyvatāl ivvāṟu caṇmuka (āṟumuka) nati eṉṟu peyar peṟukiṟatu. itu paḻaṉi nakariṉiṭiyē ōṭuvatāl, appaḻaṉiyil kōyil koṇṭirukkum caṇmukaṉiṉ peyarē ivvāṟṟukkum vaḻaṅkuvatāka ellōrum eṇṇikkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.

mēṟkūṟiya āṟukaḷ nāṉkum maturi māvaṭṭattiṉ vaṭa elliyil ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. maturi māvaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil vikiyum, ataṉ tuṇiyāṟukaḷum ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. vikiyiṉ tuṇiyāṟukaḷil pala paḻaṉimaṭiyil tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. varāka nati, mēl paḻaṉimaliyil tōṉṟi, periya kuḷattiṟkarukil pāmpāṟṟōṭu kalakkiṟatu, pāmpāṟum paḻaṉimaliyilēyē tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivvāṟu kōṭikkāṉalukkarukil ōr aḻakiya nīrvīḻcciyi uṇṭākkukiṟatu. kutirip pātikkarukiliruntu kāṇpōrukku innīrvīḻcci peruvaṉappōṭu kāṭci tarum. ivviru natikaḷum vikiyōṭu kalakkiṉṟaṉa. mañcaḷāṟu eṉṟa vēṟoru natiyum paḻaṉimalic carivil tōṉṟi ōṭi varukiṉṟatu. iti vattalakuṇṭu āṟu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. itu tēvatāṉappaṭṭikku mēl 200 aṭi uyarattiliruntu vīḻntu, ōr aḻakiya nīrvīḻcciyi uṇṭākkukiṟatu. pātiyiliruntu kāṇpōrukku avvīḻcci kaṇkavarum vaṉappōṭu kāṭciyaḷikkum, piṟaku ivvāṟu vattalakuṇṭuvi nōkki ōṭi varukiṟatu. kīḻppaḻaṉiyiliruntu tōṉṟivarum piṟitōr āṟāṉa ayyampāḷiyam āṟṟōṭu cērntu, mañcaḷāṟu vikiyil kalakkiṟatu.

kāṭukaḷ :

paḻaṉimalic carivukaḷil aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl avi ippōtu vāṭiyum, perum pakuti aḻikkappaṭṭum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aḷavaṟṟa maraṅkaḷi veṭṭiyum, tīyiṭṭup pocukkiyum pāḻpaṭutti viṭṭaṉar. ivvāṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa pōka mikavum kuṟivāṉa maraṅkaḷē iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cariviluḷḷa kāṭukaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavi, paḻaṉimaliyiṉ vaṭakiḻakkup pakutiyiluḷḷa āyakkuṭi, kaṉṉivāṭi esṭēṭṭukaḷukku iṭiyiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷē. maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷip pōlallāmal ivviṭattil nilam vaḷamuḷḷatāka uḷḷatu. vaṭa mēṟku mūliyil, kūkal ṣōlāviliruntu amarāvati āṟuvariyilum uḷḷa carivu eḷitil aṭitaṟkariyatu. ākiyiṉāl accarivukaḷiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷ aḻivuṟāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. periya kuḷattiṟku mēṟkil amintuḷḷa akkamalittoṭaril uḷḷa kāṭṭu maraṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭum, tīyiṭappaṭṭum, payirt toḻilukkāka aḻikkappaṭṭum kēṭuṟṟaṉa. āṉāl ippoḻutu araciyalār taliyīṭṭāl akkāṭukaḷ puttuyir peṟṟuḷḷaṉa, aḻikkappaṭṭa kāṭukaḷil uyarnta raka maraṅkaḷ, piṟaku nalla muṟiyil vaḷarva tilli. muṭpu tarkaḷum, payaṉaṟṟa ciṟu maraṅkaḷumē vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. paḻaṉimalic carivukaḷil potuvāka vēṅki, vekkali, veṉ tēvatāru, centēvatāru, tēkku, karuṅkāli, kālnaṭ mutaliya maraṅkaḷ mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

kīḻp paḻaṉi, mēṟ paḻaṉip pīṭapūmikaḷiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷil vaḷarum maraṅkaḷ mikavum vili uyarntavi. kīḻppaḻaṉip pīṭapūmikaḷiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷ vāḻiyum, kāḵpiyum payiriṭuvataṟkākap perum aḷavu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. ēlamum aṅku mikutiyākap payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivviṭattil amintuḷḷa paḷḷat tākkukaḷil paṭintirukkum karumaṇ vaḷamāṉatu; maṇalum kaḷiyum kalantatu. iṅku vaḷarntirukkum iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ aḻivuṟāmal ceḻippuṟṟu viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. centēvatāru maraṅkaḷum, kāl naṭ maraṅkaḷum, viṭeks, ālṭīcimā mutaliya maraṅkaḷum iṅku ceḻittu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. centēvatāru maraṅkaḷum, viṭeks, ālṭīcimā maraṅkaḷum peṭṭikaḷ ceyvataṟkum caṭṭaṅkaḷ aṟuppataṟkum peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa,

mēṟkiluḷḷa mēṟpaḻaṉip pīṭapūmi ciṟitu ciṟitāka uyarntu cellat toṭaṅkiyatum aṅkuḷḷa maṇ, vaḷam aṟṟatāka māṟukiṟatu. tāṇṭik kuṭikkum paṇṇikkāṭṭiṟkum iṭiyiluḷḷa tāḻnta malippakutiyit tāṇṭiyatum, maram ceṭi koṭikaḷellām vaḷarcciyil muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ aṅkuk kiṭiyā. eṅkup pārttālum pulveḷikaḷum, muṭputarkaḷum, kuṭṭiyāṉa maraṅkaḷumē teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅkuḷḷa īramāṉa paḷḷattākkukaḷil maṭṭum ōriru iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pattup paṉṉiraṇṭu kāṭukaḷē ōraḷavu periyavi. pulik kāṭu (Tiger Shola), perumāḷ kāṭu (Perumal Shola) vañcakkāṉal, kōṭikkāṉal, kuṇṭaṉ kāṭu (Kundan Shola} kūkal kāṭu {Kukal Shola) eṉpavi kuṟippiṭattakkavi. kōṭikkāṉaliliruntu nāṉku kal mēṟkiluḷḷa ṭākṭars ṭiliṭ (Doctor's delight) eṉṉum iḷamarakkāṭu, vaṉapōcaṉam uṇpōrukku mikavum ēṟṟa iṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. iṅku vaḷarumpaṭiyāṉa ciṟu maraṅkaḷ viṭṭaṅkaḷ aṟuppataṟkō, palakikaḷ aṟuppataṟkō payaṉpaṭātavi, iṅku pala nīr aruvikaḷ tōṉṟuvataṟkuriya īrattip pātukāppataṟkē immaraṅkaḷ peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa kāṭukaḷ muṉpu peritum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. mēṟpaḻaṉi maliyiṉ ucciyil uḷḷa ippīṭapūmi kōṭikkāṉal elliyiliruntu toṭaṅki mēṟkil tiruvāṅkūr elli variyilum, teṟkil pōṭināyakkaṉūr variyilum paraviyuḷḷatu. kāṭṭuc caṭṭattiṉ (Forest Act)paṭi iṅkuḷḷa kāṭukaḷ araciyalārāl pātukākkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, ippīṭapūmikku, āmptil veḷi, ( Ampthill downs) eṉṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ parappu 5 caturamil, ippīṭapūmiyiṉ kālpakuti kāṭukaḷ. mukkāl pakuti paranta pacumpul veḷikaḷ. iṅku 7000 aṭi mutal 8000 aṭivari uyaramuḷḷa ciṟitum peritumāṉa pala cikaraṅkaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. im mā nilattiluḷḷa mikavum aḻakiya maliveḷikaḷil āmptil veḷiyum oṉṟu.

paḻaṉimaliyiṉmītu pala iṭaṅkaḷil ceyaṟki muṟiyil kāṭukaḷ vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki. pi. 1870-ām āṇṭu kēmpel vākkar (Colonel Compbell Walker) eṉpavar, paḻaṉimaliyiṉ vaṭapuṟa aṭivārattil uḷḷa vēlaṉ kōmpi eṉṟa iṭattilum, periyāṟṟu ērikkarukiluḷḷa aṭivāramākiya vaṇṇāttippāṟi eṉṟa iṭattilum tēkku maraṅkaḷip payiriṭṭār. avviṭattil ēṟakkuṟiya 4500 maraṅkaḷ vaḷarntaṉa. āṉāl, tēkku payiriṭuvataṟkut tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa avviṭaṅkaḷ, teṉ mēṟkup paruvakkāṟṟiṉāl peyyum maḻiyiṉ muḻup payaṉiyum peṟuvatilli. vēlaṉ kōmpiyil vaḷarkkappaṭṭa tēkkuk kāṭukaḷ, appakkamāka ōṭivarum kālvāyiṉāl nalla nīr vaḷattip peṟṟiruntaṉa. āṉāl aṭikkaṭi ēṟpaṭum peruveḷḷattāl niṟiya maraṅkaḷ aḻivuṟṟaṉa, atē āṇṭil kōṭikkāṉalil nīlap piciṉ maraṅka (Blue gum trees}ḷum, āstirēliya nāṭṭic cērnta karumaraṅkaḷum payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. kōṭikkāṉalil vāḻum makkaḷukku eriporuḷ vaḻaṅkuvataṟkum, aṅkuḷḷa aḻakiya iḷamarak kāṭṭi aḻivuṟāmal kāppataṟkumē ivikaḷ payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. ivviṭamum immaraṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarccikku ēṟṟatāka illi. kōṭikkāṉaliṉ eriporuḷ tēviyi annakarukku mēṟkil 2 kal toliviluḷḷa kuṇṭaṉ kāṭṭiluḷḷa maraṅkaḷē pūrtti ceykiṉṟaṉa. ikkāṭṭil 1887-88ām āṇṭukaḷil niṟiya maraṅkaḷ payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. 1895ām āṇṭilum, 1905 ām āṇṭilum, kuṇṭaṉ kāṭṭiṉ perumpakuti tīyiṉāl aḻivuṟṟatu.

vilaṅkukaḷ :

paḻaṉi maliyiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷilum yāṉikaḷ muṉpu niṟiyat tirintaṉa, kiḻakkiluḷḷa kaṉṉivāṭi variyilum kūṭa avikaḷ vantu payirkaḷukku mikavum cētam viḷivittaṉa. ivaṟṟāl ēṟpaṭum aḻivit taṭukka araciyalār perum muyaṟcikaḷ eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉar. jervis eṉṟa oru veḷḷiyar, tām eḻutiyuḷḷa, kāviriyiṉ nīr vīḻccikaḷi nōkkic ceṉṟa payaṇattiṉ varuṇaṉi' (Narrative of a journey to the falls of Cauvery) eṉṟa nūlil paḻaṉimaliyil tiriyum yāṉikaḷippaṟṟi virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷār. kampam paḷḷattākkiṟku arukiluḷḷa malikaḷiṭiyē iyaṟkiyāka amintuḷḷa kaṇavāykaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka avikaḷ varukiṉṟaṉavām, poyk kuḻikaḷ amittu, ikkaṇavāykaḷil eḷitāka ivaṟṟip piṭikkiṉṟaṉarām. ikkaṇavāy ōriṭattil mikavum kuṟukic cellukiṟatām. akkuṟukalāṉa iṭattil oru yāṉi tāṉ nuḻiya muṭiyum. avviṭattiṟku appāluḷḷa akaṉṟa kaṇavāyil varici variciyāṉa pala kuḻikaḷi veṭṭi vippārkaḷ. yāṉik kūṭṭatti akkuṟukiya vaḻiyiṉ mūlamāka viraṭṭiṉāl, mika virivil av yāṉikaḷ kuḻikaḷil viḻuntuviṭum. oru taṭavi nāṉkumaṇi nērattil, 63 yāṉikaḷ immuṟiyil kippaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavām. mēṟpaḻaṉiyilum, carivukaḷilum puli, civiṅkip puli, karaṭi, kāṭṭerumi, māṉ, variyāṭu, kāṭṭuppaṉṟi, cennāy mutaliyaṉa vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. kōṭikkāṉalil kākkikaḷik kāṇa muṭiyātu.

payir vakikaḷ :

kīḻppaḻaṉip pīṭapūmikaḷil vāḻum kuṉṉuvarkaḷum puliyarkaḷum puṉceyp payirkaḷip payiriṭuvatōṭu, vāḻi maraṅkaḷiyum niṟiyap payiriṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṅkup payirākum vāḻippaḻam mikka cuviyum maṇamumuḷḷatu. kōṭikkāṉal vaṭṭattil vēṟeṅkum ivvaḷavu uyarnta raka vāḻi payirāvatilli. iṅkup payiriṭappaṭum vāḻi maraṅkaḷ toṭarntu 20 āṇṭukaḷukkup palaṉ koṭukkum taṉmiyuṭiyavi. iṅku, nel, kāḵpi, ēlam, iñci, mañcaḷ mutaliyaṉavum nalla muṟiyil payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅku arēpikā eṉṉum uyarnta rakak kāḵpi viḷikiṟatu. kāḵpip payiri mutaṉ mutalil payiriṭṭavar em. emiliṭi ḵpāṇṭkiḷēr eṉṉum veḷḷiyar. ciṟumaliyiṉmītu ivaruṭiya tantiyār payiriṭṭa kāḵpi vitikaḷi iṅkuk koṇarntu ivarum payiriṭṭār. ippōtu nam nāṭṭiṟku nalla varuvāy nalkum payirāka itu iṅku viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ēlamum, iñciyum viḷiya nalla niḻal vēṇṭiyiruppatāl aṭarnta kāṭukaḷilēyē ivikaḷ viḷikiṉṟaṉa. ēlam aintu āṇṭukaḷilum, mañcaḷ 18 mātaṅkaḷilum palaṉ koṭukkum. mañcaḷ tiṟanta veḷikaḷilēyē payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu. mā, palā, ārañcu, elumicci, mātuḷi, koṭittirāṭci, pḷam tirāṭci, āppiḷ, perri mutaliya paḻaṅkaḷum iṅku viḷikiṉṟaṉa.

mēṟpaḻaṉimaliyil nel, kāḵpi, maṭṭarakak kōtumi, maṭṭarakap pārli, veḷḷippūṇṭu, paḻa vakikaḷ ākiyavi niṟiya viḷikiṉṟaṉa. iṅku viḷiyum veḷḷippūṇṭu perum aḷavil nāṭeṅkum ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. maliccarivukaḷil aṭukkaṭukkāka nel vayalkaḷ amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maliyiliruntu iḻintu varum nīraruvikaḷit taṭuttut tēkki, ciṟu vāykkālkaḷiṉ mūlamāka nel vayalukkup pāyccukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ nel vayalkaḷil eruvik koṭṭi mitippatilli. tēkkiya nīri vāyk kālkaḷiṉ mūlamāka erukkuviyalkaḷiṭiyē pāycci, avaṟṟil ūṟi varum nīri nel vayalkaḷil pāyccukiṉṟaṉar. iṅku viḷiyum nel avvaḷavu uyarnta rakattic cārntatalla. neṟpayir muṟṟa eṭṭu allatu pattu mātaṅkaḷ cellum.

makkaḷ

kuṉṉuvar :

nīlakiri maliyiṉmītu paṭakar evvāṟu ciṟanta uḻavarkaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉarō, atupōl paḻaṉimaliyil kuṉṉuvar ciṟanta uḻavarkaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuṭiya tāymoḻi tamiḻ moḻiyē ākum. ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷik kuṉṉuva vēḷāḷar eṉṟu kūṟik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. kōvi māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tārāpuram, kāṅkayam pakutikaḷē taṅkaḷ muṉṉōr vāḻnta iṭameṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. pōrum pañcamum mikuntirunta kāraṇattāl, aintāṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ ivarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ camaveḷiyiliruntu iṅkuk kuṭipukuntārkaḷām. vijayanakara maṉṉar, marāṭṭiya maṉṉar, tippucultāṉ ākiyōriṉ āṭciyil, variccumi tāḷāmalum vēṟu pala tuṉpaṅ kaḷukku āṭpaṭṭum varuntiyavarkaḷ palar iṅkuk kuṭi pukuntārkaḷ. oru camayam kakkaṟkaḻicca (Cholera) liṉāl avatippaṭṭa camaveḷi makkaḷum iṅkuk kuṭi. pukuntatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kōvi māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa kuṉṉūr eṉṟa ciṟṟūriṉ peyarē taṅkaḷ kuṭippeyarāka amintateṉṟu kāraṇam kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kuṉṉuvar paḻaṉimaliyil kuṭipukuntatu paṟṟi vēṟoru ceytiyum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. virūpāṭci, āyakkuṭi ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻnta pōlikar eṉṉum vakuppār malimītuḷḷa taṅkaḷuṭiya nilaṅkaḷil paṇipurivataṟkāka ivarkaḷi iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟṟiṉārkaḷ. atuvariyil annilaṅkaḷil paṇipurintu vantavarkaḷ puliyar eṉṉum kulattār. avarkaḷ cōmpēṟikaḷ. kuṉṉuvar avarkaḷit turattiviṭṭu, avarkaḷ iṭattik kippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. atōṭu avarkaḷi māṟāta aṭimikaḷākavum ākkikkoṇṭaṉar.

kuṉṉuvar vāḻum ovvōr ūrilum avarkaḷuṭiya camūkaviyali mēṟpārkka oru talivaṉuṇṭu. avaṉukku ‘maṇṇāṭi' eṉṟu peyar. ūril avaṉ mikavum celvākku mikkavaṉākak karutappaṭukiṟāṉ. ivviṉattār, kuṉṉuvar, periya kuṉṉuvar, ciṉṉak kuṉṉuvar eṉa muppirivākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ippirivukaḷukku 'vakuppu'kkaḷ eṉṟu peyar. oru vakuppiṉar maṟṟa vakuppiṉarōṭu maṇa uṟavu koḷḷum vaḻakkam illi. āṉāl oṉṟāka amarntu uṇṇuvar. kuṉṉuvap peṇ aṇiyum uṭi oru mātiriyāka irukkum. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭiya mēlāṭiyāl mārpip pōrttu muṉṉāl muṭiyiṭṭuk koṇṭu, avvāṭiyiyē iṭuppil cuṟṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. ulōkattāl ceyta kaḻuttaṇikaḷiyum, pittaḷiyāl ceyta vaḷiyal, kolucukaḷiyum, veḷḷiyiṉāṟ ceyta kaṭakam mūkku vaḷikaḷiyum aṇikiṉṟaṉar. veḷḷi āṭiyi muṉṉāṭkaḷil peṇkaḷ aṇiyakkūṭātu eṉṟa kaṭṭuppāṭu iruntatu. āṉāl ippoḻutu perumpālōr veḷḷi āṭiyē uṭukkiṉṟaṉar.

ivarkaḷiṭiyilum māṉarīkamuṟi mikavum kaṭṭuppāṭṭōṭu kaṭippiṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. tantiyōṭu piṟanta attiyiṉ makaḷē perumpālum maṇamakaḷākat tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṟāḷ. vayatup poruttam kūṭa ivarkaḷāl kavaṉikkappaṭuvatilli. cila camayaṅkaḷil maṇamakaṉiviṭa maṇamakaḷ mikavum vayatil mūttavaḷāka iruppatuṇṭu. mikavum iḷiñaṉāṉa oruvaṉukku oṉṟuviṭṭa attimārkaḷiṉ vīṭṭiliruntu, iraṇṭu mūṉṟu peṇkaḷit tirumaṇam ceytu vippatuṇṭu. paruvamaṭiyāta acciṟuvaṉiviṭa, vayatil mūttirukkum maṉiviyar, avarkaḷ kulattilē viruppamuḷḷa vēṟu āṭavarkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koḷḷuvārkaḷ. avvāṟu toṭarpu koḷḷuvataṉ mūlam piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷ caṭṭappaṭi kaṇavaṉukkē piṟantavikaḷākak karutappaṭum. ataṉāl oṉpatu allatu pattu vayatuḷḷa cila ciṟuvarkaḷ (Putative fathers) iraṇṭu mūṉṟu kuḻantiyarukkut tantiyarāka iruppatuṇṭu. ivarkaḷuṭiya tirumaṇam caṭaṅkukaḷaṟṟatu. maṇamakaṉiṉ peṟṟōr maṇamakaḷukkup paricam (Bride Price) vaḻaṅkuvar. maṇamakaṉiṉ tamakki peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil tāliyik kaṭṭuvāḷ. piṟaku ellōrum viruntuṇpar. ittuṭaṉ tirumaṇam muṭivaṭikiṟatu.

‘vīṭṭu vippu' eṉṟa putumiyāṉa muṟiyoṉṟu ivarkaḷāl kaṭippiṭittu varappaṭukiṟatu. ētēṉum oru kuṭumpattil āṇ cantatiyillāmal oru peṇ maṭṭum iruntāl appeṇṇi yārukkum tirumaṇam ceytu koṭuppatilli. avvāṟu tirumaṇam ceytu koṭuttāl attōṭu avarkaḷ kuṭi arukiviṭṭatāka eṇṇukiṟārkaḷ. attakiya peṇṇi maṇam pēca muṟi māppiḷḷi (māmaṉ makaṉ) yārum varakkūṭātu. appeṇṇi avaḷuṭiya vīṭṭiṉ vāyililuḷḷa oru kampattiṟkut tirumaṇam ceytu vippārkaḷ. avaḷ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvataṟkup patilāka, avaḷuṭiya valatu kiyil veḷḷiyiṉāṟ ceyta vaḷiyal oṉṟi aṇivippārkaḷ. ataṉ piṟaku appeṇ avaḷ kulattic cārnta enta āṭavaṉuṭaṉum toṭarpu koḷḷalām. avaḷuṭiya varumāṉam peṟṟōriyē cērum. avaḷukku āṇ kuḻanti piṟantāl, akkuṭumpattiṟkuriya cottu muḻuvatum atiyē cārum, mēṟku pellāriyilum, tarvār, micūr ākiya nāṭukaḷukku aṇmiyilum vāḻum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ kaṭippiṭikkum 'pacavimuṟi'yōṭu iv vīṭṭu vippu muṟi ottuḷḷatu.

maṇavilakku muṟiyum ivarkaḷiṭiyē uṇṭu. paricat tokiyit tiruppik koṭuttuviṭṭāl uṭaṉē maṇavilakkup peṟalām. āṉāl kuḻantikaḷ ellām kaṇavaṉiyē cārum. kimpeṇkaḷum, maṇavilakkup peṟṟavarkaḷum mika virivil maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar, ākiyiṉāl maṟṟa iṉamakkaḷ ippaḻakkatti iḻittup pēcukiṉṟaṉar.

puliyar :

paḻaṉimaliyil mutaṉ mutal kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ puliyarkaḷē. kuṉṉuvar immaliyil kuṭiyēṟuvataṟku muṉ ivarkaḷ iṅku urimi vāḻvu vāḻntu vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ iṉattāl tamiḻarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ kaṭippiṭittu oḻukum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, camaveḷi yil vāḻum makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷōṭu peritum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷ pēcum moḻiyum tamiḻē. ikkulattāriṉ talivaṉ nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. avaṉukkut tuṇiyākac cērvikkāraṉ, tōṭṭi eṉṟa iruvar uḷḷaṉar. tōṭṭi nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉiṉ paṇiyāḷ. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻum nāṭṭāṇmikkārarip pōlavē, ikkulattāriṉ nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉum celvākku mikkavaṉāka irukkiṟāṉ. puliyarkaḷ mūṉṟu piriviṉarākap pirintu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru piriviṟkum ‘kūṭṭam' eṉṟu peyar. kōlaṉkuppaṉ kūṭṭam, pīccik kūṭṭam, maṇṭiyāṉ kūṭṭam eṉpaviyē avi. mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu pērkaḷum avarkaḷ kulattiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ.

oru peṇ paruvamaṭinta piṟakē, tirumaṇam ceykiṉṟaṉar. tirumaṇam peṟṟōrkaḷālēyē ēṟpāṭu ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. tirumaṇac caṭaṅku mikavum eḷiya muṟiyilēyē naṭipeṟukiṟatu. maṇappeṇṇukku rū. 25 paricappaṇamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. veḷḷi maṇikaḷik kōttup peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil, tāliyāka aṇivikkiṉṟaṉar. paricappaṇattiṟku īṭāṉa tokiyi oṟuppuk kaṭṭaṇa (fine) mākac celuttiviṭṭu āṇ, peṇ ākiya iruvaril yār vēṇṭumāṉalum maṇavilakkup peṟalām. maṇavilakkup peṟṟavarkaḷum, kimpeṇkaḷum tām virumpum yāriyum tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷalām. puliyarkaḷ māyāṇṭi, karu maliyāṉ, pūvāṭi (peṇ teyvam) eṉṟa teyvaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru ciṟṟūrukkum arukil amintirukkum tiṭaliṉmēl māyāṇṭiyiṉ kōvil irukkum. ikkaṭavuḷarkaḷukkup puliyarkaḷ cittirit tiṅkaḷil viḻā eṭukkiṉṟaṉar. ivviḻāvil paṉṉiraṇṭu āṭavarkaḷ cērntu āṭum āṭṭam mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ivvāṭṭattil paṅku koḷḷum paṉṉiraṇṭu āṭavarum taṅkaḷit tūymiyōṭu vittuk koḷvataṟkāka, māṭṭiṟicciyi uṇṇāmal vilakkukiṉṟaṉar. viḻāveṭutta mutal nāḷil māyāṇṭikku ōr āṭu paliyiṭukiṉṟaṉar. iraṇṭām nāḷ oru pāṉiyil rākik kaḷi camittu, karumaliyāṉ kōvilil vittu atic cuṟṟi āṭuvārkaḷ. āṭal muṭintatum ellōrukkum kaḷi vaḻaṅkappaṭum. mūṉṟām nāḷ pūvāṭi kōvilil paṭiyal tuvaṅki eṭṭu nāḷ toṭarntu naṭipeṟum. viḻā muṭivuṟumpōtu, āṭal ciṟappāka naṭipeṟum. puliyarkaḷ āṭalil peruviruppam koṇṭavarkaḷ. paṅkuṉit tiṅkaḷil āṭavarum peṇṭirum tiraḷākak kūṭi, koṭṭum paṟiyōcikkēṟpac cuṟṟiyāṭum āṭṭam kāṇṭaṟkuriyatu. puliyarkaḷ māṭṭiṟicciyiyum, paṉṟiyiṟicciyiyum virumpiyuṇkiṉṟaṉar. eliyik kūṭa uṇṇum paḻakkam avarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu. 'yārēṉum oruvaṉ periyammiyāl pīṭikkappaṭṭāl, uṟṟārum uṟaviṉarum avaṉi appaṭiyē viṭṭuviṭṭu ōṭiviṭuvārkaḷ. āṭavarum peṇṭirum uṭal toṭarpu koḷḷamāṭṭārkaḷ' eṉṟu ṭarṉpul eṉṟa veḷḷiyar tam nūlil kuṟippiṭukiṟār. kalrāyaṉ malikaḷil vāḻum maliyāḷikaḷiṭamum ippaḻakkam uṇṭu

kiruttava camayattip parappum nōkkattōṭu ki, pi, 1850-ām āṇṭil oru pātiri aṉuppappaṭṭār. āṉāl appaṇi carivara naṭipeṟavilli. iṭiyilē taṭippaṭṭatu. mīṇṭum amerikkat tiruccapiyic cārnta kiruttavap pātirimār iṅku camayattip parappap perum muyaṟci eṭuttu varukiṉṟaṉar. ki. pi. 1850-ām āṇṭu camayap piracārattiṟkākap puliyarkaḷiṭiyē ceṉṟa pātiriyār puliyarkaḷip paṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷit tam kaṭitaṅkaḷil eḻutiyuḷḷār, Madras Quarterly Missionary Journal for 1850-55-eṉṟa nūlil akkaṭitaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkaṭi taṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum ceytikaḷ piṉ varumāṟu :

'vēṭṭiyāṭuvataṟkākap puliyarkaḷ cila camayaṅkaḷil tiraḷākak kūṭuvatuṇṭu. vēṭṭiyil kiṭitta mutal vilaṅkiṉ tōliyō allatu vēṟu cila uṟuppukkaḷiyō arukiluḷḷa kōvilukkuk kāṇikkiyāka aṉuppuvar. avvāṟu ceyvataṉ mūlam, avarkaḷuṭiya kaṭavuḷ maṉa niṟivu koṇṭu avarkaḷukku vēṭṭiyil atikamāṉa vilaṅkukaḷ kiṭippataṟku aruḷpurivārām. vēṭṭiyiṉ pōtu yārēṉum iṟantupaṭṭāl, mikavum mariyātiyōṭu avaṉ uṭalik kāṭṭil aṭakkam ceyvārkaḷ. avvāṟu iyantapaṭṭavaṉ. puliyarkaḷiṉ mariyātikkuriyavaṉākavum, vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriyavaṉākavum karutappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. puliyarkaḷ, avarkaḷuṭiya āṇṭi (Masters) kaḷāṉa kuṉṉuvarkaḷāl mikavum aṭimittaṉattil āḻttappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. kuṉṉuvar puliyarkaḷukkuk kaṭum vaṭṭikkup paṇam koṭuttu, avarkaḷāl koṭukka iyalātapōtu, taṅkaḷ aṭimikaḷāka ākkik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. kuṉṉuvarkaḷ avarkaḷi iravil viḷakku vittuk koḷvataṟkum, kaṭṭilil paṭuttuṟaṅkuvataṟkumkūṭa aṉumatippatilli. nōy noṭikaḷit tīrppataṟkuk kuṉṉuvarkaḷ puliyarkaḷiyē peritum nāṭavēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl kāṭṭiluḷḷa mūlikikaḷiṉ taṉmikaḷi naṉkuṇarntavarkaḷ puliyarkaḷē, pēy piṭittavarkaḷik kuṇappaṭuttum tiṟamiyum avarkaḷiṭamē uḷḷatu. ēṉeṉṟāl ammali mītu vāḻum pēy picācukaḷi vacappaṭuttum āṟṟali avarkaḷ peṟṟiruppatākak kuṉṉuvarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. pulikaḷi nañcūṭṭik kollum kaliyip puliyarkaḷ naṉku aṟivārkaḷ. yārēṉum oru puliyaṉ oru puliyi nañcūṭṭik koṉṟa ceytiyi, maṟṟa puliyarkaḷ aṟintavuṭaṉ ellōriṭamum paṇam vacūlittu avaṉukkup puttāṭi vāṅki vaḻaṅkuvar. avaṉi oru nāṟkāliyil amartti ūrvalamākak koṇṭu varuvar. ūrvalattiṉpōtu avaṉic cuṟṟip palar āṭikkoṇṭu varuvar.

paḷiyar :

paḷiyarkaḷ, paḻaṉimaliyil vāḻvōril mikavum piṟpaṭṭa iṉattār. mēl paḻaṉimaliyilum, varucanāṭṭup paḷḷattākkilum uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil, ciṟu ciṟu kūṭṭaṅkaḷākac citaṟi vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mañcampaṭṭikku arukiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl ivarkaḷuṭiya uccarippu mikavum vēṟupaṭṭum, purintukoḷḷa muṭiyāmalum amintuḷḷatu. puliyarkaḷiviṭa nākarikattil ivarkaḷ mikavum kēvalamāṉa niliyil uḷḷaṉar. āṉāl ivarkaḷ māṭṭiṟicciyi uṇpatilli. perumpālum ivarkaḷ marattiṉ mēl amitta paraṇkaḷilum, kukikaḷilum pāṟi iṭukkukaḷilum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. cila camayaṅkaḷil puṟkaḷāl amikkappaṭṭa kuṭicikaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. uṭali maṟikkap pōtiya aḷavaṟṟa āṭiyiyē aṇikiṉṟaṉar. avvāṭiyōṭu taḻikaḷiyum, puṟkaḷiyum cērttu iṭuppil cuṟṟik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ āṭi mikavum aḻukkēṟit tūymiyaṟṟirukkum. ilikaḷiyum, kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum (perumpālum maravaḷḷik kiḻaṅku) tēṉiyumē mukkiya uṇavākak koḷkiṉṟaṉar. kiḻaṅkukaḷi oru kuḻiyiṉuḷ pōṭṭu, ataṉmēl cuḷḷikaḷip parappit tīyiṭṭuc cuṭṭut tiṉkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ iruppiṭattiṟku muṉṉāl eppoḻutum tī erintukoṇṭē irukkum. kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷiṭamiruntu taṅkaḷit taṟkāttuk koḷḷavē avvāṟu ceykiṉṟaṉar.



kāṭṭil erintukoṇṭirukkum tīyiṉ mūlamākavē, paḷiyarkaḷ avviṭattil irukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟintukoḷḷa muṭiyum. ēṉeṉṟāl paḷiyarkaḷ mikavum añcum iyalpiṉar. piṟa iṉa makkaḷik kāṇa avarkaḷ virumpuvatilli. avvāṟu kaṇṭālum ōṭi oḷintu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. cakkikmukkik kalliṉ utaviyiṉālēyē ivarkaḷ neruppu uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkut tuṇip poruḷākak kāṭṭumaraṅkaḷil kiṭikkum pañcu pōṉṟa poruḷip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. tirumaṇaṅkaḷ evvitac caṭaṅkumiṉṟi naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. 'tirumaṇam ōr icinta ēṟpāṭu' (Marriage is an adjustment) eṉṟu oru mēli nāṭṭu aṟiñaṉ kūṟiya kūṟṟu ivarkaḷ vāḻkkikku mikavum poruttamē, ivarkaḷ vāḻkkiyil evvita iṭarppāṭum kiṭiyātu. kaṇavaṉ uṇavup poruḷit tēṭik koṇara vēṇṭum. maṉivi atic camittuk koṭukka vēṇṭum. ivarkaḷuṭiya vāḻkkip poṟuppukkaḷ ivaṟṟōṭu muṭivuṟukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷil yārēṉum oruvar iṟantuviṭṭāl, appiṇatti irunta iṭattilēyē viṭṭuviṭṭu ellōrum vēṟu iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟu viṭuvārkaḷ. avviṭattiṟkuc cila tiṅkaḷ variyil yārum cellamāṭṭārkaḷ. tiruvāḷar tarsṭaṉ eṉṟa oru veḷḷiyar, tirunelvēlik kāṭukaḷil vāḻum paḷiyarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyip paṟṟi virivāṉa kuṟippukaḷip paṭaṅkaḷōṭu oru nūlāka veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṟār. tirunelvēlip paḷiyarkaḷ iṟantavaṉip putittu, apputikuḻiyiṉmēl oru kalli nāṭṭiviṭṭuc ceṉṟu viṭuvar. piṟaku avviṭatti akkuṭumpattār eppōtum kāṇa virumpuvatilli, mañcampaṭṭikku arukiluḷḷa malip pakutikaḷil ivarkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar,



kōṭikkāṉal :

kōṭikkāṉal vaṭṭam (taluk) maturi māvaṭṭattic cārntatākum. āṅkilēyarkaḷ kīḻp paḻaṉi mali, mēṟpaḻaṉi mali ākiya iru pakutikaḷiyum cērttu ki. pi. 1889 ām āṇṭu ivvaṭṭatti amittaṉar. mēṟpaḻaṉimaliyiṉ mēl amintirukkum kōṭikkāṉal nakarattiṉ peyarāl ivvaṭṭamum aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu.

peyark kāraṇam :

kōṭikkāṉal eṉṟa peyar araciyalāriṉ kuṟippukkaḷil ki. pi. 1860-ām āṇṭiṟku mēl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ippeyar innakarattiṟku evvāṟu ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟa muṭiyātu. 'kāṉal' eṉṟa collukkuk kāṭu allatu cōli eṉṟu poruḷ. itil evvita aiyappāṭum illi. āṉāl ippeyaril amintirukkum nili moḻi (muṉ col) yāṉa 'kōṭi' etik kuṟikciṟatu eṉpatē ārāyccikkuriyatāka irukkiṟatu. innakaram amintuḷḷa kāṭu, paḻaṉimalikaḷiṉ kaṭiciyil uḷḷa toṭariṉmītu iruppatāl itu "kōṭikkāṉal' eṉṟu mutalil aḻikkappaṭṭup piṟaku kōṭikkāṉal āyiṟṟu eṉpar. vēṟu cilar 'koṭikkāṉal' eṉṟa peyarē kōṭikkāṉal āyiṟṟu eṉpar. innakaric cūḻntirukkum kāṭṭil, piṉṉip paṭarntirukkum eṇṇaṟṟa koṭikaḷum, liyāṉā (Liana) mutaliya kāṭṭumaraṅkaḷiṉ toṅkum vērkaḷum, ippeyari uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. kōṭik kālattilum - pacumiyāṉa iḷamarakkāṭukaḷip peṟṟiruppatāl, itu kōṭikkāṉal eṉap peyar peṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvārumuṇṭu. caṅka ilakkiyattil kūṭap pacumarak kāṭṭik kuṟippiṭak 'kōṭikkāṉal' eṉṟa toṭar āḷappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. 'koṭikkāṉalē' kōṭikkāṉal ākiyirukkum eṉṟu cilar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl itu yārukku yārāl koṭip poruḷāka aḷikkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu viḷaṅkavilli. kōṭikkāṉal perumaḻip piratēcattil amintuḷḷatu. āṇṭukku 91 aṅkula maḻiyip peṟukiṟatu. iṅku vāḻum makkaḷ eppōtum kuṭiyum kiyumākat tirivatāl 'kuṭikkāṉal' eṉṟa peyarē kōṭik kāṉalāka māṟiyirukkum eṉṟum kūṟalām.

vaḷarcciyum varalāṟum :

kōṭikkāṉal nakar periyakuḷattiṟku nēr ucciyil kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu 7000 aṭi uyarattilamintuḷḷatu. ceṅkuttāka uyarntu cellum teṉpakka ucciyiṉ ōrattil innakaram uḷḷatu. mali ucciyil tāḻvāṉa iṭattic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa carivukaḷil innakaram kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ nīḷam 2 kal; akalam 1 kal. ivvucciyiliruntu kāṇpōrukkuc camaveḷiyiṉ tōṟṟam teḷivākat tōṉṟum. innakariṉ vaṭakkil maliyāṉatu uyarntum, ceṅkuttākavum cellukiṟatu. mēṟkil uyarnta malittoṭar taṭuttu niṟkiṉṟatu. kiḻakkup pakkattil maliyāṉatu ciṟitu ciṟitākat tāḻntu kīḻppaḻaṉi malikaḷi nōkkic cellukiṟatu. appakkattil tāṉ maṟṟa ellāc cikaraṅkaḷiyumviṭa uyarntu aḻakiya tōṟṟattōṭu perumāḷ mali niṟkiṟatu. itaṉ uyaram 7326 aṭi. kōṭikkāṉal amintuḷḷa carivukaḷil teṉpakkattuc carivu, pacu maraṅkaḷit taṉṉakattil niṟiyak koṇṭirukkiṟatu. tolivil iruntu kāṇpōrukkut toṅkum tōṭṭamāka {hanging wood) atu teṉpaṭum. ikkāṭē kōṭikkāṉal eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭu, nakarukkum āki vantatu. nakarukku naṭuvil uḷḷa tāḻviṭam mutalil catuppu nilamāka iruntatu. oru ciṟṟōṭi ataṉ naṭuvil ūrntu ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntatu. maturi māvaṭṭat taṇṭalarāka irunta tiruvāḷar liveñ (Mr. Levenge) eṉpār, ki. pi. 1863 ām āṇṭu tiṭṭamiṭṭu, araciyalāriṉ naṉkoṭiyiyum, tam kippaṇattiyum celaviṭṭu acciṟṟōṭiyic cuṟṟik kari amittu, ēriyāka māṟṟiṉār. aḻakiya avvēriyic cuṟṟip pacumaraṅkaḷ aṭarnta vaḻikaḷ pala amintuḷḷaṉa. avvaḻikaḷilēyē eḻilpeṟu maṉikaḷ pala eḻuppappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvēri uruvattāl oru naṭcattira mīṉi (Star fish) ottu irukkiṟatu. itaṉ kuṟukkaḷavu ½ kal irukkum. āṉāl itaṉ cuṟṟaḷavu 3 kal nīḷam irukkum. ērikkariyic cuṟṟi oṭṭiṉāṟpōl oru pāti cellukiṟatu. appātikkuc caṟṟu mēlē oru pātiyum, ataṟku mēl maṟṟoru pātiyum carivukaḷil amintuḷḷaṉa. avikaḷ muṟiyē 'naṭu ērip pāti' (Middle Lake Road) eṉṟum, 'mēl ērip pāti' (Upper Lake Road) eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. immūṉṟu peruñcālikaḷum, iṭiyiṭiyē pala ciṟu pātikaḷāl iṇikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

nakarukku veḷiyil aintu mukkiyap pātikaḷ cellukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ mēṟkākac cellum pāti tūṇ pāṟi (Pillar Rocks) kaḷi nōkkic cellukiṟatu. mēṟkup pakkattil cellum pāti, 12 kal tolivil amintirukkum 'pūmpāṟi' eṉṉum ciṟṟūri aṭikiṟatu. avviṭattil ciṟanta vāṉāyvuk kūṭam oṉṟu uḷḷatu. vaṭakkup pakkattil cellum naṭipāti tirunelvēlik kuṭiyēṟṟa (Tinnevelli settlement)ttiṉ vaḻiyāka vilpaṭṭiyi aṭikiṟatu. nīrvīḻccikku aṇmiyil ceṅkuttāṉa carivukaḷukkiṭiyil ivvaḻakiya ciṟṟūr amintuḷḷatu. kiḻakkup pakkattil cellum 'lāskāṭ' eṉṉum vaḻiyāṉatu mēṟpaḻaṉi maliyiyum kīḻppaḻaṉi maliyiyum pirikkum iyaṟki elliyāṉa niyūṭral cēṭal (Neutral Saddle) eṉṟa iṭattiyaṭikiṟatu. ivviṭam perumāḷ cikarattiṉ aṭivārattiluḷḷatu. teṟkil cellum pāti kutirip pātiyākum. itu ceṅkuttāka amintuḷḷatu. 12 kal nīḷamuḷḷa ippāti, ceṇpakaṉūr aṭivārattiluḷḷa kiṣṭṉamā nāyakkar tōppu (Kistnama Nayak's Tope) vaḻiyākac camaveḷiyi aṭikiṟatu. kiṣṭṉamā nāyakkar tōppu, (potuvākat tōppu eṉṟē aḻikkap peṟum) maturiyil nāyakka maṉṉariṭam amiccarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya oruvaruṭiya uṟaviṉar peyarāl amikkappaṭṭatu. maturiyil nāyakkar āṭci aḻivuṟṟatum, apparampariyiṉar periya kuḷattiṟku ōṭivantu taṅkiṉar. avarkaḷ parampariyiṉarē toṭarntu ki. pi. 1870-ām āṇṭuvari, vaṭakari eṉṉum ūrukkuk kirāmat talivarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟi vantaṉar.

it tōppiliruntu periyakuḷam 5 kal tolivil amintuḷḷatu. periya kuḷattiliruntu ammiya nāyakkaṉūr puki vaṇṭi niliyam 28 kal toliviluḷḷatu. paṇṭikkālattil kōṭik kāṉali aṭiya virumpuvōr ammiya nāyakkaṉūriliruntu tōppiṟkuc cellum 33 kal tolivi māṭṭu vaṇṭiyilēyē kaṭantu cella vēṇṭi yiruntatu. tōppiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuk kutiriyil ēṟiyō, naṭantō, ceṇpakaṉūr vaḻiyāka maliyucciyi yaṭiya vēṇṭum. naṭantu cella muṭiyātavarkaḷi, paḻaṉimalip puliyarkaḷ tiṟanta pallakkup (Canvas Chir) pōṉṟa irukkiyil amarttit tūkkic celvar. cellumpōtu avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ moḻiyil urakkap pāṭikkoṇṭē. vaḻi naṭip payaṇatti maṟantu celvar.



utakamaṇṭalattiliruppatip pōṉṟē kōṭik kāṉalilum taṭpa veppa nili uḷḷattik kavarum taṉmiyatāy amintuḷḷatu. āṉāl utakiyil peyvativiṭa iṅku maḻi atikam. āṇṭukku ēṟakkuṟiya 100 aṅkulam maḻi poḻikiṟatu. utaki teṉmēṟkup paruvak kāṟṟiṉāl jūṉ, jūli, ākasṭut tiṅkaḷkaḷil maḻiyip peṟukiṟatu. āṉāl kōṭikkāṉal vaṭa kiḻakkup paruvak kāṟṟiṉāl maḻiyip peṟukiṟatu. mē, jūṉ tiṅkaḷkaḷil pārviyāḷarkaḷ (Visitors) iṅku niṟiyak kūṭukiṉṟaṉar. appōtu maḻi peytu avarkaḷukkut tolli koṭuppatilli. āṉāl atē camayattil utaki ceṉṟāl nāḷ tōṟum maḻiyiṉāl avatippaṭa nēriṭum. kacivum, veppa nili vīḻcciyum utakiyiliruppatip pōl avvaḷavu atikamāka iṅku iruppatilli. kuḷir kālattilkūṭa iṅkuk kuḷiriṉ kaṭumi mikavum atikamilli eṉṟē collalām. iṅkuḷḷa nilam maṇalum kallum kalantatāka irukkiṟatu. ataṉāl iṅkuḷḷa pātikaḷum, maṭṭip pantāṭṭa mitāṉaṅkaḷum, maḻi peyta ciṟitu nērattil ularntu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. kōṭikkāṉalukku mēṟkil aṭuttaṭuttu amintuḷḷa ceṅkuttāṉa malit toṭarkaḷ, avikaṭkappāluḷḷa aṭarnta kāṭukaḷi maṟittu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ik kāṭukaḷukkuḷ eḷitāka yārum cella muṭiyātu. ik kāṭukaḷilum, kōṭikkāṉaluk karukiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷilum varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa kaṟ karuvikaḷum, camātikaḷum kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. perumāḷ cikarattiṟkut teṉmēṟkup pakutiyiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷilum (vilpaṭṭi cellum pātiyil) pālamaliyilum ivikaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kīḻp paḻaṉi maliyil maccūr, paṇṇikkāṭu, tāṇṭikkuṭi, kāmaṉūr, pāccalūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum ivikaḷ niṟiyat teṉpaṭuvatāka ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ic ciṉṉaṅkaḷip pōl intiya nāṭṭiṉ vēṟu ep pakutiyilum kiṭikkavilli. ic camātikaḷ perumpālum 8 aṭi nīḷamum 3 aṭi akalamum uṭiyaṉavāy coracorappāṉa kaṟkaḷāl peṭṭi pōl ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. oru pakkam tiṟantirukkiṟatu. turup piṭitta arivāḷ oṉṟum, cenniṟa, karuniṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kōṭikkāṉalic cuṟṟik kāṇat takunta pala iṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. nakaric cuṟṟi amintuḷḷa mūṉṟu nīr vīḻccikaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavi ; kaṇkavarum vaṉappuṭiyavi. ivikaḷi yaṭitaṟku nalla vaḻikaḷum amintuḷḷaṉa. lā malit toṭaril (Law's ghat) veḷḷi vīḻcci (Silver 'cascade) amintuḷḷatu. itu parappāṟu eṉṉum aruviyāl uṇṭākiṟatu. kiḷeṉ vīḻcci (Glan falls) yāṉatu vilpaṭṭikku vaṭakkil cellum ciṟu pātiyarukil amintuḷḷatu. ituvum parappāṟṟiṉ oru piriviṉālēyē uṇṭākkappaṭukiṟatu. kōṭikkāṉaliṉ teṉmēṟkil pāmpāṟṟiṉāl mōkiṉi vīḻcci (Fairy Falls) uṇṭākkappaṭukiṟatu. kōkkars naṭiveḷi (Coaker's walk) yāṉatu, kōṭik kāṉaliṉ elliyil, paḻaṉi maliyiṉ teṉpakkac cariviṉ ucciyil amintuḷḷatu. (leḵpṭiṉeṇṭ kōkkar eṉpavar ōr ciṟanta poṟiyiyal vallunar. ivar ki. pi. 1870 mutal 1872 vari maturi māvaṭṭattil paṇiyāṟṟi vantār. ki. pi. 1870-ām āṇṭu kōṭikkāṉaliṉ paṭatti (Map) varintu veḷi yiṭṭavar ivarē). in naṭi veḷiyiliruntu kāṇ pōrukkuc camaveḷiyil amintuḷḷa ūrkaḷ aḻakōṭu kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. vāṉam nirmalamāka irukkum nāṭkaḷil 47 kal tolivil amintuḷḷa maturi nakaram kūṭat teṉpaṭuvatākak kūṟukiṟārkaḷ, at teṉpakkap pīṭa pūmiyilēyē, kōkkar naṭi veḷiyiliruntu 3 kal ceṉṟāl tūṇ pāṟikaḷik kāṇalām. mūṉṟu uyaramāṉa parutta pāṟikaḷ perum perum tūṇkaḷippōl uyarntu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷiṉ uyaram ēṟakkuṟiya 400 aṭi irukkum. ivaṟṟiṉ naṭuvilum aṭiyilum pala kukikaḷum, piḷavukaḷum amintuḷḷaṉa. ip pāṟikaḷiṉ ucciyiliruntu kāṇpōrukku atikamaliyiṉ aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷum, kampam paḷḷattākkiliruntu uyarntu cellum ceṅkuttuc carivukaḷum, cama veḷiyum naṉku teriyum.

tūṇ pāṟikaḷiliruntu iraṇṭu kal tolivil ṭākṭars ṭiliṭ {Doctor's Delight) eṉṟa uyarnta mēṭu oṉṟu uḷḷatu. itu tūṇ pāṟikaḷiviṭa mikunta pēraḻakōṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. kōṭik kāṉaliliruntu 9½ kal tolivil 'hēmilṭaṉ kōṭṭi ' (Fort Hamilton} uḷḷatu. mējar ṭakḷas hēmilṭaṉ eṉpavariṉ peyarāl itu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. ceṉṉi mānilat talivarāka viḷaṅkiya car cārlas ṭirevelyāṉ eṉpavariṉ āṇiyiṉpaṭi ivar ki. pi. 1859, 1861; 1862 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil paḻaṉi maliyil taṅki, ataṉ nilappaṭatti varintār. appaṭam viḷakkamākavum, peritākavum uḷḷatu. potu nūlakaṅkaḷilum, aluvalakaṅkaḷilum ip paṭam toṅkik koṇṭirukkiṉṟatu. paḻaṉi maliyip paṟṟi avar eḻutiya iraṇṭu kuṟippukaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavi. avi muṟiyē ki. pi. 1862-ām āṇṭilum ki. pi. 1864-ām āṇṭilum ceṉṉiyil acciyaṟṟi veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. hēmilṭaṉ kōṭṭi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭum iṭattil kōṭṭi etuvumilli. orē oru kuṭicitāṉ uḷḷatu. avviṭam mutalil oru periya ēriyāka iruntatu eṉṟa cuviyāṉa uṇmiyit tiruvāḷar hēmilṭaṉ mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭu veḷiyiṭṭār. ivvēriyip paṟṟiya muṉ kuṟippukkaḷō, cevi vaḻic ceytikaḷō kūṭak kiṭiyātu. nīr maṭṭam irunta aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avvaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi vittukkoṇṭē avviṭattil ōr ēri iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avar muṭivu kaṭṭiṉār. avvēri ēṟakkuṟiya 5 kal nīḷamum, 1 kal mutal 2 kal vari akalamum, 30 aṭi mutal 70 aṭi vari āḻamum uṭiyatāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṟār. oru paḷḷattākkiṉ kuṟukkē nīṇṭu paṭintirunta malit toṭar, iyaṟkiyāka ivvēri amivataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntatu. it toṭar vaṭakku nōkki amarāvati āṟuvari cellukiṟatu. iv vēriyil viḻunta ōr aruviyit taṭuttu nīri it toṭar tēkki vantatu. āṉāl, tiṭīreṉṟu aṇipōl irunta at toṭar piḷavupaṭṭa kāraṇattāl ēriyil nīr tēṅkuvatilli. aṇipōl taṭuttirunta at toṭar 200 keja nīḷam uḷḷatu. atil ēṟpaṭṭirukkum piḷavu 100 keja akalamum 90 aṭi āḻamum uḷḷatu. mējar hēmilṭaṉ, 'ivviṭam ōr aḻakiya kuṟiñci nakaramō , paṭiyiṉariṉ pāṭiyō (Contonement) amippataṟku mikavum ēṟṟatu' eṉak kuṟippiṭṭār. āṉāl ivviṭam ceṉṟaṭitaṟkariyatu eṉṟu palar etirpput terivittaṉar. kōṭikkāṉalāṉatu, taṟpōtu amintirukkum iṭattil mutaṉ mutal kaṭṭappaṭṭa pōtu, 'iyaṟki aḻakumikka evvaḷavō iṭaṅkaḷ paḻaṉi mali mītu irukka ivviṭattit tērnteṭuttatu aṟiyāmi' eṉṟu palar kuṟṟam cāṭṭiṉar. avarkaḷukkum itē kāraṇattittāṉ kūṟa vēṇṭum. periya kuḷattiliruntu paḻaṉi maliyiṉ ucciyi aṭiyap puḻakkattilirunta kutirip pātikku aṇmiyilēyē, in nakari amittaṉar. kāraṇam pōkku varavu vacatiyē.

paḻaṉi maliyi mutaṉ mutalil pārviyiṭṭa airōppiyar leḵpṭiṉaṇṭ pi. es. vārṭu eṉpavar. ivar ki. pi. 1821-ām āṇṭu im maliyi aḷappataṟkāka iṅku vantār. periya kuḷattiliruntu cellum pātiyil puṟappaṭṭu, veḷḷakkavi eṉṉum ciṟṟūr vaḻiyāka maliyucciyi aṭintu avvāṇṭu mē tiṅkaḷ 25-ām nāḷ pāmpāṟu vīḻccikkuc caṟṟu mēlē taṅkiyiruntār eṉa avar kuṟippāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ak kuṟippil paḻaṉi maliyi ‘varākakiri' eṉṟum tiruvāṅkūr malikaḷik 'kaṇṇaṉ tēvaṉ malikaḷ' eṉṟum avar kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

- ki. pi. 1834-ām āṇṭu, maturi māvaṭṭat tuṇit taṇṭalarāka irunta je. ci. rāṭṭaṉ eṉpārum, teṉmaṇṭala naṭuvarāka (Judge of the Provincial court, Southern Division) irunta ci, ār. kāṭṭaṉ eṉpārum periya kuḷattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuc ceṇpakaṉūri vantaṭintaṉar. avarkaḷ varavāl cīr kēṭuṟṟirunta akkutirip pāti cīrtiruttam peṟṟatu. ki. pi. 1836-ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar viṭ eṉpavar tēvatāṉap paṭṭiyiliruntu aṭukkam cikarattiṟku aṇmiyiluḷḷa kaṇavāyi nōkkic cellum ceṅkuttāṉa kaṇavāy mūlam maliyucciyi aṭintār. avar ippōtu kōṭikkāṉal amintuḷḷa tāḻnilattiṟkuc cellavilli. avar paḻaṉimaliyil uḷḷa tāvaraṅkaḷip paṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavi.

kōṭikkāṉalil mutaṉ mutalākat taṅkuvataṟku māḷikiyik kaṭṭiyavarkaḷ, maturiyil vāḻnta amerikkap pātirimārkaḷē (American missionaries). nam nāṭṭiṉ taṭpa veppanili avarkaḷukku ottuvarāmiyāl palar nōyvāyppaṭṭaṉar. ākiyiṉāl avarkaḷuṭiya kēntiramāṉa 'jaḵpṉā'viṟku nōyuṟṟavarkaḷik koṇṭu cellavum, nōyiliruntu mīṇṭu kuṇamaṭintavarkaḷi aḻittuvaravum ki. pi. 1838ām āṇṭu oru ciṟu kappalē vilikku vāṅka muṭivu ceytaṉar. maturikku aṇmiyiluḷḷa ciṟu maliyiṉ mītu oru māḷikiyi amittu nōyuṟṟavarkaḷi aṅkuk koṇṭu cella muṭivu ceytatāl, at tiṭṭam kiviṭappaṭṭatu. ciṟu maliyiṉmītu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eḻuppappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl aṅkuc ceṉṟavarkaḷ malik kāyccaliṉāl aṭikkaṭi avatippaṭṭaṉar. ākiyiṉāl avviṭattik kiviṭṭu viṭṭup paḻaṉi maliyiṉ mītu ki. pi. 1845-ām āṇṭu ōriṭattit tērnteṭuttaṉar. kōṭikkāṉal eṉṉum iḷa marakkāṭu - amintirunta cariviṉ aṭivārattil iraṇṭu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eḻuppappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭṭiṭa vēli jūṉ tiṅkaḷil toṭaṅkap peṟṟu akṭōparil muṭivuṟṟatu.

ki. pi. 1834 mutal 1847 vari maturi māvaṭṭat taṇṭalarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya tiruvāḷar jāṉ piḷēkparṉ eṉpavar paḻaṉi maliyiṉ mītu nilavarit tiṭṭatti amulākkap peru muyaṟci eṭuttuk koṇṭār. aṭukkam kaṇavāyiṉ ucciyiliruntu 5 kal tolivil, tām taṅkuvataṟkeṉṟu oru māḷikiyi amittār. āṉāl, mutaṉ mutalāka am māḷikiyil neruppu mūṭṭiyapōtu, atu paṟṟi erintu pāḻākiyatu. ataṉuṭiya aṭippaṭic (asti vāram) cuvari iṉṟum kāṇalām. ki.pi. 1848-49-il maturi māvaṭṭat tuṇit taṇṭalarāka irunta tiruvāḷar tāmas kiḷār (ivar 1853-ām āṇṭu mēt tiṅkaḷil, paḻaṉi maliyip paṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭa nūl kuṟippiṭat takkatu) eṉpārum, māvaṭṭa naṭuvarāka irunta tiruvāḷar ci. ār. pēṉs eṉpārum, taṇṭalarāka irunta tiruvāḷar ār. ṭi. pārkkar eṉpārum, kōṭikkāṉaliṉ ucciyil, camaveḷikaḷip pārttāṟ pōṉṟu kōṭik kālak kuḷirmaṉikaḷi eḻuppiṉar. ippōtu uḷḷa ‘pāmpāṟu maṉi' (Pambar House) kkum, 'rōcaṉīt' (Roseneath) tiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa iṭattilēyē avikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. aluvalakak kuṟippukaḷil kāṇappaṭum maṉi amippu (House plans) kaḷi vittukkoṇṭu pārttāl, tiruvāḷar pārkkar amitta maṉi, ippoḻutu pāmpāṟu maṉi amintirukkum iṭattilēyē iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. tiruvāḷar pēṉs kaṭṭiya maṉi pāmpāṟu maṉikku aṭuttāṟ pōl, kiḻakkil amintuḷḷatu. ippōtu atu rōmaṉ kattōlikka camayattārukku urimiyuṭiyatāka uḷḷatu. tiruvāḷar kiḷārk kaṭṭiya maṉi ippōtuḷḷa 'rōcaṉīt 'tiṉ oru pakutiyākum.

•rōcaṉīt' eṉṟa ik kaṭṭiṭam, maṟṟavikaḷi viṭa varalāṟṟuc ciṟappup peṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi ārāyntu, oppuyarvaṟṟa oppilakkaṇatti (Comparative grammar) eḻutiya kālṭuvel pātiriyār im maṉiyil tāṉ vāḻntār. avar eḻutiya oppilakkaṇam tirāviṭa moḻiyārāyccit tuṟikku vaḻi vakuttatōṭallāmal, tamiḻ moḻi āriyattiṉiṉṟum muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭa uyartaṉic cemmoḻi eṉṟa uṇmiyiyum ulakiṟku uṇarttiyatu. kālṭuvel pātiriyār tam iṟuti nāṭkaḷi im maṉiyilēyē kaḻittu iyaṟki eytiṉār. kēpṭaṉ ṭapiḷyū. ec. ārssi eṉṟa oru poṟiyiyal vallunar, pēṉs, kiḷārk ākiya iruvariṉ maṉikaḷukkiṭiyē oru kaṭṭiṭatti eḻuppiṉār. aṭuttāṟpōl amerikkaṉ miṣaṉic cērnta pātirimār taṅkaḷukkeṉa oru maṉiyi amittaṉar. atu ippoḻutu 'kiḷavarak' eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. pampāyp paṭṭāḷattic cērnta mējar jē. em. pārṭriṭj eṉpavar ki. pi. 1852ām āṇṭu iṅku vantu kūṭāramaṭittu ērikkarukil taṅkiṉār. kuḷiriṉ koṭumiyiyum, puyaliṉ vēkattiyum tāḷa muṭiyāta avar oru ciṟu maṉiyi amittut taṅkiṉār. avviṭam ippōtu 'pampāy ṣōlā' (Bombay Shola) eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. avarukku urimiyāṉa tōṭṭam oṉṟum aṅku iruntatu. mutaṉ mutalākap paḻaṉi maliyil nīlap piciṉ maram eṉṟa āstirēliya mara vakiyi naṭṭup payiriṭṭavar ivarē. ivar vīṭṭiṉ muṉṉāl irunta iraṇṭu nīlap piciṉ maraṅkaḷil oṉṟu iṉṟum uḷḷatu. ap pakutiyil mikavum periya maram atutāṉ. kōṭikkāṉal vācikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil mutaṉ mutalāka icik kaliñarkaḷik kuṭiyēṟṟa muyaṉṟavarum ivarē. ivar vīṭṭiṟkarukil oru kaṭiyum iruntatākak kuṟippukaḷiliruntu aṟiya muṭikiṟatu.

mēṟkūṟiya eḻuvarumē kōṭikkāṉaliṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ (Pioneers) eṉṟu kūṟalām. ki. pi. 1853-il ivarkaḷukkurimiyāṉa ēḻumaṉikaḷē kōṭikkāṉaliliruntaṉa. ki. pi. 1861-ām āṇṭu, mēlum mūṉṟu vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. ki. pi. 1854. ām āṇṭu uccikkuc cellum kutirip pātiyic ceppaṉiṭa rū 4,500 celaviṭappaṭṭatu. kōṭik kāṉalil vāḻnta kiruttavap pātirikaḷ oru kal nīḷamuḷḷa pātiyoṉṟi nakaril amittaṉar. mēlum 6 kal nīḷamuḷḷa pāti aṅku vāḻvōrāl amikkappaṭṭatu. 1860 ām āṇṭu ceṉṉi mānila āḷunarāka irunta car cārlas ṭirivelyāṉ eṉpavar tōppiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuk kutirip pātiyiṉ vaḻiyākap paḻaṉimaliyucciyi aṭintu, kōṭikkāṉalip pārvi yiṭṭār. (ivar varukiyiliruntē innakar, araciyaṟ kuṟippēṭukaḷil kōṭikkāṉal eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu) paḻaṉi maliyippaṟṟi avar koṇṭa karuttukkaḷi aḻakiya oru kuṟippu nūlāka eḻutiyuḷḷār. tiruvāḷar kiḷārkkiṟku urimiyāka irunta rōcaṉīt, maṉiyilēyē ivar taṅki yiruntār. ki. pi. 1871-ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar nēppiyar pirapu iṅku vantiruntār. ippoḻutu kōṭik kāṉalil 'nēppiyar villā' eṉṟa oru maṉi uḷḷatu. nēppiyar pirapu avviṭattil ciṟitu nēram niṉṟu ceṉṟatāl, ammaṉi appeyar peṟṟatākak kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.

ki. pi. 1860-ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar verheṉṟiliveñ maturi māvaṭṭat taṇṭalarāka amarttappaṭṭār. 1867 vari appaṇiyil iruntu viṭṭu, ōyvu peṟṟavarākak kōṭikkāṉalil kuṭippukuntār. ki. pi. 1885-ām āṇṭu ceṉṉiyil uyir nīttār. iṟappataṟkuc cilavāraṅkaḷ muṉ variyil kōṭikkāṉaliluḷḷa 'pāmpāṟu maṉi' yilēyē vāḻntu vantār. kōṭikkāṉalil avar vāḻat toṭaṅkiyatum, āṅkila araciyalārāl pirapu nili (Baronetcy) kku uyarttappaṭṭār. avar māvaṭṭat taṇṭalarāka irunta pōtum, ōyvupeṟṟu iṅkut taṅkiya pōtum, kōṭikkāṉaliṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkup peritum uḻittār. kōkkar naṭi veḷikkuc caṟṟumēl avaruṭiya camāti amintuḷḷatu. atil nāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa ciluviyil 'kōṭikkāṉaliṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṉ perum pakuti ivariyē cārum' eṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. mutalilēyē kuṟippiṭṭatu pōl, kōṭikkāṉal ēri ivaratu muḻu muyaṟciyālum, kipporuḷālum amikkappaṭṭatu. periya kuḷattiliruntu varum kutirippātiyi, pīṭapūmiyiṉ teṟku elliyiluḷḷa pāmpaṭi ṣōlā (Pambadi Shola) vari amittār. nakariṉuḷḷum pala pātikaḷi amittār. airōppiyap paḻavakikaḷiyum pūvakikaḷiyum paḻaṉimaliyiṉ mītu payiriṭuvataṟkup perumuyaṟci eṭuttukkoṇṭavarum ivarē.

ki. pi. 1853-ām āṇṭu amerikkaṉ miṣaṉic cērntavarkaḷ 'caṉṉiciṭ' (Sunny Side) eṉṟa iṭattiṟkarukil taṅkaḷukkurimiyāṉa nilattil oru kōvil kaṭṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. atu 1856-ām āṇṭu muṭivuṟṟatu. āṅkilikaṉ miṣaṉic cārntōrum akkōvililēyē vaḻipāṭu nikaḻttalām eṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. akkōvilic cuṟṟi ōr iṭukāṭu amikkappaṭṭatu. (ippōtu atu mūṭappaṭṭu viṭṭatu) mutaṉ mutalāka avviṭukāṭṭil amikkappaṭṭa camāti iru kuḻantikaḷiṉuṭiyatu. akkuḻantikaḷ ki. pi. 1819-ām āṇṭu iṟantaṉa; nakarukkuc ciṟitu toliviluḷḷa nēpōmali {Mount Nebo) yil putikkappaṭṭaṉa. avikaḷ mīṇṭum tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭu, iṅkuk koṇṭu vantu putikkappaṭṭaṉa. ki. pi. 1896-ām āṇṭu ik kōvil kōṭikkāṉal kaḻaka (club) ttiṟku aṇmiyil putitākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṭattiṟku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. piṟaku paḻiya kōvil aḻivuṟṟatu. ki. pi. 1863-ām āṇṭu ceyiṇṭ cir, kōṭikkāṉalukku vantār. kattōlikkaril mutaṉ mutalāka vanta pātiriyār ivarē. ivar tiruvāḷar pēṉsiṉ maṉiyi vilikku vāṅkiṉār ; ippōtu kōṭikkāṉalilirukkum rōmaṉ kattōlikkak kōvilukkuk kālkōḷiṭṭār. āṅkilikaṉ tiruccapik kōvili amippataṟkāka nēpō maliyil kālṭuvel pātiriyāriṉ peyarukkuk ki. pi. 1883ām āṇṭu araciyalārāl nilam vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. atē āṇṭil kōṭikkāṉal vaṭṭat taliva (Tahsildar) riṉ aluvalakam amippataṟku araciyalāriṭamiruntu aṉumatiyum kiṭittatu. ceṇpakaṉūriliruntu cellum malivaḻip pātik karukil airōppiyarukkuriya iṭukāṭu oṉṟu amintuḷḷatu. kōṭikkāṉalukkuḷ nuḻintatum itu nam kaṇkaḷil paṭum. itu ki. pi. 1900-ām āṇṭu kaṭṭi muṭikkappaṭṭatu. ovvoru camayattārkkum taṉit taṉip pirivu itil uṇṭu.

kōṭikkāṉal, muṉṉāḷil vilpaṭṭiyiṉ oru pakutiyāka amintiruntatu. ki. pi. 1899-ām āṇṭu akṭōpar tiṅkaḷil iṅku nakarāṭci maṉṟam (Municipality) niṟuvappaṭṭatu. ceṉṉi mānilattilēyē mikavum ciṟiya nakarāṭci maṉṟam itutāṉ. mikak kuṟinta eṇṇikkiyuḷḷa makkaḷē iṅku vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. mutaṉ mutalāka innakarāṭci maṉṟattil 12 uṟuppiṉarkaḷ iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ yārum tērnteṭukkap paṭṭavarkaḷallar. muṉpellām, innakaril vāḻvōrukkut tēviyāṉa kuṭinīr, kiṇaṟukaḷiliruntum, aruvikaḷiliruntum peṟappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1902-ām āṇṭu kuṭinīr vacatikkāṉa tiṭṭam oṉṟu vakukkappaṭṭatu. attiṭṭattiṉpaṭi pāmpāṟṟil, mōkiṉi vīḻccikkumēl 370 keja tūrattil ōr aṇikaṭṭi nīrit tēkki, kuḻāykaḷiṉ mūlam an nīri nakariṉ ellāp pakutikaḷukkum vaḻaṅkuvateṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. ittiṭṭattiṟkāka rū 43,000 celaviṭuvateṉṟum muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. piṟaku, in nīrttēkkam cuṇṇāmpuk kalaviyāl uṟutiyākak kaṭṭappaṭa vēṇṭu meṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatāl ittiṭṭattiṉ celavu rū 62,250 kku uyarttap paṭṭatu. nakarāṭci maṉṟam pūrāc celaviyum ēṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭiyāta niliyil iruntatāl, araciyalāriṉ utaviyi nāṭavēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.

ḵpiṣar cīmāṭṭiyiṉ irukki :

tiruvāḷar ḵpiṣar eṉṟa oru veḷḷiyar maturiyil aluvalil iruntār. ceṇpakaṉūril avarukkuc contamāṉa māḷiki oṉṟiruntatu. atil tam maṉiviyi viṭṭuviṭṭu maturikkuc ceṉṟu viṭuvār. ōyvu nāṭkaḷil ceṇpakaṉūr tirumpuvār. paḻaṉi maliyiṉmītu varum kutirip pātiyiṉ ucciyil amintuḷḷa vaḷivil, aḻakiya ciṟiya oru kōṭimaṉi amittār. taṉimi varuttum pōtu, ḵpiṣar cīmāṭṭi avvaḷivil amarntukoṇṭu maturi nakar amintuḷḷa tikkip pārtta vaṇṇam iruppārām, kuṟippiṭṭa nāṭkaḷil tam kaṇavar kutiri ūrntu malivaḻip pātiyil ēṟivarum kāṭciyi aṅkiruntu kaṇṭu kaḷippatuṇṭām. ataṉāl avviṭam iṉṟum ḵpiṣar cīmāṭṭiyiṉ irukki (Lady Fischer's seat) eṉṟu ellōrālum aḻikkap paṭukiṟatu. 'vaṉa pōcaṉam' uṇpataṟku itu mikavum ēṟṟa iṭamāka ellōrālum ippoḻutu karutappaṭukiṟatu.

vāṉāyvukkūṭam :

kōṭikkāṉalil amintuḷḷa vāṉāyvuk kūṭam ceṉṉi mānilattilēyē mikavum kuṟippiṭat takka oṉṟākum. itu kōṭikkāṉalukku iraṇṭu kal tolivil, pūmpāṟikkuc cellum pātikku aṇmiyil amintuḷḷa oru cikarattiṉmēl niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ki. pi. 1899-ām āṇṭu intiya nāṭṭiṉ vāṉa ārāyccik kūṭaṅkaḷ tiruttiyamikkappaṭṭapoḻutu, ceṉṉiyilirunta vāṉāyvuk kūṭam iṅku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. utakamaṇṭalattilum, kōtakiriyilum vāṉa ārāyccik kūṭaṅkaḷ niṟuva muyaṟcikaḷ eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl avviṭaṅkaḷiviṭak kōṭikkāṉalē vāṉa ārāyccikku ēṟṟa iṭam eṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. ivviṭattiṉ poruttamāṉa taṭpa veppa niliyum, mappu mantāramaṟṟa teḷinta vāṉamum ivvārāyccikkup peritum tuṇipurikiṉṟaṉa.

19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil kōṭikkāṉalil oru vīḻkoḷḷi (Meteorite) tiruvāḷar lōkaṉ eṉpāriṉ tōṭṭakkāraṉāl tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu. nila ārāyccit tuṟiyiṉar, intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum iraṇṭāvatu irumpu vīḻ koḷḷi ituvē eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ niṟi 35 rāttal. itu ippōtu kalkattā poruṭkāṭci cāliyil vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iv vīḻkoḷḷi vāṉa ārāycciyāḷariṉ kavaṉattip peritum kavarntatāl, 105 āṇṭukaḷākac ceṉṉiyil paṇipurintu koṇṭirunta ceṉṉi vāṉāyvuk kūṭam kōṭikkāṉalukku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. mutaṉ mutalil kiḷeṉ vīḻccikkarukil taṟkālikamāka ikkūṭam niṟuvappaṭṭatu. mikavum uyarntuḷḷa, kōṭikkāṉalukku arukil amintirukkum taṟpōtiya iṭattiṟkup piṟaku itu māṟṟappaṭṭatu.

taṟpōtuḷḷa vāṉāyvukkūṭam tiruvāḷar ār. eḵp, sṭōṉi eṉṟa oru ciṟanta poṟiyiyal vallunarāl kaṭṭi muṭikkappaṭṭatu. ceṉṉimānila āḷunarāka irunta tiruvāḷar veṉlāk eṉpavar tām ki. pi. 1895-ām āṇṭu ikkaṭṭiṭattiṟkuk kal nāṭṭiṉār. kutirip pātiyiṉ vaḻiyākavē kutiriyūrntu avarum kōṭikkāṉalukku vantār. iti nalla vāyppākak koṇṭa kōṭikkāṉal makkaḷ ikkuṟiñci nakari iruppuppāti mūlamākavō, vaṇṭippāti mūlamākavō iṇikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttik kēṭṭuk koṇṭār. veṉlāk avarkaḷ iṅku vantapōtu, maḻikkāla iravil iraṭṭi vaṇṭiyil payaṇam ceytum, kutirippātiyil naṭantum, puraviyūrntum mikavum tollikaḷukkuḷḷākik kōṭik kāṉaliyaṭintār. kōṭikkāṉalukku oru nalla pāti avaciyam eṉpati avarum uṇarntirukka vēṇṭum.

car ārtar hāvlāk eṉpavar 1899-ām āṇṭu vāṉāyvukkūṭattip pārviyiṭṭār. ceṉṉik kiruttavak kallūriyil pērāciriyarāka iruntavarum, ceṉṉi araciyalāriṉ vāṉa ārāyccittuṟit talivarāka iruntavarumāṉa tiruvāḷar micci smit eṉpavar kōṭikkāṉal vāṉāyvuk kūṭattiṉ poṟuppi ēṟṟukkoṇṭu ciṟitukālam paṇi ceytār. avar aluvaliliruntu ōyvu peṟṟatum, tiruvāḷar jē. evarceṭ eṉpavar poṟuppēṟṟār. evarceṭ avarkaḷiṉ maṉiviyārum vāṉa ārāyccik kaliyil nalla pulami peṟṟavar, avvammiyār 'teṉmaṇṭala viṇmīṉkaḷiṉ vaḻi kāṭṭi' (Guide to the Southern Stars) eṉṟa oru nūli eḻutip patippittār.

ki. pi. 1910-ām āṇṭu tiruvāḷar kēṉāṉ mīcci eṉpārum avar maṉiviyārum, tirunelvēlik kuṭiyēṟṟattiliruntu taṅkaḷ illattiṟkut tirumpik koṇṭiruntapōtu, vāṉattil oru periya vālmīṉik (Comet) kaṇṭārkaḷ. āṅkilak kaḻakat (English Club) tiṉarukē niṉṟu atip paṟṟi, vāṉa ārāycci vallunarāṉa micci smittik kēṭṭārkaḷ. atik kaṇṭa micci smit, atu oru hāli vālmīṉ (Halley's Comet) eṉṟu kūṟi viṭṭu, 'jerucalam' eṉṟa tam kutirimēl tāvi ēṟi, vāṉa āyvukkūṭattiṟku ōṭi, atippaṟṟi ārāyntār. atupōṉṟa vālmīṉ pala nūṟṟāṇṭu kaṭku orumuṟitāṉ teṉpaṭumām. ki. pi. 1910-ām āṇṭu, kōṭikkāṉalil vāḻnta oruvar tam naṇparukku eḻutiya kaṭitattil, 'nāṉ kōkkar naṭi veḷiyil atikāli 4-40 maṇikkuc ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntapoḻutu oru vālmīṉi vāṉattil kaṇṭēṉ. ataṉ pēraḻaku eṉṉi viyappil āḻttiyatu. atu pōṉṟa vālmīṉi nāṉ eṉṟum kaṇṭatilli. atu aṭivāṉattiliruntu naṭuvāṉam vari nīṇṭu pēroḷiyōṭu viḷaṅkiyatu' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭār.

tiruvāḷar evar ceṭ oyva peṟṟakam. ṭākṭar ṭi. rāyṭs eṉpavar poṟuppēṟṟār. 1936-ām āṇṭu jūli 19-ām nāḷ ēṟpaṭṭa 'pūraṇa cūriya kirakaṇatti ' (total eclipse of the sun)p paṟṟi ārāyvataṟkāka avarum jappāṉukku aṉuppappaṭṭār.

kōṭikkāṉaliluḷḷa ivvāṉāyvuk kūṭam, 'intiya araciyalāriṉ vāṉakkali āyvukkūṭam' (The Government of India Solar Physics Observatory) eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ik kūṭam cūriyaṉippaṟṟi ārāyntum, cuṇṇa oḷi (Calcium light) yiyum, nīrvāyu oḷi (Hydrogen light) yiyum paṭam piṭittum, pala uṇmikaḷi veḷiyiṭṭu vāṉakkali vaḷarccikkup peritum pāṭupaṭṭu varukiṟatu. mēlum puvikkavarcci (Terrestrial magnetism) yip paṟṟiyum, aṉilōṟpaṉṉak kali (Meteorology) yip paṟṟiyum, pūkampaviyal (Seismology) paṟṟiyum itu ārāykiṟatu. amerikka aikkiya nāṭṭil vilcaṉmali (Mt. Wilson) mītu amintuḷḷa vāṉāyvuk kūṭamum ḵprāṉs nāṭṭil miyūṭaṉ (Meudon) eṉṟa iṭattil amintuḷḷa vāṉāyvukkūṭamum āṟṟum attaṉi ārāyccikaḷiyum itu iṅku āṟṟukiṟatu. mikavum putumiyāṉa vāṉa ārāyccik karuvikaḷum, aṉilōṟpaṉṉak kaliyip paṟṟi ārāyum karuvikaḷum iṅkup poruttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ki. pi. 1937-ām āṇṭu ṭākṭar rāyṭ aluvaliliruntu ōyvu peṟṟatum ṭākṭar ē. el. nārāyaṇaṉ ivvāṉāyvuk kūṭattiṉ poṟuppi ēṟṟār.

mutalilēyē vāṉa āyvukkūṭap poṟuppāḷariṭam aṉumati peṟṟu, aṅku amikkappaṭṭuḷḷa periya tolinōkkāṭi (Telescope)yiṉ mūlam vāṉa maṇṭalattil tiriyum kōḷkaḷiyum viṇmīṉkaḷiyum kāṇalām. vāṉa maṇṭalattiṉ taṉmiyi aṟintu iṉpaṭivataṟkāka ovvoru veḷḷikkiḻami kāliyilum potumakkaḷ ivvāṉāyvuk kūṭattiṟkuḷ aṉumatikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. intiya nāṭṭuk kāla aḷavip (Indian Standard Time) potumakkaḷukku uṇarttuvataṟkāka nāḷtōṟum kāli pattu maṇikku oru koṭi ivvāṉāyvuk kūṭattil uyarttappaṭukiṟatu. vāṉa ārāycciyiṉ mūlamākavum, ulakattiṉ maṟṟa pakutikaḷil amintuḷḷa vāṉāyvuk kūṭaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷiyākum rēṭiyō kālak kuṟippukaḷiṉ mūlamākavum, iṅku amintuḷḷa maṇippoṟi cariyāṉa kāla aḷavik kāṭṭi niṟkiṟatu. piṉ maraṅkaḷum, nīlappiciṉ maraṅkaḷum kōṭikkāṉalic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa kāṭukaḷil uyarntu vaḷarntiruppatāl iṅku ēṟṟappaṭum koṭi, ellā iṭaṅkaḷukkum teṉpaṭuvatilli.



kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷ

puṉita itayak kallūri :

puṉita itayak kallūri (Sacred Heart Collage) ki. pi. 1895-ām āṇṭu tuvakkappaṭṭatu. itil ēṟakkuṟiya 2000 māṇavarkaḷ payiṉṟu veḷiyēṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. intiya māṇavarkaḷallāmal, iṅkilāntu, āstirēliyā, kolampiyā, parmā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntum māṇavarkaḷ iṅku vantu kalvi payilkiṉṟaṉar. itu kōṭikkāṉalukkuc cellum pātiyil, ceṇpakaṉūril amintuḷḷatu. itu rōmaṉ kattōlikkarukkariya camayak kallūriyākum. ippaḷḷi tuvakkappaṭṭa kālattil pātikkumēl ḵpreñcu nāṭṭu iḷiñarkaḷ kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. ulakap pōrkaḷiṉāl veḷināṭṭiliruntu varum māṇavarkaḷiṉ toki kuṟintuviṭṭatu. ik kallūri kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum iṭam ki. pi. 1878-ām āṇṭiliruntu palataṭavi ciṟuciṟu pakutikaḷāka vāṅkappaṭṭatu. oru vivacāyap paḷḷiyiyum, toḻiṟpaḷḷiyiyum niṟuvum nōkkattōṭu ivviṭam vāṅkappaṭṭatu. ciṉkōṉā maraṅkaḷum vēṟu cila payirkaḷum iṅku mutalil payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl avi nalla muṟiyil viḷiyāta kāraṇattāl vivacāya, toḻil kallūrikaḷ naṭattum eṇṇam kiviṭappaṭṭatu. piṟaku ippoḻutu uḷḷa camayak kallūri tuvakkappaṭṭatu.

kōṭikkāṉalil vāḻum makkaḷukku ik kallūri mikavum aṟimukamāṉa oṉṟu. ceṇpakaṉūriluḷḷa yūkilipṭas kāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyāka varumpōtu, kaṇkavarum aḻakiya tōṭṭattiṉ naṭuvē kaliyaḻakumikka ikkallūrik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ nimirntu niṟpatik kaṇṭu makiḻalām. kōṭikkāṉalukku varum untu vaṇṭi, ceṇpakaṉūr añcal niliyattiṟku arukil niṉṟāl, arukiluḷḷa ikkallūriyiṉ vāyil teṉpaṭum. kōkkar naṭiveḷiyil ceṉṟōmāṉāl ikkallūriyiṉ maṇiyoli nam kātil mōtum. kaḻuttiliruntu kālvari nīṇṭa āṭi uṭuttuk koṇṭu, iṭuppil cenniṟappaṭṭi aṇintu koṇṭu, kiyil puttakamō, mātirippeṭṭiyō (Specimen-case) tāṅkiya vaṇṇam pātiri naṭi veḷi (Priest's walk) yilum, kōṭikkāṉaliṉ iyaṟkiyaḻaku coriyum ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum im māṇavarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭattik kāṇalām. ak kallūrikkuḷ pārviyāḷarkaḷ aṉumatikkap paṭuvatilli. avarkaḷ kalvimuṟi, vēlit tiṭṭam ākiyavi paṟṟi yārukkum teriyātu.

iṭikkaliyō (Intermediate), ativiṭa uyarnta kalviyō kaṟṟa māṇavarkaḷē iṅkuc cērttuk koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṅku eṭṭāṇṭukkalvi ellārukkum payiṟṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. mutal iraṇṭāṇṭu, camayavāḻvil āḻnta paṟṟuk koḷḷum vakiyil māṇavarkaḷ payiṟṟappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. piṟaku mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷ ilattīṉ, kirēkkam ākiya moḻikaḷil aṟivu peṟukiṟārkaḷ. pala tāymoḻikaḷip pēcum māṇavarkaḷ iṅkuk kūṭuvatāl potuvāka āṅkilamum, ilattīṉum pēccu moḻiyākavum kaṟpikkum moḻiyākavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kaṭici mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷum tattuva ārāycciyilē kaḻikiṉṟaṉa, kirēkka rōma nāṭṭup periyārkaḷ aruḷic ceyta paḻamiyāṉa tattuvaṅkaḷum, intiya nāṭṭut tattuvaṅkaḷum, mēli nāṭṭut tattuvaṅkaḷum teḷivākak kaṟpikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kallūriyil varalāṟu paṭittavarkaṭku iṅku aṟiviyalum (Science), aṟiviyal paṭittavarkaṭku varalāṟum kaṟpikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu eṭṭāṇṭuk kalvi muṟṟup peṟṟatum, ammāṇavarkaḷ pūṉāviṟkō, kurciyāṅkiṟkō cella vēṇṭum. aṅku, nāṉkāṇṭuc camayak kalviyum, pātirit toḻilukkēṟṟa ōrāṇṭup payiṟciyum avarkaḷukku aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, añcal niliyattiṟkarukil ikkallūriyiṉāl naṭattappaṭum poruṭkāṭcicāli oṉṟuḷḷatu. tōṇṭi yeṭukkappaṭṭa putiporuḷkaḷum paḻaṉi maliyiluḷḷa tāvara vakikaḷum, vilaṅkukaḷum, palavakiyāṉa pāmpukaḷum aṅku vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṇṇil mūḻkiya putiporuḷkaḷit tēṭiyeṭuttu ārāyvatil, ikkallūrip pērāciriyarkaḷ palar pērūkkum kāṭṭiṉar; kāṭṭivarukiṉṟaṉar.

hikiḷerk paḷḷi :

pala āṇṭukaḷ tiṭṭamiṭappaṭṭuk kōṭikkāṉalil 'hikiḷerk paḷḷi' ki. pi. 1901-ām āṇṭu tuvakkappaṭṭatu. ippaḷḷi tuvakkappaṭuvataṟku muṉ 'hikiḷerk paḷḷi' (Highclerc boarding School) eṉṟa oru niṟuvaṉam kōṭikkāṉalil iruntatu. kōṭimātaṅkaḷil iṅkut taṅka varum amerikka, āṅkilēya makkaḷiṉ kuḻantikaḷ ippaḷḷiviṭutiyil taṅkip paṭittaṉar. appoḻutu inniṟuvaṉattip 'pātirimāriṉ kuḻantikaḷ payilum kōṭik kāṉal paḷḷi' (The Kodikanal School for Missio naries children) eṉṟum, 'kōṭippaḷḷi' eṉṟum, 'hikiḷerk, eṉṟum aḻittaṉar. mutaṉ mutalākap patiṉmūṉṟu māṇavarkaḷē ippaḷḷiyil cērntaṉar. tirumati em. el. eṭṭi eṉṟa amerikka mātu, taṉ makaṉāṉa ṣervuṭ eṭṭiyik kāṇa intiyā vantār. kōṭikkāṉal makkaḷ avari ippaḷḷiyiṉ mutalvarākap paṇi ēṟkumāṟu vēṇṭiṉārkaḷ. anta ammiyārum, poṟuppēṟṟār. tāymiyuḷḷam koṇṭa avvammiyār māṇavarkaḷiṉ tēviyi yaṟintu avarkaḷ taṅkip paṭipputaṟkāka, irāppaḷḷikaḷip pōṉṟu iraṇṭu ciṟiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷik kaṭṭiṉār. avi periya, 'paḷḷi yaṟi', 'ciṟiya paḷḷiyaṟi' eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. appaḷḷiyic cuṟṟi rōjāppūt tōṭṭamum, piṉ marakkāṭum pērikkāyt tōṭṭamum amikkappaṭṭaṉa.

ki. pi. 1902-ām āṇṭu ippaḷḷi 'ceṇṭral havucukkum (Central House) rāk kāṭṭēju (Rock cottage)kkum māṟṟappaṭṭatu. appōtu tirumati eṭṭi amerikkā ceṉṟiruntārkaḷ. mīṇṭum intiyā tirumpiyapōtu, rū 10,000 tiraṭṭikkoṇṭu vantār. appaṇattik koṇṭu cantiveḷiyil amintirunta hikiḷerk paḷḷiyi vilikku vāṅkiṉār. ki.pi.1902 mutal 1904 vari ivviṭam kumāri ōrlpār eṉpavarāl kuṭikkūlikku eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. avar koṭittaṉmi mikkavar; intiyāvil pala iṭaṅkaḷil taṅkum uṇavuviṭutikaḷiyum (Boarding houses), kiruttavap pātirikaḷukkāṉa taṅkal maṉikaḷiyum niṟuviyavar. mīṇṭum ip paḷḷi hikiḷerkkiṟku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. ulakappōrkaḷ naṭanta camayattil pala nāṭukaḷic cērnta āciriyarkaḷ iṅkup paṇiyāṟṟiṉar. potuvāka amerikka āṅkila nāṭṭuk kalvimuṟikaḷ iṅkuk kaṭippiṭikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa. āṉāl, cila camayaṅkaḷil amerikka māṇavarkaḷ mikuntirunta kāraṇattāl amerikka nāṭṭuk kalvittiṭṭamē ippaḷḷiyilum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. āṇ peṇ ākiya iru pālarum iṅkuk kalvi payilkiṉṟaṉar. kōṭiyil taṅkuvataṟkākak kōṭikkāṉal varum peṟṟōrkaḷiṉ, iḷañciṟuvar ciṟumiyarkaḷukkāṉa 'kiṇṭar kārṭaṉ paḷḷi' oṉṟum āṇṭutōṟum mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷkaḷukku iṅku naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. amerikkaṉ miṣaṉic cārnta kiruttavariṉ kuḻantikaḷ intiyāviṉ pala pakutikaḷiliruntum, ilaṅki, arēpiyā, pāracīkam ciyām mutaliya aṇṭi nāṭukaḷiliruntum iṅku vantu kalvi payilkiṉṟaṉar. ōr iraṭṭimāṭik kaṭṭiṭamum, vēṟupala kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum, ippaḷḷikkākak kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. pārṭaṉ (Barton), ērli (Airlie) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷum ippaḷḷikkāka vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. peṇṭarlāc( Benderloch) eṉṟa iṭam paḷḷittalivar taṅkuvataṟkāka vāṅkappaṭṭatu. villisṭaṉ (Williston), liṭṭil villi (Little Willie) eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷ āciriyarkaḷiṉ kuṭiyiruppukaḷukkāka vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. 'viṉsṭaṉ' (Winston) eṉṟa iṭam paḷḷikkuriya ilavaca maruttuvamaṉiyākap payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. ki. pi. 1932-ām āṇṭu itu ōr uyar nilippaḷḷiyāka māṟṟappaṭṭatu. 'iṅku naṭattappaṭum iṟutit tērvu meṭrikulēṣaṉukku oppāṉatu. itil kalvi kaṟṟu veḷiyēṟum māṇavarkaḷ amerikkā, kāṉaṭā nāṭṭuk kallūri vakuppukkaḷil cērttukkoḷḷap paṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṅkup payilum māṇavarkaḷ cīṉiyar kēmpiriṭj' tērvilum kalantukoṇṭu nalla veṟṟiyaṭikiṉṟaṉar. ērikkarukil, ippaḷḷikkāka oru paranta viḷiyāṭumiṭam amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ṭākṭar aleks, tirumati aleks, es. vilcaṉ, kumāri privāsṭ, tiruvāḷar kārl, tirumati kārl, ṭapiḷyū ḵpels ākiyōr, nīṇṭa nāḷ paḷḷippoṟuppāḷarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟiṉar. potumakkaḷiṭattil paṇam tiraṭṭi, tirumati mārkaraṭ eṭṭiyiṉ niṉivuc ciṉṉamāka ippaḷḷiyil oru kōvilik kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.

piraceṇṭēṣaṉ kāṉveṉṭ :

kōṭikkāṉalilē kuṟippiṭattakka maṟṟoru paḷḷi, 'piraceṇṭēcaṉ kāṉveṇṭ' eṉṟa peṇkaḷ uyar nilippaḷḷiyākum. ceṉṉip piraceṇṭēcaṉ tiruccapiyic cārnta cēviyar tāyār (Rev. Mother Xavier) avarkaḷum vēṟu cila kaṉṉiyarum 1914-ām āṇṭu kōṭikkāṉalukku vantiruntaṉar. avvāṇṭu 'hilciṭ' (Hill Side) eṉṟa iṭattil taṅkiṉar. ki. pi. 1915-ām āṇṭu 'keṉmūr' (Kenmure) eṉṟa iṭattil taṅkiṉar. appoḻutu avarkaḷuṭaṉ cila kuḻantikaḷum taṅkiyiruntaṉar. avvāṇṭu ellōrum ceṉṟa piṟaku, cēviyar tāyārum, ikṉēciyas tāyārum, ḵpōr vīri eṉṟa iṭattil taṅki, oru paḷḷi niṟuvuvataṟku ēṟṟa iṭattit tēṭiṉar. kiḷeṉ vīḻccikku arukil amintirunta maliyucciyil (muṉpu vāṉāyvukkūṭam irunta iṭam) paḷḷiyi niṟuvuvatu eṉṟu muṭivu ceytaṉar. appaḷḷiyi amippataṟku vēṇṭiya poruḷātārattip paṟṟit tiruvāḷar ī. ār. lōkaṉ eṉpāruṭaṉ ālōcaṉi naṭattiṉar.

ki. pi. 1916-ām āṇṭu ēpral tiṅkaḷil piraceṇṭēcaṉ tiruccapiyic cārnta kaṉṉiyarkaḷ, 'hil ciṭ' eṉṟa iṭattilēyē 17 māṇaviyarkaḷik koṇṭu ippaḷḷiyit tuvakkiṉar. 1916-ām āṇṭu jūṉ tiṅkaḷ 16-ām nāḷ ippōtuḷḷa paḷḷik kaṭṭiṭattiṟkuk kal nāṭṭu viḻā naṭattappaṭṭatu. maraṅkaḷum naṭappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭṭiṭa vēli muṭintatum ki. pi. 1917-ām āṇṭu ḵpepruvarit tiṅkaḷil, paḷḷi putiya kaṭṭiṭattiṟku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. paḷḷiyil oru kōvilum amikkappaṭṭatu. akkōvil kāliyil toḻukikkuriya iṭamākavum, māliyil paḷḷiyākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. mutalil mikavum eḷiya muṟiyilēyē ip paḷḷi toṭaṅkiyatu. iṅkup paṇipurinta kaṉṉimārkaḷukkup pōtiya vacatiyum illi. cāmāṉkaḷ koṇarnta peṭṭikaḷē nāṟkālikaḷākap payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. māṭṭu vaṇṭiyil poruttappaṭṭa pīppāykaḷiṉ mūlam neṭuntoliviliruntu nīr koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. appōtu pōkkuvaravu vacatikaḷum mikak kuṟivu. ki. pi. 1916-ām āṇṭu naṭunilippaḷḷiyākat tuvaṅkiya itu ki. pi. 1919-ām āṇṭu uyar nilippaḷḷiyāka māṟiyatu. ki. pi. 1920- ām āṇṭu kōṭikkāṉalukku vanta veliṅkṭaṉ cīmāṭṭi, ippaḷḷikkāṉa iraṭṭi māṭik kaṭṭiṭam oṉṟukkuk kālkōḷ iṭṭār. 1 laṭcam rūpāy poruṭ celavil ikkaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. 'kiṇṭar kārṭaṉ' vakuppiliruntu, cīṉiyar kēmpiriṭj vakuppu vari, ikkaṭṭiṭattil naṭipeṟṟaṉa.

ippaḷḷi nāḷaṭivil nalla viḷamparamum muṉṉēṟṟamum peṟṟu oru ciṟanta airōppiyap paḷḷiyāka māṟiyuḷḷatu. pattu vayatu muṭiyumvari, ciṟuvarkaḷum iṅku māṇavarkaḷākac cērttuk koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ paḷḷiyiṟuti vakuppuvari paṭippatōṭu, ṭiriṉiṭi kallūri icit tērvukkum payiṟci peṟukiṉṟaṉar. cēviyar tāyār ōyvu peṟṟatum, akasṭiṉ tāyār paḷḷit talivarākap poṟuppēṟṟār. ki. pi. 1926-mutal 1944 muṭiyap paṇiyāṟṟiṉār. avarukkup piṟaku jōcaḵpiṉ tāyār, paḷḷit talivarākap paṇiyāṟṟi varukiṟār. kōṭi nāṭkaḷil ippaḷḷiyil naṭipeṟum āṇṭu viḻā kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ceyiṇṭ jāṉ cōtariyālum, kumāri elsperk eṉpavarālum iṅku māṇavikaḷukkuc ciṟanta kalip (Fine Arts) payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṇṭu viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil māṇavikaḷ nikaḻttum āṭal pāṭal nikaḻccikaḷ pārāṭṭutalukkuriyavi,



kōheṉ niṉivup paḷḷi :

ki. pi. 1912-ām āṇṭu, kōṭikkāṉalil lāc eṇṭu' (Loch End) eṉṟa ōr iṭatti, amerikka lūtaraṉ camayattic cārntavarkaḷ, tiruvāḷar ṭēṉiyal maknāyar eṉṟa poṟiyiyal vallunariṭamiruntu vilikku vāṅkiṉārkaḷ. ki. pi. 1922-ām āṇṭu, taṅkaḷ kuḻantikaḷ payiluvataṟ keṉṟu oru paḷḷiyi niṟuviṉārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ tantiyar nāṭāṉa amerikka nāṭṭukkuk kalvi muṟiyi appaḷḷiyil pukuttiṉar. kōṭikkālattil iṅku vantu taṅkiyavarkaḷiṉ kuḻantikaḷuk cāṉa paruvappaḷḷi (Season school) yākavē itu mutalil tuvakkappaṭṭatu. tiruvāḷar pācmēṉ eṉṟa āciriyar paḷḷikkākac ciṟiya oru kaṭṭiṭattik kaṭṭiṉār. piṟaku māṇavarkaḷukkut taṅkumiṭamum, kōvilum amittār. ivviraṇṭiṉ kaṭṭiṭak kaliyaḻaku kōṭikkāṉalil kuṟippiṭattakkatu, tiruvāḷar puccāṉaṉ putiporuḷ ārāycciyil ārvamuḷḷavar. paḻaṉimalikaḷil ki. pi. 1942-ām āṇṭu avar tōṇṭiyeṭutta putiporuḷkaḷ ceṇpakaṉūriluḷḷa poruṭkāṭci cāliyil vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippaḷḷiyil 8 vakuppukkaḷ ippoḻutu uḷḷaṉa. tiruvāḷar miyūller eṉpārum tirumati hekkal ammiyārum āciriyarkaḷāka iṅkup paṇiyāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.

svīṭiṣ paḷḷi :

intiyāvil vāḻum svīṭiṣ kuḻantikaḷ payilvataṟkeṉṟu kōṭikkāṉalil oru paḷḷi uḷḷatu. svīṭiṣ kuḻantikaḷ mutalil hikiḷerk paḷḷikkē aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl irupatāṇṭukku muṉ svīṭiṣ kuḻantikaḷ taṉiyākap payilvataṟ keṉṟu ippaḷḷi tuvakkappaṭṭatu. ippaḷḷiyiṉ peyar 'cālvik' (Solvik) eṉpatākum. hikiḷerk paḷḷi irukkum atē maliyiṉmītu tāṉ ituvum amintuḷḷatu. iṅkup payilum māṇavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ tāy moḻiyilēyē kalvi peṟukiṉṟaṉar. hikiḷerk paḷḷiyil payilumpōtu pala nāṭṭu māṇavarkaḷōṭu cērntu paḻakum vāyppu iruntatu. ippoḻutu atu illi. intiya nāṭṭil uḷḷa svīṭiṣ paḷḷi itu oṉṟutāṉ. eṉavē, intiya nāṭṭiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷilum vāḻum svīṭiṣkārarkaḷ, taṅkaḷuṭiya kuḻantikaḷi 'cālvik'kiṟkē aṉuppukiṉṟaṉar. kaṭanta cila āṇṭukaṭku muṉpāka, svīṭaṉ nāṭṭup pārāḷu maṉṟamum iti aṅkīkarittatu. ippaḷḷi uruvattil ciṟiyatāka iruntālum īṭaṟṟa aḻakōṭu ēriyip pārtta vaṇṇam amintuḷḷatu.

tamiḻp paḷḷikaḷ :

kōṭikkāṉalil vāḻum tamiḻ māṇavarkaḷ payiluvataṟkeṉṟu pala tamiḻp paḷḷikaḷ iṅku amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. yūṉiyaṉ carc kōṭikkāṉalil niṟuvappaṭṭa pōtu pātiriyāriṉ illattiṟkarukil, amerikkaṉ miṣaṉic cārntōr oru tuvakkap paḷḷiyi niṟuviṉar. kōṭikkāṉalil makkaṭ perukkam ēṟpaṭṭatum, nakarāṭciyiṉar vāṉāyvuk kūṭap pātiyilum, untu vaṇṭi niliyattiṟkarukilum tamiḻ māṇavarkaḷukkeṉṟu pala tuvakkap paḷḷikaḷi niṟuviṉar. ki. pi. 1942-ām āṇṭu 'tōpikāṉā'viṟku etiril oru naṭunilip paḷḷi tuvakkappaṭṭatu. itu ippoḻutu uyar nilip paḷḷiyāka vaḷarntuḷḷatu. rōmaṉ kattōlikkac camayattārāl mūṉṟu tamiḻp paḷḷikaḷ naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi mēl ērip pātiyilum, kāṉveṇṭ pātiyilum amintuḷḷaṉa.



ēḻi māṇavar paḷḷi :

ki. pi. 1919-ām āṇṭu intiya ēḻi māṇavarkaḷukkāka ōr ilavacap paḷḷi tuvakkappaṭṭatu. ippōtu atu 'ceyiṇṭ cēviyar intiya ēḻi māṇavar paḷḷi' eṉṟa peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. 80 māṇavarkaḷukku ilavaca uṇavum uṭiyum iṅku aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. rōmaṉ kattōlikka māṇavarkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru kuḻu ip paḷḷiyil, amikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ik kuḻu , kattōlikka camayattic cārnta ēḻikaḷukkut toṇṭu puriyap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu.

aṉātip paḷḷi :

ki. pi. 1906-ām āṇṭu ōr āṅkilap pātiriyār, 'āṇkaḷ āṅkilappaḷḷi' eṉṟa oru paḷḷiyi ḵperṉhil eṉṟa iṭattil tuvakkiṉār. āṉāl ap paḷḷi cila nāṭkaḷil mūṭappaṭṭu viṭṭatu. 1908-ām āṇṭu kumāri kār eṉpavar cila āṅkilak kuḻantikaḷukkākac ciṟiya paḷḷiyoṉṟip peṉ lomāṇṭ' eṉṟa iṭattil (speṉcar kaṭikkup piṉṉāl) tuvakkiṉār. atuvum kiviṭappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1915-ām āṇṭu , imayamalic cāraliluḷḷa 'kālimpāṅ viṭuti' (Kalimpong Homes) yip pōṉṟu, āṅkilō intiya aṉātik kuḻantikaḷukkākak kōṭikkāṉalilum oru viṭutiyi amittaṟkāṉa oru tiṭṭam uruvākiyatu. ceṇpakaṉūr toṭaril, pḷēkparṉ ṣōlā irunta iṭattil (aṭukkam kaṇavāyiṉ ucciyil) av viṭutiyik kaṭṭa muṭivu ceytaṉar. itaṟkuḷḷākak kōṭikkāṉalil vāṭakik kaṭṭiṭam oṉṟil ivviṭuti tuvakkappaṭṭatu, speṉcar kaṭikku aṭuttāṟ pōl uḷḷa 'kiḷeñcil' (Glengyle) eṉṟa iṭattil vayatu vanta peṇkaḷukkāṉa oru viṭutiyum, ḵperṉhil maṉiyil āṇkaḷukkum iḷañ ciṟumiyarkkumāṉa viṭutiyōṭu paḷḷi oṉṟum niṟuvap paṭṭaṉa. ik kaṭṭiṭattiṟkup 'pḷēkparṉ' eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. āṉāl ivvaṉātip paḷḷi nīlakiri maliyiṉ mītuḷḷa keyṭi paḷḷattākkukku māṟṟappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. ippoḻutu aṅkup perum aḷavil ciṟappāka ip paḷḷi naṭattappaṭukiṟatu.

māṇṭicōrip paḷḷi :

tirumati māṇṭicōri ammiyār ki. pi. 1942 mutal 1944 vari kōṭikkāṉalil taṅki yiruntār. appoḻutu ciṟu kuḻantikaḷukkāṉa māṇṭi cōrip paḷḷi oṉṟum, māṇṭicōrik kalvi muṟiyip payiṟṟuvataṟkāka āciriyap payiṟcip paḷḷi oṉṟum avar naṭattiṉār. pala nāṭṭuc ciṟu kuḻantikaḷum ap paḷḷiyil kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. 'rōs pēṅk'kil ap paḷḷiyiruntatu. pukaḻ peṟṟa ip pērāciriyariṉ kālaṭiyilamarntu ciṟanta kalvi peṟum vāyppi intiya nāṭṭiṉ pala pakutiyiliruntum vanta kuḻantikaḷ iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ peṟṟaṉar.

kaḻakaṅkaḷ

paṭakuk kaḻakam :- kōṭikkāṉaliluḷḷa kaḻakaṅkaḷil paṭakuk kaḻakam kuṟippiṭat takkatu. ārampattiliruntē ēriyil paṭakukaḷ iruntaṉa. cila tōṭṭa mutalāḷikaḷ taṅkaḷ conta upayōkattukkākak kīḻ ērip pātiyil cila paṭaku vīṭukaḷi {Boat houses) amittaṉar. rōmaṉ kattōlikkap pātirimārkaḷ 'vesṭ varṭu hō'viṟku arukil oru paṭaku vīṭṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar. taṉippaṭṭavarkaḷ, paṭakukaḷi vāṅkik kōṭikkāṉalukkuk koṇṭu varuvatu avvaḷavu eḷitākat tōṉṟavilli. ākiyiṉāl ik kuṟiyip pōkka ki. pi. 1890-ām āṇṭu potumakkaḷāl paṭakuk kaḻakam tuvakkappaṭṭatu. mutalil taṉippaṭṭavariṭamiruntu cila paṭakukaḷi ik kaḻakam vāṅkiyatu. kaḷḷik kōṭṭiyiluḷḷa 'cālṭar aṇṭu kampeṉi' (Salter and co.) yiliruntu, 'kēṭḵpli' (Gadfly), cēḵpar (Chafer) eṉṟa paṭakukaḷum vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. paṭakuk kaḻakattiṉ mutal kuravac ceyalāḷarāka iruntavar kēpṭaṉ kiḷārk eṉpavar.

nāykaḷip paṭakukaḷil aṉumatippatilli. merukiṭappaṭṭa paṭakukaḷil ciṟuvarkaḷi ēṟṟuvatilli. ciṟuvarkaḷukkāka 'lili' eṉṟa paṭaku uḷḷatu. ki. pi. 1894-ām āṇṭuk kāvaṟ paṭik kuṟippu (police reports) kaḷil paṭakuk kaḻakam tīkkiriyāṉatākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉāl rū. 1779 peṟumāṉa poruḷkaḷ cētamuṟṟaṉavām. potumakkaḷiṭam naṉkoṭiyākap poruḷ tiraṭṭip paṭakuk kaḻakattiṟkup puttuyir koṭuttatōṭu, pōkkuvaraviṟku iṭiñcalāka ērik kariyōraṅkaḷil muḷittirunta nāṇalkaḷiyum akaṟṟiṉar. ovvōr āṇṭum 60 mutal 80 uṟuppiṉarkaḷ vari cērntukoṇṭē yiruntaṉar. uṟuppiṉar toki perukiyatum, niṟiyap paṭakukaḷ vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ki. pi. 1897-ām āṇṭu , paṭakuk kaḻakattiṉ ceyaṟkuḻuvil, putukkōṭṭi maṉṉariṉ iḷavalākiya turi rājāviṉ peyar kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. tiruvāḷar micci smit, vāṉ āyvukkūṭap poṟuppāḷar paṇiyiliruntu ōyvu peṟṟatum, 'viṉsḵ pōrṭu' (Winsford) māḷikiyil taṅki vāḻntār. appōtu paṭakuk kaḻakattiṉ kuravac ceyalāḷarākap poṟuppēṟṟār. avar viṉsḵpōrṭu māḷikiyil amarntavaṇṇam, tam muṭiya toli nōkkāṭiyiṉ mūlamākap paṭakukaḷik kaṇkāṇippār. paṭakuk kaḻakattiṉ vitikaḷ oḻuṅkākak kaṭippiṭikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉavā eṉak kavaṉippār. mutaṉ mutalil talimip paṭakōṭṭiyākap paṇiyāṟṟiyavaṉ peyar 'mikēl' eṉpatākum. paṭakukaḷ tēvippaṭumpōtu, pirayāṇikaḷ kariyil niṉṟu koṇṭu mikēl' eṉṟu kattuvar. ataṉāl ellāp paṭakōṭṭikaḷum 'mikēl' eṉṟa peya rālēyē aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar.

kōṭikkāṉal ēri, kātalarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavukku mikavum ēṟṟa iṭamākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. taṉimiyi virumpum airōppiyak kātalarkaḷ iṇi iṇiyākap paṭakūrntu poḻutik kaḻippar, airōppiyak kātalarkaḷip paṟṟiya nakic cuviyāṉa ceytikaḷ pala iṅkuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kōṭikkāṉalukkup putitāka oru pirāṭesṭaṇṭup pātiri vantiruntār. avar iḷiñar. aḻakiya tōṟṟamum, eṭuppāṉa uṭaṟkaṭṭum uṭiyavar. avvūril vāḻnta celvak kuṭumpattic cārnta aḻakiya naṅki oruttiyuṭaṉ cila kālam neruṅkip paḻaki vantār. avaḷit tirumaṇam ceytukoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟa āci avaruḷḷattil ēṟpaṭṭatu. tam uḷḷak karutti avvaḻakikkuk kūṟa niṉitta avar, paṭakil ulavivara aḻittār. appōtu avarip piṉpuṟamiruntu oru naṇṭu kavviyatu. vali poṟukka muṭiyāta avar mallānta vaṇṇam vīḻntār. appeṇ ak kāṭciyik kaṇṭu vayiṟu kuluṅkac cirittāḷ. avamāṉam tāṅka muṭiyāta ap pātiriyār eḻuntu virivāka vīṭu ceṉṟu viṭṭār. piṟaku ap peṇṇiṭam tam karuttik kūṟa avar maṉam ēṉō tuṇiyavilli! pāvam ! kātalil avarukku ēṟpaṭṭa tōlvi iraṅkattakkatu. āṉāl kōṭik kāṉal ēriyil toṭaṅkiya ellāk kātalum ippaṭi muṭiyavilli. pala, 'iṉpiyal' muṭivukaḷip peṟṟu veṟṟikaramāka muṭintaṉa.



kōṭikkāṉal kaḻakam :

kōṭikkāṉalil pala nāṭṭu makkaḷum kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅkiyatum, māli nērattil iṉpamākap poḻutik kaḻippataṟku oru kaḻakam tēvi eṉpati ellōrum uṇarntaṉar. ataṉ viḷivākavē kōṭikkāṉal kaḻakam tōṉṟiyatu. carverli veñc avarkaḷum, naṭuvar kirakām avarkaḷum kōṭikkāṉalil vāḻntapōtu, 'pāmpāṟu maṉiyil' aṭikkaṭi viruntum, kūṭṭaṅkaḷum naṭattuvārkaḷ. kōṭikkāṉalil vāḻnta camaya vātikaḷum, potumakkaḷum cērntu oru kaḻakatti niṟuva vēṇṭum eṉṟu muṭivu ceytaṉar. ippōtu ik kaḻakak kaṭṭiṭam amintuḷḷa iṭam maturi amerikka miṣaṉāl aṉpaḷippāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl ñāyiṟṟukkiḻamikaḷil mitāṉa viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷum (Out door games), kuṭiyum aṅku iṭam peṟak kūṭātu eṉṟa nipantaṉikaḷi vitittaṉar. potu makkaḷiṭam paṇam vacūlikkappaṭṭuk kaḻakak kaṭṭiṭamum, maṭṭip pantāṭṭa mitāṉamum amikkappaṭṭaṉa. kaḻakam ki. pi. 1887-ām āṇṭu tuvaṅkiyatu. kōṭikkāṉalil makkaḷ toki perukiyatum, ovvoru camayattārum taṅkaḷukkeṉat taṉiyāṉa amippukaḷi niṟuvikkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl, ik kaḻakam kavaṉippāraṟṟuc ciṟitu kālam kiṭantatu. kaḻaka muṉṉēṟṟattukkākat taṉippaṭṭārum, irāṇuva atikārikaḷum, poruḷutavi ceyya maṟuttuviṭṭaṉar. iti yaṟinta maturi amerikka miṣaṉic cārntavarkaḷ mīṇṭum ik kaḻakattiṉpāl ūkkam kāṭṭiṉar. kaḻakattiṉmītu cumattiyirunta kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷiyum nīkkiṉar. piṟaku ik kaḻakam nalla muṟiyil vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu. kaṭṭiṭam mēlum periyatākavum, vacatiyuṭiyatākavum kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. vēṟu pala maṭṭip pantāṭṭa mitāṉaṅkaḷum tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭaṉa. kaḻakak kaṭṭiṭattil oru potu aṟiyum, oru paṭikkum aṟiyum, pilliyārṭu viḷiyāṭum oru aṟiyum āṇkaḷukkāka amintuḷḷaṉa. cīṭṭāṭṭa aṟi (Playing cards room) oṉṟum, oppaṉi aṟi (dressing room) oṉṟum peṇkaḷukkāka amintuḷḷaṉa. ik kaṭṭiṭattiṉ naṭuvil amintuḷḷa aṟi mikavum periyatu. atil oru mēṭiyum amintuḷḷatu. kōṭikkāṉalil naṭipeṟum mukkiyak kūṭṭaṅkaḷum, nāṭakaṅkaḷum iṅkutāṉ iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

kōṭikkāṉal pātirimār kaḻakam :

naṉṟākap paṇiyāṟṟum kaḻakaṅkaḷil kōṭikkāṉal pātirimār kaḻaka (Kodikanal Missionary Union)mum oṉṟākum. ki. pi. 1890-ām āṇṭu cepṭampar tiṅkaḷ putukkōṭṭi aracariṉ tampiyāṉa turirājā avarkaḷi ṭākṭar ḵpērpēṅk avarkaḷum, ṭākṭar rēci avarkaḷum 'ārkōṭiyā' (Arcotia) eṉṟa iṭattil oru paṭippakattiyum oru kaḻakattiyum tiṟantu vippataṟkāka aḻittaṉar. ataṉ piṟaku am mūvarum pūppantu, maṭṭip pantu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷiṉ poruṭṭum, puttakattēnīr viruntiṉ (book-teas) poruṭṭum aṭikkaṭi kūṭiṉar. ik kaḻakam ciṟitu vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu. 'rākkāṭṭēj' eṉṟa iṭattil ik kaḻakam ciṟitu nāḷ amintiruntatu. makkaḷ toki kōṭikkāṉalil kuṟivāka iruntapōtu pātirimār, potumakkaḷ, āṅkilēyar, amerikkar ākiya ellōrum neruṅkiya kūṭṭuṟavōṭu iruntaṉar. āṉāl makkaḷ toki perukiyavuṭaṉ ak kūṭṭuṟavu citiyat toṭaṅkiyatu. ataṉāl aṅku vāḻnta tiruvāḷarkaḷ vikkāḵp. ṭiyati, hēkker, jōṉs, ṭākṭar kēmpel mutaliyōr maṟupaṭiyum ik kaḻakatti , 'ārkōṭiyā lāṭjil tuvakkiṉar. ik kaḻakattiṟkuc contamāka oru kaṭṭiṭam niṟuva muṭivu ceytaṉar. hikiḷerk paḷḷikkarukiluḷḷa piyar tōṭṭattil 'viṉsṭaṉ' eṉṟa kaṭṭiṭatti vilikku vāṅkiṉar. paḷḷiyiṉ arukil ik kaḻakam amikkappaṭṭatāl maṭṭip pantāṭṭam āṭa ellōrukkum vacati ēṟpaṭṭatu. piṟaku putitāka ōr iṭattiyē vilikku vāṅki, ip poḻutuḷḷa kaḻakak kaṭṭiṭattiṟkuk kal nāṭṭu viḻāvic ceytaṉar. ak kaṭṭiṭattiṟkuk kal nāṭṭiyavar, kōṭikkāṉaliṉ nīṇṭakāla vāciyāṉa pēlis tāmcaṉ eṉṟa ammiyār. ik kaṭṭiṭattil ippōtu akaṉṟa oru naṭumuṟṟamum, nūl niliyamum, oru camayalaṟiyum, āṭavar makaḷirkkuriya taṉi aṟikaḷum, ōyvu peṟum aṟi oṉṟum, ceyaṟ kuḻu naṭipeṟum aṟi oṉṟum amintuḷḷaṉa, akaṉṟa naṭumuṟṟam, vārantōṟum putaṉkiḻamiyaṉṟu tēnīr viruntu naṭattuvataṟkum, kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, mānāṭukaḷ naṭattuvataṟkum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. itil 1200 uṟuppiṉarkaḷ uḷḷaṉar. kōṭikkālaṅkaḷil nāṉūṟu pērkaḷukkuk kuṟiyāmal iṅku varukiṉṟaṉar. itil pātirimārkaḷallāta potu makkaḷum niṟiya uḷḷaṉar.

ik kaḻakamāṉatu āṇṭutōṟum, pala kuṟippiṭattakka paṇikaḷip purikiṟatu. camūkaviyal paṟṟiya mānāṭukaḷōṭu, maruttuvam, kalvi, toḻil ākiyavi campantamāṉa mānāṭukaḷiyum itu naṭatti vikkiṟatu. teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa pirāṭasṭaṇṭu kiruttava caṅkaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟkum pālamāka iruntu itu iṇittu vikkiṟatu, teṉṉintiyāviṉ pala pakutikaḷilumuḷḷa kiruttavap pātirimārkaḷ āṇṭukkorumuṟi oṉṟu kūṭuvataṟkuriya nalvāyppiyum itu ēṟpaṭuttukiṟatu.



kuḻippantāṭṭak kaḻakam :

ki. pi. 1880-ām āṇṭil kōṭikkāṉalil oru kuḻippantāṭṭa mitāṉam amikka muyaṉṟaṉar. āṉāl ammuyaṟci palaṉaḷikkavilli. ki. pi. 1895-ām āṇṭu, kuḻippantāṭṭattil ārvamuṭiya palar kōṭikkāṉal kaḻakattil kūṭiṉar. akkūṭṭattiṟku je. ṭapiḷyū. eḵp. ṭi yūmerkiyū eṉpavar talimi tāṅkiṉār. tūṇpāṟip pātiyil mitāṉam amikkappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1926 ām āṇṭu ikkaḻakattiṟkāka oru kaṭṭiṭamum kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. kaṭṭiṭattiṟ ketiril mitāṉamum ciṟappāka amikkappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 1930-ām āṇṭu mēt tiṅkaḷ 7- ām nāḷ vīciya puyal ik kaḻakattiṉ kūrikaḷip piritteṟintatōṭu kaṭṭiṭattiṟkum kēṭu viḷittatu. āṉāl puyal vīciyatu naṉmikkē eṉṟu colla vēṇṭum. piṟaku naṉkoṭi niṟiya vacūl ceyyappaṭṭup periyatum, aḻakāṉatumāṉa maṟṟoru kaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭi muṭikkappaṭṭatu. ikkaḻakak kaṭṭiṭatti muṉṉiṉṟu kaṭṭi muṭitta ī. ō. kiṅ eṉpavar ikkaḻaka vaḷarccikkup peritum pāṭupaṭṭār.

intiyar kaḻakam :

ki. pi. 1901-ām āṇṭuk kaṇakkuppaṭi 1900 kuṭiyiruppukaḷ kōṭikkāṉalil iruntaṉa. aṅku vāḻnta intiyaril perumpālōr vaṇikarkaḷākavum, maravēli ceyvōrkaḷākavum, kottarkaḷākavum, tāḷāḷar {Clerks)kaḷākavum, vēlikkārarkaḷākavum iruntaṉar. nakarāṭci maṉṟam vaḷarcciyuṟṟatum, atil paṇipurivataṟkākap paṭitta intiyarkaḷ mikutiyākat tēvippaṭṭaṉar, kōṭikkāṉaliṉ aḻakip paṟṟiyum, varuvāy nalkum kāḵpit tōṭṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum kēḷviyuṟṟa intiya nāṭṭuc celvarkaḷ mikutiyāka iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟit taṅkaḷukkeṉa māḷikikaḷi amikkat toṭaṅkiṉar. ṭellip pārāḷu maṉṟattiṉ mēlcapi uṟuppiṉa (Member of council of states)rākap paṇiyāṟṟiyavar ṭēviṭ tēvatās eṉpavar. ki. pi. 1903-ām āṇṭu vāṉāyvuk kūṭattiṉ etiril 'otukkam' (Odookum) eṉṟa kaṭṭiṭattik kaṭṭiṉār. ilaṅki araciyalāriṉ ceyaṟkuḻu (Executive council of the Ceylon Government) uṟuppiṉarāka irunta car. pi. irāmanātaṉ kiḷeṉ vīḻccikku arukil tam māḷikiyik kaṭṭiṉār. ivvāṟu intiya makkaḷiṉ toki kōṭikkāṉalil perukiyatu.

ki. pi. 1915-ām āṇṭu intiyak kuṭumpaṅkaḷellām oṉṟu cērntu taṅkaḷukkeṉa oru taṉik kaḻakattiyum, oru paṭakuk kaḻakattiyum ēṟpaṭuttiṉar. ippaṇiyi muṉṉiṉṟu naṭatti vittavar ceṉṉiyic cārnta car. ṭi. vi. cēṣakiri aiyar eṉpavar. avaruṭiya uruvappaṭam intiyar kaḻakak kaṭṭiṭattil toṅkik koṇṭirukkiṟatu. mutaṉ mutalil kaḻakak kaṭṭiṭattil oru ciṟiya aṟimaṭṭum iruntatu. kaḻakattōṭu iṇinta maṭṭip pantāṭṭa mitāṉam oṉṟum iruntatu. cila āṇṭukaḷil uṟuppiṉar toki uyarntatu. kaḻakattiṟkuc contamāka oru nalla kaṭṭiṭamum kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. tiruvāḷar pālacuppiramaṇiya aiyar taṟpōtu ceyalāḷarāka irukkiṟār. ivar kaṭanta muppatu āṇṭukaḷāka intiyar kaḻakattiṉ uṟuppiṉarāka iruntuvarukiṟār. avaruṭiya naṇparkaḷ avarik 'kōṭikkāṉaliṉ mutiyōr' eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. es. cīṉivāca ayyaṅkār, car. ṭi. vijaya rākavāccāriyār, car. ē. laṭcumaṇacāmi mutaliyār, kē. eṉ. aiyar, jaṉāp aptul kātar, jasṭis cantiracēkara aiyar ākiya periyārkaḷ, ikkaḻakattiṉ kuṟippiṭattakka uṟuppiṉarkaḷ.
